oṃ

kāśmīra saṃskṛtagranthāvaliḥ |

granthāṅkaḥ 64

śrīmadbhagavadgītā

rājānakarāmakaṇṭhaviracitasarvatobhadrākhyavivaraṇopetā |

śrīrājarājeśvara mahārājādhirāja kāśmīranarendra śrīharisiṃhajī-
bahādurājñayā

risarca kāryālayādhyakṣa- paṇḍita madhusūdanakaula śāstriṇā

uddiṣṭakāryālayasthetarapaṇḍitasahāyena

saṃśodhanādisaṃskaraṇottaraṃ

saṃpādya

mumbayyāṃ nirṇayasāgara mudraṇālaye mudrāpayitvā 
prākāśyamupanītā |

saṃvat 2000,		kāśmīra śrīnagara		sraistābdaḥ 1943,

(asya granthasya mudrāpaṇādyadhikārāḥ proktamahārājacaryaiḥ 
svāyattīkṛtāḥ santi)






śrīsomānandanāthaprabhṛtiguruvarādiṣṭasannītimārgo
labdhvā yatraiva samyakpaṭimani ghaṭanāmīśvarādvaitavādaḥ |
kāśmīrebhyaḥ prasṛtya prakaṭaparimalo rañjayansarvadeśān
deśe'nyasminnadṛṣṭo ghusṛṇayisaravatsarvavandyatvamāpa || 1 ||

tarata tarasā saṃsārābdhiṃ vidhatta pare pade
padamavicalaṃ nityālokapramodasunirbhare |
vimṛśata śivādiṣṭādvaitāvabodhasudhārasaṃ
prasabhavilasatsadyuktyāntaḥsamutplavadāyinam || 2 ||


atha

śrīmadbhagavadgītā |

śrīrājānakarāmakaṇṭhaviracitavivaraṇopetā |

prathamo'dhyāyaḥ |


svajyotirdyotitānantanāmarūpāya te namaḥ |
anāmarūpacinmātravapuṣe paramātmane || 1 ||

pramātṝṇāmudyadvividharucibhedācchuraṇayā
vicitrābhāsatvaṃ sphaṭikataṭavatsaṃśrayati yat |
svarūpaṃ (k, g: svarūpaṃ sarveśamiti pāṭhaḥ |) sarveṣāṃ 
vigalitavikalpābhrapaṭala-
prasanne hṛdvyomni sphuratu jagatāṃ tatsvavapuṣā || 2 ||

yasmādvyomna ivāprameyavimalābhogājjagadvyaktayo
nānādarśanavādavistaragiraḥ sthātuṃ bahirna kṣamāḥ |
tadbrahmaiva paraṃ parāśarasutasyaivaṃ maharṣermataṃ
santaḥsantatasanniveśitadhiyo dhyāyantu bandhacchide || 3 ||

sakalavibudhamadhye ke'pi gītāpadānāṃ
viditaguṇaviśeṣā hantaḥ santaḥ kṛtārthāḥ |
katicidaliṣu loke te hi valganti lakṣmī-
karakamalamadhūnāṃ carvaṇe ye rasajñāḥ || 4 ||

prakṛtigurubhirgāḍhodyogairavandhyasamādhibhi-
rbahubhiramalaprajñairetāḥ kṛtāḥ kila vṛttayaḥ |
p. 2) kimadhikamiha brūyānmādṛkkimatra tu kurmahe
svamatisuhṛdo mugdhānāṃ no yadatra viḍambakāḥ || 5 ||

prasannatvādasminsugama iti sarvaḥ praviśati
praviṣṭo gāmbhīryādanadhigatagādho'tra patati |
tadevaṃ gītārthārṇavamavatitīrṣuṃ prati janaṃ
vidhāsye vākyārthānvayamiṣamimaṃ potamabhayam || 6 ||

nityānyonyaviruddhadarśanakathākṣepādinā kaḥ kṣamaḥ
saṃmugdhapratikūladurmadadhiyāṃ prauḍho'pi saṃbodhane |
tatsiddhāntatayaiva bhaktyatiśayavyakteśvarānugraha-
grastaprāyasamagrasaṃśayamalaḥ ko'pyatra bodhyo janaḥ || 7 ||

	iha khalu 
nikhilavedavedāntasiddhāntaramṛtītihāsapurāṇādivividhaśāstravistaravip
rakīrṇaṃ caturvargopadeśaṃ 
kālāpacitaprajñādyupadeśyapuruṣapratipattisaukaryāya sārataḥ 
saṃkṣipya, mahābhāratākhyamitihāsaṃ bhagavān kṛṣṇadvaipāyano 
yadivā tanmūrtimāviṣṭaḥ parameśvara eva sākṣāt nibabandha | tatrāpica 
apavargasya parapuruṣārthatvādupādeyatamasya 
mokṣadharmādiprakaraṇeṣu vividhaiḥ prakārabhedaiḥ pratipādyasya api 
vineyabuddhibhiḥ samyak pratipattumaśakyatvamāśaṅkamāno 
mṛdutaramatīnāmapi hṛdayaṃgamena prasannagambhīreṇa vākprabandhena 
itihāsakramāgatasuhṛtsaṃvādakathārūpeṇa yogyākhilalokopadeśāya 
bhagavadvaktṛkamapavargapratipādakaṃ gītākhyaṃ prakaraṇaṃ 
pratipādayāṃcakāra | yasya ayaṃ 
samastādhyātmaśāstropaniṣadbhūtaḥ siddhāntaḥ ātmaivedaṃ | 
sarvamiti yataḥ paramarṣirayamasminneva mahābhārate 
dehasaṃnyāsāvasarasamāhṛtaniruttarasamādhidaśānubhūtaparamārthā
modādhivāsitasuvimalamativacanagāṅgeyagītastavarājābhidhānabhagava
tstotraddhāreṇa tatsvarūpaṃ samastavyastamabhidhāya api upasaṃhāre

p. 3) yasminsarvaṃ yataḥ sarvaṃ yaḥ sarvaṃ sarvataśca yaḥ |
yaśca sarvamayo nityaṃ tasmai sarvātmane namaḥ ||

iti ślokena paramasārasaṃgraharūpaṃ yamarthamuddiṣṭavān, tameva iha 
prakaraṇe garbhīcakāra |  tathāca imaṃ ślokaṃ 
paramārthavivecanavicakṣaṇā evaṃ vyācakṣate- tasmai 
yacchabdapañcakaviśiṣṭaviśeṣaṇapratipādyasvarūpāya sarvātmane 
namaḥ sarvasya ātmane, sarvamātmā yasya iti 
ubhayathāvṛttisamāśrayaṇena paramakāraṇasya ātmana eva 
parameśvarasya vāstavamadvitīyatvamasahāyatvaṃ ca anena padena 
pratipāditam | yaduktamiha

yo māṃ paśyati sarvatra sarvaṃ ca mayi paśyati |
tasyāhaṃ na praṇaśyāmi sa ca me na praṇaśyati || (6/31)

iti,

sarvabhūtasthamātmānaṃ sarvabhūtāni cātmani |
īkṣate yogayuktātmā sarvatra samadarśanaḥ || (6/30)

ityādi | kasmai tasmai nama iti ekatve'pi 
sarvajñatvādiniratiśayaiśvaryapratipādakāni yacchabdaviśiṣṭāni 
viśeṣaṇāni krameṇa vyākhyāyante | tatra tasmai bhagavate namo yasmin 
sarvam idamatyantabhinnānantābhāsamayaṃ bhāvajātaṃ 
saṃvedyamānatāmātranibandhanatattatsvātmalābhaṃ sat 
sukhādisarvāvasthānugataikasaṃveditṛtvamātrasvabhāve prakāśātmani 
yasmin sthitamiti śeṣaḥ, nānātvameva idaṃ yadvaśāditthaṃ 
parisphuratītyarthaḥ | tasmai nama iti saṃbandhaḥ | anena 
samastasaṃvedyapadārthasārthaikasaṃvedakatvapratipādanapareṇa 
viśeṣaṇena īśvarasya ātmanaḥ sarvajñatvalakṣaṇo dharmaḥ śaktiviśeṣo 
vā pratipāditaḥ | tadetadihaiva uktaṃ sarvajñatvamekamātmānaṃ (k, g: 
sarvajñamekamiti pāṭhaḥ) vyapadiśatā bhagavatā

p. 4) sarvasya cāhaṃ hṛdi saṃniviṣṭo
mattaḥ smṛtirjñānamapohanaṃ ca | (15/15)

ityādinā, tathā

	matsthāni sarvabhūtāni ---------------- | (9/4)

ityādinā ca | etena ca nānājñātṛtvavādaḥ, kṣaṇike jñāne eva 
jñātṛbhramavādaḥ, tathā caitanyaviśiṣṭe śarīre eva jñātṛtvavādo 
nirastaḥ | nanu astu nāma kaścidekaḥ sarvajño yena saṃvedyamānamidaṃ 
sarvamavatiṣṭhate, kiṃtu yadidaṃ sarvaṃ tena saṃvedyate, tat kuto jātaṃ ? 
kastasya kartā ? iti saṃśayanirāsāya tasyaiva 
sarvakartṛtvapratipādanārthaṃ viśeṣaṇamāha yataḥ sarvam iti | yato 
yasmādekasmāt kartuḥ sarvamidaṃ kāryaṃ prabhavatīti śeṣaḥ | anena 
vakṣyamāṇayā yuktyā ekasya ca uktasya tattvasya sarvakartṛtvaṃ 
pratipāditam | tathāca ihaiva uktaṃ bhagavatā

mayā tatamidaṃ sarvaṃ jagadavyaktamūrtinā | (9/4)

ityādinā, tathā

na tadasti vinā yatsyānmayā bhūtaṃ carācaram || (10/39)

ityādinā ca, tathāca 
bhagavadanugrahonmiṣitadivyadṛgdṛṣṭavaiśvarūpyaparāmarśaprathitapara
vācaḥ savyasācino vacanādapi

nānyo'sti kartā jagatastvameko
dhātā vidhātā ca vibhurbhavaśca || (11/47)

ityādināpica | etena sarvajñasya akartṛtvavādo'pi nirastaḥ | nanu 
sarvakartā yadidaṃ karoti, tatkimupādāya karoti, ityāśaṃkāṃ nirasituṃ 
tasyā nirupādānaṃ sarvakartṛtvaṃ pratipādayitumanyadviśeṣaṇamāha 
yaḥ sarvam iti | sarvatvena nānābhāvabhedātmanā prasṛtamapi idaṃ 
kāryaṃ ya eva yata uktalakṣaṇāt vastuno na vyatiriktamityarthaḥ,

p. 5) tasmai namaḥ | anena 
svecchāmātravyatiriktopādānarūpavastvantaranirapekṣatvamasya 
pratipāditam | uktaṃ ca etadarjunoktyaiva iha

kartāsi sarvasya yataḥ svayaṃ vai
vibho tataḥ sarvamidaṃ tvameva || (11/48)

ityādi | etena ca māyāprakṛtyādyupādānādhīnakartṛtvavādo nirastaḥ | 
nanu bhavatu nāma evaṃ nirupādānamasya kartṛtvaṃ, kiṃtu yadidaṃ 
kāryamitthaṃ parasparavilakṣaṇabhaṅgurabhāvabhedātmanā avabhāsate, 
tat tasya svarūpavikriyāṃ kasmāt na karotītyāha sarvataśca yaḥ iti | 
sarvasmin vedye vastuni ya eva ekaḥ sphurati, tasmai namaḥ iti | anena 
tadīyādbhutamāyāśaktijanitavyāvyāmohāvasthāyāmeva sarvamidaṃ 
kāryaṃ saṃsāriṇā janena nānātvena pratipadyate, 
paramakāraṇānugrahaśaktyunmīlitavidyātmakadivyacakṣuṣā tu 
prabuddhena nirūpyamāṇe'smin sarvasmin kārye tadekatattvavyatiriktaṃ na 
kiñcit prakāśate, iti pratipāditam | yathā tattadarthakriyāviśiṣṭena 
bālādinā lokena vilokyamānāḥ kaṭakakuṇḍalakeyūrādayo 
guṇībhūtakāñcanamayasvabhāvāḥ parasparabhinnatvenaiva 
pratibhāsante, utpannatatprakṛtijijñāsairnirūpyamāṇeṣu teṣu sarveṣu 
guṇībhūtakaṭakādibhinnavyaktikāñcanamayaikasvarūpavyatiriktaṃ 
paramāṇumātramapi na upalabhyate, tathaiva māyādaśāyāṃ 
bhinneṣvapi prabodhāvasthāyāṃ pratyavamṛśyamāneṣu kāryeṣu 
sarvabhāveṣu viśuddhasaṃvinmātrasvabhāvatāvyatiriktaṃ na 
kiñcidupalabhyate- iti vāstavena rūpeṇa itthamapi sphuradidaṃ sarvaṃ 
kāryaṃ karturmanāgapi svarūpavikriyāṃ na āvahatīti pratipāditam | 
tadetadihaiva uktam

avyakto'yamacintyo'yamavikāryo'yamucyate | (2/26)

ityādinā | etena aupādhikaśaṅkāsaṃparkavādo'pi apākṛtaḥ | nanu

p. 6) evaṃ satyapi kārye nirupādhirayamātmā bhavatu nāma, yadā 
punaridaṃ kāryaṃ na ārabhate, tadā asya kiṃ 
rūpamityāśaṅkānirākaraṇāya sadā sarvātmakatvapratipādanārthaṃ 
viśeṣaṇāntaramāha yaśca sarvamayo nityam iti | 
pūrvoktaviśeṣaṇapratipāditena prakāreṇa yo nityaṃ satatameva 
sarvamayaḥ sarvātmakaḥ, tasmai nama iti | anena sphuratyapi tasmin 
anantadevatādbhutarūpe kāryakalāpe 
paranirmalasusaṃpūrṇacinmātrasvarūpāparicyuterīśvarasya ātmanaḥ 
prayojanavicāravādo'pi parihṛtaḥ | yaduktaṃ

na me pārthāsti kartavyaṃ ----------------|
----------------------pravarte'thaca karmaṇi || (3/21)

iti, tathā

na māṃ karmāṇi limpanti na me kāmaḥ phaleṣvapi |
iti māṃ yo'bhijānāti karmabhirna sa badhyate || (4/14)

ityādyapica | sarvamekaḥ sarvajñaḥ sarvakartā nirupādāno 
nirupādhirnityaṃ sarvātmakatvena avabhāsate iti | anena ślokena ātmana 
eva parameśvarasya svarūpaṃ yat tatra paramarṣiṇā pratipāditam, tadeva 
iha siddhāntatayā garbhīkṛtamiti veditavyam | tadayamevaṃsvabhāvo 
bhagavānātmā svecchāmātropakaraṇo yogīndrasargavat 
svapnapadārthavat manorājyaviracyamānavicitrabhāvābhāsavadvā 
ananyasādhāraṇaniratiśayakrīḍāmātraprayojano yadidamatyadbhutaṃ 
jagaccitramullikhati, tatra tadicchayaiva niyamitā prakriyā iyaṃ 
prakaraṇe'smin garbhīkṛtetyapi veditavyam | tathāhi 
svecchāmātrādhīnakartṛtvāt tasyaiva parasya tattvasya mukhyayā vṛttyā 
vedyatāvabhāsanamātreṇa jagataḥ kāraṇatvaṃ vyavasthitam | yadāha 
bhagavāneva

bījaṃ māṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ viddhi pārtha sanātanam | (7/10)

p. 7) ityādi, tathā

yadā bhūtapṛthagbhāvamekasthamanupaśyati |
tata evaca vistāraṃ brahma saṃpadyate tadā || (13/31)

iti, tathā

------------------ahaṃ bījapradaḥ pitā | (14/4)

iti, tathā

ahaṃ kṛtsnasya jagataḥ prabhavaḥ pralayastathā || (7/6)

ityādi | tatastadīya eva yathāpratipāditaḥ svabhāvo 
vedyatvamātrasāmānyalakṣaṇaṃ jagat 
jaḍacetanavibhaktasthūlasūkṣmaprakṛtyātmakatayā dvidhā avabhāsayati | 
yadāha

bhūmirāpo'nalo vāyuḥ khaṃ mano buddhirevaca |
ahaṃkāra itīyaṃ me bhinnā prakṛtiraṣṭadhā ||

apareyamitastvanyāṃ prakṛtiṃ viddhi me parām |
jīvabhūtāṃ mahābāho yayedaṃ dhāryate jagat || (7/5)

iti | etayośca prakṛtyorjaḍātmako yaḥ sthūlo bhedastamapi kāraṇātmanā 
kāryātmanā ca dvidhā sa eva bhagavānābhāsayati | tatra sarvasya 
vedyasya vastunaḥ sukhādyātmanā paryavasitasvarūpatvāt 
sattvādiguṇatrayamayamekaṃ sāmānyamanugatatvena yadavasthitaṃ, 
tadavāntarakāraṇatayā avabhāsayati | yacca 
mahadādipṛthivyantamanugamya kāryakāraṇakalāpātmakaśarīrabhāvena 
śabdādiviṣayabhāvena ca pariṇataṃ, tat kāryatayā avabhāsayati | 
yaduktaṃ

mayādhyakṣeṇa prakṛtiḥ sūyate sacarācaram || (9/11)

iti | yaśca cetanātmako bhedastamapi āsurasargatayā daivasargatayā ca 
dviprakāraṃ tāvadavabhāsayati | yaduktaṃ

dvau bhūtasargau loke'smindaiva āsura evaca || (16/6)

p. 8) ityādi | tatra āsurasargo'dhamottamabhedena dviprakāraḥ | 
tayoratyantanyagbhūtasattvarajaskatamomātrabahalo mlecchādirūpo 
nikṛṣṭa āsurasargaḥ, yaṃ rākṣasasaṃjñayā vakṣyati | tadapekṣayā 
rajoleśānugatatamaḥpradhāno manuṣyātmakaḥ kiṃcitprakṛṣṭo'paraḥ, 
yasya evaṃ lakṣaṇamuktaṃ

pravṛttiṃ ca nivṛttiṃ ca janā na vidurāsurāḥ |
na śaucaṃ nāpicācāro na satyaṃ teṣu vidyate ||

asatyamapratiṣṭhaṃ te jagadāhuranīśvaram |
aparasparasaṃbhūtamakiṃcitkamahetukam || (16/8)

ityādi

--------------------pradviṣanto'bhyasūyakāḥ | (16/18)

ityantam | svakrīḍāmapi yadviṣayāmevamāha bhagavān

tānahaṃ dviṣataḥ krūrānsaṃsāreṣu narādhamān |
kṣipāmyajasramaśubhāsvāsurīṣveva bhūmiṣu ||

āsurīṃ yonimāpannā mūḍhā janmani janmani |
māmaprāpyaiva kaunteya tato yāntyadhamāṃ gatim || (16/20)

iti | evaṃ tāvat dviprakāra āsuraḥ sargaḥ | daivamapi sargaṃ 
devādimanuṣyabhedena dviprakāraṃ tāvadavabhāsayati | tatrāpi 
devādyātmakaḥ prakāro dvividhaḥ | ekaḥ indrādidevatāviśeṣātmako 
yastattadadhikāranirvartanāvadhistāsu tāsu kriyāsu niyuktaḥ 
svādhikārāvasānaṃ kālamativāhya svakāraṇe pariṇamati | dvitīyo 
manuṣyatvopāttatattadyajñādikriyāphalabhūtasukhamātrānubhavamaya.
m teṣu teṣu lokāntareṣu bhogamupabhuñjānaḥ svakarmaphalāvasānaṃ 
kālamativāhya punaḥ punaḥ karmabhūmiṃ pratipadyate | ubhayarūpo'pi 
ayaṃ sattvaguṇapradhānaḥ | evaṃ guṇaviśeṣasya tāratamyādānantyācca 
sarvasargabhedānāmānantyamavaseyam | manuṣyarūpo'pi daivaḥ sargo 
dvividhaḥ | tatra ekaḥ 

p. 9) sattvaleśānugatarajoguṇapradhāno yaḥ pravṛttimārgonmukhatvāt 
tattvajñānānunmeṣe sati sattvaleśānugatarajoguṇavaśāt 
trayyādiśāstracoditayajñadānatapaḥprabhṛtikriyaikapradhānaḥ (k, g: 
pravaṇaḥ iti pāṭhaḥ |) saṃsāramanubhavati | yasya 
jñānānarhatvādevamupakramya lakṣaṇamuktaṃ

vyavasāyātmikā buddhirekaiva kurunandana |
bahuśākhā hyanantāśca buddhayo'vyavasāyinām ||

yāmimāṃ puṣpitāṃ vācaṃ pravadantyavipaścitaḥ |
vedavādaratāḥ pārtha nānyadastītivādinaḥ ||

kāmātmānaḥ svargaparā janmakarmaphalepsavaḥ |
kriyāviśeṣabahulā bhogaiśvaryagatīḥ prati ||

bhogaiśvaryaprasaktānāṃ tayāpahatacetasām |
vyavasāyātmikā buddhiḥ samādhau na vidhīyate || (2/45)

iti | tathā kriyāmātranirdiṣṭatvamuddiśya uktaṃ

traividyā māṃ somapāḥ pūtapāpā
yajñairiṣṭvā svargatiṃ prārthayante |
te puṇyamāsādya surendraloka-
maśnanti divyāndivi devabhogān ||

te taṃ bhūktvā svargalokaṃ viśālaṃ
kṣīṇe puṇye martyalokaṃ viśanti |
evaṃ trayīdharmamanuprapannā
gatāgataṃ kāmakāmā labhante || (9/22)

iti | tathā tānprati svakriyākrīḍāmapi bhagavānanenaiva āha yata 
īśvarecchayaiva te tathā saṃsāritāṃ saṃprati anubhavanti | dvitīyo 
manuṣyātmako daivaḥ sargabhedo rajoleśānugatasattvapradhāno yaḥ

p. 10) prabalavikalpānilaniścalatvāt kulaśailasthairyābhyāṃ 
puraḥsarībhyāṃ 
bhaktiśraddhābhyāmutsāryamāṇasandehādighoravighnaughāvaraṇatvād
abhivyañjitapratyāsannaparameśvaradarśanamahotsava eva 
evaṃkṛtalakṣaṇatayā iha nirdiṣṭaḥ

abhayaṃ sattvasaṃśuddhirjñānayogavyavasthitiḥ |
dānaṃ damaśca yajñaśca svādhyāyastapa ārjavam ||

ahiṃsā satyamakrodhastyāgo'saktirapaiśunam |
dayā bhūteṣvalaulyaṃ ca mārdavaṃ hrīracāpalam ||

tejaḥ kṣamā dhṛtistuṣṭiradroho'nabhimānitā |
bhavanti sampadaṃ daivīmabhijātasya bhārata || (16/3)

ityādinā | tathāvidha eva hi 
prārabdhaprakaraṇapratipādyaparamarahasyopadeśaviṣayatayā iha 
parigṛhītaḥ | yadāha upadeśyaṃ prati

evaṃ vivasvate yogaṃ proktavānahamavyayam || (4/1)

ityādi upakramya

sa evāyaṃ mayā te'dya yogaḥ proktaḥ purātanaḥ |
bhakto'si me sakhā ceti rahasyaṃ caitaduttamam || (4/3)

ityādi, tathā

caturvidhā bhajante māṃ janāḥ sukṛtinaḥ sadā || (7/16)

ityādi, tathā prakaraṇaparisamāptau

siddhiṃ prāpto yadā brahma samāsena nibodha me |
samāsena tu kaunteya niṣṭhā jñānasya yā parā || (18/50)

iti upakramya

samaḥ sarveṣu bhūteṣu madbhaktiṃ labhate parām | 
bhaktyā māmabhijānāti so'haṃ yaścāsmi tattvataḥ || (18/55)

iti, tathā śraddhādiviṣayamapi āha

p. 11) śraddhāvā/llabhate jñānaṃ saṃyatastatparendriyaḥ |
jñānaṃ labdhvā parāṃ śāntimacireṇādhigacchati || (4/39)

iti, tathā

śraddhāvanto'nasūyanto mucyante sarvakilviṣaiḥ | (3/31)

iti, tathā

śraddadhānā matparamā bhaktāste'tīva me priyāḥ || (13/20)

ityādi ca | itthaṃ sargabhedeṣu avasthiteṣu ayaṃ samanantarapratipādito 
daivasargabhedo manuṣyātmako yādṛgasya upadeśasya viṣayatvena 
parigṛhītaḥ, tasya tāvat svarūpamupakṣipyate

dhṛtarāṣṭra uvāca

dharmakṣetre kurukṣetre sarvakṣatrasamāgame |
māmakāḥ pāṇḍavāścaiva kimakurvata sañjaya || 1 ||

sañjaya uvāca

dṛṣṭvā tu pāṇḍavānīkaṃ vyūḍhaṃ duryodhanastadā |
ācāryamupasaṃgamya rājā vacanamabravīt || 2 ||

paśyaitāṃ pāṇḍuputrāṇāmācārya mahatīṃ camūm |
vyūḍhāṃ drupadaputreṇa tava śiṣyeṇa dhīmatā || 3 ||

atra śūrā maheṣvāsā bhīmārjunasamā yudhi |
yuyudhāno virāṭaśca drupadaśca mahārathaḥ || 4 ||

dhṛṣṭaketuścekitānaḥ kāśirājaśca vīryavān |
purujitkuntibhojaśca śaivyaśca narapuṅgavaḥ || 5 ||

yudhāmanyuśca vikrānta uttamaujāśca vīryavān |
saubhadro draupadeyāśca sarva eva mahārathāḥ || 6 ||

asmākaṃ tu viśiṣṭā ye tānnibodha dvijottama |
nāyakā mama sainyasya saṃjñārthaṃ tānbravīmi te || 7 ||

p. 12) sainye mahati ye sarve netāraḥ śūrasaṃmatāḥ |
bhavānbhīṣmaśca karṇaśca kṛpaḥ śalyo jayadrathaḥ || 8 ||

aśvatthāmā vikarṇaśca saumadattiśca vīryavān |
anye ca bahavaḥ śūrā madarthe tyaktajīvitāḥ || 9 ||

nānāśastrapraharaṇā nānāyuddhaviśāradāḥ |
aparyāptaṃ tvidameteṣāṃ balaṃ bhīṣmābhirakṣitam || 10 ||

paryāptaṃ tvidameteṣāṃ balaṃ bhīṣmābhirakṣitam | 
ayaneṣu ca sarveṣu yathābhāgamavasthitāḥ || 11 ||

bhīṣmamevābhirakṣantu bhavantaḥ sarva eva hi |
tasya saṃjanayanharṣaṃ kuruvṛddhaḥ pitāmahaḥ || 12 ||

siṃhanādaṃ vinadyoccaiḥ śaṃkhaṃ dadhmau pratāpavān |
tataḥ śaṃkhāśca bheryaśca paṇavānakagomukhāḥ || 13 ||

sahasaivābhyahanyanta sa śabdastumulo'bhavat |
tataḥ śvetairhayairyukte mahati syandane sthitau || 14 ||

mādhavaḥ pāṇḍavaścaiva divyau śaṃkhau pradadhmatuḥ |
pāṃcajanyaṃ hṛṣīkeśo devadattaṃ dhanaṃjayaḥ || 15 ||

pauṇḍraṃ dadhmau mahāśaṃkhaṃ bhīmakarmā vṛkodaraḥ |
anantavijayaṃ rājā kuntīputro yudhiṣṭhiraḥ || 16 ||

nakulaḥ sahadevaśca sughoṣamaṇipuṣpakau |
kāśyaśca parameṣvāsaḥ śikhaṇḍī ca mahārathaḥ || 17 ||

dhṛṣṭadyumno virāṭaśca sātyakiścāparājitaḥ |
pāñcālaśca maheṣvāso draupadeyāśca paṃca ye || 18 ||

saubhadraśca mahābāhuḥ śaṃkhāndadhmuḥ pṛthak pṛthak |
sa ghoṣo dhārtarāṣṭrāṇāṃ hṛdayāṇi vyadārayat || 19 ||

p. 13) nabhaśca pṛthivīṃ caiva tumulo vyanunādayat |
atha vyavasthitān dṛṣṭvā dhārtarāṣṭrān kapidhvajaḥ || 20 ||

pravṛtte śastrasaṃpāte dhanurudyamya pāṇḍavaḥ |
hṛṣīkeśaṃ tadā vākyamidamāha mahīpate || 21 ||

arjuna uvāca 

senayorubhayormadhye rathaṃ sthāpaya me'cyuta |
yāvadetānnirīkṣe'haṃ yoddhukāmānavasthitān || 22 ||

kairmayā saha yoddhavyamasmin raṇasamudyame |
yotsyamānānavekṣe'haṃ ya ete'tra samāgatāḥ || 23 ||

dhārtarāṣṭrasya durbuddheryuddhe priyacikīrṣavaḥ |

saṃjaya uvāca

evamukto hṛṣīkeśo guḍākeśena bhārata || 24 ||

senayorubhayormadhye sthāpayitvā rathottamam |
bhīṣmadroṇapramukhataḥ sarveṣāṃ ca mahīkṣitām || 25 ||

uvāca pārtha paśyaitānsamavetānkurūniti |
tatrāpaśyatsthitānpārthaḥ pitṛnatha pitāmahān || 26 ||

ācāryānmātulānbhrātṛnputrānpautrānsakhīṃstathā |
śvaśurānsuhṛdaścaiva senayorubhayorapi || 27 ||

tānsamīkṣya sa kaunteyaḥ sarvānbandhūnavasthitān |
kṛpayā parayāviṣṭaḥ sīdamāno'bravīdidam || 28 ||

arjuna uvāca 

dṛṣṭvemānsvajanānkṛṣṇa yuyutsūnsamavasthitān |
sīdanti mama gātrāṇi mukhaṃ ca pariśuṣyati || 29 ||

vepathuśca śarīre me romaharṣaśca jāyate |
gāṇḍīvaṃ sraṃsate hastāttvakcaiva paridahyate || 30 ||

p. 14) na ca śaknomyavasthātuṃ bhramatīva ca me manaḥ |
nimittāni ca paśyāmi viparītāni keśava || 31 ||

naca śreyo'nupaśyāmi hatvā svajanamāhave |
na kāṃkṣe vijayaṃ kṛṣṇa naca rājyaṃ sukhāni ca || 32 ||

kiṃ no rājyena govinda kiṃ bhogairjīvitena kim | 
yeṣāmarthe kāṃkṣitaṃ no rājyaṃ bhogāḥ sukhāni ca || 33 ||

ta ime'vasthitā yuddhe prāṇāṃstyaktvā dhanāni ca |
ācāryāḥ pitaraḥ putrāstathaiva ca pitāmahāḥ || 34 ||

mātulāḥ śvaśurāḥ pautrāḥ syālāḥ saṃbandhinastathā | 
etānna hantumicchāmi ghnato'pi madhusūdana || 35 ||

api trailokyarājyasya hetoḥ kimu mahīkṛte |
nihatya dhārtarāṣṭrānnaḥ kā prītiḥ syājjanārdana || 36 ||

pāpamevāśrayedasmānhatvaitānātatāyinaḥ |
tasmānnārhā vayaṃ hantuṃ dhārtarāṣṭrānsvavāndhavān || 37 ||

svajanaṃ hi kathaṃ hatvā sukhinaḥ syāma mādhava | 
yadyapyete na paśyanti lobhopahatacetasaḥ || 38 ||

kulakṣayakṛtaṃ doṣaṃ mitradrohe ca pātakam |
kathaṃ na jñeyamasmābhiḥ pāpādasmānnivartitum || 39 ||

kulakṣayakṛtaṃ doṣaṃ prapaśyadbhirjanārdana |
kulakṣaye praṇaśyanti kuladharmāḥ sanātanāḥ || 40 ||

dharme naṣṭe kulaṃ kṛtsnamadharmo'bhibhavatyuta |
adharmābhibhavāt kṛṣṇa praduṣyanti kulastriyaḥ || 41 ||

strīṣu duṣṭāsu vārṣṇeya jāyate varṇasaṃkaraḥ |
saṃkaro narakāyaiva kulaghnānāṃ kulasya ca || 42 || 

p. 15) patanti pitaro hyeṣāṃ luptapiṇḍodakakriyāḥ |
doṣairetaiḥ kulaghnānāṃ varṇasaṃkarakārakaiḥ || 43 ||

utsādyante jātidharmāḥ kuladharmāśca śāśvatāḥ |
utsannakuladharmāṇāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ janārdana || 44 ||

narake niyataṃ vāso bhavatītyanuśuśruma |
aho bata mahatpāpaṃ kartuṃ vyavasitā vayam || 45 ||

yadrājyasukhalobhena hantuṃ svajanamudyatāḥ |
yadi māmapratīkāramaśastraṃ śastrapāṇayaḥ || 46 ||

dhārtarāṣṭrā raṇe hanyustanme kṣemataraṃ bhavet |

saṃjaya uvāca

evamuktvārjunaḥ saṃkhye rathopastha upāviśat || 47 ||

visṛjya saśaraṃ cāpaṃ śokasaṃvignamānasaḥ |

	iha khalu pratipāditasvarūpasya sarvakartuḥ parameśvarasya satataṃ 
kartṛtvāvilopāt kriyāśaktinityāvirahe suvyavasthite 
tanmāyāvaibhavodbhāvitabhedānāmapi jīvānāṃ 
paramārthatastadātmakatvāt tadvadeva kriyāśaktyavirahaḥ sarvadaiva 
sthitaḥ, iti sarvātmanā karmaṇāṃ parityāgānupapatteḥ 
śāstracoditānāṃ teṣāṃ 
bandhakatvaśaktipratiṣṭambhahetuvakṣyamāṇajñānasaṃskṛtānāmadhik
ārideśakālāvasthādyapekṣaṃ mukhyakalpānukalpakrameṇa yathāśakti 
yathāvidhi ca anuṣṭhānameva sakalasya upadeśaviṣayabhūtasya janasya 
paramārthasvarūpasamāpattilakṣaṇamokṣādhigamaheturityayamiha 
upadeśo vivakṣitaḥ | ataśca kasyacidapi adhikāriṇaḥ kasyāṃcidapi 
avasthāyāṃ ghorasya api śāstracoditasya karmaṇaḥ parihārānupapattau 
sthitāyāṃ tādṛśameva 
ghorakarmādhikṛtamuktopadeśyalakṣaṇaviśiṣṭamarjunamuddiśya 
bhagavatkartṛkaṃ rahasyamidamupadeṣṭuṃ munipuṅgavaḥ prakramate | tatra 
dharmakṣetre

p. 16) ityādi kimakurvata sañjaya ityantaṃ 
dhṛtarāṣṭrapraśnacoditasaṃjayavākyāni | dṛṣṭvā tu pāṃḍavānīkam 
ityādīni tumulo vyanunādayat ityantāni 
parasparavadhādhyavasāyasaṃrabdhasakalakurupāṇḍavasainyasamāgatasu
bhaṭaghorasamarasaṃrambhodyogasūcakatvāt 
karmaghoratvapratipādanatātparyāṇi nigadavyākhyātāni | tathā atha 
vyavasthitāndṛṣṭvā ityādi upakramya saṃjayoktānyeva arjunavākyāni 
sainyanirūpaṇopakramāṇi ubhayoḥ senayormadhye ityādīni subodhāni | 
tataḥ saṃjayoktānyeva hṛṣīkeśavākyānyapi sphuṭārthānyeva | tataḥ 
tatrāpaśyatsthitānpārthaḥ ityādi sīdamāno'bravīt ityantamarjunasya 
pitṛpitāmahādibandhuvargadarśanakriyātmakaṃ saṃjayavākyamapi 
vyaktārtham | tataḥ 
prastutopadeśapratiṣedhyatattvājñānasamudbhūtapitṛpitāmahādibandhuv
adhaparyavasāyisaṅgrāmakarmavicikitsasya karuṇāveśavivaśasya 
arjunasya adharmameva dharmatvena vyapadiśato dṛṣṭvemānsvajanān 
kṛṣṇa ityādīni tanme kṣemataraṃ bhavet ityantāni 
manuṣyasvabhāvasulabhamithyājñānapratipādakavākyāni 
nigadavyākhyātānyeva | tataśca evamuktvā hṛṣīkeśam ityādi 
saṃjayavākyamapi gatārthameveti tātparyataḥ prathamo'dhyāyo 
vyākhyātaḥ, nanu gauravabhayāt prātipadyena | evamayaṃ 
prathamo'dhyāyo yathopakrānta 
upadeśyabhagavatpravṛttihetvarjunaviṣādapratipādanārthaḥ samāptaḥ || 1 
||

yogyānugrahanītināṭakavidhiprastāvaneyaṃ prabho-
rgītāsu prathamasya yā viracanādhyāyasya tāṃ dhyāyatām |
divyāsvādasudhārdravastuvikasatkautūhalānāṃ manaḥ
sabhyānāmavadhānamatra niviḍaṃ vadhnātu baddhādaram ||

iti śrīrājānakarāmakaṇṭhaviracite vākyārthānvayamātre 
sarvatobhadranāmni bhagavadgītāvivaraṇe prathamo'dhyāyaḥ || 1 ||



atha dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ 

kvacit ṣaḍbhiḥ pādaiḥ, dvacidardhaślokena 
munervākyārthaparisamāptistatra tatra itihāsabhāge dṛśyate | tadiha 
prakaraṇe tatra tatra tathaiva avagantavyam | atha evaṃ 
vyāmohavaśavisaṃsthulamarjunaṃ prati saṃjātānujipṛkṣasya bhagavataḥ 
paramārthopadeśaprakramapiśunāni vākyāni upakṣeptuṃ 

saṃjaya uvāca

taṃ tathā kṛpayāviṣṭamaśrupūrṇākulekṣaṇam |
sīdamānamidaṃ vākyamuvāca madhusūdanaḥ || 1 ||

spaṣṭārthaḥ ślokaḥ || 1 ||

yaduvāca bhagavāṃstadāha

kutastvā kaśmalamidaṃ viṣame samupasthitam |
anāryajuṣṭamasvargyamakīrtikaramarjuna || 2 ||

idam evaṃ 
vidhaprastutāvaśyakaraṇīyasvakarmakaraṇāvasādakāraṇavyāmohātmak
aṃ kaśmalaṃ pāpaṃ tvā bhavantaṃ kutaḥ kasmāt nimittāt viṣame 
saṃkaṭe upasthitam upāgatam arjuna | kīdṛśaṃ kaśmalam | anāryajuṣṭam 
aśiṣṭasevitam, ata eva asvargyaṃ svargaprāptipratikūlam akīrtikaram 
apavādasaṃpādakamiti padasaṃgatiḥ sphuṭaiva | arthaviśeṣaṃ tu imaṃ 
garbhīkṛtya bhagavānetadāha arjunaśadbo'tra na 
saṃjñimātrasambodhanārthaḥ, kiṃtu 
tadgataprakṛṣṭābhijanaprajñāparākramādiguṇagarimapratyāyanārthaḥ | 
tvacchabdo'pi paramārthato mohādimalaviviktasvabhāvasya ātmanaḥ 
pratyāyakatvena prayuktaḥ | tena vastuto yastvaṃ, tasya anena 
vyāmohakalaṅkena sambandhaḥ prakāśasya tamasā iva 
atyantamasambhāvyaḥ, iti kutastvāmidamupasthitamityanena

p. 18) vākyena garbhīkṛtam | ata evaca 
etatpāpamanāryairatattvavidbhirasādhubhiḥ sevitamityādi uktam || 2 ||

anenaiva krameṇa etat viśeṣayitumāha

mā klaivyaṃ gaccha kaunteya naitattvayyupapadyate |
kṣudraṃ hṛdayadaurbalyaṃ tyaktvottiṣṭha parantapa || 3 ||

he parantapa ! śatrujanajvarajanaka klaivyaṃ kāpuruṣatvaṃ mā 
gāḥ, yataḥ tvayi jagadvikhyātavikramādiguṇamahimni na etat 
evaṃvidhamalpasattvajanocitam upapadyate | tasmāt kṣudram asāraṃ 
hṛdayadaurbalyam āśayakārśyaṃ vihāya uttiṣṭha svaprastute ślāghye 
saṃgrāmakarmaṇi udyamaṃ gṛhṇīṣva | atrāpi tvacchabdasya 
pūrvoktārthagarbhīkāreṇa prayogo mantavyaḥ, klaivyaśabdasya ca 
vivekaśūnyatvagarbhīkāreṇa, iti | anyadapi viśeṣaṇamevaṃ yojanīyamiti 
|| 3 ||

	atha saṃprati 
anāvirbhūtātmānātmasvarūpavivecanakṣamaprajñatvāt dehādikameva 
ātmatvena pratipadyamāno 
gurvādivadhamahāpātakaparyavasāyisamarānuṣṭhānaṃ 
doṣodbhāvanena pratiṣeddhumarjuna uvāca

kathaṃ bhīṣmamahaṃ saṃkhye droṇaṃ ca madhusūdana |
iṣubhiḥ pratiyotsyāmi pūjārhāvarisūdana || 4 ||

	madhusūdana ! kathaṃ kena śāstropapannena prakāreṇa bhīṣmaṃ 
pitāmahaṃ guruṃ ca droṇaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ saṃgare sāyakaiḥ pratīpaṃ 
saṃprahāreṇa yojayiṣyāmi | yadivā pratiśabdayoge dvitīyā vyākhyeyā | 
yudhiśca akarmaka eva | parasmaipadamatra cchāndasam | anyatrāpi evaṃ 
draṣṭavyam | yatastau vidyābhidhānadhanapradānaparamopakāriṇau 
prāṇairapi paritoṣaṇīyahṛdayau pūjanameva arhataḥ | atra etena 
kathamitiprakārapraśnena prastutaraṇaparijihīrṣāyāḥ kaśmalatvaṃ 
bhagavatā upanyastaṃ dharmavirodhādanupapannameveti abhiprāyaḥ 
sūcito'rjunena || 4 ||

p. 19) atha āha

gurūnahatvā hi mahānubhāvān
śreyaścartuṃ bhaikṣyamapīha loke |
natvarthakāmastu gurūnnihatya
bhūñjīya bhogān rudhirapradigdhān || 5 ||

	yasmāt saṃgrāmaparihāreṇa gurūṇāṃ vadhaḥ parihṛto bhavati, 
tāṃśca ahatvā bhikṣānnamapi abhyavahartuṃ praśasyaṃ syānmama, na 
punarahaṃ dharmalipsustān vyāpādya tacchoṇitopacitān iṣṭān viṣayān 
samaśrīyām || 5 ||

kiṃca

naitadvidmaḥ karatanno garīyo
yadvā jayema yadi vā no jayeyuḥ |
yāneva hatvā na jijīviṣāma-
ste naḥ sthitāḥ pramukhe dhārtarāṣṭrāḥ || 6 ||

	etat eva na jānīmaḥ dvayormadhyāt katarat vastu asmākaṃ 
prayojanavattayā garīyo gurutaraṃ syāt | kayostayorvastunorityāha yadivā 
vayaṃ tān pratidvandvino jayema tiraskurvīmahi, yadivā asmān te 
tiraskurvīran | etayorarthayorekatarasya garīyastvāparijñāne kāraṇamāha- 
yasmāt yān kurūn vyāpādya vayaṃ bandhuvirahāt prāṇāneva 
dhārayituṃ na icchāmaḥ te dhṛtarāṣṭraputrā bhrātaro'smākaṃ puraḥ 
sthitāḥ | kimuktaṃ bhavati | yadi tāvadasmābhiḥ kuravo hatāḥ syuḥ, 
tadasya pakṣasya kā garīyastā duḥkhamātraphalatvāt | evaṃca 
arthādidamuktaṃ syāt yadi te'smān jayeyuḥ, sa pakṣo garīyān bhavediti || 
6 ||
	atha samarakarmānuṣṭhānaṃ dharmasukhabādhakatvāt 
parihāryatayā pratipādya api,

p. 20) svātmani vaiduṣyābhimānadoṣamāśaṅkamāno bhagavati 
saṃśayānamātmānamāvedayan sa āha

kārpaṇyadoṣopahatasvabhāvaḥ
pṛcchāmi tvāṃ dharmasaṃmūḍhacetāḥ |
yacchreyaḥ syānniśritaṃ brūhi tanme
śiṣyaste'haṃ śādhi māṃ tvāṃ prapannam || 7 ||

	he bhagavannahaṃ kārpaṇyadoṣeṇa bandhuhiṃsājanitena dainyena 
upahatasvabhāvaḥ pradhvaṃsitakṣatradharmā ata eva dharmeṣu 
śāstroditeṣu ācāreṣu saṃmūḍhacetāḥ saṃśayitamanasko bhavantaṃ 
pṛcchāmi | ataśca yat me vastu śreyaḥ praśasyataraṃ syāt, tat niścitaṃ 
niḥsandehamādiśa | yataḥ śiṣyaḥ śāsanīyastava aham | etena prapannam 
upapannaṃ santaṃ gururbhūtvā māṃ śādhi vyutpādayeti || 7 ||

kiṃca

na taṃ prapaśyāmi mamāpanudyāt
yaḥ śokamucchoṣaṇamindriyāṇām |
avāpya bhūmāvasapatamṛddhaṃ
rājyaṃ surāṇāmapi cādhipatyam || 8 ||

	yasmāt tam ahaṃ kaṃcit tvāṃ varjayitvā na tarkayāmi, yo mama 
śokaṃ śocanamapasārayet | kīdṛśam | indriyāṇāṃ 
bāhyābhyantarāṇāṃ karaṇānām ucchoṣaṇaṃ santāpakam | kiṃ kṛtvā 
| nirvipakṣaṃ sphītaṃ bhūpālādhipatyaṃ labdhvā api | athavā 
atyalpamidamucyate, devānāmapi aiśvaryamadhigamya mama 
kārpaṇyadoṣopahatasvabhāvasya śocanameva syāt, ato māṃ śādhi, iti | 
evaṃ śocanakriyāpekṣamavāptikriyāyāḥ paurvakālyamavaseyam, natu 
apanodanakriyāpekṣam;

p. 21) tathāhi sati bhinnakartṛkatvāt śabdadoṣaḥ samanvayābhāvācca 
arthadoṣo durnivāraḥ syāditi || 8 ||
	atha yuddhaniṣedhaniścitabuddhitvaṃ dhanañjayasya pratipādayituṃ

saṃjaya uvāca

evamuktvā hṛṣīkeśaṃ guḍākeśaḥ parantapa |
na yotsyāmīti govindamuktvā tūṣṇīṃ babhūva ha || 9 ||

tamuvāca hṛṣīkeśaḥ prahasanniva bhārata |
senayorubhayormadhye sīdamānamidaṃ vacaḥ || 10 ||

	ślokadvayametat gatārtham, kiṃtu hṛṣīkāṇi indriyāṇi teṣāmīśo 
niyantā bhagavān | guḍākāṃ nidrāmācakṣate, tasyā īśastayā 
anabhibhavanīya iti arjunaviśeṣaṇaṃ vyākhyeyamiti || 10 ||

	evamavidyāvyāmohaparityaktasaṃgrāmādhyavasāyamarjunaṃ 
samyak jñānopadeśena atra svakarmaṇi pravivartayiṣuḥ

bhagavānuvāca

tvaṃ mānuṣyeṇopahatāntarātmā
viṣādamohābhibhavādvisaṃjñaḥ |
kṛpāgṛhītaḥ samavekṣya bandhū-
nabhiprapannānmukhamantakasya || 11 ||

	upadeśābhāvāt saṃprati anuditadaivasargocitaprajñaḥ tvaṃ 
mānuṣyeṇa manuṣyabhāvena saṃśayaviparyajñānabahulena 
upahatāntarātmā pratyabhijñānābhāvāt tiraskṛtasatyātmasvarūpo 
vartase iti śeṣaḥ | yasmāt viṣādena śokajena anadhyavasāyena mohena ca 
viparyayapratyayahetunā vaicittyena yaste abhibhavaḥ sāmarthyabhaṃgaḥ 
kṛtaḥ, tasmāt hetoḥ visaṃjñaḥ vigatavakṣyamāṇasamyagjñāno'si | kuta 
etadavagatamityāha yatastvaṃ durnivāraprasarasya kṛtāntasya vadane 
praviṣṭān

p. 22) bāndhavān nirūpya kṛpāgṛhītaḥ gṛhītakṛpaḥ svīkṛtakaruṇaḥ 
saṃvṛttaḥ | āhitāgryāderākṛtigaṇatvāt gṛhītaśabdasya 
pūrvanipātābhāvo'tra avaseyaḥ | tena bahubrīhireva ayaṃ, na tatpuruṣaḥ, 
tasmin sati bhinnakartṛkatvaprasaṅgaḥ | anena ślokena vakṣyamāṇasya 
ātmajñānasya prathamapratipādyaṃ 
śarīraśarīrivyatirekajñānamāsūtritam || 11 ||
	yata 
ekāntavinaśvaraśarīrasaṃniveśaviśeṣabaddhabandhubuddhitvamidaṃ 
śarīrisvarūpāparāmarśanibandhanam, ata eva āha

aśocyānanuśocaṃstvaṃ prājñavannābhibhāṣase |
gatāsūnagatāsūṃśca nānuśocanti paṃḍitāḥ || 12 ||

	tvaṃ jñātigurvādivadhaparyavasāyinaḥ samaravyāpārasya 
adharmyatvaduḥkhodarkatvādupanyasyan lokadvayabādhakatvaṃ prājñavat 
buddhimāniva na jalpasi | naiṣā naiṣṭhikī buddhiḥ, 
śarīraśarīrivivekajñānābhāvajanitā vimatireva eṣā | na hi ete 
gurvādayo'vaśyaṃ vināśiśarīramātrātmakāḥ, kiṃtu nityāḥ 
śarīriṇaḥ ete tadvyatiriktāḥ | ata eva kiṃ kurvan tvaṃ 
prājñavannābhibhāṣase aśocyān vināśābhāvāt paramārthataśca 
duḥkhayogābhāvādaśocanīyān paridevanasya aviṣayabhūtān anuśocan 
śokaviṣayatvena bhāvayan | yataśca ete nityāḥ, ata eva tān gatāsūn 
tebhyastebhyaḥ śarīrebhyaḥ utkrāntaprāṇān mṛtavyapadeśabhājaḥ, 
tathāca agatāsūn teṣu teṣu śarīreṣu avasthitaprāṇatvāt 
jīvadvyapadeśabhājaḥ paṇḍitāḥ vijñātaparamārthāḥ na anuśocanti || 
12 ||
	tathāca jīvāvasthāyāmeva eṣāṃ tāvat nityatvaṃ 
pratipādayitumāha

na hyevāhaṃ jātu nāsaṃ na tvaṃ neme janādhipāḥ |
na caiva na bhaviṣyāmaḥ sarve vayamitaḥ param || 13 ||

p. 23) yaṃ māṃ vartamānaṃ manyasesaḥ ahaṃ na jātu na āsaṃ 
nañdvayena pūrvakālabhāvameva prakṛtaṃ pratipādayati 
nāhamabhūtvā bhavāmi, kiṃtu prāgapi bhūtvā idānīmapi bhavāmi | 
yathā ahaṃ, tathā tvamapi saṃprati vartamānatvena pratibhāsamānaḥ 
pūrvamapi āsīreva, natu abhūtvā bhavasi | yathāca tvaṃ, evamime'pi 
vartamānatvena pratibhāsamānāḥ sarve rājānaḥ pūrvamāsanneva, na 
punarabhūtvā bhāva eṣām | na ca sarve eva vayamitaḥ param asmāt 
śarīrādanantaraṃ na bhaviṣyāmaḥ bhaviṣyāma eveti | anena 
yuṣmadasmatparabhedena vyavasthitasya jīvalokasya kālatraye'pi 
sattāsaṃbhavāt nityatvaṃ pratijānīte | āsam ityādi tu 
bhūtādikriyākartṛtvavyapadeśo 
vināśadharmakaśarīrasāmānādhiakaraṇyabhramakṛtaḥ, natu nityasya 
bhūtādivyapadeśa upapadyate iti || 13 ||
	nanu dehaniṣṭhasya jīvasya kartṛtvajñātṛtvādyupalambhaḥ tat 
kathaṃ tadvināśe tasya nityatvaṃ syāditi durāśaṃkāṃ 
pratyakṣopalabhyanidarśanapratipādanena parihartumāha

dehino'sminyathā dehe kaumāraṃ yauvanaṃ jarā |
tathā dehāntaraprāptirdhīrastatra na muhyati || 14 ||

	asmin vartamānabuddhyā parigṛhīte dehe yathā asya dehinaḥ 
tatsaṃvediturātmanaḥ kaumāraṃ kumārabhāvo bālyaṃ, tathā yauvanaṃ 
tāruṇyaṃ, tathā jarā vṛddhabhāvaḥ ityavasthātrayamanityaṃ tena dehinā 
bhūtāditvena anusandhīyamānaṃ sambadhyate, tathā tenaiva nidarśanena 
ito dehādanyasya dehasya prāptistasya ātmano dehadvayavyāpakasya 
sthitaiva | tatra atattvajño mohaṃ gacchati śarīravināśe śarīriṇamapi 
vinaṣṭaṃ manyate, dhīrastu paṇḍitastattvavit tatra na muhyati na taṃ

p. 24) śarīravināśe naṣṭaṃ manyate nityameva tamavagacchati | kimanena 
uktaṃ bhavati | yathā bālyādyavasthāvināśe na kaścidātmavināśaṃ 
manyate pratipattāramātmānamekaṃ sthitaṃ manyate, tathaiva 
nānādehasambandhamatītānāgatatvena anusandadhānaḥ kiṃtu sarvaḥ 
kaścit pratipattā bālyādyavasthā atītānāgatatvena anusandadhānaḥ 
prabuddho nityamātmānamavagacchati | etābhiśca sthūlābhiḥ 
kaumārādyavasthābhistadgatāḥ sūkṣmā jāgarādayo'pi avasthāḥ 
pratipāditā veditavyāḥ | tathāhi tāsu anyonyabhinnarūpāsu 
pravartamānāsu utpādavināśavatīṣu upalabdhṛtvena ekena lakṣaṇena 
yuktastābhyo'tyantavilakṣaṇasvabhāva ātmā sarvasya 
svasaṃvedanasiddho vyāpakatvena vyavasthitaḥ || 14 ||
	tadanena māyaikīkṛtadehadehivyatirekopapattipratipādanadvāreṇa 
pāramārthikamātmādvaitamupakramya idānīṃ yathāpratipādite 
ātmasvarūpe yena hetunā sarvajantavo muhyanti anyathaiva taṃ 
pratipadyante, tatpratipādanapūrvaṃ tatpratiṣedhopāyamapi upadeṣṭumāha

mātrāsparśāstu kaunteya śītoṣṇasukhaduḥkhadāḥ |
āgamāpāyino'nityāstāṃstitikṣasva bhārata || 15 ||

	mīyante etābhiriti kṛtvā dehitvaviśiṣṭasya pramātuḥ 
prameyārthaparicchedasādhanaṃ mātrā indriyārthaśaktaya ucyante | 
spṛśyante vedyatayā parāmṛśyante iti sparśāḥ indriyārthāḥ | te ca 
dviprakārāḥ śrotrādibāhyendriyaspṛśyamānāḥ śabdādirūpāḥ, 
kevalāntaḥkaraṇaspṛśyamānāḥ sukhādirūpāḥ | tuśabdo vyatireke | 
paramārthato nityasya ātmano mātrāsparśāḥ punaḥ 
śītoṣṇasukhaduḥkhadāḥ iti sambandhaḥ | te ca śarīreṇa 
sambadhyamānāḥ śītamanubhavāmi uṣṇamanubhavāmi, iti pratyayaṃ 
dadati; antaḥkaraṇena sambadhyamānāḥ

p. 25) sukhamanubhavāmi duḥkhamanubhavāmi, iti pratyayaṃ dadati | 
tataśca śītoṣṇasukhaduḥkhādiṣu ātmadharmatvamāropayantaḥ 
utpādavināśasvabhāvatvāt anityāḥ ātmani 
anityatābhramamutpādayantaḥ sarvaprāṇabhṛto mohayantītyarthaḥ | 
yataśca te paramārthataḥ pratikṣaṇavināśinaḥ, tataḥ proditopapattyā 
nityamātmānaṃ pratyavamṛśan tān titikṣasva sahasva tatsamarpitena 
anityasukhādisparśamātreṇa 
nityanirvikārasvātmāvaṣṭambhapratiṣṭhāṃ mā tyākṣīḥ | yathā 
trailokyaikavīreṇa tvayā durvāradarpodbhuramāpatantaḥ pratidvandvino 
niṣprakampanijapauruṣaparispandasusthitena mānagapi 
antaranupajātavikriyeṇa asakṛt sāvajñamudvyūḍhabharatayā soḍhāḥ, 
tathaiva nisarganirvikāre svātmani ahaṅkāramāropya te'pi sparśākhyāḥ 
pratidvandvinaḥ prakṛtinissāratatsarūpanirūpaṇamātreṇa sahyantām | 
yathāsthitavastuvimarśābhāvamātrajanito'yaṃ vyāmohaḥ yadātmani 
dehabuddhyādidharmāropeṇa kṛśo'haṃ pūrṇo'haṃ sukhito'haṃ 
duḥkhito'ham ityādayaḥ pratyayāstatsāmānādhikaraṇyena 
prādurbhavanti, tattvataḥ punaste svātmaikyena avasthitā 
bahirantaḥkaraṇaśaktibhiḥ kevalaṃ spṛśyante, natu tāvatā kāṃcidapi 
ātmano vikārakaṇikāṃ sampādayituṃ kṣamāḥ | sphaṭikadarpaṇopamo hi 
ayamātmā nīlapītādyābhāsatulyābhiḥ sphurantībhirapi 
nikaṭasukhādipratītibhirna anyathākartuṃ śakyate | yaduktam

āsaktisāmarthyavaśādvicitrāḥ padārthajātasya vibhānti bhāsaḥ 
prapadyate'ntarnatu (kha, ga, pu0 prapadyate tannatviti pāṭhaḥ |) jātu 
dhīmān vikāramādarśa iva(kha, ga, pu0 ivāprasanna iti pāṭhaḥ |) 
prasannaḥ ||

iti | ata eva mātrāsparśaśabdena indriyārthavyapadeśaḥ kṛtaḥ |

p. 26) tadanena ślokena 
sarvasaṃvedyabhaṅgurasvabhāvakaumārajāgarādyavasthāvat 
sukhādyavasthāsvapi sarvaprāṇigatāsu saṃvedakatvenaiva ekena 
svabhāvena yukto nityo vyāpaka eka eva ātmā pratipāditaḥ | naca 
evatāvatā ekasya sukhitvādau sati sarvasukhitvādiprasaṃgaḥ, yato 
māyodbhāvitānyonyabhedābhāsā eva pramātāraḥ satataṃ 
parasparavibhaktamātmānamavagacchanti, natu pāramārthikaḥ paraḥ 
pramātā | tatra hi ekasmin sarvadā sarvatra sarvathā nirviśeṣasvabhāve 
sarvamidaṃ pratibaddham | yathoktaṃ

--------------------taṃ prapadye prajāsṛjam |

iti upakramya

yasminnitye tate tantau dṛḍhe saṃvyavatiṣṭhati |
sadasadgrathitaṃ viśvaṃ viśvāṅge viśvakarmaṇi ||

iti || 15 ||

tadevaṃsvabhāve satye svātmani suvyavasthitamuktalakṣaṇamiti (ka0 ga0 
gha0 gu0 suvyavasthitatvaṃ muktalakṣamiti pāṭhaḥ |) pratipādayitumāha

yaṃ hi na vyathayantyete puruṣaṃ puruṣarṣabha |
samaduḥkhasukhaṃ dhīraṃ so'mṛtatvāya kalpate || 16 ||

	yaṃ puruṣam uktalakṣaṇaṃ jñānaikatānanirvikalpakaprajñatayā 
mumukṣvavasthāyāṃ vartamānam, ata eva dhīraṃ 
parijñātasadasatpadārthaparamārtham, ata evaca samaduḥkhasukhaṃ same 
manovedyatvamātreṇa ekena rūpeṇa spṛśyamāne tulye duḥkhasukhe yasya 
tathāvidhaṃ ghaṭādivat vedyatvamātrāviśiṣṭarūpaiḥ 
sukhādibhirantaranupajanitavikāraṃ santam ete 
janmavināśadharmayogādanityāḥ sukhito'ham 
ityādiviparyayapratyayahetavaḥ sparśaśabdābhihitā viṣayāḥ śabdādayo 
na

p. 27) vyathayanti tadviviktavedakaikasvabhāvāt satyādātmano na 
pracyāvayanti, saḥ amṛtatvāya paramātmasamāpattivaśādavināśatvāya 
kalpate sampadyate mukto bhavatītyarthaḥ || 16 ||
	atha upapattyupanyāsapūrvamātmano vedakaikasvabhāvasya 
pāramārthikīṃ sattāṃ, tathā tatprameyasvarūpasya dehādeśca 
janmavināśavatastadabhāvaṃ pratipādayitumāha

nāsato vidyate bhāvo nābhāvo vidyate sataḥ |
ubhayorapi dṛṣṭo'ntastvanayostattvadarśibhiḥ || 17 ||

	iha svabhāvādeva yadavidyamānaṃ vastu tasya asato bhāvaḥ sattā 
na asti tathaiva svabhāvataḥ sato nityasya abhāvo vināśo na asti etayoḥ 
sadasatoḥ prakāśatamasoriva atyantaparasparavirodhāt; kiṃtu 
etayordvayorapi tattvavidbhiḥ sākṣātkṛtaparamārthaiḥ anto niṣṭhā 
niścayo dṛṣṭo vijñātaḥ, na tu atattvavidbhiḥ | te hi 
acintyādbhutasvabhāvaparameśvaramāyāśaktyudbhāvitaviṣayapratyayata
yā satyamātmānamapratyavamṛśanta eva asadanityatvādinā 
vāstavasadbhāvarahitaṃ dehādivastu idantayā saṃvedyamānatvāt 
sattvena pratipadyante, paramārthatastu sat 
nityamātmatattvamapratyabhijñāyamānatvādasattvena pratipadyante || 17 ||

tataśca tattvataḥ kiṃ sat, kimasaditi upadeṣṭumāha

avināśi tu tadviddhi yena sarvamidaṃ tatam | 
vināśamavyasyāsya na kaścitkartumarhati || 18 ||

	etayoḥ sadasatormadhyāt tadavināśi vināśadharmarahitatvāt 
paramārthataḥ sat viddhi jānīhi | kiṃ tat | yena ekena idam 
anyonyabhinnābhāsamayaṃ sarvaṃ carācaraṃ vastujātaṃ tataṃ 
kartṛtvavivakṣayā visphāritam, jñātṛtvavivakṣayā vā vyāptam | eka eva

p. 28) hi paramārthataḥ sarvakartā sarvajñaḥ parameśvaraḥ paramātmā 
tadavabhāsitānyonyabhedānāṃ tu kṣetrajñānāṃ dehādikameva 
ātmatvena pratipadyamānānāṃ parimitaviṣayaṃ kartṛtvaṃ jñatvaṃ ca 
na vāstavam | evaṃ asya ātmatattvābhidhānasya vastunaḥ avyayasya 
deśakālādyavacchedarahitatvādakṣayasvabhāvasya, tadvilakṣaṇaḥ 
karyajñeyarūpo na kaścidartho vināśam abhāvaṃ kartumarhati | nahi 
nityasya sarvathānavacchinnamahimno'rthasya anityo'vacchedyaḥ padārtho 
vināśāya prabhavatīti || 18 ||

sattvarūpaṃ pratipādya, asattvarūpaṃ pratipādayitumāha

antavanta ime dehā nityasyoktāḥ śarīriṇaḥ |
vināśino'prameyasya tasmādyuddhyasva bhārata || 19 ||

	sarvaśarīreṣu sarvāvasthāsu ca 
aviluptopalabdhṛtvamātraikalakṣaṇatvāt nityasya avināśinaḥ, tathā 
dehādibhiravacchettumaśakyatvāt aprameyasya 
iyattādikalanāviṣayabhūtasya śarīriṇaḥ ātmanaḥ ime 
dehāstadvilakṣaṇā anyonyabhinnāgamāpāyadharmakatvāt vināśino 
vinaśvarasvabhāvatvāt, tathā deśakālādiparicchedyatvāt antavantaḥ 
iyattādikalanāviṣayabhūtā vedyatvamātreṇa sambaddhāḥ, iti 
deharūpasya vastunaḥ paramārthavinaśvarasya jñeyatvamātrādinā 
hetunā ātmasambaddhasya vināśe sati ātmano'pi 
vināśapratipattiravidyāvijṛmbhitam | tacca tvayi na upapannam | ato 
yathāpratipāditātmatattvapratiṣṭhāhaṃkāro vakṣyamāṇayā upapattyā 
duṣpariharaṃ svakarma anutiṣṭhan yuddhyasva prastutaṃ samarakarma 
samācareti || 19 ||
	atha yuddhaṃ nāma parahananarūpatayā hiṃsātmakaṃ karma, tat 
kathamahaṃ gurujñātyādivadhalakṣaṇe ghore karmaṇi pravarteya, iti tat

p. 29) manyase, tadetadātmasvarūpānabhijñatvajanitamohavilasitameva | 
yataḥ

ya enaṃ vetti hantāraṃ yaścainaṃ manyate hatam |
ubhau tau na vijānīto nāyaṃ hanti na hanyate || 20 ||

	yo 
māyāvaśādatyantanirvikāraparamārthasantamātmānamapratyavamṛśan 
tadvilakṣaṇaṃ dehameva ātmatvena manyamāno 
hananādidehadharmādhiropaṇena enaṃ satyamātmānaṃ hantāraṃ 
hananakriyākartāraṃ vetti pratipadyate; yaśca enameva 
pratipāditalakṣaṇamavināśinamātmānaṃ 
vināśalakṣaṇadehadharmādhyāropaṇena hataṃ hiṃsitaṃ manyate; tau 
ubhau api mohavaśādanātmani ātmābhimānagrahagrastasamyagjñānau 
na jānītaḥ satyasya ātmanaḥ svarūpaṃ na buddhyete | yataḥ ayaṃ 
paramātmā sarvameva idaṃ paramanirvikāracinmātrasvabhāvaḥ 
svātmatayā sarvadā vimṛśan na hanti, naca hanyate hantṛtānibandhanaṃ 
hananakriyākartṛtvaṃ na pratipadyate, nāpi hantavyatānibandhanaṃ 
hananakriyāyāḥ karmatvam || 20 ||
	ete hi vikalpaviparyayajñānātmakavyavahārāṇāṃ 
kṣetrajñānāmeva sambhavanti, na paramātmanaḥ

na jāyate mriyate vā kadāci-
nnāyaṃ bhūtvā bhavitā vā na bhūyaḥ |
ajo nityaḥ śāśvato'yaṃ purāṇo 
na hanyate hanyamāne śarīre || 21 ||

	ayam uktalakṣaṇa ātmā 
deśakālākārāvasthādyavacchedarahitacinmātrasvarūpatvāt na jāyate 
utpadyate nityamanutpannarūpatvāt nāpi mriyate vinaśyati nāpi 
janmamaraṇāntarālavartisthityavasthārūpaṃ bhāvavikāramanubhavati | te 
hi janmasthitivināśātmakā avasthābhedā dehādyanityabhāvāśrayāḥ | 
te hi bhāvā abhūtapūrvadharmāvirbhāve

p. 30) jātā iti ucyante, bhūtapūrvadharmatirobhāve ca vinaṣṭā iti ucyante, 
tadantarāle ca tattatpratyayapravāhasādṛśyabhramāt sthitā iti ucyante | sa 
ca teṣāmapunarbhāvībhāvaḥ, ātmanastu sadābhūtasya na 
abhāvasambandhaḥ | saca ekalakṣaṇāvyabhicārācca na 
dhamāntarāvirbhāvatirobhāvāveti | 
janmasthitivināśalakṣaṇāvasthātrayapratiṣedho'sya anena ślokena 
pratipāditaḥ | idānīmeta eva bhāvavikārā viśeṣaṇairasya (kha: 
viśeṣyasya pratīti pāṭhaḥ |) pratiṣidhyante | tatra ayam ātmā na jāyate 
uktena krameṇa notpadyate iti ajaḥ janmalakṣaṇānityabhāvavikārarahitaḥ, 
tathā nityaḥ sadaikasvabhāvāviyukta ityanena asya pratikṣaṇaṃ 
dharmāntarāvirbhāvatirobhāvalakṣaṇo vipariṇāmākhyo vikāraḥ 
pratiṣidhyate | śāśvataḥ śaśvat saṃpūrṇena rūpeṇa yukta ityanena 
vivṛddhilakṣaṇo vikāro'sya pratiṣidhyate | nahi ayamanityabhāvavat 
pūrvamapacito bhūtvā upacayarūpaṃ vikāramāpadyate | tathā purāṇaḥ 
pūrvakoṭerasambhavāt satatameva cirantana ityanena apakṣayalakṣaṇo 
vikāraḥ pratiṣidhyate | nahi ayamupacito bhūtvā punarapacīyate 
dehādibhāvavat | tathā śarīre hanyamāne na ayamātmā hanyate 
śarīrasyaiva anityatvāt janmādyavasthājuṣo vināśāvasthāpi sambhavati, 
natu nityasya ātmana ityanena vināśalakṣaṇo vikāro'sya pratiṣiddhaḥ | 
astitvalakṣaṇastu ṣaṣṭho vikāraḥ prāguktāvasthātrayamadhye 
bhūtvābhāvaśabdena pratipāditaḥ ityadhunā na uktaḥ, yadivā 
kecidastitvaṃ vikāratvena necchanti iti na uktaḥ || 21 ||

evaṃ sakalabhāvavikārarahitaṃ

vedāvināśinaṃ nityaṃ ya enamajamavyam |
kathaṃ sa puruṣaḥ pārtha hanyate hanti vā katham || 22 ||

enaṃ pratipāditasvarūpaṃ avināśinam ātmānaṃ yaḥ puruṣaḥ

p. 31) pramātā nityaṃ sadaiva jānāti, sa tadabhinnamātmānaṃ 
pratipadyamānaḥ kathaṃ kena prakāreṇa hanyate vināśyate hanti vā 
katham paraṃ vināśayati | hananakriyākartṛkarmabhāvo'yaṃ 
śarīramātrapratibaddhātmābhimāneṣu apratibuddheṣu kṣetrajñeṣu eva 
vyavasthitaḥ || 22 ||

	nanu hananādinā dehavināśe sati cetanadharmānupalambhaḥ, 
tadātmano dehavināśe sati kathamavināśitvamavagamyate iti 
vyāmohavyapohāya dṛṣṭāntanidarśanapūrvaṃ punarapi nityatvaṃ 
draḍhayitumasya āha 

vāsāṃsi jīrṇāni yathā vihāya
navāni gṛhṇāti naro'parāṇi |
tathā śarīrāṇi vihāya jīrṇā-
nyanyāni saṃyāti navāni dehī || 23 ||

	yathā kaścit puruṣo jīrṇāni upabhuktāni vastrāṇi tyaktvā sa eva 
san anyāni navāni upabhogakṣamāni gṛhṇāti upādatte ityanena 
janmādivividhavikārākrāntanānāśarīrasaṃyogaviyogavaśāt na asya 
tatsaṃveditṛtvamātraikalakṣaṇaviyoga iti || 23 ||

nitya eva ayamanityaiḥ śarīradharmairmanāgapi asaṃspṛṣṭaḥ, yataḥ

nainaṃ chindanti śastrāṇi nainaṃ dahati pāvakaḥ |
na cainaṃ kledayantyāpo na śoṣayati mārutaḥ || 24 ||

	enam ātmānaṃ śastrāṇi khaḍgādīni śarīramiva na cchindanti 
dvidhā kurvanti, tathaiva agnirnainaṃ dahati na āpaḥ kledayanti naca 
mārutaḥ śoṣamupanayati || 24 ||
	chedādibhirvikriyamāṇe śarīre na tasya nisarganirvikārasya 
vikārasaṃsparśaḥ | tathāhi

acchedyo'yamadāhyoyamakledyo'śoṣya evaca |
nityaḥ sarvagataḥ sthāṇuracalo'yaṃ sanātanaḥ || 25 ||

p. 32) ayamātmā chedādikriyāvikāryo na bhavati | pūrvoktasyaiva 
nirvacanametat | tathā nityaḥ ityādīni viśeṣaṇāni avināśitvārthāni 
parasparasahāyabhūtāni pratipattidārḍhyāya bahūni prayuktāni 
veditavyāni || 25 ||

tathāca ayam

avyakto'yamacintyo'yamavikāryo'yamucyate |
tasmādevaṃ viditvainaṃ nānuśocitumarhasi || 26 ||

	śabdādivyaktaviṣayāṇāṃ 
śrotrādīnāmindriyāṇāmaviṣayabhūtatvāt ayam avyaktaḥ iti ucyate; 
tathā vedakaikasvabhāvatvādantaḥkaraṇena api idantayā 
viṣayīkartumaśakya iti acintyaḥ cintanakriyāyā agocaraḥ; tathā sarvathā 
caitanyamātrātiriktadharmāntarāsaṃsparśāt avikāryo vikriyāṃ 
netumaśakyaḥ | tasmāt evam uktena prakāreṇa sarvataḥ 
sarvendriyagocarādarthādatyantavyatiriktaṃ vedakatvena 
sarvārthavyāpakam enam ātmānaṃ buddhvā śarīravināśena 
tadvināśabhrameṇa na anuśocitumarhasi || 26 ||

	evaṃ tvaṃ mānuṣyeṇa (2/11) ityādigranthena vitatya 
pratipāditāmapi sakalaprakaraṇapratipādyasya brahmādvaitajñānasya 
śarīraśarīrivyatirekopapattipratipādanarūpāṃ prathamāṃ 
bhūmikāmapi susūkṣmanirmalaprajñāvadhānaviśeṣāvaseyatvāt 
saṃprati apratipadyamānamarjunaṃ manvāno bhagavān 
sarvapakṣāntarākṣepeṇa bodhayitumāha

athavainaṃ nityajātaṃ nityaṃ vā manyase mṛtam |
tathāpi tvaṃ mahābāho nainaṃ śocitumarhasi || 27 ||

	athavā madupadiṣṭopapattikrameṇa 
yathoktamarthamapratipadyamānaḥ tvam enam ātmānaṃ nityajātaṃ 
nityaṃ vā mṛtaṃ manyase tathāpi

p. 33) tenāpi prakāreṇa nainaṃ vināśitvena śocitumarhasi | idamatra 
tātparyam- ātmaviṣayā dvayī pratipattiḥ saṃbhāvyate nityo vā 
syādanityo vā, tṛtīyaḥ pakṣo na saṃbhavati | tatra nityatvapratipattyā yadi 
enaṃ nityajātameva sadaiva utpannaṃ vināśarahitaṃ manyase, tathāpi asya 
avināśitvāt śokasya ko'vasaraḥ | atha vināśitvapratipattyā nityameva 
mṛtaṃ sattārahitamenaṃ manyase, tathāpi nityavinaṣṭatvādasya 
śocanīyatvābhāvaḥ | anye tu evaṃ vyācakṣate- yadyenamātmānaṃ 
nityaṃ sadaiva janmamaraṇābhyāmanuṣaktamanityatvāt manyase, tathāpi 
enaṃ śocituṃ na arhasi || 27 ||

	etameva janmamaraṇapravāhāvicchedaṃ kaiścinnityatvena (ka0 ga0 
pu0 kaiścidanityatvena avagamyamānasyāpi ātmana iti pāṭhaḥ |) avagamya 
mānamasya ātmanaḥ pratipādayitumāha

jātasya hi dhruvo mṛtyurdhruvaṃ janma mṛtasya ca |
tasmādaparihārye'rthe na tvaṃ śocitumarhasi || 28 ||

	hiśabdo hetau | yasmāt jātasya janmalakṣaṇavikārabhājo dehasyaiva 
ātmatvena pratipannasya dhruvaḥ avaśyaṃbhāvī mṛtyuḥ vināśākhyo 
vikāraḥ; evameva mṛtasya vinaṣṭasya janma avaśyaṃbhāvīti kasya eṣa 
janmamaraṇalakṣaṇavikārayoga iti tattvamavicārayato'pi lokasya ayaṃ 
pravādaḥ sthita eva jāto mriyate, mṛto jāyate ityevaṃvidhaḥ, tasmāt 
lokapravādadṛṣṭyā api śokasya anupapattireva || 28 ||

	anyamapi prakāraṃ śokaparihārāya āha

avyaktādīni bhūtāni vyaktamadhyāni bhārata |
avyaktanidhanānyeva tatra kā paridevanā || 29 ||

p. 34) pṛthivyādibhūtasaṃniveśaviṣayaśarīrātmakatvāt 
sarvaprāṇabhṛto bhūtaśabdena atra ucyante | tāni etāni bhūtāni 
avyaktādīni apratyakṣapūrvarūpāṇi, tathaiva avyaktanidhanāni 
parokṣaparyantāni | nahi etāni pūrvamevaṃvidhāni āsan, punaśca 
evaṃvidhāni bhaviṣyanti, iti jñāyate | tato dvayorapi etayoḥ kāṣṭayostāni 
aśocyāni | yadyapi vyaktamadhyāni vyaktā tena tena rūpeṇa vedyamānā 
madhyāvasthā janmavināśāntarālavartinī daśā yeṣāṃ, 
tathāvidhānyapi aśocyāni eva | vyaktāvasthāyāmeṣāṃ hi 
bhramamātrameva sthairyapratipattiḥ | etāni hi yena yena prakāreṇa 
kalpyante, tena tena vinaśvarāṇi eva | kṣaṇikāni vā bhavantu, 
pariṇāmitvādāvirbhavattirobhavaddharmākrāntāni vā bhavantu, sarvathā 
vikalpyamānāni ekasya sthirasya rūpasya asaṃbhavādaśocyāni eva, iti 
dehātmakabhūtasanniveśamātratvena pratipanneṣu śarīreṣu kaḥ 
paridevanāvasaraḥ iti || 29 ||
	idānīṃ bahuprakāraṃ pratipādyamāno'pi 
ayamartho'tigambhīratvāt manasi dhārayitumanuṣṭhātuṃ ca aśakya iti 
arjunasya etadarthaviṣayamanadhyavasāyaṃ parihartuṃ 
prakṛṣṭaprayatnapratipattavyatāmasya arthasya darśayitumāha

āścaryavatpaśyati kaścidena-
māścaryadvadati tathainamanyaḥ | 
āścaryavaccainamanyaḥ śṛṇoti
śrutvāpyenaṃ veda na caiva kaścit || 30 ||

	enam ātmānaṃ yathāpratipāditasvarūpaṃ kaścit 
manuṣyasahasreṣu madhye prakṛṣṭapuṇyaparipākavaśāt bahūnāṃ 
janmanāmante paśyati tattvataḥ pratyabhijñānena sākṣātkurute | yo'pi 
paśyati, sa āścaryavat |

p. 35) yathā āścaryaṃ kaścidadṛṣṭapūrvamadbhutaṃ kadācit kathaṃcit 
kṣaṇamātraṃ paśyati, tadvadenaṃ kaścit kṛtapuṇyaḥ kṣaṇamātraṃ 
paśyati | tathaiva adbhutarūpamiva pratibhāsamānaṃ kaścideva tathāvidha 
enaṃ vadati paraprabodhanāya ācaṣṭe | tathaiva kaścit tādṛśa eva pareṇa 
pratipādyamānaṃ śṛṇoti avadhārayati | kaścit 
saṃpratibhagavadanugraheṇa anadhiṣṭhitatvādavidyopahatāntaḥkaraṇaḥ 
enaṃ yathāpratipāditasvarūpamātmānaṃ śrutvāpi kutaścit 
tatpratipādanakuśalādākarṇya api na veda tattvato na jānāti | sa ca 
tathāvidho'nupadeśya eva | tvaṃ 
punarbhaktiśraddhādiguṇagaṇasūcitabhagavadanugrahatvāt pātrameva 
etattattvopadeśasyeti atra udyukto bhavetyarthaḥ | idamatra tātparyam asya 
ātmano guruśiṣyakrameṇa svayamanubhavena vā santu jātu 
kecitpuṇyabhājaḥ pratipattāraḥ, kiṃtu 
nirantarābhyāsapariśīlanaviśīrṇakalpasakalavikalpāvaraṇatāṃ vinā 
avaimalyena sarvakālamasthiratayā ca asya pratipattimātramiti viralaviralo 
lakṣeṣu ekaḥ kaścideva caramajanmā bhavediti yathoktena krameṇa 
samyagavadhāraṇīyaṃ, natu mandotsāhena atra yogyena api bhavatā 
bhāvyam || 30 ||

	tadayamatra niścaya ityāha

dehī nityamavadhyo'yaṃ dehe sarvasya bhārata |
tasmātsarvāṇi bhūtāni na tvaṃ śocitumarhasi || 31 ||

	ayaṃ 
samanantarapratipāditadehādisamastavedyapadārthātyantavyatiriktavedakatv
amātraikalakṣaṇo dehī dehasya vedyatvamātreṇa svasya vedakatvamātreṇa 
svāmī paraḥ pramātā sarvasya dehamātrameva ātmatvena 
abhimanyamānasya māyīyasya pramāturjantujātasya tatra tatra nānārūpe 
dehe śarīre eka eva vyāpakatayā tiṣṭhan nityaṃ satatameva

p. 36) avadhyo hantuṃ vināśayitumaśakyaḥ | na hi 
āgamāpāyidharmakāṇāṃ nānāvidhānāṃ sarvadā 
vedyatvamātrasvabhāvānāṃ śarīrāṇāṃ vināśe tadvyāpakasya ekasya 
anavacchinnamahimno vedakatvamātrāvyabhicāryekalakṣaṇasya satyasya 
ātmano vināśaḥ sambhavati | yata evamatastasya avināśitvena nityasya 
anuśocyatvaṃ tāvat na astyeva | tasmāt vināśaikasvabhāvāni bhūtāni 
pṛthivyādibhūtasaṃniveśaviśeṣātmakāni śarīrāṇi eva śarīritvena 
pratipannāni vinaśyanti paśyan anuśocituṃ paridevituṃ na arhasi naṣṭasya 
nāśe vivekinaḥ kaḥ śokasya avasara ityarthaḥ || 31 ||
	atha pratipāditopapattyā tāttvikamātmasvarūpaṃ 
saṃpratipattumaśaktamarjunaṃ manyamāno bhagavān 
kṣatriyajātyucitābhimānoddīpanena avaśyānuṣṭheye svakarmaṇi 
pravartayitumāha

svadharmamapicāvekṣya na vikampitumarhasi |
dharmyāddhi yuddhācchreyo'nyatkṣatriyasya na vidyate || 32 ||

yadṛcchayā copapannaṃ svargadvāramapāvṛtam |
sukṛtātkṣatriyāḥ pārtha labhante yuddhamīdṛśam || 33 ||

atha cettvamimaṃ dharmyaṃ saṃgrāmaṃ na kariṣyasi |
tathā svadharmaṃ kīrtiṃ ca hitvā pāpamavāpsyasi || 34 ||

akīrtiṃ cāpi bhūtāni kathayiṣyanti te'vyayām |
saṃbhāvitasya cākīrtirmaraṇadatiricyate || 35 ||

bhayādraṇāduparataṃ maṃsyante tvāṃ mahārathāḥ |
yeṣāṃ ca tvaṃ bahumato bhūtvā yāsyasi lāghavam || 36 ||

avācyavādāṃśca bahūnvadiṣyanti tavāhitāḥ |
nindantastava sāmarthyaṃ tato duḥkhataraṃ nu kim || 37 ||

p. 37) hato vā prāpsyasi svargaṃ jitvā vā bhokṣyase mahīm |
tasmāduttiṣṭha kaunteya yuddhāya kṛtaniścayaḥ || 38 ||

ślokasaptakametat nigadavyākhyātam || 32-38 ||

	evaṃ svargādyanityaphalahetupravṛttilakṣaṇadharmasamāśrayaṇena 
api sarvaprakāraṃ svakarmāparityāge vyavasthite, 
niratiśayanityaphalaprāptihetunivṛttilakṣaṇadharmasamāśrayaṇena 
etadaparityāge ko doṣa iti prakṛtameva jñānopadeśaṃ punarapi 
siddhāntīkurvannāha

sukhaduḥkhe same kṛtvā lābhālābhau jayājayau |
tato yuddhāya yujyasva naivaṃ pāpamavāpsyasi || 39 ||

	evam anena prakāreṇa vartamānaḥ pāpaṃ jñātivadhāditaḥ 
sambhāvyamānaṃ kilviṣaṃ na avāpsyasi na lapsyase | kathaṃ vartamānaḥ 
| prathamaṃ tāvat 
yathopadiṣṭārthaniḥsaṃśayapratipattiprarūḍhaniṣprakampaprajñāpratyab
hijñātasamastaprāṇabhṛtsarvāvasthāvyāpakasāmānyasaṃvinmātrasva
bhāve nityaniruttarānandanirbharatayā 
svarūpavyatiriktaprāpyapadārthāntaraprepsāvirahite satye svāmini 
akṛtrimātmābhimānamāsthāya 
māyāvyāmohajanitadehādyanātmabhūtabhāvātmapratyayalakṣaṇavipar
yayajñānaiḥ sarvaprāṇibhirupādeyatayā heyatayā ca parikalpitabhede 
pratikṣaṇavinaśvare sukhaduḥkhe harṣaviṣādau same draṣṭari ātmani 
dṛśyatāmātreṇa sambaddhatvādanupajanitaviśeṣātmakavikāratayā 
nirviśeṣe kṛtvā samyak pratipattyā sampādya, evameva taddhetubhūtau 
rājyādilipsitārthalābhālābhau, tayorapi hetubhūtau jayaparājayau tathaiva 
samau nirviśeṣau sampādya tattvato yathāvasthitena rūpeṇa 
niścityetyarthaḥ | ete hi sukhaduḥkhādayo vedyatvamātreṇa saṃbaddhā 
vedakasya ātmano na kaṃcit vikāraleśamarpayituṃ kṣamāḥ | yathā 
nīlapītādayaḥ prakāśyatvamātreṇa sambaddhāḥ prakāśakasya 
ālokasya

p. 38) na kaṃcidviśeṣaṃ nīlapītādikamunmeṣayituṃ śaktāḥ, kevalaṃ 
prakāśyatvamātreṇa ekena rūpeṇa tatra sambaddhāḥ | tadetat 
sukhādīnāṃ vedyatvamātrarūpaṃ sāmyam | evaṃ sukhādīni samīkṛtya 
tataḥ 
etatsamādhipratipatteranantarametatsamādhisupratiṣṭhitamatiryuddhaśabdopa
lakṣitāya (kha0 ga0 pu0 yuddhadharmopalakṣiteti pāṭhaḥ |) svakarmaṇe 
yujyasva saṃbadhyasva yathoktajñānaniṣṭhatayā svakarma 
anutiṣṭhetyarthaḥ || 39 ||
	evaṃ jñānakriyāsamuccayameva upadeśyatayā viniścitya, 
jñānaviṣayāṃ matimuktakalpāṃ matvā, tāṃ kriyāviṣayāṃ 
vaktumupakramamāṇasta eva jñānakarmaṇī sāṃkhyayogaśabdābhyāṃ 
vyavaharannāha

eṣā te'bhihitā sāṃkhye buddhiryoge tvimāṃ śṛṇu |
buddhyā yukto yayā pārtha karmabandhaṃ prahāsyasi || 40 ||

	idamātmādi sat, idaṃ dehādi asat iti 
heyopādeyapadārthaparigaṇanā saṃkhyā | tāmadhikṛtya kṛtaṃ 
śāstramanvarthatayā mukhyaṃ sāṃkhyamiha ucyate, taddhetukaṃ 
jñānamapi sāṃkhyaṃ natu kapilādipraṇītaṃ tasya 
nānātmavādaparamārthatvāt | tasmin sāṃkhye viṣaye eṣā prabandhena 
pratipāditā buddhiḥ prajñā tava upadiṣṭā, yayā prajñayā tat 
sāṃkhyaṃ samyak yujyate budhyate vā | uktajñānasamadhigamyena 
paramārthalakṣaṇena arthena sambandho yogaḥ, taddhetukatvāt karmaiva iha 
yogaśabdena uktam | nahi kevalasya jñānasya yogārthasambandhahetutvaṃ 
saṃbhavati, kiṃtu tadanugatasya karmaṇa eveti prabandhena vakṣyate | 
ityevaṃvidhe yoge karmaṇi imāṃ vakṣyamāṇāṃ buddhiṃ śṛṇu 
avadhāraya yayā (kha0 pu0 yayā kiṃ karma kimakarmeti karmatattvaṃ 
mokṣakāraṇatayā vijñāyata iti pāṭhaḥ |) karmaiva tattvato 
mokṣakāraṇatayā vijñāyate | ata eva āha yayā vakṣyamāṇayā 
buddhyā yuktaḥ sambaddhastvaṃ karmakṛtaṃ

p. 39) jñānaśūnyajanaviṣayaṃ bandhamasvātantryaṃ prahāsyasi 
tyakṣyasi | tādṛśaṃ hi karmānuṣṭhānamiha upadekṣyate, yena 
sakalasaṃmūḍhajanabandhahetorapi karmaṇo mokṣahetutvameva 
paryavasyati || 40 ||
	ata eva asya karmayogasya 
cittavṛttinirodhalakṣaṇayogātiriktaśaktitāṃ pratipādayitumāha

nehātikramanāśo'sti pratyavāyo na vidyate |
svalpamapyasya dharmasya trāyate mahato bhayāt || 41 ||

	iha vaktuṃ prakrānte karmayoge cittavṛttinirodhalakṣaṇayogavat 
atikramanāśo nāsti | sa hi 
yathopadiṣṭakramasvabhyastasamastabhūmikābhirnirvartyasvarūpatvāt 
pūrvapūrvabhūmikānāmanavāptāvuttarottarabhūmikārohaṇarūpakram
ollaṃghanalakṣaṇe'tikrame sati vinaśyati svarūpameva na labhate | sa 
tādṛśo vināśa iha yoge samanantaravakṣyamāṇopapattyā na vidyate | 
tathā tatra tattadaṃgavaikalyādasamyaganuṣṭhite 
vyādhyādirvartamānaśarīrānubhavanīyaḥ 
punargarbhavāsaduḥkhādirbhaviṣyaccharīrānubhavanīyaḥ pratyavāyaḥ 
aniṣṭayogaḥ saṃbhavati, iha tu pūrvavat so'pi na vidyate yataḥ asya 
dharmasya yathoktajñānapūrvatayā 
śāstracoditasvakarmānuṣṭhānasamādhirūpasya sadācāraviśeṣasya 
sambandhi svalpamapi parimitakālalavānubhūtasvarūpaṃ mahataḥ 
samastabhavahenoranātmanyātmapratyayalakṣaṇāt bhayāt 
sādhvasādanuṣṭhātāraṃ yoginaṃ trāyate parirakṣati 
punastatsambandhaṃ tiraskarotītyarthaḥ | atra hi dharme 
satatamanastamitasamastavastuprathaikahetusāmānyasaṃvidrūpaparaprakā"
saikalakṣaṇasvātmapratyavamarśo niruttaraḥ samādhiḥ 
sakṛdvibhātatvenaiva nirvartyasaṃpūrṇasvarūpo vidhīyate | saca 
vibhātamātraḥ saṃpūrṇaṃ phalaṃ sampādayati | tathāca vakṣyati

sthitvāsyāmantakāle'pi brahma nirvāṇamṛcchati | (2/74)

p. 40) iti | tato'smin 
pūrvāparakramābhyastabhūmikāsaṃpādyacittavṛttinirodhalakṣaṇayogav
at vināśapratyavāyau na sambhavataḥ | naca asya prastutasya 
karmayogalakṣaṇasya dharmasya prakarṣapratipādanān nirodhayogasya iha 
sarvātmanā parihāro'vagantavyaḥ | sa hi atra tatatsnānādikriyāviśeṣavat 
(kh: tattatsthānādīti pāṭhaḥ) pūrvāpararātrādiniyatakālānuṣṭheyatayā 
niyatetikartavyatāko'ntarbhavatyeva || 41 ||

	asmiṃśca 
viśuddhabhaktiśraddhādiguṇavyaktisūcitavivekajñānāvirbhāvo 
bhavādṛśo'dhikṛtaḥ, na sarvaḥ kaściditi pratipādayitumāha

vyavasāyātmikā buddhirekaiva kurunandana |
bahuśākhā hyanantāśca buddhayo'vyavasāyinām || 42 ||

	vyavasāyo niścayaḥ, sa ātmā svarūpaṃ yasyāḥ, sā tathārūpā 
svabhāvato buddhirekalakṣaṇaiva | niścayaḥ ātmādisadarthaviṣayo'pi 
saṃbhavati, dehādyasadarthaviṣayo'pi saṃbhavati | tatra 
yo'sadarthatriṣayaḥ, sa niścayābhāso niścaya eva na saṃbhavati | ataḥ 
sadarthaviṣayamukhyavyavasāyarahitānām avyavasāyināṃ buddhyo 
bahuśākhāḥ ekasminnapi arthe nānāvikalpajālākulā aparyavasitāśca | 
yataḥ sarvābhikāṃkṣāparikṣayaikahetau paramārthasatyātmani 
alabdhabuddhipratiṣṭhā idaṃ prāptamidaṃ prāpyamiti anityamarthaṃ 
prāpyatayā ālambamānā na kacit viśrāntiṃ labhante iti || 42 ||
	evaṃvidhānāṃ puruṣāṇāṃ svargādibhogamātraparatvena 
pratipannānāṃ yā buddhiḥ, sā vaktuṃ prakrānte parasmin dharme na 
niyujyate ityāha

yamimāṃ puṣpitāṃ vācaṃ pravadantyavipaścitaḥ |
vedavādaparāḥ pārtha nānyadastītivādinaḥ || 43 ||

p. 41) kāmātmānaḥ svargaparā janmakarmaphalepsavaḥ |
kriyāviśeṣabahulā bhogaiśvaryagatīḥ prati || 44 ||

bhogaiśvaryaprasaktānāṃ tayāpahṛtacetasām |
vyavasāyātmikā buddhiḥ samādhau na vidhīyate || 45 ||

	saiṣā vyavasāyātmikā niścayasvabhāvā 
buddhirbhogaiśvaryaprasaktānāṃ puṃsāṃ samādhau 
ātmalakṣaṇaparamārthasatattvaviṣaye praṇidhāne na vidhīyate na 
anuśiṣyate tasyāstadarthapratipattiyogyatābhāvāt | ye hi 
bhogānāmiṣṭānāṃ viṣayāṇāmaiśvarye svāmye prakarṣeṇa 
vastvantaraparihārarūpeṇa atiśayena āsaktāstatparā eva, teṣāṃ 
bhogātmakānityavastuviṣayā vyavasāyātmikā buddhiḥ samādhiviṣayaṃ 
vyavasāyaṃ na avalambate eva | kīdṛśānāṃ teṣāṃ 
bhogaiśvaryaprasaktānāṃ samādhau buddhirna vidhīyate | tayā vācā 
apahṛtacetasāṃ svīkṛtamānasām | kayā vācā | yāmimāṃ puṣpitāṃ 
paramārthabodhalakṣaṇaphalarahitatvāt 
bhogarūpapuṣpamātrasaṃyuktāṃ prasiddhāṃ vācaṃ vedavādaparā 
avipaścito vadanti vyācakṣate | vedaśabdo'tra 
jñānaśūnyakriyāmātravidhāyakavedaikadeśavacano'vagantavyaḥ | 
tasya tathāvidhasya vedasya vādo'bhidhānaṃ paraṃ prakṛṣṭaṃ tadeva 
tattvaṃ yeṣāṃ te tathoktāḥ | ata eva nānyadastītivādinaḥ 
svargādiphalaprāptihetubhūtānāṃ kriyāviśeṣāṇāṃ vidhāyakāt 
vedādṛte'nyat śāstraṃ nāstyeveti vadanaśīlāḥ | ata eva avipaścitaḥ 
yajñādikriyāphalabhūtānityāśucisātiśayabhogamātrarūpamarthaṃ 
puruṣārthaphalatvena pratipannāḥ santo na vidvāṃsaḥ | ye hi 
avināśiviśuddhaniratiśayamātmatattvalakṣaṇametadviparītamarthaṃ 
phalatvena pratipadyante, te vipaścittvaṃ bhajante | ata eva 
avipaścittvapratipādakāni teṣāṃ viśeṣaṇāni āha | athāca 
kāmātmānaḥ kāmāḥ priyāḥ śabdādayaḥ

p. 42) sarvasvabhūtatvādātmā yeṣām, ata eva kāmātmakatvāt 
svargaparāḥ uttamabhogādhikaraṇaṃ svargaḥ prāpyatayā paraḥ prakṛṣṭo 
yeṣāṃ te tathāvidhāḥ, tathā janmakarmaphalepsavaḥ bhogaprakṣaye 
avaśyaṃbhāvi janma kvacidapi yonyantare sambhavati, tasmiṃśca sati 
dharmādharmātmakaṃ karma, tasmiṃśca sati sukhādilakṣaṇaṃ 
phalamityetāni āptumicchavaḥ | ata eva bhogaiśvaryasya 
iṣṭaviṣayādhipatyasya gatīrvividhamārgān (ka0 ga0 pu0 
mārgānphalabhūtānpratīti pāṭhaḥ |) prati sādhanabhūtāḥ 
kriyāviśeṣā nānāvidhā yajñadānatapaḥprabhṛtayaḥ karmabhedā 
bahulā bhūyāṃso yeṣām | na kevalaṃ nityanaimittikakriyāmātrasaktāḥ, 
yāvat kāmyānapi kriyāviśeṣān vistārayantaḥ iti evaṃvidhāḥ 
pravaktāro yāṃ vācaṃ vadanti, tayā āvarjitacittānāṃ 
bhogāsaktānāṃ buddhiḥ samādhau na vidhīyate iti sambandhaḥ | idamatra 
tātparyam- ye svayameva prabodhaśūnyatvāt bhogamātraparāḥ, 
te'nyaistathāvidhaireva tameva arthaṃ praśaṃsadbhiḥ 
paripoṣyamāṇatādṛksaṃkalpāḥ paramārthaprāptivyavasāyāt 
dūramapākṛṣyante iti tathāvidhānāṃ buddhiḥ kathaṃ samādhau 
vidheyatāmarhati || 45 ||

	ataśca svabhāvataḥ 
prastutavijñānopadeśapātrabhūtastvamavipaścitprarocanāvacanavahnaya
mānacittatāṃ (kha0 pu0 vacanavaśyamānacittatāmiti pāṭhaḥ |) vihāya 
yathopadiṣṭavijñānaniṣṭho bhavetyāha

traiguṇyaviṣayā vedā nistraiguṇyo bhavārjuna |
nirdvandvo nityasattvastho niryogakṣema ātmavān || 46 ||

	sattvarajastamāṃsi trayo guṇā eva traiguṇyam | 
sukhaduḥkhamohamayabuddhimayatvāt traiguṇyaṃ viṣaya āśrayo yasya sa 
traiguṇyaviṣayaḥ | saṃsāripuruṣavargaḥ upacārāt traiguṇyaśabdena atra 
ucyate | tadevaṃvidhaṃ

p. 43) traiguṇyaṃ viṣayo gocaro yeṣāṃ, te tathābhūtā vedāḥ | ye hi 
sukhādyarthinaḥ, te tatprāptihetuṣu kriyāviśeṣeṣu vedairniyujyante | 
yatastaiḥ sarvatovyatiriktasvarūpaḥ sarvagata eko yathoktaviśeṣaṇaḥ satya 
ātmā ātmatvena na pratipadyate, tatasteṣāṃ sanneva ātmā nāsti | tvaṃ tu 
nistraiguṇyaḥ sannātmavān bhava niṣkrāntastraiguṇyāt nistraiguṇyo 
guṇatrayātīto vedyatvamātreṇa 
sukhādyātmabhirguṇairvedakaikasvabhāvaḥ saṃbandhī, natu taddharmako 
guṇebhyo'tyantaviviktasvabhāvaḥ paramārthato'yamātmā 
tamīdṛśamātmatvena pratyavamṛśan ātmavān bhava 
paramārthasantameva ātmānamuktopadeśapratipattiśaithilyādasantamiva 
mā kārṣīrityarthaḥ | yadāca 
guṇatrayātītasvabhāvamātmānamātmatayā pratipadyase, tadā nirdvandvo 
dvandvebhyaḥ sukhaduḥkhatṛṣṇājarāmaraṇādibhyaḥ kleśebhyo 
niṣkrānto bhava | etāni hi dvandvāni anātmani triguṇe 
dehādāvātmapratyayanibandhanāni | yataśca nirdvandvaḥ, tato 
nityasattvasthaḥ api bhava nityamavināśi yat sattvaṃ sadbhāvastatra sthita 
upalabdhapratiṣṭhaḥ | tādṛśaḥ san niryogakṣemo'pi bhava aprāptasya 
prāpyasya vastunaḥ prāptiryogaḥ, prāptasya parirakṣaṇaṃ kṣemaḥ, 
niṣkrāntastābhyāṃ niryomakṣemaḥ, tathāvidho bhava | nahi 
nityasaṃpūrṇatvāt sarvato nirabhilāṣātmanaḥ prāpyaṃ kiṃcit 
sambhavati | na ca apūrvatayā prāptasya kasyacidarthasya 
vināśāśaṃkayā parirakṣaṇam | ato 
viparyayapratyayagṛhītātmābhāsadehādinibandhane eva yogakṣeme iti tato 
vyāvṛttātmapratyayatayā satyātmapratyavamarśādātmavān bhava || 46 ||
	naca bhogaiśvaryaprasaktatvāt 
pravṛttidharmānuṣṭhānaikaniṣṭhopadeśyapuruṣaviṣayatvena 
vidhīyamānakriyāviśeṣetikartavyatopadeśaśatasaṃkule

p. 44) kule vedaśāstre sarvamanuṣṭheyatayā avagantavyam, kiṃtu yāvatā 
paramārthabodhalakṣaṇasvābhipretārthakriyāsiddhirbhavati, tāvadeva 
tataḥ pratipattavyamupādeyatayā budhenetyāha

yāvānartha udapāne sarvataḥ saṃplutodake |
tāvānsarveṣu vedeṣu brāhmaṇasya vijānataḥ || 47 ||

	sarvataḥ sarvāsu dikṣu saṃplutodake samṛddhasalile udapāne 
hradaprasravaṇādāvarthino yāvānarthaḥ yāvanmātramekadeśāt 
tṛṣṇāpraśamanādyarthakriyāsamarthasalilalavopayogamātraṃ 
prayojanaṃ, tāvān eva vijānato vijñānavato brāhmaṇasya brahmavedinaḥ 
sarveṣu nikhileṣu sarahasyeṣu vedeṣu arthaḥ prayojanam | yāvatā 
śāstraikadeśena paramārthopalabdhilakṣaṇārthakriyānirvṛttirbhavati, 
tāvadeva tena tato vidhisahasrasaṃkulādapi upādātavyam | kimuktaṃ 
bhavati | yathā tṛṣitena arthinā susaṃpūrṇāt samudrakalpādapi 
jalāśayāt svārthakriyāsaṃpattimātrakṣamamudakaṃ stokaṃ 
samupādīyate, na sarvo jalāśayaḥ | evaṃ 
jñānakriyāviṣayavividhārthasārthasaṃpūrṇādapi vedaśāstrāt 
tattvajijñāsunā tadavagamamātropayogī eva arthaḥ upādātavyaḥ, natu 
sarvameva vedaśāstramiti || 47 ||
	atha vijānatā sarvaśāstrebhyo yadupādeyaṃ, tadarjunaṃ puraskṛtya 
upadeṣṭumāha

karmaṇyastvadhikāraste mā phaleṣu kadācana |
mā karmaphalaheturbhūrmā te saṃgo'stvakarmaṇi || 48 ||

	tava saṃpratyeva 
upadiṣṭatattvajñānopapattiprarūḍhadṛḍhapratipattivaśādanityebhyo 
dehādibhyo vyāvartamānātmapratyayatve sati advaitacinmayajyotīrūpaṃ 
paraṃ tattvamahamiti ātmatvena pratipadyamānasya sataḥ 
karmaṇyadhikāro'stu yat karma 
kāyavāṅmanobhirnirvartyavyāpāraviśeṣātmakaṃ

p. 45) varṇāśramavibhāgena 
deśakālāvasthāpekṣamukhyagauṇavividhetikartavyatākalāpena 
nityādibhedena avaśyakartavyatayā śāstreṣu vihitaṃ, tasminnadhikāraḥ 
kartṛtvaṃ bhavati; tadadhikṛtaḥ svārthasiddhaye pravartasvetyarthaḥ | yataḥ 
karma tāvat śarīratvāvyabhicāri na kenacit tyaktuṃ śakyam | atha 
yatyādyavasthāyāṃ kaścit niyamaviśeṣo'valambanīya eva, tataśca sa eva 
karmaviśeṣatāṃ prāpnoti āśramāntarakarmaniyamavat; itthamaśakyaḥ 
sarvātmanā karmatyāgaḥ | kiṃca gṛhyatāṃ nāma atyāgo'pi 
tyāgābhidhānaḥ | sa tu tattvajñānabāhyatāmeva āviṣkaroti 
tathāvidhasya | yato dveṣyatāṃ vinā dveṣyatā saṃbhavati, iti 
karmatyāgābhidhāne rāgadveṣaprasaṃgo durnivāraḥ | yadica tattvasya 
ekatvāt karmaṇāṃ yatheṣṭamaniyamena anuṣṭhānameva tyāgatvena 
abhimatam, tathāpi pūrvavadrāgadveṣānyathānupapattyā so'pi 
atattvajñatāmeva anuṣṭhāturabhivyanakti, iti akarmatā vā 
aviruddhakarmatā vā niruttarānantanijānandanirbharaṃ paraṃ 
tattvamātmatvena pratipadyamānasya sarvasāmyalakṣaṇaṃ paramaṃ 
samādhimadhirurukṣatastattvajñānābhiniveśino yogino dvayamapi 
anupapannamiti tathāvidhasya tava karmaṇyeva adhikāro'stu, kiṃtu teṣāṃ 
karmaṇāmanātmajñajanasamucitanānābhisandhānaprārthiteṣu 
dṛṣṭānuśravikavividhabhogātmakeṣu bandhahetuṣu phaleṣu adhikāro mā 
bhūt | ye hi atattvajñatayā phalāni adhikṛtya teṣu teṣu karmasu pravartante, 
teṣāṃ tāni kriyamāṇāni eva karmāṇi vāsanārūpatayā 
antaḥkaraṇabhūmau bījabhūtānyeva tiṣṭhante, ataśca 
anityakriyāphalopabhogaprakṣaye vividhavāsanārūpasya teṣu teṣu 
yonyantareṣu tasya tasya janmanaḥ punaḥ punaḥ kāraṇatāṃ pratipadyante, 
janma ca tasya tasya karmaṇaḥ, karma ca

p. 46) tasya tasya phalasya hetutvaṃ bhajate iti 
vāsanājanmakarmaphalacakrametadaniśamāvartamānamajñānāṃ 
bandhāya kalpate iti tvamapi sāmānyapuruṣavat karmaphalaheturmā bhūḥ 
uktayā prakriyayā karmaṇāṃ śubhāśubhānāṃ phalāni 
sukhādyātmakāni heturjanmano nimittaṃ yasya sa tathāvidho yathā na 
bhavasi uktajñānaniṣṭhaḥ san kurvanneva karmāṇi tena jñānāgninā 
dagdhabījatāmāpādayan parameśvarasvabhāvasamāpattilakṣaṇaṃ 
mokṣamavāpnuhītyarthaḥ | anyacca kasyāṃcidavasthāyāṃ prastutasya 
karmaparihārasya vidhīyamānatvāt yadakarmeti manyase, tatra tādṛśe'pi 
akarmaṇi te tava saṅgaḥ mābhūt | tatrāpi hi saṅgaḥ karmāntaravat 
bandhahetureveti uktameva prāk || 48 ||

yata evam, ataḥ

yogasthaḥ kuru karmāṇi saṅgaṃ tyaktvā dhanañjaya |
siddhyasiddhyoḥ samo bhūtvā samatvaṃ yoga ucyate || 49 ||

	iha śāstravihitānāṃ karmaṇāmanuṣṭhāne 
yathoktātmādvaitajñānaniṣṭhatvalakṣaṇaḥ samādhiryoga ucyate | yathā 
kapilādipraṇītasya sāṃkhyasaṃjñakasya jñānasya pātañjalādipraṇīte 
cittavṛttinirodhalakṣaṇe yoge niṣṭhā, tathā iha upadiṣṭasya 
ātmādvayajñānasya karmānuṣṭhānasamādhau niṣṭhā, iti yogaśabdena 
so'pi uktaḥ | tatra tādṛśe yoge sthitaḥ āśramāntaraparihāreṇa 
kṛtapratiṣṭhaḥ san yathoktāni karmāṇi anutiṣṭha | katham | saṅgaṃ 
tattadabhisaṃhitaphalaprāptihetutvapratipattyā 
tāvanmātropāyalīnacittattvaṃ parihṛtya, ata eva siddhau phalasampattau, 
asiddhau ca tadvighāte dvayorapi nirvikāratvāt samo nirviśeṣo bhūtvā 
yathoktajñānaniṣṭhatvāt 
nityaparipūrṇayathoktātmatattvapratyavamarśajanitānandatṛptasya 
karmaṇaḥ phalena anityena siddhena asiddhena vā na kaścit 
sukhaduḥkhātmako viśeṣa iti sarvasāmyadaśādhirūḍho

p. 47) bhūtvā yathoktayoganiṣṭho bhava | yataḥ samatvaṃ sarvāsu 
avasthāsu vikāraleśāsaṃspṛṣṭatvameva yoga ucyate sāmyaprāptireva 
asya niṣṭhetyarthaḥ || 49 ||
	ata eva etadyoganiṣṭhasya yogino lakṣaṇamāha

yasya sarve samārambhā nirāśīrbandhanāstviha |
tyāge yasya hutaṃ sarvaṃ sa tyāgī sa ca buddhimān || 50 ||

	yasya yathoktayogādhikāriṇaḥ puruṣasya sarve samārambhāḥ 
sarvāsu kriyāsu pravṛttayo'vaśyaṃbhāvino'pi nirāśīrbandhanāḥ 
bhavanti, sa tyāgī iti sambandhaḥ | āśīranityabhogaviṣayā ākāṃkṣā, 
tadeva bandhanaṃ saṃsārahetutvādasvātantryamāśīrbandhanam, 
niṣkrāntā āśīrbandhanāt nirāśīrbandhanā bhogātmakabandharahitā 
ityarthaḥ | ata eva avaśyānuṣṭheyakarmarūpaṃ sarvaṃ śubhamaśubhaṃ 
ca tyāge phalāśāsaṃnyāse hutam agrāviva bījajātaṃ 
phalaprasavasāmarthyavināśāya nikṣiptaṃ, sa evaṃvidho 
nityakarmānuṣṭhātaiva tyāgī tyāgavān, na punaḥ kleśabhīrutvādinā 
karmāṇi eva tyajan tyāgī bhavati iti | yuktiratra prāgeva uktā | sa evaca 
evaṃvidhaḥ praśasyajñānayuktaḥ, natu yaḥ karmatyāgādimātraṃ 
jñānaṃ manyate || 50 ||
	ata evaṃvidhotkṛṣṭabuddhiyuktaṃ karma samācaret, na kevalamiti 
āha

dūreṇa hyavaraṃ karma buddhiyogāddhanañjaya |
buddhau śaraṇamanviccha kṛpaṇāḥ phalahetavaḥ || 51 ||

	yasmāt buddhiyogāt yathoktajñānasamādhānāt dūreṇa atiśayena 
kevalaṃ tadvirahitaṃ karma avaraṃ nikṛṣṭaṃ jñānaśūnyaṃ karma 
saṃsārahetutvāt tato'tyantādhamamityarthaḥ | ataśca buddhau yathokte 
samyagjñāne śaraṇam āśrayam anviccha abhikāṅkṣitāmeva 
upāyatayā avalambasvetyarthaḥ |

p. 48) yato ye phalahetavaḥ anityaṃ bhogalakṣaṇaṃ phalameva 
hetustatsādhanabhūtāsu kriyāsu prayojakaṃ kāraṇaṃ yeṣāṃ, te 
jñānaśūnyakarmamātrānuṣṭhāyinaḥ kṛpaṇāḥ tucchaphalārthitvāt 
dīnāstattajjātiśarīrādyabhimānāvacchinnam, ata eva 
anityabhogātmakaphalatṛṣṇāturamātmānaṃ manyamānāḥ santo 
durācārāḥ || 51 ||
	ata eva tadvilakṣaṇasya jñāninaḥ svarūpaṃ pratipādayitumāha

buddhiyukto jahātīha ume sukṛtaduṣkṛte |
tasmādyogāya yujyasva yogaḥ karmasu kauśalam || 52 ||

	iha ātmaiva parameśvara ekaṃ tattvam | sa evaca svamāyāmahimnā 
jñeyabhāvāpādanena sarvasya kāryajātasya ekaḥ kartā pāramārthikaḥ, 
vicitraṃ ca idaṃ kāryaṃ nirmimāṇaḥ svecchāmātravyatiriktaṃ na tasya 
upādānamapekṣate, nāpi sahakāriṇam | naca 
idamitthamavabhāsamānamapi kāryaṃ cinmātrasvarūpatvādatiriktaṃ 
vikāraleśaṃ kaṃcidarpayituṃ prabhavati | 
tadevaṃvidhanityaniratiśayaiśvaryayuktamekaṃ tattvamātmatvena 
niṣprakampayā pratipattyā yaḥ pratyavamṛśati, sa buddhyā parameṇa 
prabodhena samyagjñānena yuktaḥ saṃbaddhaḥ iha ucyate | saca evaṃvidha 
iha upadiśyamāne karmayoge'dhikṛtaḥ kurvannapi karmāṇi 
īśvarabhāvānupraveśāt sarvaṃ samatvena paśyan, idaṃ 
svargādisukhaphalahetutvāt sukṛtaṃ satkarma, idaṃ duḥkhahetutvāt 
duṣkṛtaṃ duṣṭakarma, iti anutpannavivekajñānairviṣamadarśibhiḥ 
parikalpitabhede sukṛtaduṣkṛte dve api jahāti tyajati na tasya yoginaḥ 
sarvasamatvasamāhitasya kriyamāṇamapi karma sukṛtaduṣkṛtalakṣaṇaṃ 
bhedaṃ bhajatītyarthaḥ | evamanena ślokārdhena tattvajñānavān niyataṃ 
karma kuryādeva, naca tatra apāramārthikaṃ sadasadvikalpamāropayediti 
upadiṣṭaṃ bhavati | yata evaṃ tasmāttvaṃ 
yathoktatattvajñānābhiniviṣṭaḥ san yogāya

p. 49) samādhaye yujyasva karmānuṣṭhānadvāreṇa yukto bhava, yataḥ 
karmasu kriyamāṇeṣu yat kauśalam uktajñānaniṣṭhatvena 
sādhukāritvaṃ tadeva yogaḥ samādhiriha, na anyaḥ || 52 ||

	etameva arthaṃ hetūpanyāsapūrvaṃ vivarītumāha

karmajaṃ buddhiyuktā hi phalaṃ tyaktvā manīṣiṇaḥ |
janmabandhavinirmuktāḥ padaṃ gacchantyanāmayam || 53 ||

	yataḥ karmajam atattvajñaiḥ kriyamāṇāt karmaṇo'vaśyambhāvi 
sukhādyātmakaṃ phalaṃ sarvadā sarvato nirabhikāṃkṣasya 
ātmaniṣṭhatvādanabhisandhānena tyaktvā vihāya 
phalābhisandhyanuṣṭhīyamānakarma(kha0 bha0 pu0 pratibaddhājjanmana iti 
pāṭhaḥ |)pratibaddhatvāt  janmanaḥ punarbhavalakṣaṇāt 
bandhanādasvātantryāt nirmuktāḥ santo nirvighnatayā anāmayam 
anityasukhādisaṃkalpadoṣarahitaṃ padaṃ 
pāramārthikasvarūpapratiṣṭhātmakaṃ dhāma gacchanti prāpnuvanti | 
atastvaṃ buddhiyukto yogāya yujyasveti pūrveṇa sambandhaḥ || 53 ||
	yadāca etat paramaduravagāhaṃ tattvajñānamuktamapi jhagiti 
manasi pratiṣṭhāṃ na nibadhnāti, tadā tvayā atra śithilotsāhena na 
bhavitavyaṃ yataḥ krameṇa etat jñāsyase, ityāha

yadāsya mohakalilaṃ buddhyā vyatitariṣyasi |
tadā gantāsi nirvedaṃ śrotavyasya śrutasya ca || 54 ||

śrutivipratipannā te yadā sthāsyati niścitā |
samādhāvacalā buddhistadā yogamavāpsyasi || 55 ||

	yadā yasmin kāle tvam asya upadiṣṭasya jñānasya 
upapannatvamātrapratipattyā mohakalilaṃ 
anādijanmābhyastadehādyātmapratyayasvarūpamajñānakardamaṃ 
vyatitariṣyasi viparyayatvapratipattyā laṅghayiṣyasi tadā idaṃ śrutam, 
idaṃ śrotavyamitisaṃśayāviṣṭajanocitayornānāśāstraviṣayayoḥ 
śrotavyaśrutayoḥ nirvedaṃ virāgaṃ

p. 50) gamiṣyasi || tataśca niścayābhāvāt, idaṃ śrutam, idaṃ 
śrotavyamitivicikitsāviṣṭatayā yā śrutirnānāśāstraśravaṇaṃ, tasyā 
anupādeyatvāt vipratipannā pratikūlā satī tava matiḥ niścitā 
yathoktātmajñānanivṛttasaṃdehā, ata eva acalā vikalpairbalavadbhirapi 
aprakampyā satī yadā sthāsyati asmin samādhau sthitiṃ pratiṣṭhāṃ 
lapsyate tadā anena krameṇa yathoktaṃ yogaṃ karmasamādhilakṣaṇam 
avāpsyasi pratipatsyase || 55 ||
	atha te tadā matiḥ sthāsyati iti bhagavadvacanaṃ śrutvā, 
sthitaprajñatvasya samyak tattvamanavagacchan

arjuna uvāca

sthitaprajñasya kā bhāṣā samādhisthasya keśava |
sthitadhīḥ kiṃ prabhāṣeta kimāsīta vrajecca kim || 56 ||

	he bhagavan keśava ! karmasu kila kauśalaṃ yoga uktaḥ, 
niścalaprajñatā ca sthitaprajñatā | tadevaṃvidhasya asya sthitaprajñasya 
kā bhāṣā kathamasau bhāṣate, kimasya vyākhyānaṃ ? viruddhamiva asya 
ceṣṭitaṃ yato vāṅmanaḥkāyaistribhirvyāpāraṇaṃ karma ucyate 
tāstisro'pi ceṣṭā na asya upapadyante | kim asau prabhāṣeta ! 
bhāṣaṇakriyāṃ ca anutiṣṭhet, nahi asya ekatattvaniścalaprajñatvāt 
bhāṣaṇaṃ vacanakarma upapadyate | kimasau vrajet gamanakriyopalakṣitaṃ 
kāyavyāpāramācaret, so'pi asya pūrvavat na upapadyate | kimāsīta 
vāṅmanasapracāraparihāreṇa āntaryaiva vṛttyā tiṣṭhet, sāpi asya 
karmakauśalalakṣaṇe yoge vartamānasya ubhayakarmavṛttyātmikā na 
upapadyate- iti svarūpānavadhāraṇāt kā asya bhāṣeti uktam | etacca 
praśnatrayamavivakṣitakarmakakriyāmātraviṣayaṃ veditavyam || 56 ||

atha

yasya sarve samārambhāḥ------------------ | (50)

p. 51) ityādau buddhimacchabdena uktasya sthitaprajñasya 
svarūpamuktaprāyamapi duradhigamatvāt samyaganavadhāritam, idānīṃ 
kenāpi aṃśena viśeṣya punarucyamānamavadhārayeti arjunaṃ 
bodhayituṃ

bhagavānuvāca

prajahāti yadā kāmānsarvānpārtha manogatān |
ātmanyevātmanā tuṣṭaḥ sthitaprajñastadocyate || 57 ||

	iha khalu sarvarahasyānāmupaniṣadbhūte'smin vijñāne kaścideva 
lakṣeṣu eko 
janmasahasrapariśīlitaparyavabhātaikātmatāparāmṛṣṭarajastamomayamala
tayā sattvamātraśeṣatve sati, 
samupajātasvarūpapratyavamarśayogyaprajñaścaramajanmā 
puruṣātiśayo'dhikārī 
yastathāvidhāvasaropajātānujighṛkṣaparameśvarapreṣitābhyāṃ 
satatasahacarībhyāṃ bhaktiśraddhābhyāṃ dattadṛḍhahastāvalambo 
yathoktajñānaniḥśreṇīsthiraniveśitapadaḥ 
paramakāraṇasamāpattipratibimbarūpaṃ paramaṃ dhāma 
adhiroḍhumadhyavasitaḥ, yamuddiśya ātmatattvameva ahantayā vyapadiśan 
vakṣyati

manuṣyāṇāṃ sahasreṣu kaścidyatati siddhaye |
yatatāmapi siddhānāṃ kaścinmāṃ vetti tattvataḥ || (7/3)

iti, tathā

	bahūnāṃ janmanāmante jñānavānmāṃ prapadyate | (7/19)

ityādi | tādṛśaśca san yadā idamācarati tadā sthitaprajñobhavati | 
kimācarati | yadā manogatān saṃkalpavartinaḥ kāmān 
atattvajñānuṣṭheyakarmaphalabhūtān sukhādīn prajahāti prakarṣeṇa 
tyajati manāgapi tān na saṃkalpayati, natu karmāṇi prajahāti | kathametat 
saṃbhavati karmāṇi ca karoti phalaṃ ca prajahāti, iti | etadupapādayituṃ 
viśeṣaṇamāha ātmanyevātmanā tuṣṭaḥ iti | na sarvaḥ kaścit 
svasaṃvidadhikaraṇaṃ toṣaṃ prītimanubhavati, kiṃtu aprāptapūrveṇa 
kenacidanityena prāpyena sukhasādhanabhūtena vastunā prāptena 
anityameva

p. 52) toṣaṃ prāpnoti | ayaṃ tu yogī 
satatasannihitānastamitacitprakāśātmanā parāmarśābhāvamātrāt 
vyavadhānamiva prāptena sarvadā 
susaṃpūrṇasvamahimatvādakhilavastuspṛhāvicchedakāriṇā 
svayaṃnihitavismṛtena mahānidhānena iva punaḥ pratyuditasmṛtivaśāt 
pratilabdhena ātmanaiva satyena svabhāvena niruttareṇa lābhena 
labdhasahajānandaḥ | evaṃvidhaśca ayaṃ 
kṛpaṇaprārthanīyānutpādavināśadharmakān kāmān kathaṃ na 
prajahyāt || 57 ||
	yadāca itthaṃbhūtaḥ san sarvakarmāṇi kurvannapi kāmān 
prajahāti, tadā sthitaprajño yathoktasvabhāvalabdhapratiṣṭhabuddhirbhavati, 
iti sāmānyena sthitaprajñalakṣaṇamuktvā tadeva viśeṣato vaktumāha

duḥkheṣvanudvignamanāḥ sukheṣu vigataspṛhaḥ |
vītarāgabhayakrodhaḥ sthitadhīrmunirucyate || 58 ||

	iha karmaphalabhūte sukhaduḥkhe prāpsyamāne prāpte ca 
dviprakāratayā sarvaprāṇināṃ vikārahetutvamāpadyete | ayaṃ tu 
uktalakṣaṇaḥ sthitaprajñaḥ prāpsyamāneṣu satsu duḥkheṣu 
anudvignamanāḥ nirbhayacitta eva bhavati, 
yathoktaparipūrṇātmasvarūpalabdhapratiṣṭhatvāt 
sarvasāmyasamādhimāpannaḥ, tathā bhayaṃ 
sukhapratīghātahetoraśakyapratīkārāt santrāsaḥ, tathā krodhaḥ 
śakyapratīkāraṃ tathāvidhaṃ prati roṣaḥ, ete vītā vigatā yasya, sa 
tathāvidhaḥ sthitaprajñaḥ ucyate || 58 ||
	evaṃ prāpsyamānasukhaduḥkhanirvikāratayā sthitaprajñasya 
lakṣaṇaviśeṣamuktvā, prāptasukhaduḥkhasya nirvikāratayā taṃ 
pratipādayitumāha

yaḥ sarvatrānabhisnehastattatprāpya śubhāśubham |
nābhinandati na dveṣṭi tasya prajñā pratiṣṭhitā || 59 ||

yogī tattat anantabhedaṃ sukhasādhanabhūtaṃ duḥkhasādhanabhūtaṃ 
vā

p. 53) sukhātmakaṃ duḥkhātmakameva vā śubhāśubhaṃ prāpya 
nābhinandati na apūrvakṣaṇikaharṣalakṣaṇaṃ kṣobhaṃ śrayati, yataḥ 
sarvasmin vastuni anabhisnehaḥ idaṃ mama sarvasvam, etena vinā mama 
sarvamanyat duḥkhakāraṇamiti abhīṣṭheṣu vastuṣu anurāgo'bhisnehaḥ, sa 
yasya nāsti, sa tathoktaḥ | tathā na dveṣṭi na tatpratiṣedhaprayatnalakṣaṇaṃ 
kṣobhamupayāti | ya evaṃvidhaḥ prāptayorapi 
priyāpriyayornityanirvikārasvātmamātrasuvyasthitabuddhiḥ, tasya 
pratiṣṭhitā prajñā ucyate || 59 ||
	evaṃ pratiṣṭhitaprajñatvaṃ yena karmaṇā upāyabhūtena 
niṣpadyate, tadvānapi sthitaprajña eveti pratipādayitumāha

yadā saṃharate cāyaṃ kūrmo'ṅgānīva sarvataḥ |
indriyāṇīndriyārthebhyaḥ sthitaprajñastadocyate || 60 ||

caśabdo vyatireke | yadā punarayaṃ yogī sarvataḥ sarvebhya 
indriyārthebhyaḥ śabdādibhya indriyāṇi śrotrādīni saṃharate 
vyatirecayati svabhāvataścidarkaraśmirūpāṇi tanniṣṭhānyeva karoti | 
katham | kūrmaḥ kacchapo yathā svāvayavān svaśarīraniṣṭhāneva karoti | 
tadā asau sthitaprajña iti ucyate || 60 ||
	atho viṣayebhya indriyasaṃhāraḥ kiṃ śabdādyagrahaṇena, kiṃvā 
gṛhyamāṇeṣu api śabdādiṣu nirvikāratvena atra uktaḥ, iti 
pakṣadvayasambhāvanāyāṃ 
pūrvapakṣasthairyapratipādanapūrvamuttarameva pakṣaṃ 
samarthayitumāha

viṣayā vinivartante nirāhārasya dehinaḥ |
rasavarjaṃ raso'pyasya paraṃ dṛṣṭvā nivartate || 61 ||

	abhyupagamya idamucyate astyetat nirāhārasya śāstravihitānapi 
śabdādīnanāharatastān dūrataḥ pariharato viṣayāḥ iṣṭāḥ śabdādayo 
vinivartante pratyāsattyabhāvāt svakāryaṃ rāgādikaṃ na ārabhante, 
kiṃtu rasavarjaṃ tadviṣayaṃ rasamabhilāṣaṃ rāgākhyaṃ kleśaṃ

p.54) varjayitvā, sa hi tadānīṃ prasuptastanurvā vartate | yadivā 
paramanaiṣṭhikasya raso'pi nivartate nāma paraṃ prakṛṣṭaṃ 
samādhyupeyaṃ vastu dṛṣṭvā sākṣātkṛtya | yadivā eṣa yogī yadā 
paraṃ tattvaṃ viṣayaparihārasamādhikrameṇa paśyati, tadā tat 
dṛṣṭavatastasya raso'pi jātu nivartate iti katvāntaṃ niṣṭhārthatvena 
vyākhyeyam || 61 ||
	evaṃ viṣayaparihāraparamasamādheḥ parāmapi kāṣṭhāṃ 
prāptasya sacchalatvaṃ pratipādayan, 
yathoktajñānapūrvamaniṣiddhaviṣayasevātmakamacchalaṃ samādhiṃ 
siddhāntīkurvannāha

yattasyāpi hi kaunteya puruṣasya vipaścitaḥ |
indriyāṇi pramāthīni haranti prasabhaṃ manaḥ || 62 ||

tāni saṃyamya manasā yukta āsīta tatparaḥ |
vaśe hi yasyendriyāṇi tasya prajñā pratiṣṭhitā || 63 ||

	yat yasmāt tasyāpi viṣayānāharaṇasamāhitasya 
paratattvadarśanayogyatāmapi prāptasya viduṣo vipaścitaḥ 
jñānavighātajāgarūkatvāt chidrānveṣaṇaparāṇi pramathanaśīlāni 
indriyāṇi prasabhaṃ prasahyaiva kaṃcidavakāśaleśaṃ labdhvā manaḥ 
cittaṃ haranti labdhādapi samādhiphalāt vyapakarṣanti 
viṣayābhimukhatāṃ nayanti | śrūyante hi tathāvidhā 
viṣayapratiṣedhayoganiṣṭhā yoginaścirādapi indriyaiḥ kāmān prati 
pravartanena pramathitacittāḥ | yata evamayaṃ yathoktajñānaniṣṭhena 
cetasā saṃyamya ātmavaśāni kṛtvā yukto yathoktalakṣaṇe svātmatattve 
samāhitaḥ sarvāsu avasthāsu tadekaniṣṭhaḥ san tatparaḥ 
śāstrāniṣiddhaśabdādigrahaṇapravṛttendriyaniṣṭha eva āsīta | yasmāt 
yathoktena krameṇa yasya yogina indriyāṇi vaśe vidheyatve vartante, tasya 
pratiṣṭhitālabdhaniścalasthitiḥ prajñā ucyate | atra ayaṃ 

p. 55) dṛṣṭāntaḥ kaścit gāruḍikastādṛśaḥ saṃbhavati yo 
mantraprabhāveṇa bhujagān pratiṣeddhumalam | sa yadā 
tādṛṅmantropāsanaśaithilyalakṣaṇaṃ pramādamātiṣṭhati, tadā te 
cchidrānveṣiṇo bhujagāstaṃ vyāpādayanti eva | kaścittu 
gāruḍaśāstrarahasyaveditvāt tādṛśātiśayayukto bhavati yena taireva 
bhujagaiḥ sārdhaṃ sragdāmabhiriva krīḍati, taṃ ca te na kadācidapi 
abhibhavituṃ prabhavanti | evaṃ yo yogī kayācit yuktyā kaṃcitkālaṃ 
viṣayān parihṛtya samādhimabhyasyati, tasya manāgapi avadhānavicchedāt 
te labdhāvakāśā viṣayāścittaṃ pramathnanti eva | yastu yathokte 
pāramārthike svātmani eva sadā suvyavasthitāḥ, sa viṣayaiḥ krīḍannapi 
na kathaṃcit bādhituṃ śakyaḥ | yathoktaṃ

vasanviṣayamadhye'pi na vasatyeva buddhimān |
saṃvasatyeva durbuddhirasatsu viṣayeṣvapi ||

iti || 63 ||

	atha kūrmo'ṅgānīva iti upamānanirdeśāt 
viṣayebhyo'tyantavyāvartanameva indriyāṇāṃ saṃhāraḥ saṃyamo 
yastebhyo'nyathaiva (tebhyo viṣayebhyaḥ, tamindriyāṇāṃ saṃhāram, 
anyathā anyena prakāreṇa pratipādayan, rāgadveṣavimuktendriyāṇāṃ 
pravṛttireva saṃhāra ityanyathātvamityarthaḥ |) taṃ pratipādayan 
pratiṣṭhitaprajñatvasya ślokapañcakena mukhyaṃ lakṣaṇamāha

dhyāyato viṣayānpuṃsaḥ saṃgasteṣūpajāyate |
