saṃgātsaṃjāyate kāmaḥ kāmātkrodho'bhijāyate || 64 ||

krodhādbhavati saṃmohaḥ saṃmohātsmṛtivibhramaḥ |
smṛtibhraṃśādbuddhināśo buddhināśātpraṇaśyati || 65 ||

	iha khalu acintyaparameśvaramāyāmuṣitasamyagjñānatayā 
śarīramātramātmatvena pratipannasya saṃsāriṇaḥ sataḥ puṃsaḥ 
sannihitamapi mukhyamavyayaṃ nirupamānantanijānandasudhāsāgaraṃ 
satyaṃ svātmānamavimṛśato 
duḥkhaikapariṇāmānityakṛttrimasukhasādhanaspṛhatayā śabdādīn 
viṣayān

p. 56) dhyāyataḥ satataṃ cintayataḥ teṣu saṅgo niviḍanimagnamānasatvam 
upajāyate utpadyate | tasmāt tathāvidhāt saṅgāt kāmaḥ 
tadviṣayo'bhilāṣo rāgākhyaḥ kleśaḥ saṃjāyate samudbhavati | tasmācca 
tatpratikūleṣu vastuṣu krodhaḥ dveṣākhyaḥ kleśaprakāraḥ abhijāyate 
prabhavati | tataśca saṃmohaḥ 
ātmānātmādivastuvivekavirahātmakamajñānaṃ prabhavati mohākhyaḥ 
kleśaḥ unmajjatītyarthaḥ | tataśca smṛtirvibhrāmyati- satyaṃ 
smartavyamātmānamaspṛśantī svarūpalopamiva āpnoti | tādṛśācca 
tasmāt smṛtibhraṃśāt buddhiḥ paramārthaniścayātmakaṃ jñānaṃ 
naśyati tirobhavati | tasmācca hetoḥ puruṣaḥ 
satyasvarūpapratiṣṭhābhāvalakṣaṇaṃ nāśamupagacchatīti || 64-65 ||
	saṃsāriṇaṃ puruṣamuddiśya apratiṣṭhitabuddhitvasya 
hetuparamparāṃ pratipādya, 
idānīmanujighṛkṣākṣaṇākṣipyamāṇasandehatiraskariṇītirodhānatayā 
prakāśamānayathoktasvasvabhāvaprakāśasya prāptaprabodhasya 
mumukṣoḥ puṃsaḥ pratiṣṭhitaprajñatvasya kāraṇakramaṃ 
pratipādayitumāha

rāgadveṣavimuktaistu viṣayānindriyaiścaran |
ātmavaśyairvidheyātmā prasādamadhigacchati || 66 ||

prasāde sarvaduḥkhānāṃ hānirasyopajāyate |
prasannacetaso hyāśu buddhiḥ paryavatiṣṭhate || 67 ||

nāsti buddhirayuktasya nacāyuktasya bhāvanā |
na cābhāvayataḥ śāntiraśāntasya kutaḥ sukham || 68 ||

indriyāṇāṃ hi caratāṃ yanmano'nuvidhīyate |
tadasya harati prajñāṃ vāyurnāvamivāmbhasi || 69 ||

	vidheyātmā pratyavamarśaviṣayabhūtatvāt svādhīna ātmā 
svabhāvo yasya sa tādṛśaḥ puruṣa ātmavaśyaiḥ tādṛśasya satyasya 
ātmanaḥ

p. 57) svaśaktitayā pratipādyamānatvāt 
vidheyaistadadhīnapravṛttisthitisaṃhṛtibhirbāhyantaraiḥ indriyaiḥ karaṇai 
kiraṇairiva arkaḥ padārthān viṣayān caran gacchan prasādaṃ 
sukhādikalaṅkasamparkābhāvarūpaṃ vaiṣadyam adhigacchati pratilabhate 
| ata eva kīdṛśairindriyairviṣayāṃścaran | rāgadveṣavimuktaiḥ 
rāgadveṣau hi viṣayamātradhyānasaṃprasaktasya vismṛtasyātmanaḥ 
puṃso viṣayān prati pravartamāneṣu indriyeṣu parisphurataḥ | yasya tu 
sarvataḥparipūrṇatvāt nityanirabhilāṣasvarūpapratyavamarśapūrvakaḥ 
sarvajñānakriyāvyavahāraḥ, syata rāgadveṣābhyāṃ vimuktāni 
indriyāṇi svaṃ svaṃ viṣayamabhipravartante iti 
tādṛśairindriyairviṣayāṃścaran prasādamadhigacchati, iti uktam | 
evaṃvidhe ca nisarganirmalasvarūpābhivyaktilakṣaṇe prasāde sati 
rāgādikleśanibandhanānāṃ 
sarveṣāmādhyātmikādidaivikādhibhautikānāṃ duḥkhānāṃ hāniḥ 
parikṣayaḥ asya puṃsaḥ upajāyate | yato yathoktena prakāreṇa prasannaṃ 
nīrāgadveṣakalaṅkaṃ ceto yasya tādṛśasya puṃsaḥ āśu 
prasādasamanantarameva buddhiḥ prajñā paryavatiṣṭhate paritaḥ samantāt 
satye svātmani nirviplavāṃ pratiṣṭhāṃ labhate || 66-69 ||

yata evaṃ

tasmādyasya mahābāho nigṛhītāni sarvataḥ |
indriyāṇīndriyārthebhyastasya prajñā pratiṣṭhitā || 70 ||

	tasmāt kāraṇāt yasya puruṣasya sarvataḥ sarvebhyaḥ sukhahetubhyo 
duḥkhahetubhyaśca indriyārthobhyaḥ viṣayebhyo duḥkhahetuṣvapi 
dveṣavirahitatvena sukhahetuṣvapi nīrāgatayā aprasṛtānyeva nigṛhītāni 
niyamitāni bāhyābhyantarāṇi indriyāṇi tasya tathāvidhasya 
pratyāsannaparameśvarasāyujyasya prajñā buddhiḥ pratiṣṭhitā svātmani 
labdhasthitiḥ, natu anyasya yaḥ pratanuprayatnalakṣaṇasamādhiniruddhāni

p. 58) anutkhātarāgadveṣamayamūlatvāt prasṛtāni kāryakarāṇyeva 
nigṛhītāni indriyāṇi manyate || 68 ||

	ata eva samastasaṃsāripuruṣavilakṣaṇāṃ prabuddhasya vṛttiṃ 
pratipādayitumāha

yā niśā sarvabhūtānāṃ tasyāṃ jāgarti saṃyamī | 
yasyāṃ jāgrati bhūtāni sā rātriḥ paśyato muneḥ || 71 ||

	sarvavastuprakāśakasvarūpaprakāśanirūpaṇarūpā vṛttireva iha 
saṃsāriṇaḥ prati niśāśabdena uktā, tadviṣayatvena hi sarvabhūtāni 
anunmiṣitadṛkśaktitvāt prasuptāni tiṣṭhanti | evaṃvidhā yā 
sarvabhūtānāṃ niśā tasyāṃ tadviṣaye saṃyamī yathoktaprakāreṇa 
nigṛhītasarvendriyaḥ prabuddho jāgarti 
pravikasitasvabhāvanirmalavijñānamayavilocanavibhāvyamānāṃ tāmeva 
avimuñcan vigatamohanidra āste | yasyāṃ tu 
pratikṣaṇavinaśvarasukhaduḥkhasādhanabhūtaviṣayagrahaṇotsargamātrav
yagrasamagrendriyagrāmāyāṃ saṃsāravyavahāravṛttau sarvabhūtāni 
samastaprāṇabhṛto jāgrati tāvanmātrāvahitatvāt vinidratāmiva 
anubhavanti, sā tādṛśī vṛttiḥ paśyataḥ 
sadasatpadārthasārthaparamārthamālokayato muneḥ saṃyaminaḥ 
prabuddhasya rātriḥ niśā tāṃ prati tasya niravadhānatvāt || 71 ||
	evaṃ satatamanastamitasāmānyasaṃvitprakāśātmani svabhāve 
suvyavasthitaprasannabuddhiḥ prabuddhaḥ pavana iva 
sadasadāmodairvividhaiḥ padārthaiḥ paricayamupagacchannapi 
tatsaṃparkasaṃjātaiḥ sukhādibhirmanāgapi na vikṛtiṃ nīyate iti 
upamānanidarśanena sphuṭataraṃ tatsvarūpaṃ pratipādayitumāha

āpūryamāṇamacalapratiṣṭhaṃ
samudramāpaḥ praviśanti yadvat |

p. 59) tadvatkāmā yaṃ praviśanti sarve
sa śāntimāpnoti na kāmakāmī || 72 ||

	sa prabuddhaḥ puruṣātiśayaḥ śāntiṃ 
sakalavikalpakallolakṣobhaparikṣayāt nistimitacittavṛttitām āpnoti 
adhigacchati | ko'sau | yaṃ kāmāḥ viṣayāḥ tadvat praviśanti 
nirabhilāṣameva avagāhante | katham | yathā samudraṃ 
nānādigantarāgatasaridrupā āpaḥ | kīdṛśam | tābhiḥ āpūryamāṇaṃ 
bhriyamāṇamapi acalapratiṣṭhaṃ 
niṣprakampāpāragambhīranijāvasthānam | anena upamānena 
pratyāsattisāmarthyāt sambadhyamānairapi sukhādihetubhiḥ 
śabdādibhiryaḥ kṣobhaṃ netuṃ na śakyate, sa eva śānta iti ucyate, natu 
yasteṣu sannihiteṣu aśāntaḥ | yathoktaṃ

vikārahetau sati vikriyante yeṣāṃ na cetāṃsi ta eva dhīrāḥ |
						(ku0 saṃ0 1/58)

iti | ata eva āha na kāmakāmī śāntimāpnoti iti || 72 ||

manasā kāmān sukhasādhanabhūtān viṣayān 
kāmayamāno'bhilaṣyanneva indriyavyāpāraparihāramātreṇa na 
śamamāpnoti, ityevamabhiprāyaṃ prakaṭayitumāha

vihāya kāmānyaḥ sarvānpumāṃścarati niḥspṛhaḥ |
nirmamo nirahaṃkāraḥ sa śāntimadhigacchati || 73 ||

	yaḥ sarvān aśeṣān kāmān iṣṭān śabdādiviṣayānabhilāṣān 
manasaiva vihāya parityajya indriyairviṣayān carati avagāhate sa śāntim 
uktarūpām adhigacchati prāpnoti | kīdṛśaḥ san | 
nityatṛptādvayacinmātrapratilabdhātmapratyayatvāt niḥspṛhaḥ spṛhāto 
viṣayasukhalolupatāyā niṣkrāntaḥ, tathā nirmamaḥ pratiniyateṣu 
dehendriyaviṣayādiṣu bhāveṣu mamaiva idamiti mamatvābhimānāt 
niṣkrāntaḥ, tathā nirahaṃkāraḥ dehabuddhiprāṇādiṣu 
ātmapratyayarūpādavacchinnādahaṃkārādapi niṣkrāntaḥ iti || 73 ||

p. 60) etadantena granthena apavargamārgapravaṇasya puṃsaḥ prathamameva 
sahajabhaktyādyavyabhicāricihnasūcitopadeśayogyabuddhitayā 
sadgurūpadiṣṭadehadehivyatirekajñānanirantarābhyāsavaśāt 
dehādivargavyāvartamānātmapratyayasya 
advaitasāmānyasaṃvitprarohādahaṃpratīteścidarkaraśmirūpāṇāmanta
rbahiṣkaraṇaśaktīnāṃ śāstracoditeṣu viṣayeṣu visṛṣṭatvameva 
nigrahaṃ niruttarapadaprāptihetuṃ siddhāntīkṛtya 
saptadaśādhyāyapradarśitaparamarahasyopadeśaprāṇabhūtārthapratipā
dakaṃ dvitīyamadhyāyamupasaṃharannāha

eṣā brāhmī sthitiḥ pārtha naināṃ prāpya vimuhyati | 
sthitvāsyāmantakāle'pi brahma nirvāṇamṛcchati || 74 ||

	eṣā vihāya kāmānyaḥ sarvān ityantena granthena pratipāditā, 
brahmaṇaḥ parasya tattvasya paramātmasaṃjñakasya sambandhinī sthitiḥ | 
etayā brahmaṇi sthīyate iti viṣayāntaranivṛttagatitayā pratiṣṭhā kriyate | 
ata eva enāṃ prāpya yathoktena krameṇa svātmīkāreṇa svīkṛtya 
abhiyukto na muhyati dehādiṣu ātmapratyayarūpaṃ vaicittyaṃ na bhajate, 
yataḥ asyām antakāle'pi uparamasamaye'pi sthitvā viśramya nirvāṇaṃ 
nānāvikalpakṣobhapratyayāt praśāntaṃ brahma paraṃ tattvaṃ svaṃ 
svabhāvamayam ṛcchati gacchati tadaikātmyamāpadyate iti || 72 ||

dvitīye'dhyāye'smin hṛdaya iva gambhīravimale
gṛhīte gītānāṃ vivudhadayitānāṃ praṇayataḥ |

tadāśleṣānandāmṛtarasamapūrvaṃ rasayatāṃ
sadā vaḥ pumbhāvo bhavatu kṛtakṛtyaḥ kṛtadhiyām ||

iti śrīmadrājānakarāmakaṇṭhaviracite vākyārthānvayamātre 
sarvatobhadranāmni bhagavadgītāvivaraṇe dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ || 2 ||



atha tṛtīyo'dhyāyaḥ

	atha jñānasya karmaṇaśca bhagavadbhāṣitāni pṛthak 
praśaṃsāvacanāni anuvimṛśan samyak samuccayārthamanavadhārya 
kimetayorupādeyataraṃ syāditi saṃśayāno

bhagavantamarjuna uvāca

jyāyasī cetkarmaṇaste matā buddhirjanārdana |
tatkiṃ karmaṇi ghore māṃ niyojayasi keśava || 1 ||

vyāmiśreṇaiva vākyena buddhiṃ mohayasīva me |
tadekaṃ vada niścitya yena śreyo'hamāpnuyām || 2 ||

dūreṇa hyavaraṃ karma buddhiyogāt ---------- | (2/51)

ityādi vadatastava karmaṇaḥ sakāśāt yadi buddhirjyāyasī matā jñānaṃ 
prakṛṣṭataratvena abhipretaṃ, tat kiṃ kena hetunā bandhahetutvāt sarvasmin 
karmaṇi dāruṇavipākatvāt ghore viśeṣato'smin 
gurujñātighātādimahāpātakahetau saṃgrāmarūpe māṃ

karmaṇyastvadhikāraste----------------| (2/48)

ityādinā niyojayasi pravartayasi | itthaṃca 
parasparaviruddhajñānakarmapraśaṃsārūpatvāt vyāmiśreṇa 
saṃkīrṇena vākyena mama buddhiṃ prajñāṃ mohayasīva 
niścayopadeśena prabodhanamicchan saṃśayāpādakavacanapraṇayanāt 
vaicittyamiva māṃ prāpayasītyarthaḥ | tasmāt 
kāraṇādetayorjñānakarmaṇormadhyāt ekaṃ jñānaṃ karma vā 
idameva anuṣṭheyamiti niścitya nirṇīya brūhi, yena ekatareṇa sugṛhītena 
satā śreyaḥ apavargalakṣaṇamabhyudayam ahaṃ prāpnuyāṃ labheyeti || 
1-2 ||

p. 62) atha anyathaiva mayoktamanyathaiva tvayā pratipannamiti arjunaṃ prati 
upālambhamiva āviṣkurvan tatsaṃśayavyudāsāya

bhagavānuvāca

loke'smindvividhā niṣṭhā purā proktā mayānagha | 
jñānayogena sāṃkhyānāṃ karmayogena yoginām || 3 ||

	saṃkhyā 
yathāpratipāditalakṣaṇaśarīriśarīrādyupādeyaheyapadārthasaṃkhyāna.
m, tadvido mukhyatayā sāṃkhyā ucyante | yogo 
yathoktasāṃkhyaniścitārthasākṣātkārakaraṇaṃ kriyāviśeṣaḥ, sa 
vidyate yeṣāṃ, te'pi mukhyatayā yoginaḥ, kapilādayo 
hairaṇyagarbhādayaśca gauṇyā vṛttyā sāṃkhyā yoginaśca ucyante | 
yathoktalakṣaṇasya ātmāderarthasya teṣāmaniścayādasākṣātkārācca 
tadevaṃvidhānāṃ sāṃkhyānāṃ yogināṃ ca purā asmādadhyāyāt 
pūrvaṃ dvitīye'dhyāye mayā ekaiva niṣṭhā uktā niścayasiddhāntaḥ 
pratipāditaḥ | kīdṛśī niṣṭhā | dvividhā dve ekānuṣṭhātṛpuruṣaniṣṭhe 
yugapatprayojye vidhe vidhāne prakārau yasyāḥ, sā tathoktā | kena 
prakāreṇa dvividhā, ityāha jñānayogena sāṃkhyānāṃ karmayogena 
yoginām iti | yathāpratipāditādvayacinmātrasvarūpasya ātmatattvasya 
pratyabhijñānalakṣaṇā pratipattiriha jñānamucyate, tadviṣayo yogaḥ 
samādhiḥ karmaṇyapi sati yathoktasvarūpāpracyutilakṣaṇaṃ 
praṇidhānam | tena jñānayogena yathoktānāṃ mukhyānāṃ 
sāṃkhyānāṃ niṣṭhā mayā uktā | te hi naiṣṭhikāḥ sāṃkhyā yeṣāṃ 
karmaṇi pravṛttānāmapi yathoktātmajñānaviṣayaḥ samādhiraviplutaḥ | 
karma yathāvarṇaṃ yathāśramaṃ śāstracoditā sā sā kriyā, tatra yogaḥ 
samādhiryathoktajñānāparityāgena tadanuṣṭhānaniṣṭhatā | tena 
karmayogena mukhyānāṃ yogināṃ niṣṭhā uktā | evaṃ jñānayogasya 
karmāvinābhāvāt karmayogasya ca jñānāvinābhāvādekaiva 
prakāradvayanirvartyā

p. 63) niṣṭhā mayā pūrvamuktā | tatpratipādakaśca granthastatra uktaḥ 
kathaṃ vismṛto bhavataḥ | tathāca

dūreṇa hyavaraṃ karma------------------| (2/51)

ityādinā jñānasya prakarṣapratipādanamupakramya 
tatsamādhipratipādanāya uktaṃ tasmādyogāya yujyasva karmaniṣṭho 
bhava yataḥ karmasu yo yogastat kauśalaṃ sā buddhimattā jñānitvamiti | 
anena jñānayogena sāṃkhyānāṃ niṣṭheti tatra uktam | tathā 
karmaṇyastvadhikāraste tasmādyogāya yujyasva ityādinā 
karmaṇo'vaśyakaraṇīyatvaṃ vyavasthāpya tatsamādhipratipādanāya 
uktaṃ

karmajaṃ buddhiyuktā hi phalaṃ tyaktvā manīṣiṇaḥ | 
karmabandhavinirmuktāḥ padaṃ gacchantyanāmayam || (2/53)

tathā

rāgadveṣavimuktaistu viṣayānindriyaiścaran |
ātmavaśyairvidheyātmā prasādamadhigacchati || (2/66)

iti | anenāpi karmayogena yogināṃ niṣṭhā tatra uktā | kiṃ bahunā

dūreṇa hyavaraṃ karma-----------------| (2/51)

ityataḥ prabhṛti adhyāyaparisamāptiṃ 
yāvadekapuruṣānuṣṭheyadvividhaniṣṭhāpratipādanaparatvena sarvo 
granthastatra vyākhyātaḥ | tat kā me vyāmiśravacanatā yayā evaṃ te 
buddhimoha utpannaḥ || 3 ||
	atha manyase yathoktātmalakṣaṇaikārthāvahitatvaṃ jñānamiti 
uktam, karma ca nānāviṣayahānādānarūpavyāpārātmakaṃ; 
tadetayorjñānakarmaṇoḥ 
parasparaviruddhayoryugapadekapuruṣānuṣṭheyatvaṃ kathamupapadyate iti | 
atra ucyate samyaganavadhāritā jñānakarmānuṣṭhānaniṣṭhā tava

p. 64) evaṃvidhabuddhimohakāraṇam | atastāṃ 
punarucyamānāmavadhāraya, iti manasi kṛtvā āha

na karmaṇāmanārambhānnaiṣkarmyaṃ puruṣo'śrute |
naca saṃnyasanādeva siddhiṃ samadhigacchati || 4 ||

	agṛhītayathāpratipāditasamucitasamuccayārthaniścayatvāt sarvathā 
karmaṇāṃ jñānavirodhitvaṃ manvānasya tava yadetat naiṣkarmyam 
avidyamānakarmatvaṃ siddhihetutvena pratibhāti, tat siddhikāmaḥ puruṣaḥ 
karmaṇāṃ sarvakriyāṇām anārambhāt ananuṣṭhānāt na aśnute na 
prāpnoti vakṣyamāṇayā yuktyā śarīritvāvyabhicāriṇāṃ 
śāstrīyāṇāṃ ca karmaṇāṃ duṣpariharatvāt | ato na 
karmaṇāmeṣāmakaraṇamātramabhāvatvena iha vivakṣitam, kintu 
bandhalakṣaṇasvakāryasampādanasāmarthyabhaṅgena 
eṣāmabhāvāpādanamiha abhipretam | atha 
vihitakarmānanuṣṭhānamātraṃ karmatyāgaḥ saṃnyāse'bhimataḥ | 
tadevaṃvidhāt saṃnyasanamātrādeva kevalāt 
yathāpratipāditajñānaśūnyāt na puruṣaḥ siddhim apavargalakṣaṇāṃ 
sampattiṃ samadhigacchati samāsādayati ||
	yadi iha jñānarahitān vihitakarmaparihāramātrāt 
mohalakṣaṇaphalāvāptiḥ syāt, tat suṣuptādayo mohātmakatvāt 
naiṣkarmyamāvahanto'vasthāviśeṣāḥ siddhihetavaḥ syuḥ, naca 
vihitakarmānārambhamātrāt naiṣkarmyasiddhiriti yaduktaṃ, tatra 
hetumāha

nahi kaścitkṣaṇamapi jātu tiṣṭhatyakarmakṛt |
kāryate hyavaśaḥ karma sarvaḥ prakṛtijairguṇaiḥ || 5 ||

	hiśabdo hetau | yasmāt kaścit dehī jātu kadācit 
tattadavasthāviśiṣṭe kasmiṃścidapi kāle na kṣaṇamapi susūkṣmapi 
kālaṃ tadavayavam akarmakṛt tiṣṭhati karma akurvāṇaḥ sthitimanubhavati 
vartate ityarthaḥ | tathāca bālyayauvanavārdhakarūpāsu sthūlāsu 
avasthāsu manovākkāyaiḥ

p. 65) sarvaḥ kaścidehī karma kurvannāste iti suvyaktameva tadantargatāsu 
ca jāgarasvapnasuṣuptarūpāsu sūkṣmāsu avasthāsu na kaścidakarmakṛt | 
yato jāgarāvasthāyāṃ 
śabdādiviṣayagrahaṇavyagrendriyavyāpārarūpāyāṃ sarvasya 
tatkarmaniṣṭhatvaṃ sphuṭameva, svapnāvasthāyāmapi 
viṣayagrahaṇāvilopāt na kasyacidakarmatvena sthitiḥ, suṣuptāvasthāyāṃ 
tu tatkālavyatiriktaviṣayasaṃvedanābhāve'pi pratibodhakāle 
sukhaduḥkhamoharūpānubhūtaviṣayapratyavamarśānyathānupapattyā 
mānasaṃ karma vidyate eva | yā api yogiviṣayā etā jāgarādayo'vasthāḥ, 
tāsvapi eṣaiva vārtā | yato 
jāgarasvapnayorgrāhyagragītrādivikalpānuparamāt, 
suṣuptāvasthāyāmapi tatkālaṃ 
grāhyagrahaṇagrahītṛbhedasaṃvedanābhāve satyapi vyutthāne 
tathāvidhasamādhyavasthāpratyavamarśānyathānupapattiriti tisṛṣvapi 
yogyavasthāsu na kaścidakarmakṛt vartitumarhati, iti 
vihitakarmānārambhamātrāt na naiṣkarmyasiddhiriti pūrveṇa sambandhaḥ 
| itthaṃ sarvakarmābhāvasya anupapattau hetumāha kāryate iti | hiśabdo 
hetau | yasmāt sarvaḥ kaściddehabhṛt avaśaḥ paratantra eva san prakṛtijaiḥ 
pradhānabhavaiḥ guṇaiḥ sattvādibhiḥ karma śāstravihitaṃ 
śarīritvāvyabhicāri ca prāhyate'nuṣṭhāpyate | iha hi 
parameśvarecchāparikalpitakāraṇabhāvaṃ prakṛtyākhyaṃ 
tattvamanyonyaguṇapradhānabhāvapariṇatasattvādiguṇamayaṃ 
tattaccharīramaśeṣaśarīriṇāmārabhate, guṇāśca 
anyonyāvinābhāvavṛttitvāt rajoguṇānugatatve sati sarvaṃ 
kaṃciccharīriṇaṃ sāttvikādikarmādhikṛtaṃ kurvate iti prakṛtijairguṇaiḥ 
sarvaḥ karma kāryate iti uktam || 5 ||
	evaṃca sarvadehināṃ sarvāvasthāsu sarvakarmocchedānupapattau 
sthitāyāṃ vihitānāṃ karmaṇāmanārambhamātreṇa 
naiṣkarmyābhimānino yoginaḥ 
śarīritvāvyabhicāriduṣpariharakarmāntarasadbhāvāt 
kaivalyalakṣaṇasiddhyabhāvaḥ

p. 66) prasaktaḥ iti tathāvidhasya prāyeṇa ayogitvapratipādanārthamāha

karmendriyāṇi saṃyamya ya āste manasā smaran |
indriyārthānvimūḍhātmā mithyācāraḥ sa ucyate || 6 ||

	uktayā upapattyā mānasasya karmaṇaḥ uparamābhāvāt yo yogī 
manasā antaḥkaraṇena indriyārthān śabdādīn smaran cintayan āste 
naiṣkarmyabuddhimālambya avatiṣṭhate sa mithyācāra ucyate tādṛśaṃ 
yogācaraṇaṃ phalavisaṃvādāt vṛthaivetyarthaḥ | yataḥ karmendriyāṇi 
karmendriyaśabdena iha bahiṣkaraṇāni śrotrādīni vāgādīni ca gṛhyante, 
tena śabdādigrahaṇavyāpārādhikṛtāni śrotrādīni akṣāṇi niyamya 
prayatnaviśeṣeṇa svavyāpārāt pratiṣidhya asau kaṃcit kālaṃ tathā āste, 
natu tāvatā sarvātmanā sarvakleśocchedalakṣaṇakaivalyaphalaprāptiḥ | 
yatastathāvidhāyāṃ samādhyavasthāyāṃ yadi paraṃ kleśāḥ prasuptā 
bhaveyuḥ, tathāpi teṣāṃ vyutthāne punaḥ prabodhādanucchedo gamyate || 
6 ||
	yasmiṃstu samādhau dagdhabījakalpatāmāpādyamānāste 
kleśāḥ sati api ālambane prarohasāmarthyaṃ na bhajante, tadadhikṛtasya 
iha vivakṣitasya yoginaḥ pūrvasmāt viśeṣaṃ

rāgadveṣavimuktaistu viṣayānindriyaiścaran | (2/66)

ityādinā pūrvoktamapi smārayan punarāha

yastvindriyāṇi manasā niyamyārabhate'rjuna |
karmendriyaiḥ karmayogamasaktaḥ sa viśiṣyate || 7 ||

	tuśabdo vyatireke | sa punaḥ asaktaḥ saṃgarahito yogī pūrvasmāt 
yogino viśiṣyate prakarṣatvena vyatiricyate | ko'sau | yo manasā 
yathāpratipāditalakṣaṇasatyātmalabdhapratiṣṭhatvāt 
sarvasāmyadaśādhirūḍheṇa

p. 67) antaḥkaraṇena buddhyā śrotrādīni niyamya 
rāgādidoṣasaṃsparśāt, natu śabdāderviṣayāt, pratiṣidhya evaṃvidhaiḥ 
rāgādisparśaśūnyaiḥ karmendriyaiḥ karmasu śabdādigrahaṇarūpeṣu 
vyāpāreṣu adhikṛtairakṣaiḥ karmayogamārabhate karmaṇā 
śāstracoditaśabdādigrahaṇarūpayā kriyayā yogaṃ sambandhamārabhate 
prastauti || 7 ||
	rāgadveṣaparihāraḥ prakaraṇapratipādyasiddhisādhanaṃ, natu 
karmaṇāmanārambhaḥ | tasmāt

niyataṃ kuru karma tvaṃ karma jyāyo hyakarmaṇaḥ |
śarīrayātrāpi ca te na prasidhyedakarmaṇaḥ || 8 ||

	niyataṃ varṇavibhāgena āśramavibhāgena ca śāstreṇa 
niyantritaṃ karma tāṃ tāṃ kriyāṃ kuru anutiṣṭha mā kadācana 
tyākṣīḥ | yataḥ akarmaṇaḥ karmābhāvāt kriyātyāgāt karma 
vihitakriyānuṣṭhānaṃ jyāyaḥ śreṣṭhaṃ yataḥ keṣāṃcideva 
karmaṇāmanārambhe karmābhāvabhramo na siddhihetuḥ 
kleśānāmanucchedāt | yathoktena samādhinā tu anuṣṭhīyamānaṃ karma 
vyutthānahetuṣu viṣayeṣu satsu api kleśonmeṣābhāvāya paryavasyat 
praśasyataram | naca śarīritve sati sarvakarmābhāva upapadyate yatastava 
akarmaṇaḥ sarvātmanā karmarahitasya sthāṇukalpasya śarīrayātrāpi 
dehavṛttirapi na prasidhyet na ghaṭeta | naca 
pānāśanaśayanāsanāgamanākuñcanakaṇḍūyanādikriyākalāpamanta
reṇa kenacit vartituṃ śakyam || 8 ||
	itthaṃ ca karmaṇaḥ sarvathā abhāvānupapattau sthitāyāṃ na 
sarvaṃ nāma karma anuṣṭhīyamānaṃ siddhaye kalpate ityāha

yajñārthātkarmaṇo'nyatra loko'yaṃ karmabandhanaḥ | 
tadarthaṃ karma kaunteya muktasaṃgaḥ samācara || 9 ||

yajño yajanaṃ mukhyayā vṛttyā daivatapūjanaṃ; daivatamapi

p. 68) yasminsarvaṃ yataḥ sarvaṃ-------------|

ityādiślokavyākhyātasvarūpaḥ parameśvaraḥ paramātmaiva ekaṃ 
tattvamiha abhipretam, tasya pūjanamapi 
yathāpratipāditavijñānapariśīlanaviśuddhayā saṃvidā svabhāvatvena 
parāmarśalakṣaṇamārādhanam ; so'rthaḥ prayojanaṃ yasya tādṛśaṃ 
yat karma, tasmāt tathāvidhāt karmaṇo'nyatra tādṛśaṃ karma varjayitvā 
anyādṛśaṃ karma kurvāṇaḥ sarvaḥ ayaṃ lokaḥ karmabandhanaḥ bhavati 
karma śāstracoditamapi anuṣṭhānaṃ bandhanaṃ saṃsāradāyitvāt 
pāratantryaheturyasya, sa tathā | yathoktātmatattvajñānaśūnyatayā 
vidhivadanuṣṭhīyamānaṃ śāstrīyamapi karma bandhanāyaiva kalpate, 
yathāpratipāditavijñānaniṣṭhatayā anuṣṭhīyamānaṃ tu 
parameśvarārādhanamukhyayāgahetutvāt parasiddhidaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ | 
tathā ca vakṣyati

yataḥ pravṛttirbhūtānāṃ yena sarvamidaṃ tatam |
svakarmaṇā tamevārcya siddhiṃ vindati mānavaḥ || (18/46)

iti | yata evaṃ, tatastvaṃ tadarthaṃ tādṛśasya yajñasya sampattaye 
karmaśāstracoditāṃ kriyāṃ samācara samyak kuru ityarthaḥ | 
kīdṛśastasya samyakkaraṇe'dhikṛta iti viśeṣaṇamāha muktasaṃgaḥ iti 
mukta asaṃkalpāt parihṛtaḥ saṃgo rāgadveṣādidoṣahetuviṣayeṣu 
nimajjanaṃ tadekanimagnacittatvaṃ yena, sa tathāvidhaḥ sanniti | anena 
mumukṣostāvat karmāparityāgaḥ pratipāditaḥ || 9 ||
	idānīṃ kaścit pravṛttilakṣaṇadharmādhikṛtaḥ, kaścit 
nivṛttilakṣaṇadharmādhikṛtaḥ iti dvividhaśāstropadeśaviṣayabhūte loke 
na kasyacidapi karmaparityāga upapadyate iti krameṇa pratipādayituṃ 
pravṛttidharmādhikṛtasyaiva tāvadāgamopanyāsapūrvaṃ 
karmāparityāgamupapādayitumāha

p. 69) sahayajñāḥ prajāḥ sṛṣṭvā purovāca prajāpatiḥ |

prajāśabdena atra karmabhūmijanmānaḥ parajñānarahitāḥ 
svargādigārthino manuṣyā eva gṛhyante teṣāmeva 
prakrāntaprajāpatyupadeśaṣayatvopapatteḥ | tena prajāpatiḥ 
parameśvarecchākalpitasargalakṣaṇarmādhikāro devatāviśeṣo yathoktāḥ 
prajāḥ sahayajñāḥ yajñena nādevatāyajanātmakena vidhiviśeṣeṇa saha 
sārdhaṃ sṛṣṭvā nirmāya vāca upadideśa ||

kimuvāca, ityāha

anena prasaviṣyadhvameṣa vo'stviṣṭakāmadhuk || 10 ||

devānbhāvayatānena te devā bhāvayantu vaḥ |
parasparaṃ bhāvayantaḥ śreyaḥ paramavāpsyatha || 11 ||

iṣṭānbhogān hi vo devā dāsyante yajñabhāvitāḥ |
tairdattānapradāyaibhyo yo bhuṃkte stena eva saḥ || 12 ||

anena sahajanmanā yajñena kriyamāṇena yūyaṃ prasaviṣyadhvaṃ 
savaṃ santānamavāpsyatha vṛddhiṃ gamiṣyathetyarthaḥ | 
prasaviṣyadhvatiprayogaśchāndasaḥ, anyatrāpi evaṃ draṣṭavyaḥ | eṣa ca 
yuṣmākanuṣṭhīyamānaḥ san iṣṭakāmadhuk bhavatu kriyāphalatvena 
abhimatān pumān svargādibhogān dugdhāṃ pūrayatu | kimanena kurma 
ityāha manena devān indraprabhṛtīn bhāvayata 
śāstropadiṣṭetikartavyatāviṣṭena yajñena tarpitān satastṛptilakṣaṇāṃ 
sattāṃ lambayatetyarthaḥ |  ? ca evaṃ bhavadbhiriṣṭatvāt tṛptāḥ santo 
yuṣmān bhāvayantu vardhantu 
narakatiryaktvādipatanalakṣaṇādabhāvāduttārayantu vā | 
evamyonyabhāvanaparāḥ santaḥ paraṃ śreyaḥ yathābhimatamabhyudayam 
avāpsyatha lapsyadhve | yasmāt yajñena yāgena bhāvitāḥ santo 
devāṣṭān? abhipretān kāmān bhogān yuṣmabhyaṃ dāsyante

p. 70) pratipādayiṣyanti | evaṃ taiḥ devaiḥ dattān bhogān ebhyaḥ devebhyo 
yajñavidhinā anupahṛtya yaḥ tān kāmānaśnāti, sa stenaḥ caura eva 
devadravyāpahāripratyavāyena yujyate ityarthaḥ | etadantaṃ 
prajāpativacanam, ataḥ paraṃ bhagavadvākyam || 12 ||

yajñaśiṣṭāśinaḥ santo mucyante sarvakilviṣaiḥ |
bhuñjate te tvadhaṃ pāpā ye pacantyātmakāraṇāt || 13 ||

	itthaṃ prajāpatiniyamamanatikramanto yathoktāt yajñādanuṣṭhitāt 
śiṣṭaṃ vighasasaṃjñamannamaśnanto bhuñjānāḥ sarvaiḥ 
samastairnityādiyajñānanuṣṭhānasambhavaiḥ kilviṣaiḥ pāpaiḥ mucyante 
tyajyante na te stenā bhavantītyarthaḥ | te punaḥ pāpāḥ pāpinaḥ aghaṃ 
pāpameva bhuñjate ye ātmakāraṇāt svahetornatu devādyarthaṃ pacanti 
paktiṃ sādhayanti, te tyaktaśāstrīyakriyāḥ santo nirayameva 
prāpnuvantītyarthaḥ || 13 ||
	yasmāt sarvametadakṣarasaṃjñāt parasmāt brahmaṇaḥ pravṛttam, 
tasmādanatikramaṇīyameveti kramamāha

annādbhavanti bhūtāni parjanyādannasambhavaḥ |
yajñādbhavati parjanyo yajñaḥ karmasamudbhavaḥ || 14 ||

karma brahmodbhavaṃ viddhi brahmākṣarasamudbhavam | 
tasmātsarvagataṃ brahma nityaṃ yajñe pratiṣṭhitam || 15 ||

	annāt prāṇasandhāraṇahetorodanāderabhyavahāryāt bhūtāni 
sarvaprāṇabhṛto bhavanti sattāṃ labhante | prāṇavṛttinibandhanaṃ hi 
bhūtānāṃ jīvitam, annanibandhanā ca prāṇavṛttiḥ | tadevaṃvidhasya 
annasya parjanyāt meghāt sambhavaḥ | sa ca evaṃvidhaḥ parjanyo yajñāt 
somārkāgnīndrādidevatāyajanātmakāt vidhiviśeṣāt bhavati | yajñe hi 
tarpitā devatā vrīhyādisampattaye parjanyaṃ niyuñjate | sa ca kāle 
varṣādinā brīhīn sampādayati | yathoktam

p. 71) agnau prāstāhutiḥ samyagādityamupatiṣṭhate |
ādityājjāyate vṛṣṭirvṛṣṭerannaṃ tataḥ prajāḥ || (ma0 smṛ0 3/76)

iti | īdṛśaśca yajñaḥ karmasamudbhavaḥ karmaṇo 
viśiṣṭetikartavyatārūpāt vidhānāt sambhavo yasya tathāvidhaḥ | tacca 
īdṛśaṃ karma brahmodbhavaṃ brahmaṇaḥ śāstrarūpādudbhavo yasya tat 
tathāvidham | apareṇa śabdātmakena vedādiśāstrarūpeṇa brahmaṇā 
yajñasampādanasādhakaṃ karma upadiśyate | tacca itthaṃbhūtaṃ 
śabdātmakaṃ brahma akṣarasamudbhavam na kṣarati saṃcalati, na 
svarūpāt cyavate iti akṣaraṃ paraṃ brahma paramakāraṇaṃ, 
tasmādudbhavo yasya, tat tādṛśam | parasmāddhi brahmaṇaḥ 
paramakāraṇāt svarūpamajahata eva śāstraṃ vastusthityā 
citsvarūpatāmavyabhicarat sūryādiva prakāśo 
vyapadeśamātrabhinnasvarūpaṃ prasarati, ata eva tadapi brahmaśabdena 
uktam | yata evaṃvidhā iyaṃ prakriyā pravṛttā, tataḥ paraṃ brahma 
sarvagatatvena samastavastuvyāpakatvena vyavasthitaṃ sat nityaṃ sadaiva 
yathoktasvarūpe yajñe pratiṣṭhitam yadarthameva prasṛtam, tasya arthasya 
yajñe kila pratiṣṭhā sthitiḥ || 15 ||

	yata evam, ataḥ

evaṃ pravartitaṃ cakraṃ nānuvartayatīha yaḥ |
aghāyurindriyārāmo moghaṃ pārtha sa jīvati || 16 ||

	evaṃ yathoktena prakāreṇa parabrahmasvarūpeṇa parameśvareṇaiva 
jagatkrīḍāsthitaye pravartitaṃ pravṛttilakṣaṇadharmaniṣṭhatayā 
āviṣkṛtaṃ cakraṃ śāstrādipadārthasamūhaṃ ya iha loke nānuvartayati 
svayaṃ ca yathāśāstramananuvartamāno'nyānapi janān na 
anuṣṭhāpayati, sa tādṛśaḥ parameśvarapravartitaprakriyāvyutthāpakatvāt 
aghāyuḥ pāpāyaiva āyurjīvitaṃ yasya, sa tādṛśaḥ san moghaṃ 
niṣphalaṃ jīvati tādṛśasya hi 
tattvajñānaśūnyatvādapavargalakṣaṇottamaphalābhāvādīśvaravyavast
hāpitaniyamātikramācca 

p. 72) svargādibhogalakṣaṇāvaraphalābhāvādapi niṣphalameva jīvitam || 
16 ||
	evaṃ pravṛttilakṣaṇadharmādhikṛtasya upadeśyasya saṃsāriṇaḥ 
śāstroktakarmaparihārānupapattiṃ pratipādya, idānīṃ 
nivṛttilakṣaṇadharmānuṣṭhānapratiṣṭhitabuddherapi mumukṣostāṃ 
pratipādayitumāha

yaścātmaratireva syādātmatṛptaśca mānavaḥ |
ātmanyeva ca santuṣṭastasya kāryaṃ na vidyate || 17 ||

naiva tasya kṛtenārtho nākṛteneha kaścana |
na cāsya sarvabhūteṣu kaścidarthavyapāśrayaḥ || 18 ||

tasmādasaktaḥ satataṃ kāryaṃ karma samācara |
asakto hyācarankarma paramāpnoti pūruṣaḥ || 19 ||

	iha sarvasya kasyacit jantoraprāptaṃ sukhaṃ 
prāptumicchatastatsādhanabhūte śabdādau viṣaye ratiḥ rāgo'bhilāṣo 
jāyate, tataḥ prayatna viśeṣeṇa tasmin prāpte sati tṛptiḥ sampūrṇocchatā, 
tataḥ santoṣo'bhikāṃkṣānivṛttiriti ete ratitṛptisantoṣāḥ sadā sannihite 
niruttarānandanirbhare svātmani eva pratiṣṭhitāḥ | tasya 
tadvyatiriktaprāpaṇīyavastvantaranirapekṣaṇīyasya 
aprāptasvārthasādhanabhūtaṃ kāryaṃ karaṇīyaṃ na vidyate na 
sambhavati | yataḥ sadā kṛtakṛtyatvāt tasya naiva kaścin kṛtena 
prāgakṛtatvādupādeyatayā idānīṃ nirvartitena prayojanaṃ nāpi akṛtena 
heyatvāt parihṛtena kaścin arthaḥ prayojanam | ata eva asya evaṃvidhasya 
svātmapratiṣṭhitabuddheryoginaḥ sarvabhūteṣu samasteṣu sthāvareṣu 
jaṃgameṣu ca sattveṣu madhye na kaścidarthavyapāśrayaḥ arthasya 
prāpyasya vastuno vyapāśraya ālambanam | idamatra tātparyam 
prākpratipāditasthirabuddhitvadaśāmadhirūḍhasya yoginaḥ phalalipsayā 
mamaivedamiti asādhāraṇatayā

p. 73) kāryaṃ sādhyaṃ vastu na kiñcana vidyate, 
sarvavarṇāśramasādhāraṇaṃ ca śāstracoditaṃ kāryaṃ 
tattanniyatakaraṇīyaṃ pratipāditavijñānaniṣṭhasya vidyata eva | yata evaṃ, 
tasmāt kāraṇam mumukṣuḥ san svaṃ kāryaṃ karma 
śāstravihitatvādavaśyānuṣṭheyaṃ yuddhāditavijñānaniṣṭhasya vidyata 
eva | yata evaṃ, tasmāt kāraṇāt mumukṣuḥ san svaṃ kāryaṃ karma 
śāstravihitatvādavaśyānuṣṭheyaṃ yuddhādivyāpāram asaktaḥ 
kṣaṇikasukhalavasādhanabhūteṣu viṣayeṣu animagnacitta vṛttiḥ san 
samācara samyak yathāpratipāditātmatattvapratiṣṭhitabuddhitve manāgapi 
apramattatayā ācara anutiṣṭha | yasmāt puruṣo mumukṣuḥ saṃsārāsakto 
muktasaṃgaḥ san śāstraniyaṃtritaṃ karma ācaran kurvan paraṃ 
prakṛṣṭamapavargalakṣaṇaṃ prāpyaṃ vastu āpnoti labhate, natu 
karmatyāgāt siddhiḥ kasyacidasti sarvakarmatyāgānupapatteḥ prāgeva 
pratipāditatvāt kasyacidvā karmaṇastyāge 
sarvakarmatyāgābhimānagrahāt rāgadveṣādidoṣāvirbhāvasya 
durnivāratvāt || 19 ||

	atraiva udāharaṇamāha

karmaṇaiva hi saṃsiddhimāsthitā janakādayaḥ |

	iha loke janakādayaḥ rājarṣayaḥ 
śrutismṛtītihāsapurāṇādiprasiddhāḥ karmaṇaiva 
svavarṇāśramaniyatena kriyāviśeṣeṇa prajāpālanādinā saṃsiddhiṃ 
niruttarapuruṣārthaprāptilakṣaṇaṃ phalam āsthitāḥ svīkṛtavantaḥ | 
tathāca mokṣadharmeṣu

ekasminnapyadhiṣṭhāne saṃvādaḥ śrūyatāmayam |
chatrādiṣu ca muktasya muktāyāśca tridaṃḍakaḥ ||

iti upakrānte sulabhājanakasaṃvāde janakavākyāni

sukhī so'hamavāptārthaḥ samaloṣṭāśmakāñcanaḥ |
muktasaṃgaḥ sthito rājye viśiṣṭo'nyaistridaṃḍibhiḥ ||

mokṣe hi trividhā niṣṭhā dṛṣṭā pūrvairmahātmabhiḥ |
jñānaṃ lokottaraṃ yacca sarvatyāgaśca karmaṇām ||

p. 74) jñānaniṣṭhāṃ vadantyevaṃ mokṣaśāstravidho janāḥ |
karmaniṣṭhāṃ tathaivānye munayaḥ sūkṣmadarśinaḥ ||

vāgdaṃḍo'tha manodaṃḍaḥ kāyadaṃḍastathaiva ca |
yasyaite niyatā daṃḍāḥ sa tridaṃḍīti kīrtitaḥ ||

prahāyobhayamapyetajjñānaṃ karma ca kevalam | 
tṛtīyeyaṃ samākhyātā niṣṭhā tena mahātmanā ||

iti tṛtīyā samuccayarūpetyarthaḥ, tathā anyatra

akaiñcanye na mokṣo'sti kaiñcanye nāsti bandhanam |
kaiñcanye cetare caiva janturjñānena mucyate ||
(ma0 bhā0 śā0 pa0 325 a0)

iti ||

atha tattvavido'pi anyaṃ karmāparityāgahetumāha

lokasaṃgrahamevāpi saṃpaśyankartumarhasi || 20 ||

yadyadācarati śreṣṭhastattadevetaro janaḥ |
sa yatpramāṇaṃ kurute lokastadanuvartate || 21 ||

	lokaśabdena trivargamātropadeśaviṣayabhūto jano'tra vivakṣitaḥ, 
tasya saṃgrahaḥ śāstrārthānuṣṭhātṛlakṣaṇasvapakṣasvīkāraḥ | taṃ 
vakṣyamāṇahetunā avaśyakaraṇīyatayā saṃpaśyan samyak 
budhyamānastvaṃ kartuṃ śāstravihitāni karmāṇi ācaritum arhasi | ataḥ 
śreṣṭhaḥ kulavidyāvṛttādibhirjyāyān jano yadyat kiñcit karma ācarati 
anutiṣṭhati tattadeva nānyat itaro janaḥ svayaṃ 
kāryākāryavivekaśūnyamatirloko'nutiṣṭhati | yataḥ sa śreṣṭho jano yat 
vastu pramāṇaṃ kurute svayamanuṣṭhānādupapannatvena vyavasthāpayati 
tat lokaḥ anuvartate anusarati | tasmāt paramārthajñena api 
lokasaṃgrahārthaṃ śāstracoditaṃ karma sarvathā anuṣṭheyamiti || 21 ||

p. 75) atra ātmānameva udāharaṇaṃ kurvannāha

na me pārthāsti kartavyaṃ triṣu lokeṣu kiñcana |
nānavāptamavāptavyaṃ pravarte'thaca karmaṇi || 22 ||

	he pārtha mama svārthasādhanatayā kartavyaṃ kāryaṃ na kiñcit 
asti yasmāt triṣu lokeṣu samaste jagati anavāptaṃ 
pūrvamanāsāditamidānīm avāptavyaṃ svecchāpūraṇāya labdhavyaṃ 
na kiñcidasti | alabdhalābhārthe hi sarvaḥ kaścit svakāryamārabhate, 
mama tu yathāpratipāditatattvajñānaniṣṭhāpratiṣṭhitaprajñasya na kiñcit 
prāpyamasti | ataśca tādṛśo'pi san lokavat karmaṇi śāstravihite pravarte 
satatamudyukto bhavāmi | anena

yastvātmaratireva syāt ----------------|
------------tasya kāryaṃ na vidyate | (3/17)

iti upakramya

tasmādasaktaḥ satataṃ kāryaṃ karma samācara || (3/19)

iti yat prāguktaṃ, tadekānuṣṭhātṛpuruṣaviṣayameveti niḥsaṃśayatayā 
pratipāditavān || 22 ||
	atha tattvajño'pi san bhagavān lokavat karmaṇi pravartate ityāha

yadi hyahaṃ na varteyaṃ jātu karmaṇyatandritaḥ |
mama vartmānuvarteranmanuṣyāḥ pārtha sarvaśaḥ || 23 ||

	yadi ahaṃ varṇāśramocite karmaṇi atandritaḥ asaṃjātālasyaḥ san 
kadācidapi na varteyaṃ na tiṣṭheyam | tat sarva eva manuṣyāḥ karmaṇi 
adhikṛtāḥ mānavāḥ pramāṇabhūtacaritasya mama vartma 
mārgamācāram anuvarteran anusareyuḥ || 23 ||

p. 76) kiṃca ata ityāha

utsīdeyurime lokā na kuryāṃ karma cedaham |
saṃkarasya ca kartā syāmupahanyāmimāḥ prajāḥ || 24 ||

	yadi ahaṃ lokavat śāstracoditaṃ karma na ācareyam, tadamī lokāḥ 
samastāni bhuvanāni 
saṃsāripuruṣocitatattatsvakarmaphalopabhogādhikaraṇabhūtāni utsīdeyuḥ 
utsādaṃ vināśaṃ bhajeyuḥ | karmanibandanā hi lokānāṃ sthitiḥ | kiṃca 
tathāvidhaṃ karma akurvan saṃkarasya varṇavibhāgamiśrīmāvasya kartā 
pravartako bhaveyam | sarve hi puruṣā māṃ śāstrīyaṃ karma 
akurvāṇamanuvartamānāḥ svaṃ svaṃ tattajjātivihitaṃ karma 
yatheṣṭamanyajātivihitatvādinā virodhena api ācarantaḥ 
saṃkīrṇavarṇatāmāpnuyuḥ | ato'hameva varṇasaṃkaradoṣasya kartā 
syām | kiṃca ataḥ imāḥ prajāḥ etān karmabhūmijanmanaḥ 
śāstracoditakarmādhikṛtān sarvān saṃsāriṇaḥ puruṣān upahanyām 
upaghātena vināśena yojayeyam | etā hi prajāḥ yathāvihitaṃ karma 
anācarantyaḥ svargādiprāpterapetā nisargatastattvajñānaśūnyatvāt 
kaivalyalakṣaṇāduttamāt puruṣārthāt dūrabahiṣkṛtā 
naryakatiryagbhāvaprāptilakṣaṇaṃ vināśamāpnuyuḥ | taditthaṃ 
parameśvarecchāparikalpitajagatkrīḍāniyamavyutthāpakatvaṃ mayaiva 
ācaritaṃ bhavet || 24 ||

ataḥ 

saktāḥ karmaṇyavidvāṃso yathā kurvanti bhārata |
kuryādvidvāṃstathāsaktaścikīrṣurlokasaṃgraham || 25 ||

	yathā yena prakāreṇa avidvāṃsaḥ 
yathoktātmatattvajñānaśūnyatvāt mukhyayā vidvattayā rahitāḥ 
puruṣāḥ karmaṇi kṣaṇikasukhasādhanabhūte yajñādikriyātmake 
vyāpāre saktāḥ tadeva paratvena 
manyamānāstāvanmātraparisamāptapuruṣārthāḥ santaḥ kurvanti 
vihitāni

p. 77) karmāṇi ācaranti, tathaiva tenaiva prakāreṇa 
sāmānyajanadurlakṣyasvahṛdayasaṃvedyatattvajñānaniṣṭhatvāt 
tādṛśābhiyogarūpeṇa vidvān paramārthapaṃḍitaḥ kuryāt karmāṇi 
ācaret | kīdṛśaḥ san | asaktaḥ 
niruttarānandāmṛtāsvādasatataparitṛptatvāt lokavat 
svargādibhogaviṣayena tattatkarmaphalasaṃgena rahitaḥ | kimarthamevaṃ 
kuryāditi hetugarbhaṃ viṣeṣaṇamāha cikīrṣurlokasaṃgraham lokasya 
abuddhasya jagasya saṃgrahaṃ pūrvoktaṃ cidīrṣuḥ sampipādayiṣuḥ | 
viditavedyena hi viduṣā tattvajñānopadeśayogyatāśūnyaṃ lokaṃ 
narakapātādiduḥkhādujjihīrṣuṇā tathā karma ācaraṇīyaṃ, yathā 
lokastadanuvartamānaḥ svakarmānuṣṭhānāt svargāderapi phalāt na 
vyapaiti || 25 ||
	ayogyasya tattvajñānopadeśo vināśāya eva paryavasyediti āha

na buddhibhedaṃ janayedajñānāṃ karmasaṃginām | 
joṣayetsarvakarmāṇi vidvānyuktaḥ samācaran || 26 ||

	vidvān prabuddho yuktaḥ yathoktātmajñānasahitaḥ san samyak 
yathāśāstraṃ jātyādiniyatāni karmāṇi ācarannanutiṣṭhan ajñānāṃ 
svabhāvata eva samprati 
parameśvarecchānanugṛhītatvādanāvirbhūtavivekajñānānāmata eva 
karmasu yajñādisādhanabhūteṣu bhogaiśvaryaprasaktatvādinā hetunā 
saṃgināṃ tatraiva saktānāṃ buddheḥ prajñāyā bhedaṃ tādṛśāt 
saṃgāt pracyāvanaṃ na utpādayet | te hi ajñāḥ santo yadi tattvajñāne 
karmatyāgamātreṇa vyutpādyante, tat teṣāmajñatvādeva tattvajñāne 
pratiṣṭhāmalabhamānānāṃ svargādihetośca karmasaṃgāt 
pracyuterbuddhirbhedaṃ prāptā satī ubhayabhraṃśāyaiva paryavasyet | 
atastān tathāvidhānajñān sarvāṇi karmāṇi, yeṣu hi te gṛhītāsaṃgāḥ, 
tāni joṣayet prītyā sevayet svayaṃ tadanuṣṭhānaprakhyāpanena || 26 ||

p. 78) itthaṃ parameśvarecchāniyamitajagadvyavasthāvyutthānavicchedāya 
svasmin svasmin karmaṇi lokamaprabuddhaṃ pravartayituṃ prabuddhasya api 
lokavat vihitānāṃ karmaṇāmavaśyamanuṣṭhānamupapāditam | ataśca 
prabuddhāprabuddhayostulyameva kartṛtvaṃ, tulyāni evaca karmāṇi, 
asaktatvakṛtastu phalabhedaḥ iti sthite tulye kartṛtve tulyeṣu ca karmasu 
kriyamāṇeṣu kathaṃ phalabheda iti 
anavadhāritaprabuddhāprabuddhaviśeṣasya arjunasya 
saṃśayamāśaṃkamānaḥ saṃsāriṇo yoginaśca samāne'pi hi vyāpāre 
yathā phalabhedopapattiḥ, tathā vibhajya viśeṣaṃ pratipādayitumāha

prakṛteḥ kriyamāṇāni guṇaiḥ karmāṇi bhāgaśaḥ |
ahaṃkāravimūḍhātmā kartāhamiti manyate || 27 ||

tattvavittu mahābāho guṇakarmavibhāgayoḥ |
guṇā guṇārthe vartante iti matvā na sajjate || 28 ||

	iha yat kiñcit śārīraṃ vācikaṃ mānasaṃ vā 
śāstroktamaśāstroktaṃ vā karma yena kenacit kartrā kriyate, tatra na asau 
kevalaḥ kāraṇaṃ; kintu

adhiṣṭhānaṃ tathā kartā -------------| (18/14)

ityādinā vakṣyamāṇāḥ paṃca padārthāḥ kāraṇam | te ca sarva eva 
sattvarajastamolakṣaṇaprākṛtaguṇamayā eveti vakṣyati | ataḥ prakṛteḥ 
sambandhibhiḥ guṇaiḥ kriyamāṇāni sarvāṇi karmāṇi vyavasthitāni | 
katham | bhāgaśaḥ sāttvikamadhiṣṭhānādi kāraṇaṃ sāttvikaṃ karma 
ārabhate, rajomayaṃ rājasaṃ, tamomayaṃ tāmasamiti vibhāgena | itthaṃ 
karturapi

muktasaṃgo'nahaṃvādī--------------------| (18/26)

ityādinā traiguṇyasya vakṣyamāṇatvāt guṇānāmeva kartṛtve paryavasite 
ahaṃkāreṇa śarīrādiṣu ātmapratyayena vimūḍhātmā 
aprabuddhasvabhāvaḥ

p. 79) saṃsārī aham eva kevalaḥ kartā iti 
adhiṣṭhānādikāraṇāntaranirapekṣaṃ kartṛtvaṃ śarīrādirūpe kṛtrime 
svātmani āropayati | sa ca guṇaparatantre tādṛśi ātmani 
ahaṃkāramāropayan guṇeṣu eva sakto na kadācit saṃsārabandhahetubhyo 
vimucyate | guṇavibhāgasya karmavibhāgasya ca tattvavit paramārthavedī 
yogī punaḥ guṇāḥ sattvadayaḥ guṇārthe svaprayojane 
dehendriyaviṣayādyātmake vartante tatraiva tiṣṭhanti, na ātmanaḥ sarvadā 
svatantrasuprasannasaṃvinmātrasvabhāvasya svoparāgamarpayituṃ 
prabhavanti, iti sarvadā satyātmapratiṣṭhatayā na guṇeṣu na karmasu sajjate 
ahaṃkāramamakārau teṣu na karotītyarthaḥ | atha kīdṛśo guṇānāṃ 
vibhāgaḥ, kīdṛśaśca karmaṇāṃ, kiṃca atra tattvamiti vyākhyāyate | iha 
parameśvareṇa 
acintyasvecchāvilasitasvabhāvātmakasakalajagatprapaṃcakāraṇatvena 
avabhāsitayā pāramārthikātmatattvābhedākhyātimātraśarīrayā, ata 
eva bhinnārthaviṣayajñānakriyāniyamātmakasattvādiguṇamayyā 
prakṛtyākhyayā anādyavidyāprabandhasambaddhānāmīśvarecchayaiva 
anyonyabhedena avabhāsamānānāṃ jīvānāṃ guṇaireva 
ārabdhatanukaraṇabhuvanabhogādikalāpatvāt sarvameva guṇādhīnamiti 
prāyeṇa guṇā eva teṣāṃ kartāraḥ iti ayaṃ kṣetrajñaviṣayatvena 
guṇavibhāgaḥ | tathāvidhāśca te jīvā yāni karmāṇi ārabhante te teṣu 
viṣayasukhatṛṣṇāturatayā sajjanti tadbādhakabhūteṣu anyeṣu dveṣeṇa 
parābhavantīti etāvanmātraniyatacittavṛttitvāt vimūḍhā bhavanti, iti 
tadviṣayatvena sarvakarmāṇi rāgadveṣamohātmakatvāt 
saṃsārabandhakāraṇatayā paryavasyantīti ayaṃ teṣāṃ karmavibhāgaḥ | 
satyātmatattvapratiṣṭhitabuddhīnāṃ tu yogināmanyathā guṇānāṃ 
karmaṇāṃ ca vibhāgaḥ | tathāhi te guṇāḥ sattvādayo guṇānāmeva 
arthe prayojane

p. 80) saṃsāriviṣayatvena jagatprapañcaviracanārūpeṇa vartante, na 
asmādviṣayatvena, eṣāṃ 
sarvadānastamitasaṃvitprakāśamātravyatiriktaṃ kiñcit rūpāntaraṃ na 
bhavatīti manvānā nityaniratiśayanijānandanavasudhāsvādanaikaniratāḥ 
kathaṃ teṣu rāgādiviṣamaviṣarasamayeṣu sajjantīti yogiviṣayatvena guṇā 
na guṇatvena sphurantītyayaṃ yogiviṣayo guṇavibhāgaḥ | evaṃvidhāśca 
ete yāni karmāṇi ārabhante, tāni kriyamāṇānyeva 
yathoktajñānāgnidagdhatvāt na saṃsārakāraṇatāmāpādayitumeṣāṃ 
prabhavanti, ityayaṃ yogiviṣayaḥ karmavibhāgaḥ | tādṛśo yogī 
guṇātītaḥ ucyate | udvakṣyati

na dveṣṭi saṃpravṛttāni na nivṛttāni kāṃkṣati | 
udāsīnavadāsīno guṇātītaḥ sa ucyate || (14/25)

iti | tattvamapi etayorguṇakarmavibhāgayoretadvyākhyānenaiva 
vyākhyātam || 28 ||
	evaṃ prabuddhāprabuddhayostulyeṣu karmasu kriyamāṇeṣu 
mokṣasaṃsārabandhalakṣaṇaṃ phalabhedamupapādya, idānīṃ 
prabuddhasya 
yathāpratipāditajñānakarmasamuccayānuṣṭhānapariniścitabuddherlokavi.
sayamātmaviṣayaṃ ca vyavahāraṃ niyamayitumāha

prakṛterguṇasammūḍhāḥ sajjante guṇakarmasu |
tānakṛtsnavido mandān kṛtsnavinna vicālayet || 29 ||

	prakṛteḥ uktalakṣaṇāyāḥ sambandhibhirguṇaiḥ 
sattvādibhiranādisambandhaparicitatvāt vāsanārūpatayā 
śarīrādyārambhakatayā ca vyatiriktatvena avasthitaiḥ 
sammūḍhāstādṛśaguṇamayā eva śarīrādāvātmapratyayalakṣaṇaṃ 
saṃmohamāpannāḥ saṃsāriṇo guṇamayeṣu eva karmasu 
svargimartyabhāvatiryaktvādiphalahetuṣusajjantevivekajajñānābhāvāt 
tānyeva paratvena manyamānāsteṣu nimajjanti | ataḥ tānkṛtsnavidaḥ

p. 81) guṇaparispandaparimitīkṛtajñānatvāt sarvaṃ heyopādeyarūpaṃ 
jñeyajātamajānānān, ata eva mandān alpadhiyaḥ kṛtsnavit 
samastajñeyatattvavedī na vicālayet yatraiva guṇātmake karmaṇi phale ca 
āsaktāḥ, tato na pracyāvayet teṣāṃ buddhidvaidhaparihārāya sarvaṃ 
vihitaṃ karma anutiṣṭhediti || 29 ||
	lokasaṃgrahāpekṣayā anuṣṭhīyamānamapi karma yogino yathā 
kaivalyāya paryavasyati, tathā tasya praguktamapi jñānaniṣṭhatvena 
karmānuṣṭhānamarjunamukhena niścinvannāha

mayi sarvāṇi karmāṇi sannyasyādhyātmacetasā |
nirāśīrnirmamo bhūtvā yudhyasva vigatajvaraḥ || 30 ||

	sarvāṇi śāstravihitāni yajñadānatapaḥprabhṛtīni karmāṇi 
adhyātmacetasā yathāpratipāditasvarūpātmaniṣṭhena manasā mayi 
paramakāraṇe paramagurau saṃnyasya samyak samarpya eka eva 
parameśvaraḥ prathamameva pratipāditasvarūpaḥ sarvaprāṇibhiḥ 
kriyamāṇānāṃ sarvakarmaṇāṃ kartā svecchāmātreṇa 
svaśaktivibhavarūpāṇāṃ nirmātā | na tadvyatirekeṇa kvacidapi 
kartṛtvaṃ pāramārthikamasti, guṇaparatantratayā tu dehādau tadabhimāno 
māyāvilasitamevetyanayā bhāvanayā saṃnyāsavidhiṃ sampādya, ata eva 
āśaṃsanīyavastuvirahāt nirāśīḥ āśīrbhyaḥ prārthanābhyo 
niṣkrāntaḥ, ata eva sarvabhāveṣu parigrahābhāvāt nirmamaḥ mamedamiti 
saṃsāripuruṣocitāyāḥ pratipatteḥ niṣkrānto bhūtvā vigatajvaraḥ 
yathoktavijñānāmṛtāvahāhanāt nivṛttasandehādisamastasantāpaḥ san 
yudhyasva samarātmakamavasaropanatamavaśyakāryaṃ svakarma 
anutiṣṭheti || 30 ||
	etāvatā granthena prāyaḥ parisamāpto'yaṃ 
jñānakarmasamuccayānuṣṭhānātmaka upadeśasāro yataḥ prathame 
tāvat prastāvanā asya pratipāditā,

p. 82) dvitīye vijñānasvarūpaṃ parighaṭitam, tṛtīye tatsaṃskṛtasya 
karmaṇo'vaśyānuṣṭheyateti manyamāno bhagavānasya paramarahasya 
bhūtasya svamatasya prabhāvapratipādanārthaṃ bodhābodhayoḥ 
phalamāha

ye me matamidaṃ nityamanuvartanti mānavāḥ |
śraddhāvanto'nasūyanto mucyante sarvakilviṣaiḥ || 31 ||

ye tvetadabhyasūyanto nānuvartanti me matam |
sarvajñānavimūḍhāṃstānviddhi naṣṭānacetasaḥ || 32 ||

	itthaṃ mayā upanyastaṃ mataṃ madīyaṃ 
sarvaśāstrasarvasvabhūtamabhiprāyaṃ nityaṃ sarvakālaṃ ye mānavāḥ 
śāstrādhikṛtā manuṣyā anuvartanti samyagavagamya anutiṣṭhanti te 
sarvakilviṣaiḥ 
avidyāprabhavarāgadveṣādikleśamūlairghorasaṃsāraduḥkhadāyibhiḥ 
kukarmācaraṇajanitaiḥ kilviṣaiḥ pāpaiḥ mucyante na punarvadhyante | 
kīdṛśāḥ santaḥ | śraddhāvantaḥ 
yathāpratipāditamanmataprarūḍhanirvikalpapratipattijanitayathoktaphalasamb
hāvanātmikayā śraddhayā yuktāḥ, ata eva 
etanmataviṣayāmasūyāmatattvajñajanocitāmakurvantaḥ | ye punaḥ etat 
evaṃvidhaṃ manmatamabhyasūyanto mohatimiratiraskṛtasamyagjñānatvāt 
doṣādhiropaṇādinā vigarhanto nānuvartanti na anucaranti, tān sarveṣu 
samasteṣu heyopādeyaniścayaviṣayeṣu jñāneṣu śāstrarūpeṣu 
vimūḍhān viparyayapratyayān viśeṣeṇa mūḍhān vicittān vinaṣṭān 
viddhi bhogāpavargalakṣaṇādubhayato'pi phalāt, pracyutān sato 
durgatiprāptilakṣaṇaṃ vināśaṃ prāptān jānīhi yatasteṣāṃ 
ceto'ntaḥkaraṇasamyagdarśitvāt sadapi nāstyeva || 32 ||
	idānīmuktameva arthaṃ sudṛḍhapratipattaye vineyopakārāya 
uktibhedairnānārūpaiḥ pratipādayan karmaṇāṃ tyāgānupapattimeva 
draḍhayitumāha

p. 83) sadṛśaṃ ceṣṭate svasyāḥ prakṛterjñānavānapi | 
prakṛtiṃ yānti bhūtāni nigrahaḥ kiṃ kariṣyati || 33 ||

	iha sarvaḥ kaścit dehavānanādiprabandhapravṛttayā prakṛtyā 
sambaddhaḥ | yataḥ sarvasya śarīrendriyaviṣayādikaṃ sarvameva triguṇam, 
ato jñānavānapi āvirbhūtadehadehivivekaprajño'pi puruṣaḥ svasyāḥ 
tena tena prakāreṇa śarīrādyārambhakatayā 
pariṇatatvādātmīyatvamāpannāyāḥ prakṛteḥ īśvarecchayā 
jagatkāraṇabhāvena udbhāvitāyā guṇatrayātmikāyā avidyāyāḥ 
sadṛśam anurūpaṃ ceṣṭate vyavaharati | kimanena uktaṃ bhavati | 
dṛṣṭatattvo'pi prakṛtyārabdhaśarīrāditvāt manovākkāyairyat kiṃcit 
karma ācarati, tat triguṇameva upapadyate | tathāvidhaṃ ca tatkarma 
jñānavatā api aśakyaparihāramiti prāgapi pratipāditam | itthaṃ 
sarvabhūtāni prakṛtiṃ svasvabhāvaṃ yānti sarvathā tāmeva āśrayante, 
natu tadvyatiriktatvena śvasituṃ, spandituṃ vāpi utsahante | tato nigrahaḥ 
nedaṃ svajātivihitaṃ karma ahaṃ kariṣyāmīti niyamaḥ kiṃ kariṣyati iti 
prākpratipāditaprakāreṇa sarvakarmapratiṣedhasya aśakyatvādakiṃcitkara 
eva bhaviṣyati, yadivā nigraho'pi karmaiva, ataśca 
hānopādānanibandhanatvāt rāgadveṣānuviddhaṃ sat guṇātmakatvāt 
prākṛtameva sampadyate, ityevamapi akiñcitkaro nigrahaḥ || 33 ||

itthamaparihārye karmaṇi jñānavān mumukṣuḥ kiṃ kuryādityāha

indriyasyendriyasyārthe rāgadveṣau vyavasthitau |
tayorna vaśamāgacchettau hyasya paripanthinau || 34 ||

	yo yasya indriyasya artho viṣayaḥ śrotrādeḥ śabdādiḥ, tatra tatra 
arthe rāgadveṣau vyavasthitau | yadāhi śabdādiviṣayaṃ sukhasādhanatvena 
śrotrādinā indriyeṇa pumānupādatte, tadā tasya tasmin

p. 84) rāgo vyavatiṣṭhate; yadā tu duḥkhakāraṇatvena tameva viṣayaṃ 
heyatvena pratipadyate, tadā tasmin indriyeṇa parihriyamāṇe dveṣo'sya 
vyavatiṣṭhate iti sarvasmin viṣaye rāgadveṣau vyavasthitau | 
jñānavānāvirbhūtavivekajñānaḥ tayorvaśaṃ vidheyatāmajñavat na 
āgacchet yataḥ asya jñānavataḥ tau rāgadveṣau paripanthinau virodhinau 
śatrū | tatastāveva sarvatyāgena parihāryau, natu viṣayāḥ | nahi 
tayoraparijitayorviṣayaparājayaḥ kṛto bhavati | tau hi yathoktajñānāsinā 
yāvat na kṣatavikṣatatvamāpāditau, tāvat parivarjite'pi 
viṣaye'ntarvijṛmbhamāṇau puruṣaṃ vaśīkartuṃ prabhavata eva || 34 ||
	tadevaṃguṇopapattivaśāt sarvasmin karmaṇi prabuddhasya 
aprabuddhasya vā sarvasya kasyacidaśakyapratiṣedhe sthite 
svakarmānuṣṭhānalakṣaṇo dharmaḥ prabuddhena api ācaraṇīyaḥ ityāha

śreyānsvadharmo viguṇaḥ paradharmātsvanuṣṭhitāt |
svadharme nidhanaṃ śreyaḥ paradharmodayādapi || 35 ||

	yo yasya varṇāśramanaiyatyena śāstravihito dharmaḥ sadācāraḥ 
svatvena vyavasthitaḥ, sa kutaścit kāraṇāt mukhyena kalpena 
anuṣṭhīyamānatvāt viguṇaḥ api svairguṇairvirahito'pi san svanuṣṭhitāt 
suṣṭhu sampūrṇasaṃvidhānatayā anuṣṭhitādācaritāt paradharmāt 
vijātīyasamācārāt śreyān praśasyataraḥ 
śāstravihitātikramavihitānanuṣṭhānajapratyavāyadoṣasya abhāvāt | yata 
evaṃ svadharme'nuṣṭhīyamāne pāralaukikaśubhaphalāptiḥ, atastasmin 
svadharme sati nidhanaṃ maraṇamapi yat, tat śreyaḥ praśasyataraṃ tat syāt 
parasambandhino dharmāt svanuṣṭhitāt sataḥ ya udayo 
rājyalābhādistasmādapi tasya lokāntare viruddhaphalahetutvāt || 35 ||

p. 85) evamātmajñānaniṣṭhatayā 
śāstracoditasvakarmānuṣṭhānamapavargalakṣaṇasiddhiheturiti 
bhagavatpratipāditasakalaprakaraṇasiddhāntaṃ śrutvā 
pratipāditasvarūpasya ātmanaḥ pāratantryamevaṃvidhamanupapannaṃ 
manyamāno'rjuna uvāca

atha kena prayokto'yaṃ pāpaṃ carati pūruṣaḥ |
anicchamāno'pi balādākramyeva niyojitaḥ || 36 ||

	atha evaṃ bhagavatā pratipāditalakṣaṇaḥ ayam ātmā kena 
svāminā iva bhṛtyaḥ prayuktaḥ preritaḥ san kukarmarūpaṃ pāpaṃ 
durgatiphalaṃ kilviṣamanicchannanabhilaṣyannapi balādākramya 
haṭhādiṣṭaviṣayapravṛttiṃ nivārya niyojitaḥ vyāpārita iva ācarati 
anutiṣṭhati || 36 ||

atra uttaraṃ bhagavānuvāca

kāma eṣa krodha eṣa rajoguṇasamudbhavaḥ |
mahāśano mahāpāpmā viddhayenamiha vairiṇam || 37 ||

	yena prayukto'yamuktasvarūpānādyavidyādikleśapāśaparavaśaḥ 
puruṣaḥ *?pamācarati, sa eṣa kāmaḥ 
pariṇāmavirasakṣaṇikasukhalavasā*?nabhūtaviṣayonmukhaḥ saṃkalpaḥ, 
eṣa eva cetanācetanarūpapratipakṣa*?tihanyamānatvāt krodhaḥ 
tadupaghātapravṛttiheturdveṣaḥ sampadyate | to?'yamicchādveṣātmako 
doṣo rajoguṇasamudbhavaḥ rajoguṇāt 
viṣayasukhābhilāṣātmakarāgadveṣahetoḥ prakṛtidharmāt samudbhavati, 
ata eva mahāśanaḥ uttarottaravivardhamānagarvatvāt 
mahadaparyavasitamaśanaṃ viṣayaharaṇalakṣaṇo'bhyavahāro yasya sa 
tathā; tataśca sarvasya tasya bhogasya duḥkhaikavipākatvāt mahāpāpmā 
gurutarapākamayo durgatihetutvāt | tadevaṃvidham iha saṃsāre vairiṇaṃ

p. 86) duruttaradāruṇaduḥkhadāyitvādātatāyinaṃ viddhi jānīhi | tena 
prayukto'yaṃ puruṣaḥ pāpamācarati || 37 ||
	atha tasya vaitatyena svarūpaṃ jijñāsurarjuna uvāca

bhavatyeṣa kathaṃ kṛṣṇa kathaṃcaiva vivardhate |
kimātmakaḥ kimācārastanmamācakṣva pṛcchataḥ || 38 ||

	eṣa vairitvena pratipāditaḥ kāmādyātmako doṣaḥ kathaṃ bhavati 
kena prakāreṇa sthitilakṣaṇāṃ sattāmanubhavati, kena prakāreṇa 
vivardhate prasarati | kaśca asya ātmā svarūpaṃ, kaśca asya ācāraḥ 
kimācaratīti | tat sarvaṃ mama saṃśayaṃ pṛcchataḥ brūhi || 38 ||

atra praśnacatuṣṭaye krameṇa uttaraṃ bhagavānuvāca

eṣa sūkṣmaḥ paraḥ śatrurdehināmindriyaiḥ saha | 
sukhatantra ivāsīno mohayanpārtha tiṣṭhati || 39 ||

kāmakrodhamayo ghoraḥ stambhaharṣasamudbhavaḥ |
ahaṃkāro'bhimānātmā dustaraḥ pāpakarmabhiḥ || 40 ||

harṣamasya nivartyaiṣa śokamasya dadāti ca |
bhayaṃ cāsya karotyeṣa mohayaṃstu muhurmuhuḥ || 41 ||

	eṣa rajoguṇaprabhavaḥ kāmakrodharūpatayā prasṛto doṣaḥ 
śarīriṇāmatipratyāsannatve'pi durlakṣyatvāt paraḥ sūkṣmaḥ śaṃtruḥ | 
sa ca ahaṃkāra eva paryavasyati | yataḥ

indriyāṇi parāṇyāhuḥ-------------| (3/47)

ityādinā tasyaiva paramasūkṣmatvaṃ pratipādayiṣyati | kimātmaka iti 
praśne kimātmakatvameva asya adhunaiva abhidhatte | sa ca evaṃvidhaḥ 
śatruḥ parivārabhūtaiḥ indriyaiḥ sahitaḥ 
kāmakrodhādiniṣṇātaśastraprahāribhiḥ | taireva ayaṃ sarvaprāṇino 
viśasati | tasya durjayatvapratipādanārthaṃ 
sarvaśatruvilakṣaṇamavasthānamāha sarvaduḥkhahetutve

p. 87) sati sukhatantre sukhātmake varge ivāsīnaḥ upaviṣṭo'ta eva mohayan 
tiṣṭhati dehādyātmapratyayalakṣaṇaṃ viparyayajñānamāropayan 
sthitimanubhavatīti bhavatyeṣa katham ityasya praśnottarametat vyākhyātam, 
atha kathaṃ vivardhate ityasya vyākhyāyate kāmakrodhamayaḥ 
kāmakrodhāvuktarūpau prakṛtiryasya, sa tathāvidhaḥ | stambhasya garvasya, 
tathā harṣasya viṣādapariṇāmasya pramodasya samudbhava utpattisthānam; 
ata eva ghoraḥ dāruṇa eva kāmakrodhastambhaharṣādidoṣātmanā 
vivardhate ucchrāyaṃ bhajatīti uktaṃ bhavati | atha kimātmakaḥ iti 
praśnottaraṃ vyākhyāyate eṣa sarvadehināṃ śatrurahaṃkārasaṃjñaḥ 
abhimānātmā abhimāno jātyādyavacchedabhāji 
guṇatrayamayatvādanityatvādidoṣaikasamāśraye śarīrādāvahaṃ 
brāhmaṇo'haṃ devadatto'hamabhijanavānahaṃ vapuṣmānahaṃ 
vidagdho'hamāḍhyaḥ ityevamādi anātmanyātmapratipattinibandhanaṃ 
mithyājñānaṃ sa ātmā svabhāvo yasya, tādṛśaḥ | ata eva 
pāpakarmabhirdustaraḥ samyagjñānābhāvāt 
heyopādeyavivekaśūnyatve sati pāpamaghamayaṃ ghoraṃ 
saṃsāranirayanipātanimittabhūtaṃ karma ceṣṭitaṃ yeṣāṃ, 
tādṛśairāsuraṃ sargaṃ bhajadbhiḥ puruṣairduratikramaḥ | atha 
kimācāraḥ ityatra uttaraṃ vyākhyāyate asya kṣetrajñasya eṣaḥ 
ahaṃkārākhyaḥ śatruḥ harṣaṃ satyātmasaṃvidupalabhyamānānandaṃ 
nivartya apasārya śokaṃ śocanaṃ dadāti samarpayati | anena hi śatruṇā 
vyāmohitaḥ śarīrādi ātmatvena pratipannaṃ 
vyādhipriyaviprayogādyupadravopahanyamānamanuśocati kaṣṭaṃ 
vinaṣṭosmi, iti paridevate | ata eva asya eṣa śatruḥ bhayamapi karoti 
iṣṭaviṣayopaghātahetubhyaḥ santrāsamutpādayati | kiṃ 
kurvannevamācarati | pratikṣaṇaṃ mohayan 
yathāpratipāditaviparyayalakṣaṇaṃ vaicittyamāvahanniti | anena 
ahaṃkārākhyena

p. 88) śatruṇā indriyaparivāreṇa sukhivadvyavasthitena 
kāmakrodhastambhaharṣādidoṣātmanā vivardhamānena 
harṣāpahārādikamācaratā prayuktaḥ san kṣetrajñaḥ pāpamācarati || 41 
||
	arjunapraśnaṃ vinirṇīya tasyaiva ahaṃkārasya vitatya svarūpaṃ 
pratipādayitumāha

sa eṣa kaluṣaḥ kṣudraśchidraprekṣī dhanañjaya |
rajaḥpravṛtto mohātmā manuṣyāṇāmupadravaḥ || 42 ||

	sa eṣa yathāpratipāditasvarūpo'haṃkāraḥ samastamartyānām 
upadravaḥ sarvadoṣanidhānasaṃsārahetutvāt yataḥ kaluṣaḥ 
guṇatrayātmakatvāt nityamalinaḥ, tata evaca anityatvāt kṣudraḥ 
bhaṃgurastathā cchidraprekṣī bādhāvakāśajāgarūkaḥ | śiṣṭaṃ 
spaṣṭārtham || 42 ||

	tena asya kṣetrajñasya kiṃ kṛtamityāha

dhūmenāvriyate vahniryathādarśo malena ca |
yatholvenāvṛto garbhastathānenāyamāvṛtaḥ || 43 ||

	etena ahaṃkāreṇa ayaṃ saṃsāripuruṣāṇāmātmā tathāvṛto 
niruddhaśaktitvamāpāditaḥ | upamānena etat prakaṭayati | kathamāvṛtaḥ | 
yathā ārdrendhanatvādidoṣajanmanā kalaṃkena dhūmākhyena vahniḥ 
agniḥ āvriyate niruddhabhāsvaraśaktiḥ sampādyate, tathaiva ayamātmā 
māyāprabhaveṇa anenaiva ahaṃkārātmanā doṣeṇa 
niruddhasvasvarūpapratyavamarśātmakasvaśaktiḥ kriyate | tathā yathā 
ādarśo darpaṇo rajoniḥśvāsādisambandhajanmanā malena āvriyate 
niruddhayathāvasthitabhāvasphuraṇalakṣaṇasvaśaktiḥ kriyate | tathā yathā 
ādarśo darpaṇo rajoniḥśvāsādisambandhajanmanā malena āvriyate 
niruddhayathāvasthtiabhāvasphuraṇalakṣaṇasvaśaktiḥ kriyate, tathaiva 
ayamātmā anena āvriyate 
niruddhajagatpadārthaparamārthaprathanasāmarthyaḥ kriyate | yathā 
garbhaḥ jananīkoṣṭhavartī jīvaḥ ulvena garbhaśayyayā āvriyate

p. 89) niruddhasarvendriyaparispandanaśaktiḥ kriyate, tathaiva ayamātmā 
anena niruddhasarvakartṛtvalakṣaṇasvaśaktiḥ kriyate iti | anena 
upamānatrayeṇa ahaṃkārasya ātmānaṃ prati 
svabhāvasarvajñatvasarvakartṛtvalakṣaṇasvadharmatrayanirodhakatvaṃ 
pratipāditam || 43 ||
	yadyapi sarvaśaktinirodhako'yamātmano'haṃkārākhyo 
doṣastathāpi jñānasya prādhānyavivakṣayā idamāha

āvṛtaṃ jñānametena jñānino nityavairiṇā | 
kāmarūpeṇa kaunteya duṣpūreṇānalena ca || 44 ||

	etena yathāpratipāditena dehādyātmapratyayalakṣaṇena śatruṇā 
jñāninaḥ svātmasaṃvedanalakṣaṇamukhyajñānavataścetanasya 
tādṛśaṃ mukhyaṃ jñānamāvṛtaṃ niruddhaṃ yataḥ kāmo 
bāhyārthanibandhano'bhilāṣo rūpaṃ yasya, ata eva asau duṣpūro na 
kenacit viṣayeṇa prāptena satā pūrayituṃ śakyaḥ | yato'sya 
āhriyamāṇā viṣayā rāgaṃ vivardhayanta indriyāṇāṃ ca 
uttarottaraviśiṣṭataraviṣayarasajñatāṃ puṣṇantastebhyo 
viratimāpādayanti | ata eva analaḥ na asya alaṃbhāvo viṣayebhyo 
nivāraṇamastīti | evaṃvidhena anena svabhāvasaṃvedanalakṣaṇaṃ 
mukhyaṃ jñānamāvṛtaṃ, tasmiṃśca āvṛte sarvāḥ śaktayo'sya 
āvṛtā iti tātparyam || 44 ||

atha kimadhiṣṭhāya ayaṃ jñānamasya āvṛṇotītyāha

indriyāṇi mano buddhirasyādhiṣṭhānamucyate |
etairvimohayatyeṣa jñānamāvṛtya dehinam || 45 ||

	asya ahaṃkārasya 
pratipāditasvarūpasthitivivṛddhiviceṣṭitalakṣaṇacatuṣṭayasya indriyāṇi 
cakṣurādivāgādīni tathā manaḥ saṃkalpātmakaṃ buddhiḥ 
adhyavasāyātmikā adhiṣṭhānam | eteṣu hi kṛtasthitirayaṃ yathoktaṃ 
jñānamāvṛtya dehinaṃ kṣetrajñaṃ mohayati

p. 90) svarūpānabhijñatvakiñcijjñatvakiñcitkartṛtvakāriṇā mohena 
ajñānena yunaktiḥ || 45 ||

	tadayamevaṃvidhaḥ śatruḥ kathaṃ tiraskāryaḥ ityāha

tasmāttvamindriyāṇyādau niyamya bharatarṣabha |
pāpmānaṃ prajahīhyenaṃ jñānavijñānanāśinam || 46 ||

	yata evaṃvidho'sau, tato'sya adhiṣṭhānabhūtāni 
manobuddhisahitāni buddhīndriyakarmendriyāṇi sarvāṇyeva prathamaṃ 
niyamya yathoktasatyātmasvarūpapratiṣṭhātmapratyayatayā 
rāgadveṣādidoṣonmeṣaniṣedhena nigṛhya enaṃ pāpmānam 
ajñānamayaṃ mahākilviṣavapuṣam, ata eva jñānasya śāstrādidvāreṇa 
paramārthabodhasya tathā vijñānasya svātmasākṣātkāralakṣaṇasya 
viśiṣṭasya jñānasya nāśinaṃ tiraskāriṇaṃ paramaṃ vairiṇaṃ 
prajahīhi tiraskuru || 46 ||
	eṣa ca paramasūkṣmatvāt pareṇa udyogena apramādādinā ca 
parājayyaḥ śatruriti asya tāṃ sūkṣmatāṃ pratipādayitumāha

indriyāṇi parāṇyāhurindriyebhyaḥ paraṃ manaḥ |
manasastu parā buddhiryo buddheḥ paratastu saḥ || 47 ||

	viṣayāḥ śabdādayastāvadindriyadvāreṇa sarvasya 
kasyacidupalabhyatāmupagacchanti, atastadapekṣayā indriyāṇi parāṇi 
tebhyaḥ sūkṣmāṇi āhuḥ kathayanti tattvavidaḥ | nahi indriyāṇi 
śabdādivadupalabhyāni sākṣāt kasyacit | tebhyastathāvidhebhyaḥ 
sūkṣmebhyaḥ indribhyo manaḥ tatpravṛttinivṛttikāraṇaṃ sūkṣmataram | 
yathā indriyāṇi kathaṃcit gamyante, na tathā aticaṃcalaṃ manaḥ | 
athāvidhāt ca manaso buddhiḥ parā sūkṣmetyarthaḥ | yathāhi manaḥ 
kathaṃcit gamyate, na tathā 
nirvikalpakapratyakṣakṣaṇavartyadhyavasāyātmikā buddhiḥ | tasyāśca 
evaṃvidhāyā buddheryaḥ parataḥ paramasūkṣmatvena sthitaḥ,

p. 91) saḥ pūrvokto'haṃkārākhyaḥ śatruḥ | yathāhi buddhiḥ 
kathaṃcidabhiyuktairgamyate, na tathā ayamavidyāprathamāṃkuraḥ 
kenacidanunmiṣitaparameśvarānugrahaprabhavajñānacakṣuṣā 
lakṣyīkartumapi śakyate ||

	tasmāt

evaṃ buddheḥ paraṃ buddhā saṃstabhyātmānamātmanā | 
jahi śatruṃ mahābāho kāmarūpaṃ durāsadam || 48 ||

	evaṃ yathoktaprakāreṇa buddheḥ param enaṃ śatruṃ buddhvā 
sudurlakṣyatvāt paramadurjayaṃ matvā ata eva ātmanaiva ātmānaṃ 
saṃstabhya dhairyaṃ lambayitvā durāsadaṃ duḥkhena āsādayituṃ 
paribhavituṃ śakyamenaṃ jahi ghātaya | kīdṛśam | kāmarūpaṃ kāmo 
viṣayeṣu abhilāṣo rūpaṃ yasya, sa tathāvidhaḥ | pūrvaṃ hi 
kāmakrodhādyātmanā asya avasthitiruktā | tena yadyapi ayaṃ 
susūkṣmatvāt durjayaḥ, tathāpi asya pūrvoktena prakāreṇa 
kāmātmikāmavasthitiṃ nighnan tadvihanane samartho 
bhaviṣyasyevetyarthaḥ | evaṃ bhagavatā kṣatriyocitenaiva prakāreṇa 
arjunaḥ prastutopadeśaphalaprāptipratidvandvino'haṃkārasya nivarhaṇe 
samutsāhitaḥ | tvayā hi mahendreṇa api durjayā vahavaḥ śatravaḥ 
sahāyanirapekṣeṇa bahuśo nipātitāḥ, tadenamapi śatrumātmaikasahāyo 
nipātayitumarhasīti om || 48 ||

vijñānatattvatapanīyaguṇaprakarṣa-
prakhyāpanaikanikaṣo nijakarmayogaḥ |
asminnṛṇāṃ prakaṭitaḥ paramārthasiddhyai
so'dhyāya eṣa vivṛto vidhivattṛtīyaḥ ||

iti śrīmadrājānakarāmakaṇṭhaviracite sarvatobhadranāmni 
vākyārthānvayamātre gītāvivaraṇe tṛtīyo'dhyāyaḥ || 3 ||



atha caturtho'dhyāyaḥ |

	evamasya samastādhyātmaśāstrasarvasvabhūtārthagarbhasya 
prakaraṇasya jñānakarmasamuccayalakṣaṇamabhidheyaṃ 
paramātmasamāpattilakṣaṇaṃ ca prayojanaṃ pratipādya idānīṃ 
sambandhaviṣayayoḥ pratipādanadvāreṇa arjunasya ātmani 
puruṣatvamātrapratipattivyāmohanivṛttaye paramakāraṇatāmāviṣkurvan 
bhagavānuvāca

evaṃ vivasvate yogaṃ proktavānahamavyayam |
vivasvānmanave prāha manurikṣvākave'bravīt || 1 ||

evaṃ paramparākhyātamimaṃ rājarṣayo viduḥ |
sa kālenaiva mahatā yogo naṣṭaḥ parantapaḥ || 2 ||

sa evāyaṃ mayā te'dya yogaḥ proktaḥ purātanaḥ |
bhakto'si me sakhā ceti rahasyaṃ hyetaduttamam || 3 ||

	evam anena prakāreṇaiva yathā tubhyaṃ prokto yo yogastathaiva 
vivasvate mahāyogīśvarāya savitre yogam enam ahaṃ parameśvara eva 
kasmiṃścit kāle 
nijayogaiśvaryaparikalpitaniratiśayaprabhāvamayavigrahaḥ proktavān 
upadiṣṭavān | kīdṛśaṃ yogam | avyayaṃ paramakāraṇavat na kadācit 
vyeti kṣīyate yaḥ | sa hi jagatsargādilakṣaṇaṃ karma anutiṣṭhannapi 
parameśvaraḥ 
prākpratipāditādvayacinmātraikalakṣaṇasvabhāvavimarśaikaniṣṭhatayā 
jñānakarmasamuccayānuṣṭhānātmakasya asya yogasya 
prathamo'nuṣṭhātā, iti tadvat yogasya prādhānyena sambandhamuktvā 
krameṇa anuṣṭhānasampradāyatayā tameva sambandhaṃ vivṛṇoti 
vivasvānmanave prāha iti sa vivasvān putrāya manusaṃjñāya ādirājāya 
prāha provāca | prāheti liṭaśchāndasa āhādeśaḥ | so'pi 
manurikṣvākusaṃjñāya putrāyaiva avravīt | evaṃ

p. 93) parameśvarādeva prabhṛti paramparayā 
yogyaputrādiśiṣyarūpaprabandhapravṛttyā khyātaṃ loke prathitaṃ 
santamanye janakaprabhṛtayo rājarṣayo rājyalakṣaṇaṃ karma kurvāṇā api 
yathoktajñānaniṣṭhatvādṛṣayo munayaḥ imaṃ yathoktaṃ viduḥ 
vidāṃcakruriti asminnarthe cchāndasaḥ prayogaḥ | mahatā kālena 
yugasahasrādisaṃkhyena lokasya 
kramāpacīyamānaprajñādisarvaguṇatvāt yogyapratipattrabhāvāt naṣṭaḥ 
antardhānamāgataḥ paramakāraṇe eva vyavasthitaḥ ityarthaḥ | sa eva 
abhraṣṭasvarūpaḥ adya asmin kāle mayā parameśvareṇaiva 
jagadupakārāya yādavakulāvatīrṇena te tubhyaṃ purātano nityo yogaḥ 
proktaḥ yato mama bhakto'si śuśrūṣustvaṃ tathā sakhā suhṛt | ato 
bhaktisauhārdalakṣaṇamukhyaśiṣyaguṇasampannāya mayā 
tubhyapadiṣṭam | yata uttamaṃ sarvarahasyānāṃ prakṛṣṭametadrahasyam || 
3 ||
	evamanena asya śāstrasya sambandhaviṣayāvapi pratipāditau | 
niruttaravijñānamayadivyacakṣurdānena 
purastātpratyakṣīkariṣyamāṇapāramārthikasvarūpasya arjunasya 
svātmani yadukulodbhūtamanuṣyamātrapratipattinirākaraṇadvāreṇa 
bhagavatā pāramaiśvaryamātmanaḥ kathayituṃ ca prastutam | ata eva 
saṃjātasaṃśayo'rjuna uvāca

aparaṃ bhavato janma paraṃ janma vivasvataḥ |
kathametadvijānīyāṃ tvamādau proktavāniti || 4 ||

	bhavataḥ tava asmin kalidvāparasandhau sambhūtatvāt aparam 
apakṛṣṭaṃ janma utpattiḥ vivasvataḥ āsargāt vyavasthitasya paraṃ 
prakṛṣṭamitthaṃ kathametat budhyeya tvamaidaṃyugīnajanmā 
sucirātītakālajanmane'pi asmai sūryāya enaṃ yogaṃ proktavāniti || 4 ||

p. 94) atra bhagavānuttaramuvāca

bahūni me vyatītāni janmāni tava cārjuna |
tānyahaṃ veda sarvāṇi na tvaṃ vettha parantapa || 5 ||

	satyaṃ mama tava ca aparisaṃkhyeyāni janmāni 
tattaccharīraparigraheṇa vyatītāni atikrāntāni | tāni punastava ca 
ātmanaśca samastānyeva ahaṃ veda jānāmi tvaṃ tu mama ātmanaśca 
tāni na jānāsi || 5 ||

	ato'ham

ajo'pi sannavyayātmā bhūtānāmīśvaro'pi san |
prakṛtiṃ svāmadhiṣṭhāya saṃbhavāmyātmamāyayā || 6 ||

	ajo'vyayātmā nityatvāt janmavināśarahito'pi tathā 
sarvabhūtānāṃ sthāvarajaṃgamānāṃ sthitivināśādiṣu 
yatheṣṭakartṛtvāt īśvaraḥ prabhurapi san svāṃ prakṛtim ātmīyaṃ paraṃ 
svabhāvamadvayacinmātralakṣaṇam adhiṣṭhāya tatraiva sthitvā tasmāt 
svabhāvādapracyutaḥ svamāyayā svayā svātmādhīnayā māyayā 
dvaitāvabhāsanamātraprāṇena śaktiviśeṣeṇa hetubhūtena sambhavāmi 
janmādisattāṃ parigṛhṇāmi | ato nityatvādīśvaratvācca 
svecchāparigṛhītatvācca svaprādurbhāvān tvādṛśāṃ ca sarvadehināṃ 
bhūtān bhāvino vartamānāṃśva janmādivikārān 
satatamaviplutasarvajñatvaśaktirahameva jānāmi || 6 ||

itthaṃ svajanmaparigrahasya prayojanamāha

yadā yadā hi dharmasya glānirbhavati bhārata |
abhyutthānamadharmasya tadātmāṃśaṃ sṛjāmyaham || 7 ||

paritrāṇāya sādhūnāṃ vināśāya ca duṣkṛtām |
dharmasaṃsthāpanārthāya saṃbhavāmi yuge yuge || 8 ||

yadā yadā yatra yatra kāle dharmasya 
varṇāśramaniyamavibhaktasya

p. 95) sadācārasya glāniḥ upakṣayo bhavati tataśca tadviparītasya 
adharmasya varṇādivyavasthāvyutthānahetorduṣṭācārasya abhyutthānam 
udbhavo bhavati, tadā tatra tatra kāle ahaṃ paramakāraṇabhūtasya 
ātmanaḥ kaṃcit kaṃcidaṃśaṃ śaktileśaṃ 
svecchāparikalpitajagatkrīḍāparirakṣaṇāya sṛjāmi utpādayāmi | ato 
devāsurasargavibhedena svayamavabhāsite jagati daivasargabhājāṃ 
śāstropadeśaviṣayabhūtānāṃ sādhūnāṃ 
sattvaguṇotkarṣanirmalaprajñānāṃ paritrāṇāya 
satkriyāvighātahetubhyo vighnebhyo rakṣaṇāya tathā duṣkṛtāṃ 
śāstrīyaniyamātikramāt jagatsthitiviplāvakaduṣṭakarmakāriṇāmasatāṃ 
vināśāya pradhvaṃsāya, ata eva dharmasya 
jātideśakālādipravibhaktaviśeṣasya sadācārasya saṃsthāpanārthāya 
samyakpraropaṇāya pratiyugaṃ tena tena ākāreṇa samudbhavāmi 
utpattimattāṃ darśayāmi, natu vastuto'haṃ 
janmavināśādibhāvavikārakalaṃkasparśamanubhavāmi ||

	ata eva

janma karma ca me divyamevaṃ yo vetti tattvataḥ |
tyaktvā dehaṃ punarjanma naiti māmeti so'rjuna || 9 ||

	evam uktena prakāreṇa janma karma ca yaḥ prabuddho me tattvataḥ 
paramārthato jānāti yatra śarīritve'pi sati yathoktāt parataḥ 
svabhāvādapracyutiḥ, yatra tattatkriyāphalāsaṃsparśaśca ata eva divyam 
alokasāmānyaṃ, sa tādṛśo yathoktavijñānaniṣṇātamatiḥ dehaṃ 
tyaktvā dehe'haṃpratipattimṛtsṛjya janma naiti nityasvabhāvābhivyakte 
janmavināśavattvavyāmohaṃ jahāti, ato māmeti matsvabhāvamāpadyate 
|| 9 ||

	yata etena

vītarāgabhayakrodhā manmayā madvyapāśrayāḥ |
bahavo jñānatapasā pūtā madbhāvamāgatāḥ || 10 ||

p. 96) yathoktajñānalakṣaṇena niratiśayaśuddhihetunā vrataviśeṣeṇa 
pūtāḥ pavitrāḥ santo bahavo yogino madbhāvaṃ māmikāṃ sattām 
āgatāḥ madabhedaṃ prāptā ityarthaḥ | yato vijñānapariśīlanāt 
vītarāgabhayakrodhāḥ rāgo viṣayeṣu abhilāṣaḥ, tathā 
tadvighātahetubhyo'śakyapratīkārebhyo bhayaṃ trāsaḥ, tathā 
śakyapratīkāreṣu krodhaḥ iti | ete saṃsāradurgadurucchedanigaḍabhūtā 
doṣā vītā vigatāstruṭitā yeṣāṃ te, ata eva manmayāḥ 
madekasvabhāvāḥ madvyapāśrayāḥ ahameva vyapāśrayo nirapāyatvāt 
viśiṣṭo'pāśrayaḥ sthitiheturyeṣāṃ, te tathāvidhāḥ || 10 ||
	nanu prabandhato bhagavatā ekameva tattvaṃ 
yathāpratipāditaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭaṃ paramārthasattvena pratipāditaṃ, 
tadevaca idānīmahantayā vyapadiśyate | tat kathaṃ kecideva 
madbhāvamāgatā iti ucyate iti arjunasya 
samyagagṛhītapārameśvarakrīḍāvidhervikalpamāśaṃkya āha

ye yathā māṃ prapadyante tāṃstathaiva bhajāmyaham |
mama vartmānuvartante manuṣyāḥ pārtha sarvaśaḥ || 11 ||

	satyameva ahaṃ 
samastānāmanyonyabhinnanānāvidhasvarūpāṇāṃ 
phenabudbudakallolakalāpānāmudadhiriva udayāstamayanilaya eka eva | 
tādṛśena ca mayā nijamāyāśaktyā 
tattanniyatasāmarthyāvacchinnadehādipratiṣṭhitāhaṃkāratvena 
nānārūpā ye kṣetrajñā avabhāsitāḥ, teṣu madhye yathā yena 
prakāreṇa yādṛśena saṃkalpena ye kecana māṃ prapadyante māṃ 
prārthayante tatkālaṃ matsamāpannā bhavanti, tānahaṃ tathaiva 
tattatsaṃkalpocitaphaladānarūpeṇa prakāreṇa bhajāmi saṃśrayāmi | 
yataḥ sarva eva manuṣyāḥ tattadarthakriyāsampādanārthitāvasare mama 
sarvebhyaḥ sarvamanorathaprasādhanaikacintāmaṇeḥ vartma prāptihetuṃ 
nirvikalpotsāhalakṣaṇaṃ paṃthānam anuvartante anukurvanti | yathā ahaṃ

p. 97) pareṇa utsāhena eka eva sarvaṃ karomi, jānāmi ca; tathā te 
pratiniyataṃ kāryaṃ jñeyaṃ ca kurvanto jānantaśca 
matsamāpattisaṃvedanaśaktiśūnyatvādahaṃ karomyahaṃ jānāmīti 
kiñcitkartari, kiṃcijjñe ca tādṛśe svātmani kartṛtvādikaṃ manyante | 
kimanena uktaṃ bhavati | yathā arkālokena anugṛhītadṛṣṭayastiryañco 
bālādayaśca tattadarthakriyāniṣṭhāste te svaṃ svaṃ viṣayaṃ paśyanti, 
naca tatra arkālokaṃ kāraṇatvena manyante mūḍhatvāt yatastādṛśena 
vyavahāreṇa vyutpannāḥ tathaiva sarve kṣetrajñāstattadarthakriyārthino 
mayaiva tatkṣaṇaprārtitena svaprakāśānugṛhītasaṃvido 
dṛṣṭaprayojanamadṛṣṭaprayojanaṃ vā sarvaṃ kurvanti jānanti ca 
māyāvimohitatvādātmānaṃ tatra kāraṇatvena manyante, naca māṃ tatra 
kāraṇatvena manyante || 11 ||

	ata eva kiṃ vāñchantaste mama vartma anuvartante ityāha

kāṅkṣantaḥ karmaṇāṃ siddhiṃ yajanta iha devatāḥ |
kṣipraṃ hi mānuṣe loke siddhirbhavati karmajā || 12 ||

	dṛṣṭaprayojanena karmaṇāṃ tattadabhimataprāptilakṣaṇāṃ 
siddhiṃ karmaphalaṃ prārthayamānāḥ, tathā adṛṣṭaprayojanena 
yajñādinā tattallokāntarabhogyasukhaprāptilakṣaṇaphalābhilāṣiṇaḥ 
santo devatāḥ indrādirūpā yajantaḥ havirdānādinā pūjayantaḥ | kimatra 
tātparyam | ā brahmaṇaḥ kīṭaparyantaṃ yo yaḥ kaścit yena yena 
abhisandhinā yadyat kiñcana cikīrṣati jijñāsate vā, 
tattatphalasampattestatkālavaśyambhāvinī asaṃcetyamānā 
matsamāpattireva ekaṃ kāraṇaṃ yasmādasmin mānuṣe loke sarvasya 
pratyakṣabhūte karmajā tattatsaṃkalpamūlavyāpārasamutthā siddhiḥ 
phalaprāptiḥ kṣipraṃ tatkālameva matsamāpattibalādeva sampadyate 
paramārthataḥ karturjñāturvā anyasya asambhavāt || 12 ||

p. 98) nanu tvameva cedekaḥ kartā, tat kṣetrajñabhāvena kṛtaiḥ 
karmabhistvameva tatphalena sambadhyase, tataśca kṣetrajñavat 
sukhādisambandhastava api prasakta iti arjunaśaṃkāṃ parihartukāma āha

cāturvarṇyaṃ mayā sṛṣṭaṃ guṇakarmavibhāgataḥ |
tasya kartāramapi māṃ viddhyakartāramavyayam || 13 ||

	satyaṃ guṇavibhāgena ayaṃ sāttviko'yaṃ rājaso'yaṃ tāmasa 
ityevaṃrūpeṇa sargabhedamūlena tathā karmavibhāgena brāhmaṇena 
adhyetavyaṃ yaṣṭavyaṃ dātavyamityevamādirūpeṇa mayā 
yathoktasvarūpeṇa ekena kartrā cāturvarṇyaṃ sṛṣṭaṃ catvāro varṇā 
brāhmaṇādayo nirmitāḥ | tasya evaṃvidhasya cāturvarṇyasya 
svaguṇakarmavibhāgasamucitakarmānurūpatattatphalabhājaḥ kartāraṃ 
nirmātāramapi māmakartāraṃ tadvat tādṛkkarmaphalasambandhābhāvāt 
kartṛdharmarahitaṃ jānīhi | kuta iti hetugarbhaṃ viśeṣaṇamāha kīdṛśaṃ 
santamakartāraṃ viddhi | avyayaṃ nityamekasmāt svarūpāt yato'haṃ na 
vyemi na vyapagacchāmi advayacinmātrasvabhāvatāmeva na jahāmi | 
evaṃca avyayaṃ santaṃ māmakartāraṃ viddhi | kṣetrajñā hi 
manmāyodbhāvitānityadehādyahaṃkāramayāstriguṇāḥ 
saṃkalpakāriṇaḥ sukhādibhiḥ phalairyujyante, natu ahaṃ 
guṇātītasvabhāvastairjātu yujye || 13 ||

	tataśca

na māṃ karmāṇi limpanti na me kāmaḥ phaleṣvapi |
iti māṃ yo'bhijānāti karmabhirna sa badhyate || 14 ||

	karmāṇi puṇyāpuṇyalakṣaṇāḥ kriyāḥ na māṃ limpanti 
sukhaduḥkhādilakṣaṇasvaphalarūpeṇa kalaṃkena na malinayanti yato 
nityanijānandatṛptasya mama karmaṇāṃ phaleṣu 
tattaduttarottaraprakṛṣṭasvarūpeṣu api na kāmaḥ na abhilāṣo 
yathāpratipāditasvarūpaikamayatvena

p. 99) sarvamidamanupaśyataḥ | yaśca yathoktena jñānāmṛtanirjhareṇa 
nirdhautasakalavikalpakalaṃkatvāt nirmalaprajño yogī 'iti' evaṃ 
yathoktasvarūpaṃ 'māmabhijānāti' pratipadyate śa' madvat kriyamāṇaiḥ 
'karmabhirna badhyate' na rāgādikleśapāśaniyantritaḥ kriyate || 14 ||

kiṃca 

evaṃ jñātvā kṛtaṃ karma pūrvairapi mumukṣubhiḥ |
kuru karmaiva tasmāttvaṃ pūrvaiḥ pūrvataraṃ kṛtam || 15 ||

evaṃ yathāpratipādite karmatve'pi akarmatvaparyavasāyitvāt 
tattvaṃ jñātvā adhigamya pūrvairapi manvādibhiḥ mumukṣubhiḥ karma 
śāstracoditaṃ kṛtam anuṣṭhitam | tasmāt tvamapi 
yathoktajñānaniṣṭhatvena karmaiva svajātivihitaṃ yuddhādivyāpārameva 
anutiṣṭha | pūrvaiḥ atītaviprakṛṣṭakālajanmabhistaistaiḥ pūrvataram 
āditaramanuṣṭhitam || 15 ||

kintu 

kiṃ karma kimakarmeti kavayo'pyatra mohitāḥ |
tatte karma pravakṣyāmi yajjñātvā mokṣyase'śubhāt || 16 ||

kiṃ karma anuṣṭheyaṃ kiṃ ca akarma parihāryamityatra 
pravibhāgajñāne kavayo'pi draṣṭāro'pi munayo mohitāḥ te'pi 
samyagetayorna vibhāgaṃ jānate | tasmādakarmaparihāreṇa tat 
anuṣṭheyaṃ karma tubhyaṃ prakarṣeṇa idānīṃ kathayiṣyāmi, yat viditvā 
saṃsārabandhalakṣaṇāt aśubhāt akalyāṇāt mokṣyase mukto bhaviṣyasi 
|| 16 ||

yataḥ 

karmaṇo hyapi boddhavyaṃ boddhavyaṃ ca vikarmaṇaḥ |
akarmaṇaśca boddhavyaṃ gahanā karmaṇo gatiḥ || 17 ||

sāmānyena anuṣṭheyatayā upadiṣṭasya karmaṇo boddhavyaṃ 
viśeṣātmakaṃ 

p. 100) vastu vidyate, akarmaṇaśca karmaparihārasya api boddhavyaṃ vidyate 
vikarmaṇaśca viruddhasya api karmaṇastathaiva boddhavyaṃ vidyate 
yato'sya triprakāratve vyavasthitasya karmaṇo gahanā duravadhāratattvā 
gatiḥ vyāptiḥ || 17 ||

sā ca yathā avadhāryate, tathā pratipādayitumāha 

karmaṇyakarma yaḥ paśyedakarmaṇi ca karma yaḥ |
sa buddhimānmanuṣyeṣu sa coktaḥ kṛtsnakarmakṛt || 18 ||

yathoktajñānaniṣṭhatayā anuṣṭhīyamāne 
śāstracoditasvavyāpāre yaḥ tattvavit akarma karmābhāvaṃ 
saṃsārabandhalakṣaṇakarmakāryākaraṇāt putre'pi aputravat paśyati, 
tathā akarmaṇi yathāvihitakarmapratiṣedhe pūrvoktopapattyā 
rāgādidoṣānuṣaṅgasya durucchedatvāt 
bandhalakṣaṇakarmakāryakaraṇāt karmatvaṃ paśyati jñānadṛṣṭyā 
niścinoti sa manuṣyeṣu madhye praśasyayā buddhyā yuktaḥ | sa evaca 
sampūrṇapuruṣārthasampādakatvāt kṛtsnasya avikalasya karmaṇaḥ kartā 
anuṣṭhātā ucyate | bhogamātraphalaṃ hi karma kṛtsnamavikalāṃgamapi 
anuṣṭhitamapavargalakṣaṇaphalaṃ prati ahetutvāt na kṛtsnaṃ bhavati, 
atastadanuṣṭhātā na kṛtsnakarmakṛdityarthaḥ | evamanena ślokena 
karmaṇaśca akarmaṇaśca boddhavyamasti, - ityetat vyākhyātam || 18 ||
	atha karmaiva akarma bhavatītyetat pūrvamuktamapi dṛḍhapratipattaye 
smārayan vivṛṇoti 

yasya sarve samārambhāḥ kāmasaṃkalpavarjitāḥ |
jñānāgnidagdhakarmāṇaṃ tamāhuḥ paṇḍitaṃ budhāḥ || 19 ||

kāmyante iti kāmāḥ karmaphalatvena prārthyamānāste te 
bhogāsteṣu saṃkalpa icchā tena rahitāḥ sarve samastā 
dṛṣṭānuśravikaviṣayaniṣṭhāḥ samārambhāḥ karmapravṛttayo yasya taṃ 
yathoditaṃ yathoktātmatattvajñānarūpeṇa 

p. 101) analena dagdhakarmāṇaṃ dagdhāni 
saṃsārāṃkuraprasavasāmarthyaharaṇāt bhasmīkṛtāni bījāni yena taṃ 
tādṛśaṃ paṇḍitaṃ vidvāṃsam āhuḥ paramārthavidaḥ, yataḥ 
sarvātmanā kṛtakṛtyatvaparyavasāyinī vidvattā, sā ca yathoktajñānavat 
eva saṃbhavati || 19 ||
	evaṃ karmaṇyeva akarma vidvān paśyatīti vyākhyātam | tadeva 
viśinaṣṭi yataḥ 

tyaktvā karmaphalāsaṃgaṃ nityatṛpto nirāśrayaḥ |
karmaṇyabhipravṛtto'pi naiva kiñcitkaroti saḥ || 20 ||

saḥ evaṃvidho yogī 
paramānandamayaparamasvabhāvasuvyavasthitabuddhiḥ nityatṛptaḥ 
sakalabāhyaviṣayarasaparikṣīṇākāṃkṣo'ta eva nirāśrayaḥ 
svarūpavyatiriktāt bāhyādālambanāt niṣkrāntaḥ karmaphalāsaṃgaṃ 
karmaṇāṃ yajñadānatapaḥ prabhṛtīnāṃ phalaṃ 
svargādibhogalakṣaṇamāsaṃgamāsaktiṃ tadeva prakṛṣṭaṃ phalamiti tatra 
līnacittatvaṃ tyaktvā sahaje karmaṇi abhipravṛttaḥ anuṣṭhāne vyavasito'pi 
naiva kiñcit karoti tathāvidhasya yoginaḥ sarvasya karmaṇo 
bandhalakṣaṇasvakāryasampādanāsāmarthyāt || 20 ||

	yathoktasya yoginaḥ praśaṃsārthameva āha 

nirāśīryatacittātmā tyaktasarvaparigrahaḥ |
śarīraṃ kevalaṃ karma kurvannāpnoti kilviṣam || 21 ||

yadṛcchālābhasaṃtuṣṭo dvandvātīto vimatsaraḥ |
samaḥ siddhāvasiddhau ca kṛtvāpi na nibaddhyate || 22 ||

prāgeva vyākhyātametat | yadyapi karmākarmaṇoreva boddhavyaṃ 
vastu vyākhyātam, tathāpi vikarmaṇo'pi 
boddhavyamityetadvyākhyānenaiva vyākhyātam | yataḥ karmaparihārasya 
jñānaśūnyasya sato yatra bandhakatvamuktam, tatra 
varṇāśramasamayaviruddhasya karmaṇo bandhakatve 

p. 102) kā bhrāntiriti tasya api sarvātmanā parihāryatvalakṣaṇaṃ 
boddhavyaṃ vastu vyākhyātameva || 22 ||
	idānīṃ karma sarvathā kartavyamiti sāmānyena 
karmamātramanuṣṭheyatvena upadiṣṭaṃ prāk tasya 
	yajñārthātkarmaṇo'nyatra loko'yaṃ karmabandhanaḥ | (3|9) 
ityanena sūcitāṃ yajñalakṣaṇaviśiṣṭarūpatāṃ mokṣaikaphalatvaṃ ca 
yogisvarūpapratipādanadvāreṇaiva prastāvayannāha 

gatasaṃgasya muktasya jñānāvasthitacetasaḥ |
yajñāyārabhataḥ karma samagraṃ pravilīyate || 23 ||

yathokte jñāne'vasthitacetasaḥ pratilabdhapratiṣṭhaprajñasya ata eva 
nivṛttaviṣayābhyāsaṃgasya tataśca muktasya 
ātmānātmavivekasamādhisiddhilābhāt dehādyātmapratyayarūpāt 
mahato bandhanāt niṣkrāntasya sato vakṣyamāṇaprabhedāya yajñāya 
yajanāya tadarthaṃ karma śāstravihitāṃ kriyāmārabhamāṇasya kurvata 
eva samagraṃ kāyikavācikamānasikabhedena saṃgrahāt 
triprakāramavāntarabhedena tu aparisaṃkhyeyaṃ vilīyate vigalati 
brahmaprāptihetutvāt vinaśvaraphalārambhakatvalakṣaṇaṃ svarūpamasya 
naśyatītyarthaḥ || 23 ||
	kathaṃ kriyamāṇameva karma pravilīyate, - iti 
vivekasamādhisiddhināntarīyikāṃ brahmādvaitasiddhiṃ 
karmapravilayakāraṇabhūtāṃ pratipādayitumāha 

brahmārpaṇaṃ brahmahavirbrahmāgnau brahmaṇā hutam |
brahmaiva tena gantavyaṃ brahmakarmasamādhinā || 24 ||

tena gatasaṃgādiviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭena yoginā brahmaiva gantavyam 
ataḥ samagraṃ tasya karma pravilīyate iti sambandhaḥ | tadetat vyākhyāyate | 
tena yoginā bṛhattvādidharmayogāt brahmaśabdavācyaṃ 

p. 103) paramārthatattvameva yogyatāvaśāt nirvikalpatayā 
prakāśamānaṃ gantavyaṃ sampradānatvena upasthātavyam | nahi 
tathāvidhasya yoginastattatphalābhisaṃdhinā tattat devatāntaraṃ 
sampradānatayā gantavyatvamarhati | kīdṛśena tena brahmaiva gantavyam | 
brahmakarmasamādhinā samunmiṣite vijñānātmake divye cakṣuṣi 
yathoktabrahmābhedena prathamānaṃ karma brahmakarma, tadeva 
samādhirniruttaro yogo yasya tena tādṛśena | kīdṛśaṃ brahma gantavyam | 
brahmaṇā brahmābhinnasvarūpatayā svātmānaṃ manyamānena 
yajamānena hutaṃ tarpitaṃ brahmarūpatayā pratipanne'gnau jātavedasi | 
anyacca kīdṛśaṃ brahma | brahmārpaṇaṃ brahmarūpatayaiva 
pratīyamānamarpaṇamājyapayaḥsomādiyāgadravyopakaraṇasādhanaṃ 
hastādi yatra, tat tādṛśam; tathā brahmahaviḥ brahmatvenaiva 
pratipadyamānaṃ havirhūyamānamājyādi dravyaṃ yatra, tat tathāvidham 
| idamanena pratipāditam �yadyat vastu yathā yathā prakāśate, tattat 
prakāśamānatvādeva prakāśādabhinnasvarūpamataḥ 
paramātmavyatiriktaṃ paramārthataḥ padārthajātaṃ na kiñcit 
sambhavatīti tattvavidbhiḥ svasaṃvedanasaṃvedyatvāt 
niṣprakampopapattyupalabdhipradarśanakrameṇa vyavasthāpito'pi 
ayamātmaiśvaryādvayanayo na yasya kasyacit 
pratipattigocaratāmupagacchati parameśvareṇaiva vihitaniyamatvāt | 
tadayamaiśvaro niyamaḥ svecchāvabhāsitānyonyabhinnasvarūpeṣu api 
aparisaṃkhyeyeṣu pramātṛṣu kaṃcideva svecchāvaśādasāvanugṛhṇāti | 
tasya ca anugṛhītamātrasya 
tattvāvalokavyavadhāyakasandehamahāgirīndranirdalanadambholidhārā 
bhaktirunmiṣati | tatprabhāvācca yathoktaṃ tattvajñānaṃ 
śāstrātmakamāptopadeśādinā upanamati | tasmiṃśca 
viśuddhaśraddhāsiddhasindhunirdhautanikhilavikalpakalaṃkayā paramayā 
prajñayā nirantaramanuśīlyamāne 
dehadehivivekajñānalakṣaṇamadvaitapuradvāramapāvṛtaṃ bhavati | tatra 
labdhapraveśo'smin 

p. 104) brahmārpaṇavidhāne'dhikṛto yogī bhavati, - ityata uktaṃ 

gatasaṃgasya muktasya jñānāvasthitacetasaḥ |
yajñāyārabhataḥ karma samagraṃ pravilīyate || (4|23)

tena ca brahmaiva gantavyamiti | yajñākhyasya karmaṇaḥ prādhānyāt 
dṛṣṭādṛṣṭaviṣayasakalakriyopalakṣaṇārthatvena grahaṇamiha veditavyaṃ 
yataḥ 

paśyañśṛṇvanspṛśañjighran -------------------------- | (5|8)

ityādinā sarvāsu kriyāsu etāṃ brahmādvaitabhāvanāmabhidhāsyatyeva 
|| 

idānīṃ varṇāśramavibhaktādhikāryavasthādivibhedāt yeṣu yeṣu 
yajñaprabhedeṣu eṣā bhāvanā yoginā vidhātavyā anuṣṭhātṛmukhena, 
tāṃ pratipādayaṃsteṣāṃ tāvat bāhyābhyantarasādhanaprādhānyena 
prakāradvayaṃ tasyaiva jñānayajñakāraṇabhūtaprakāradvayasahitaṃ 
ślokatrayeṇa āha 

daivamevāpare yajñaṃ yoginaḥ samupāsate |
brahmāgnāvapare yajñaṃ yajñenaivopajuhvati || 25 ||

śrotrādīnīndriyāṇyanye saṃyamāgniṣu juhvati |
śabdādīnviṣayānanya indriyāgniṣu juhvati || 26 ||

sarvāṇīndriyakarmāṇi prāṇakarmāṇi cāpare |
ātmasaṃyamayogāgrau juhvati jñānadīpite || 27 ||

	apare kecit yoginaḥ samprati anutpannayathoktatattvajñānatvāt 
kriyāmātrasamāhitā daivameva yajñaṃ samupāsate 
devānāmagnīṣomaprajāpatīndrādityādīnāṃ prādhānyena 
ijyamānatvāt devasambandhinaṃ yāgamanuṣṭhānena bhajante, na anyaṃ 
yatra ṛtvigājyapayaḥsomādi cetanācetanaṃ bāhyaṃ dravyamupakaraṇam | 
apare tebhyo'pi anye tattva vidastenaiva yajñena 
tattadupakaraṇabhāvāvasthitātmamayena yajñam ātmānameva upajuhvati 
prīṇanti | kva | brahmāgnau brahma tattvajñānaṃ, sa eva 
paraprakāśātmakatvāt sakalaśubhāśubhakarmaphaladāhahetutvāt ca 
agnistasmin | 

p. 105) etaduktaṃ bhavati-brahmārpaṇavidhānavido daivameva yajñaṃ 
jñānayajñatvamāpādayanti || atha anye kecana 
yoginaścittavṛttinirodhanalakṣaṇasamādhiniṣṭhā abahiḥsādhanasādhyaṃ 
yajñamanutiṣṭhantaḥ śrotrādīni akṣāṇi 
tattaddhyeyārthasamādhirūpeṣu saṃyameṣu eva agniṣu juhvati 
viṣayebhyaḥ pratyāhṛtya teṣu teṣu samādhiṣu ālīnānīndriyāṇi kurvanto 
yajñasampattimabhimanyante ityarthaḥ | anye punaḥ śabdādīn viṣayān 
yoginaḥ parihāreṇa śrotrādiṣu indriyeṣu juhvati niṣiddhabahiṣprasarāsu 
indriyavṛttiṣveva labdhasaṃyame cetasi śabdādīnāmagrahaṇameva 
indriyeṣu agnikalpeṣu havanaṃ manyamānāstathāvidhaṃ 
yajñamanutiṣṭhantītyarthaḥ || apare kecana evaṃvidhasya yogayajñasya 
jñānayajñatvasampattaye sarvāṇi aśeṣāṇi bāhyānāmāntarāṇāṃ ca 
indriyāṇāṃ karmāṇi prāṇasya ca sāmānyarūpasya ekasya 
prāṇāpāṇasamānodānavyānādibhedapravṛttāni samastāni karmāṇi 
ātmasaṃyamayogāgnau juhvati ātmani pratipāditalakṣaṇe parasmin 
kāraṇe yaḥ saṃyamaḥ samyak cittasya nibandhanaṃ, tadātmako yo yogaḥ 
samādhiḥ, sa eva agnistatra tāni nilīnāni kurvate | kīdṛśe | jñānadīpite 
ātmādvayalakṣaṇena jñānena bodhena dīpite dyotite | kimuktaṃ bhavati | 
teṣu teṣu nānāvidheṣu yogeṣu manaḥprāṇayorvyāpārān 
havirvadātmaniṣṭhe samādhyagnau nikṣipantastādṛśasya yogajñānasya 
jñānayajñatvamāpādayantītyarthaḥ || 27 ||

evaṃ yajñānāṃ vyāpārabhedena caturvidhatvaṃ pratipādya 
idānīmāśramabhedapratipādanadvāreṇa yajñabhedān pratipādayituṃ 
ślokadvayaṃ sārdhamāha 

dravyayajñāstapoyajñā yogayajñāstathāpare |
svādhyāyajñānayajñāśca yatayaḥ saṃśitavratāḥ || 28 ||

p. 106) apāne juhvati prāṇaṃ prāṇe'pānaṃ tathāpare |
prāṇāpānagatī ruddhā prāṇāyāmaparāyaṇāḥ || 29 ||

apare niyatāhārāḥ prāṇānprāṇeṣu juhvati |

	dravyeṇa havirādinā yajñaḥ pākayajñādiryāgavidhiryeṣāṃ, te 
dravyayajñāḥ tattaddevatāyajanapradhānakratukriyānuṣṭhāyinaḥ 
sambhavantīti śeṣaḥ | te ca gārhasthyāśramavartinaḥ prādhānyena atra 
abhipretāḥ, yato gṛhasthānāmeva dravyasādhane devayajñe mukhyatayā 
adhikāraḥ | tatra pākayajñāḥ sarvavarṇasādhāraṇā yataḥ śūdrasya api 
pākayajñaiḥ svayaṃ yajet |

iti 

vihito'sya namaskāro mantra --------------------- |

ityādinā teṣu adhikāro vihitaḥ | tathā anye tapoyajñāḥ tapasā tena tena 
varṇaviśeṣavibhaktena niyamena yajño yāgavidhiryeṣāṃ te prādhānyena 
vānaprasthāśramiṇo'tra gṛhyante teṣāmeva mukhyatayā taponiṣṭhatvāt |

	āśramā vihitāḥ sarve varjayitvā nirāśiṣam |

ityādinā śūdrasya api atra adhikārāt tapoyajñeṣu adhikāraḥ | tathā anye 
yogayajñāḥ yogena yamaniyamādyaṃganirvartyena sabījanirbījarūpeṇa 
cittavṛttinirodhaikalakṣaṇena samādhinā yajño yeṣāṃ, te yogayajñāḥ 
prādhānyena caturthāśramiṇasteṣāmeva prādhānyena yogaparatvāt | 
atrāpi śūdrasya adhikāro vihito yato bhagavatā vyāsamuninaiva 
caturvarṇasādhāraṇān dharmān nirdiśatā tanmadhye ātmajñānamuktam 
| tathāca janakaparāśarasaṃvāde 

viśeṣadharmān varṇānāṃ prabrūhi bhagavanmama |
tathā sāmānyadharmāśca sarvatra kuśalo hyasi || 

p. 107) iti praśne viśeṣadharmānuktvā bhagavān parāśaraḥ 

ānṛśaṃsyamahiṃsā cāpramādaḥ saṃvibhāgitā |
śrāddhakarmātitheyaṃ ca satyamakrodha evaca ||

sveṣu dāreṣu santoṣaḥ śaucaṃ nityānasūyatā |
ātmajñānaṃ titikṣā ca dharmaḥ sādhāraṇo nṛpa ||

atraiva anyatra 

nacāpi śūdraḥ patatīti niścayo 
nacāpi saṃskāramihārhatīti vā |
śrutipravṛttaṃ naca dharmamaśnute 
nacāsya dharmapratiṣedhanaṃ kṛtam ||

atraiva anyatra api 

sarve varṇā dharmakāryāṇi samyak 
kṛtvā rājan satyavākyāni coktvā |
tyaktvādharmaṃ dāruṇaṃ jīvaloke 
yānti svargaṃ nātra kāryo vicāraḥ || (ma0bhā0śā0pa0 302 a0)

iti | svādhyāyajñānayajñāścāpare svādhyāyo 
vedavedāṃgasmṛtītihāsapurāṇatarkadaṃḍanītivārtāderyathāsvaṃ 
svasya śāstrasya adhyayanam | tatra prādhānyena 
brahmacaryāśramiṇo'dhikṛtā yato mukhyatayā teṣāṃ 
svādhyāyaniṣṭhatvaṃ vyavasthitam | tatra ca kasmiṃścidaṃśe kathañcit 
śūdrasyāpi adhikāro yataḥ svayaṃ yajet iti pākayajñeṣu tasya api 
adhikārābhyupagamāt taditikartavyatājñānahetoḥ kasyacit 
śāstrāvayavasya kenacit prakāreṇa adhyayanamavaśyameva prasaktam | 
tathā jñānayajñā jñānena 
prabandhapratipāditātmādvaitabodhātmanā yajño yāgavidhiryeṣām | 
tatra sarvavarṇāḥ sarvāśramiṇo 

p. 108) gṛhyante | tataḥ śūdrasya api adhikāraḥ | tathāca uktamihaiva 
parabrahmasvarūpamātmānaṃ vyapadiśatā bhagavatā 

māṃ hi pārtha vyapāśritya ye'pi syuḥ pāpayonayaḥ |
striyo vaiśyāstathā śūdrāste'pi yāṃti parāṃ gatim || (9|33)

iti | naca atiśayoktimātrametat yato muninaiva mokṣadharmeṣu 
piṃgalāsulabhādyāḥ striyaḥ, tulādharādyāśca vaiśyāḥ, 
dharmavyādhādayaḥ śūdrā āvirbhūtatattvajñānāstatra tatra 
udāhṛtāḥ | tadanena jñānayajñena pūrvoktā yajñabhedā 
jñānayajñatāmāpnuvantīti uktaṃ bhavati saṃśitavratāḥ iti viśeṣaṇaṃ 
svādhyāyayajñānāmatyantaniyamaparatvāt jñānayajñānāṃ ca 
paramārthaniṣṭhatvāt mukhyayatilakṣaṇayogāt veditavyam | atha 
prāṇāyāmayajñasya sādhāraṇyāt pṛthak svarūpaṃ 
pratipādayannāha- apare kecit yoginaḥ apāne pūrakavṛttivyavasthite 
śārīre maruti prāṇaṃ recakavṛttivyavasthitavāyuṃ juhvati prāṇākhyaṃ 
vāyuṃ havirbhāvena kalpayitvā apānaṃ ca agnitvena 
yāgavidhimanutiṣṭhantītyarthaḥ | recitaḥ prāṇo yadā apānavṛttyātmanā 
parāvartamānaḥ svarūpaṃ jahāti, tadā asāvapāne huto bhavatīti kṛtvā 
apāne juhvati prāṇam iti uktam | anenaiva krameṇa prāṇe agnibhāvena 
parikalpite apānaṃ havirbhūtamanye juhvati iti | recakavṛttipūrvakatvena 
pūrakavṛttiviśrāntirūpatayā pūrakavṛttipūrvakatvena ca 
recakavṛttiviśrāntirūpaṃ prāṇāyāmakramadvayaṃ pratipādya 
kumbhakavṛttirūpaṃ kramaṃ pratipādayitumāha apare anye 
prāṇāpānayordvayorapi gatī vyāpārau ruddhvā stambhayitvā 
yāvacchakti prāṇāyāmaparāyaṇāḥ tathāvidhānāṃ ruddhānāṃ 
prāṇānāmāyāmaścirakālāvasthānāt dīrghīkaraṇaṃ parāyaṇaṃ 
niṣṭhā yeṣāṃ, te tādṛśāḥ prāṇāyāmaparāyaṇāḥ | anena 
recakapūrakavṛttyorapi prāṇānāmāyāma 

p. 109) eva ukto veditavyaḥ | te ca evaṃvidhā niyatāhārāḥ 
mātrākālāvacchinnahitapavitrāśinaḥ santaḥ prāṇān 
nānānāḍīvāhino vāyūnājyabhūtān madhyameṣu prāṇeṣu 
agnibhūteṣu juhvati tatra līnān kurvate 
madhyamaprāṇavṛttyavalambinastiṣṭhanti | ayaṃ prāṇayajñakramaḥ 
pratipāditaḥ | eṣo'pi pūrvoktajñānaniṣṭhatayā anuṣṭhīyamāno 
jñānayajñatvamāpadyate yataḥ pūrvayordvayoḥ prakārayoḥ 
prāṇodayāstamayasandhisamāhitatvena 
paramātmasamāpattihetutāmāpadyamānayorvijñānayajñatā jāyate | 
tṛtīye'pi krame madhyamaprāṇapariṇatacittatayā asau jāyate | asya ca
