prāṇāyamakramasya yogāṃgatvena vyavasthitasya api punarvacanaṃ 
kevalasyaiva yajñaviśeṣapratipādanārthaṃ veditavyam || 29 ||

evamanuṣṭhātṛbhedena yajñabhedān pratipādya 
sarveṣāmapavargaphalatvaṃ yathābhavati, tathā pratipādayitumāha

sarve'pyete yajñavido yajñakṣapitakalmaṣāḥ || 30 ||
yajñaśiṣṭāmṛtabhujo yānti brahma sanātanam |

ete yathoktavividhayajñānuṣṭhātāraḥ sarve'pi samastā eva 
nirviśeṣatayā sanātanaṃ nityamakṣayaṃ brahma paramātmatattvaṃ yānti 
gacchanti tadeva sampadyante ityarthaḥ | kīdṛśāḥ | yajñavidaḥ santo yadi 
yathāpratipāditabrahmārpaṇavidhānakrameṇa yajñaṃ yathoktaṃ 
nānārūpaṃ yāgaṃ jānanti nirvikalpatayā pratipadyante, 
tatastathābhūtena yajñavidhinā kṣapitakalmaṣāḥ 
vināśitājñānalakṣaṇamukhyamahāpātakāḥ santaḥ, tathā anuṣṭhitāt 
yajñāt śiṣṭaṃ tādṛgyāgavidhānānantaramāharaṇīyaṃ yat, tadeva 
brahmārpaṇavidhānadṛṣṭyā 
brahmamayatvādamṛtamanuttarānandāsvādasundaraṃ sudhārasaṃ 
bhuñjate parasaṃvinmayasvātmaparipoṣaya carvayanti ye, tathāvidhāḥ 
santo yathoktaṃ brahmalakṣaṇaṃ 

p. 110) padaṃ prāpnuvantītyarthaḥ | brahmārpaṇajñānavyatirekeṇa etān 
yāgaviśeṣānanutiṣṭhantastebhyastattat nānārūpaṃ parimitaṃ phalaṃ 
prāpnuvantīti arthāt pratipāditaṃ bhavati ||
	yastu kevalena kriyāmātreṇa jñānakriyāsamuccayena vā 
anayorekatamena api vidhinā yajñaṃ na kañcidanutiṣṭhati, tasya 

nāyaṃ loko'styayajñasya kuto'nyaḥ kurusattama || 31 ||

ayajñasya ubhayarūpatayā api yajñarahitasya ayaṃ lokaḥ dṛṣṭa eva 
nāstikatvāt sarvajanadveṣyasya sato nāsti | yaduktaṃ 

satyadharmacyutātpuṃsaḥ kruddhādāśīviṣādiva |
nāstiko'pi hyudvijate janaḥ kiṃ punarāstikaḥ ||

iti tasya evaṃvidhasya pāpaśīlasya kuto'nyaḥ etasmāt lokāt paraḥ 
svargāpavargātmakaphalabhogāspadamadṛṣṭasaṃjño bhavati || 31 ||

	atha eteṣāṃ sarvayajñānāṃ karmajatvapratipādanena 
jñānayajñatvāpattiṃ pratipādayitumāha 

evaṃ bahuvidhā yajñā vitatā brahmaṇo mukhe |
karmajānviddhi tānsarvānevaṃ jñātvā vimokṣyase || 32 ||

	evaṃ yathoktena prakāreṇa bhūriprakārāḥ yajñāḥ brahmaṇaḥ 
vedādeḥ śāstrasya mukhe taddvāreṇetyarthaḥ vitatāḥ 
svagativiśeṣapratipādanakrameṇa vistāritāḥ | tāṃśca sarvānkarmajān 
vāṅmanaḥkāyavyāpāranirvartyatvāt kriyāsamudbhavān viddhi na 
anyathā eṣāmātmalābhopapattiriti niścinu | ye'pi hi 
buddhimātrasādhanā yajñāsteṣu 
tādṛksūkṣmabuddhivyāpārāvyabhicāraḥ sthita eva | tataḥ 
sahasṛṣṭatvādaśakyaparihārāṇāṃ karmātmakānāṃ yajñānāṃ 
yathoktajñānaniṣṭhatayā anuṣṭhānamapavargasiddhiriti | evaṃ jñātvā 
samyak pratipadya vimokṣyase svayameva kevalībhaviṣyasi na 
nimittāntaramapekṣiṣyase 

p. 111) iti samuccayajñānasya mokṣaṃ prati saukaryapratipādanāya 
vimokṣyase iti karmakartari prayogaḥ || 32 ||

atha etasya jñānapraśaṃsārthamāha

śreyāndravyamayādyajñājjñānayajñaḥ parantapa |
sarvaṃ karmākhilaṃ pārtha jñāne parisamāpyate || 23 ||

	dravyamayādyajñāt iti 
yathoktapratipāditaprabhedayajñopalakṣaṇārthaṃ veditavyam | tena 
sarvebhyo yajñebhyo jñānayajñaḥ śreyān praśasyataraḥ | jñānamayo 
hi yajñaḥ sarvān dravyayajñādīn mokṣaphalān sampādayati, tena jñāne 
sati sarvayajñā eva bhavantīti uktaṃ bhavati | sarvayajñaniṣṭhatvena hi 
jñānasya jñānayajñatvaṃ, mokṣaphalaṃ jñānaṃ natu pṛthak yasmāt 
yathoke jñāne dravyayajñādivibhāgena sarvaṃ samastaṃ karmākhilaṃ 
svagativiśeṣāpekṣayā samagraṃ jñāne parisamāpyate pravilayaṃ 
gacchati kṛtamapi dagdhabījavat svaphalaprasavasāmarthyaṃ na bhajate || 33 ||

evaṃvidhaṃ ca

tadviddhi praṇipātena paripraśnena sevayā |
upadekṣyanti te jñānaṃ jñāninastattvadarśinaḥ || 34 ||

tat jñānaṃ tubhyaṃ jñāninaḥ 
paramātmasamāpattilakṣaṇaniruttaraphalaprāptyupāyabhūtabodhayuktā 
gurava upadekṣyanti pratipādayiṣyanti | kiṃ śāstramātrajñāḥ | neti āha 
tattvadarśinaḥ yathāpratipāditasya pramārthasya svasaṃvedanena 
draṣṭāraḥ | kena hetunā upadekṣyanti | praṇipātena 
pādapraṇāmapūrveṇa sarvātmanā prahvatvena, tathā paripraśnena 
paritaḥ samantāt sarvasaṃśayacchedārtha yaḥ praśnaḥ punḥ punaḥ 
prārthanāpūrvamanuyogaḥ, tena, tathā sevayā āsannasvasamarpaṇāt 
sarvatra 

p. 112) nirvikalpayā śuśrūṣayā | nirvyājabhaktiśraddhārādhitā gurava 
evaṃvidhaṃ mahodayamarthamupadiśantīti viddhi niścayena jānīhi || 34 ||

kīdṛśaṃ tat jñānamiti āha 

yajjñātvā na punarmohamevaṃ yāsyasi pāṃḍava |
yena bhūtānyaśeṣeṇa drakṣyasyātmanyatho mayi || 35 ||

	yajjñātvā samyak pratipadya punaḥ tatpratipatteruttarakālam evam 
avaśyānuṣṭheye vairivadhalakṣaṇe svadharme eva adharmapratipattirūpeṇa 
prakāreṇa mohaṃ viparyayalakṣaṇamajñānaṃ na prāpyasi; yena ca 
jñānena karaṇabhūtena sarvabhūtāni carācaraprāṇabhṛto mayi 
paramakāraṇe, tathā ātmani sthitāni drakṣyasi | kimuktaṃ bhavati | 
paramakāraṇābhinnāt svātmanaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ 
vāstavamabhedamanena jñānena sākṣātkariṣyasi | atra svayaṃ 
jñānamupadiśannapi jñāninaste jñānamupadekṣyanti iti bhagavān 
yadāha, tatra sākṣāttattvānubhavalakṣaṇe viśeṣajñāne arjunasya 
abhikāṃkṣotpādanaṃ prayojanam | yataḥ krameṇa 
utpannaviśeṣajñānajijñāsātiśayaṃ

pāpasambandho yato'sya jñānasya ayaṃ prabhāvaḥ 

api cedasi pāpebhyaḥ sarvebhyaḥ pāpakṛttamaḥ |
sarvaṃ jñānaplavenaiva vṛjinaṃ saṃtariṣyasi || 36 ||

	api cedasi yadyapi sarvebhyaḥ pāpebhyaḥ samastebhyaḥ pātakibhyo 
jñātigurvādivadhādiduṣkṛtakāritvāt pāpakṛttamaḥ tadapekṣayā

durlaṃghyaṃ yathoktajñānalakṣaṇena plavena yānapātreṇa 
laṃghayiṣyasi || 36 ||

paśyañśṛṇvanspṛśañjighrannaśnangacchañśvasansvapan || 8 ||

pralapanvisṛjangṛhṇannunmiṣannimiṣannapi |
indriyāṇīndriyārtheṣu vartanta iti dhārayan || 9 ||

	ato'sau yukto vyākhyātasvarūpaḥ karmayogasamāhitaḥ kurvannapi 
na lipyate | yato naiva kiñcitkaromīti manyate sarvāsu kriyāsu lokavat 
vartamāno'pi naiṣkarmyaphalameva pratipadyate iti saṃbandhaḥ | kiṃ 
kurvannevaṃ manyate | paśyan ityādinā sarvendriyavṛttīḥ prāṇavṛttīśca 
pravartayannapi | iti dhārayan evaṃvidhāmasya karmayogasya 
yathoktaphalaprātyupāyabhūtāṃ dhāraṇāmudvahan | kathaṃ dhārayan | 
darśanādikriyāsu pravṛttānyapi indriyāṇi prāṇāśca indriyārtheṣu 
sveṣu sveṣu viṣayeṣu vartante saṃbandhamātraṃ tairanubhavanti, natu teṣu 
svaviṣayasaṃbaddheṣu nityaniratiśayacitprakāśasvabhāvasya 

p. 122) mama lokavat rāgādikalaṅkasparśo'ntaḥ saṃbhavatīti | paśyan 
ityādinā nimiṣan ityantena granthena sarvendriyaprāṇavṛttayaḥ 
saṃgṛhītāḥ | tathāca buddhīndriyavṛtayaḥ 
śravaṇasparśanadarśanarasanajighraṇarūpāḥ kramamanapekṣaya paśyan 
ityādinā aśnan ityantena padapancakena pratipāditāḥ | evameva 
vacanagrahaṇagamanavisargaprajananarūpāḥ karmendriyavṛttayaḥ pralapan 
gṛhṇan gacchan visṛjan iti padacatuṣṭayena pratipāditāḥ | yato visṛjanniti 
padena visargaprajananarūpe dve vṛttī pratipādite; svapannityanena 
manovṛttiḥ pratipāditā, svāpāvasthāyāṃ hi mana eva vṛttimanubhavati, 
nendriyāṇi; unmiṣannimiṣannitipadadvayena buddhivṛttiḥ pratipāditā, 
niścayātmikā hi buddhivṛttiriti sā bhāvaviṣayatvena bhavantī 
unmeṣaśabdena uktā, abhāvaviṣayatvena bhavantī nimeṣaśabdena | 
bhāvābhāvau hi buddhivṛttireva niścinute | ahaṅkārarūpā tu vṛttiḥ 
paśyañśṛṇvanniti kartṛtayā vyapadiṣṭe puruṣe eva vartate iti | etā 
antarbahiṣkaraṇavṛttayaḥ | śvasannityanena prāṇavṛttiḥ pratipāditā | 
prāṇadhāraṇameva hi śvasanamiti | tadidamasya vākyasya tātparyam �

yaśmin sarvaṃ --------------------------------- |
------------------------------------------------------ ||

iti vyākhyātasvarūpasya parasya tattvasya ātmana eva parameśvarasya 
tādṛksvabhāvaparāmarśamātrātmikā yā śaktiravyatiriktā, saiva 
tadicchāvabhāsitajñeyakāryātmakajagatkrīḍanakaviṣayatvena prasarantī 
tanmāyājanitaviparyayadarśanānāṃ sāṃsārikapruṣāṇāṃ 
saṃgraheṇa jñānakriyārūpā api antarbahiṣkaraṇaprāṇavṛttibhedena 
caturdaśamārgaprasṛtatvena avabhāsamānā saṃsārabandhahetutvena 
paryavasyati; 

p. 123) parameśvarānugrahādeva tu 
unmiṣitaparimṛṣṭadvaitakalaṅkanirmalanijajñānacakṣuṣāṃ 
sarvavyavahāravyāpṛtānāmapi 
yathoktasvabhāvamātraparāmarśaikarūpatayā 
yathoktakarmayogināmavabhāsamānā 
paramātmasamāpattilakṣaṇamokṣaphalaprāptyarthameva kalpate iti | ato 
yukto naiva kiñcitkaromīti manyate ityādi uktam || 9 ||

idānīmetadeva prakaṭayitumāha �

brahmaṇyādhāya karmāṇi saṅgaṃ tyaktvā karoti yaḥ |
lipyate na sa pāpena padmapatramivāmbhasā || 10 ||

brahmaṇi paramātmani sarvāṇi kriyamāṇāni karmāṇi ādhāya 
tacchaktitvena viniveśya ata eva saṅgaṃ svarūpavyatiriktatvena 
avabhāsamāneṣu viṣayeṣu nimagnacittatvaṃ laukikaṃ tyaktvā yathoktayā 
jñānadṛśā parameśvarabhāvānupraviṣṭo yaḥ karoti sarvāḥ kriyāḥ 
samācarati sa laukikavat pāpena karmaphalarūpeṇa sukhādyātmanā na 
lipyate na kaluṣīkriyate ambhasā salilena nirvyavadhānatayā 
susaṃśliṣṭamapi kamaladalamiva || 10 ||

yata evamataḥ �

kāyena manasā buddhyā kevalairindriyairapi |
yoginaḥ karma kurvanti saṅgaṃ tyaktvātmasiddhaye || 11 ||

kāyagrahaṇena karmendriyāṇi, manobuddhigrahaṇena 
buddhīndriyāṇi atra gṛhyante | etaiḥ kevalaiḥ 
tadviṣayādahaṅkārādyaparigrahāt viśuddhaiḥ pṛthagbhūtairvā 
karmayathāsvaṃ vyāpāraṃ śāstracodanayā śarīrādyavyabhicāritayā 
ca dustyajaṃ yoginaḥ karmayogasamāhitāḥ kurvanti anutiṣṭhantyeva, kintu 
yathoktasya satyasya ātmanaḥ siddhaye'hantāpratītiniṣpattaye saṅgaṃ 
tyaktvā kurvanti | kimuktaṃ bhavati | saṅgatyāga eva saṃnyāsaḥ, na 
karmatyāgaḥ || 11 ||

p. 124) sa ca saṅgatyāgaḥ karmaphalatyāgāt prabandhapratipāditāt 
bhavatītyāha �

yuktaḥ karmaphalaṃ tyaktvā śāntimāpnoti naiṣṭhikīm |
ayuktaḥ kāmakāreṇa phale sakto nibadhyate || 12 ||

	yo jñānaniṣṭhatayā karmaṇi yuktaḥ sannanabhisandhānena 
karmaphalaṃ tyaktvā naiṣṭhikīṃ niṣṭhāyāṃ parasyāṃ kāṣṭhāyāṃ 
sthitāṃ śāntiṃ 
dvaitapratyayalakṣaṇakṣobharahitasatyātmasvarūpaviśramātmakaṃ 
praśamaṃ samadhigacchati, yastu nisargadustyaje karmaṇi sarvadā pravṛttaḥ 
san yukto na bhavati yathoktena krameṇa samāhito na bhavati, so'vaśyameva 
phalasaṅgitvāt badhyate saṃsāre niyamyate || 12 ||
	atha evaṃvidhayoḥ karmayogasaṃnyāsayoradhyāye'smin 
prabandhena pratipāditayorupasaṃhārāvasare svarūpānuvādapūrvaṃ 
tadanuṣṭhāturnaiṣṭhikīṃ phalasiddhiṃ sphuṭīkartumāha �

sarvakarmāṇi manasā saṃnyasyāste sukhaṃ vaśī |
navadvāre pure dehī naiva kurvanna kārayan || 13 ||

	iha manaḥśabdena buddhireva vivakṣitā | tat ca dvividhaṃ manaḥ 
laukikaṃ, yigisaṃbandhi ca | tatra 
acintyeśvaramāyāśaktivaśādudbhūtadvaitapratyayatimiramayaṃ 
laukikaṃ yena loka ekameva paramārthasattattvaṃ nānātvena manyate, 
yogisaṃbandhi tu yena bhagavadanugrahāvirbhāvita 
vidyāśaktivimalīkṛtena yoginaḥ sarvabhāvān 
yathoktaparamātmaikarūpatayā manyante | 
tadevaṃvidhamubhayātmakatvaṃ manaso muninaiva anugītāsu 
bhagavadvacasaiva arjunabodhāyaiva itivṛttaviracanakrameṇa pradarśitam | 
yadāha �

ubhe vāṅmanasī gatvā bhūtātmānamapṛcchatām |
āvayoḥ śreṣṭhamācakṣva cchindhi nau saṃśayaṃ vibho ||

p. 125) mana ityeva bhagavāṃstadā prāha sarasvatīm |
ahaṃ kāmadughā tubhyamiti taṃ prāha vāgatha ||

sa tāmāha �

sthāvaraṃ jaṅgamaṃ caiva viddhyubhe manasī mama |
sthāvaraṃ matsakāśe vai jaṅgamaṃ viṣaye tava ||

iti | evaṃvidhena susthiraparamātmasvarūpamananaikaniṣṭhatvāt sthiratayā 
sthāvaraśabdoktena parameśvarābhyāśavartinā manasā antaḥkaraṇena 
sarvakarmāṇi cittavākkāyanirvartyāḥ sarvāḥ kriyāḥ śāstracoditāḥ 
śarīrāvyabhicāriṇīśca saṃnyasya 
bandhahetunānāphalābhisandhityāgāt brahmaṇyeva nikṣipya tenaiva 
krameṇa vaśī jitātmā svīkṛtasvabhāvaḥ san karmayogī sukhamāste 
joṣamavatiṣṭhate nisarganivṛttanikhilakleśasaṃparkaniruttarānandamaye 
svātmani viśrāmyatītyarthaḥ | kīdṛśaḥ sannāste | asminneva pure dehe eva 
adhiṣṭhānasāmānyāt gṛhatvena nirūpite, ata eva kīdṛśe | navadvāre 
cakṣuḥ śrotraghrāṇacchidrāṇi ṣaṭdvārāṇi 
darśanaśravaṇajighraṇakriyāpravṛttivartmāni, tathā mukhapāyūpasthāni 
trīṇi rasanavisargakriyāpravṛttivartmānīti nava dvārāṇi yasmiṃstatra | 
advayacinmātraparamātmasvabhāvaviśrāntatvāt sarvāḥ kriyāḥ sarvāṇi 
kāryāṇi svarūpamātratvena pratipadyamāno na kurvan itaraśarīrivat 
karmābhimānamananubhavan | kecit kil pratiśarīraṃ bhinnān śarīriṇaḥ 
svābhāvikena pratiniyatena kartṛtvena yuktān manyante, tathā na kārayan 
na anyān prayojayan | apare hi kecana saṇnidhimātreṇa jaḍāyāḥ prakṛteḥ 
kriyāpravṛttau prayojakamātmānaṃ manyante, asya tu ubhayathā api 
kartṛtvaṃ pratiṣidhyate | ayaṃ hi paramātmasamāpannatvāt na karoti na 
kārayati || 13 ||

p. 126) ata eva paramātmasvarūpaṃ smārayannāha �

nakartṛtvaṃ na karmāṇi lokasya sṛjati prabhuḥ |
na karmaphalasaṃyogaṃ svabhāvastu pravartate || 14 ||

yasmādadvayacinamātraikasvabhāvapratiṣṭhatvāt prabhuḥ īśvaraḥ 
paramātmā vāstavena rūpeṇa lokasya 
tanmāyāmahimodbhāvitaparasparabhedasya jīvavargasya na kartṛtvaṃ tāsu 
tāsu kriyāsu pratiniyatāsu pratiniyataphalāsu nauṣṭhātṛtvaṃ sṛjati nāpi 
puṇyapālakṣaṇāni karmāṇi asua kasyacit sṛjati, naca 
sukhādyātmakaistatphalaiḥ saṃbandham | atastatsvarūpasamāpannaḥ 
karmayogī tādṛśa eva bhavatīti pūrvaślokena saṃgatiḥ | nanu yadyevaṃ 
yathoktasvarūpamātraniṣṭhatvādīśvaro na karoti, na kārayati; tat 
kuto'yaṃ jagdvyavahāra ityāha svabhāvastu pravartate iti 
svabhāvaśabdena prakṛtyapraparyāyā māyāśaktireva acintyasvarūpā 
ucyate, sā pravartate 
anantabhāvabhedodbhūtavicitrajagadgandharvanagarāvabhāsanāya prasarati 
| sā hi vāstavasarvabhāvābhedākhyātimātrajīvitā prākpratipāditayā 
niyatyā sarvamidamābhāsayati || 14 ||

	evamavabhāsamānamapi etat tattvajñānadṛśā 
parāmṛśyamānaṃ yathoktaparamātmasvarūpāt na vyatiricyate | tataḥ 
parameśvarasya pratiniyatakāryakartṛtvādirna vāstavo dharmaḥ | tasmāt 
māyākṛta eva bandhamokṣavibhramo'yamiti pratipādayitumāha �

nādatte kasyacitpāpaṃ na caiva sukṛtaṃ vibhuḥ |
ajñānenāvṛtaṃ jñānaṃ tena muhyanti jantavaḥ || 15 ||

jñānena tu tadajñānaṃ yeṣāṃ nāśitamātmanaḥ |
teṣāmādityavajjñānaṃ prakāśayati tatparam || 16 ||

p. 127) vibhuḥ yathāpratipāditasvarūpa īśvaro na kasyacit jantoḥ 
paramārthataḥ pāpaṃ bandhakāraṇabhūtaṃ kalmaṣam ādatte apaharati 
yena asau jantustadapaharaṇādapavargalakṣaṇam śreyaḥ prāpnoti | naca 
mokṣakāraṇaṃ sukṛtaṃ kasyacidādatte yena asau tadādānāt 
saṃsārabandhalakṣaṇamaśreyaḥ prāpnoti | kastarhi bandhaheturjantūnāṃ 
mokṣaheturvetyāha ajñānena 
paramārthikaparamātmābhedāprathanamātrasvarūpeṇa jñānābhāvena 
mithyājñānena saṃtamasena iva padārthasvarūpaṃ jñānaṃ 
yathāpratipāditatattvaprathanātmanko bodha āvṛttaṃ saṃprati tiraskṛtam | 
tena hetunā sarvaprāṇinaḥ muhyanti 
śarīrādāvātmapratipattigrahaṇalakṣaṇaviparyayajñānamayadṛṣṭayo 
bandhamanubhavanti || yeṣāṃ punaḥ tat yathāpratipāditam ajñānaṃ 
yathoktena ātmanaḥ jñeyasya tattvasya jñānena nāśitam apadhvaṃsitaṃ, 
teṣāṃ parameśvarānugrahāt 
yathoktakarmayogasaṃnyāsānuṣṭhānādhikṛtānāṃ yogināṃ tat 
jñānaṃ paraṃ prakṛṣṭamupeyaṃ paramātmasvarūpalakṣaṇaṃ vastu 
prakāśayati prathayati | katham | ādityavat yathā ādityo bhagavān 
samayābhuditaḥ san durnivāramapi sāndramandhakāraṃ prabhāmātreṇa 
paryasya dṛśyaṃ vastu sarvaṃ niḥsaṃśayatayā prakaṭīkaroti, tathaiva 
jñānamīśvarānugrahecchāvasaronmiṣitaṃ sat 
svarūpatirodhānakāraṇamajñānaṃ tiraskarotīti upamānārthaḥ || 15, 16 ||

	tadevaṃvidhaṃ yat paravastuprakāśakamajñānapradhvaṃsakaṃ ca 
jñānaṃ, tadabhyāsaphalapratipādanāya āha-

tadbuddhayastadātmānastanniṣṭhāstatparāyaṇāḥ |
gacchantyapunarāvṛttiṃ jñānanirdhautakalmaṣāḥ || 17 ||

buddhirnānābhūtabodhyavastusādhanabhūtaṃ kāraṇaṃ prādhānyāt, 
tayā 

p. 128) samastaḥ karaṇavarga upalakṣito boddhavyaḥ, ātmaśabdena ca 
boddhā puruṣa ucyate iti | tatra yathokte jñāne eva paryavasitā 
buddhiryeṣāṃ, te tadbuddhayaḥ tathā tatraiva jñāne ātmā yeṣāṃ, te 
tadātmāno yathoktajñānaniṣṭhāhaṃpratītaya iti 
sakalalokavyavahāranibandhanaṃ buddhyātmalakṣaṇaṃ 
dvayamadvaitajñānamayaṃ yeṣāṃ, te tathāvidhāḥ | tathā 
niṣṭhāśabdena paramopeyavastusīmā atra ucyate, parāyaṇaśabdena ca 
tatprāptyupāyo vivakṣitastat paro'yyate yeneti | tat jñānaṃ yathoktaṃ 
niṣṭhā yeṣāṃ, te tanniṣṭhāḥ tadeva parāyaṇaṃ yeṣāṃ, te 
tatparāyaṇāḥ iti upeyopāyalakṣaṇadvayaṃ yogivyavahāraviṣayaṃ 
yathoktajñānātmakameva yeṣāṃ, te 
evaṃvidhāstattvajñānanibandhanasakalasaṃsārāpavargaviṣayapadārthas
ārthāḥ, ata evaṃvidhena jñānena nirdhautaṃ niḥśeṣeṇa 
prakṣālitamajñānarūpaṃ kalmaṣaṃ pāpaṃ yeṣāṃ, te tathāvidhā 
yoginaḥ apunarāvṛttiṃ gacchanti abhūyaḥsaṃbhavaṃ 
mokṣamāpnuvantītyarthaḥ || 17 ||

	atha īdṛśānāṃ yogināṃ sakalalokavilakṣaṇāṃ cittavṛttiṃ 
pratipādayitumāha �

vidyāvinayasaṃpanne brāhmaṇe gavi hastini |
śuni caiva śvapāke ca paṇḍitāḥ samadarśinaḥ || 18 ||

	vidyāśāstraṃ, vinayastadarthānuṣṭhānaviṣayo 
niyamaviśeṣastābhyāṃ sapannaḥ saṃbaddho yo brāhmaṇastasmiṃstathā 
gavādiṣu tiryakṣu bhayāhāramaithunamātraparyavasitasarvavṛttiṣu tathā 
śvapāke manuṣyabhāve'pi tiryakprāye paṇḍitāḥ 
yathoktatattvajñānaprabuddhāḥ samadarśinaḥ samaṃ 
sāmānyamekamaśeṣaviśeṣaṇāsaṃspṛṣṭasvabhāvaṃ cinmātraṃ 
draṣṭuṃ pratipattuṃ śīlaṃ yeṣāṃ, te tathāvidhāḥ | kimanena uktaṃ 
bhavati | ye samujjṛmbhitadivyajñānacakṣuṣaste 
māyopahatadṛṣṭibhirnānātvena 

p. 129) paridṛśyamānaṃ samastaṃ bhāvajātaṃ brahmaikarūpatayā 
nityamālokayanti | naca evaṃ mantavyaṃ, yathā te brāhmaṇādau vihitaṃ 
samācāraṃ śvapacādiṣu prayuñjate, śvapacādiṣu vihitaṃ 
brāhmaṇādiṣviti vikarmaṇi sthitāḥ santaḥ pārameśvarīṃ 
jagadvyavasthāṃ vyutthāpayanti | te hi yathā sthitameva 
jagadbhāvavistāraṃ brahmātmakaṃ paśyanti, natu vyutthānamācarantīti 
|| 18 ||

	tāmeva samadarśitāṃ vivṛṇoti �

na prahṛṣyetpriyaṃ prāpya nodvijetprāpya cāpriyam |
sthirabuddhirasaṃmūḍho brahmavidbrahmaṇi sthitaḥ || 19 ||

	yo brahmavit āgamato yathoktabrahmatattvavedī sthirabuddhiḥ 
prabalairapi vikalpairaprakampyamatirata eva asaṃmūḍhaḥ 
heyopādeyavastunorasandigdhabuddhiḥ san tasmin śāstrādhigate brahmaṇi 
paramātmani sthitaṃ satatapariśīlanāt viśrāntaḥ, sa priyāpriyaprāptyā 
praharṣodvegau viṣamadarśitvapiśunau saṃsāripuruṣocitau na bhajate | tato 
brāhmaṇagavādiṣu paṇḍitāḥ samadarśina iti uktam || 19 ||

īdṛśe ca brahmaṇi sthitiḥ sarvathā bāhyaviṣayaparihāreṇaiva 
bhavatīti kevalajñānavādināṃ pakṣaḥ | sa pūrvoktopapattyā na 
saṃbhavati, kathaṃcit vā saṃbhavannapi sacchūla iti 
śāstravihitaviṣayatayaiva brahmaṇi sthitrnirviplaveti samuccayapakṣameva 
nirvāhayitumāha �

bāhyasparśeṣvasaktātmā vindatyātmani yaḥ sukham |
sa brahma yogayuktātmā sukhamavyayamaśnute || 20 ||

ātmani parasmin tattve svabhāvasiddhaṃ yat sukhaṃ 
nityānandalakṣaṇo dharmastat bāhyasparśeṣu idantāvadhāryeṣu 
śabdādiṣu aviruddheṣu asaktātmā samyagjñānavaśādātmaikaniṣṭhatve 
sati animagnāntaḥ karaṇo yo yogī vindati upalabhate sa yogayuktātmā yoge 
karmasamādhau yuktātmā 

p. 130) jñānaniṣṭhatayā nityāvahitāntaḥkaraṇaḥ sukham akleśena 
avyayam vicchinnaṃ kṛtvā brahma paramakāraṇam aśnute svātmatayā 
vyāpnoti || 20 ||

	kathaṃ bāhyasparśeṣu ātmavarti sukhaṃ yogī na vindatīti 
pratipādayitumāha �

ye hi saṃsparśajā bhogā duḥkhayonaya eva te |
ādyantavantaḥ kaunteya na teṣu ramate budhaḥ || 21 ||

	hiśabdo hetau | yasmāt ye saṃsparśajāḥ 
śabdādiviṣayasaṃparkajanmāno bhogāḥ kāmāḥ te 
janmavināśayaktatvādanityāḥ santaḥ prāpteṣu satsu viyogajasya 
duḥkhasya avyabhicārādaprāpteṣu ca prārthanātmakasya duḥkhasya 
avyabhicārāt duḥkhayonaya eva duḥkhameva te janayanti | tataḥ teṣu 
bhogeṣu budhaḥ jñānavān na ramate na prītimanubhavati | kimanena uktaṃ 
bhavati | karmayogī satyaṃ saṃsāripuruṣavadindriyairviṣayān bhajate natu 
tajjanitena sukhena asau sukhito bhavati | sa hi svābhāvikamātmavarti 
sukhamanubhavatīti || 21 || 

	budhasya evaṃvidhasya eva yogitvaṃ ca sukhitvaṃ ca 
pratipādayitumāha �

śaknotīhaiva yaḥ soḍhuṃ prāk śarīravimocanāt |
kāmakrodhodbhavaṃ vegaṃ sa yogī sa sukhī mataḥ || 22 ||

	ya ihaiva asmin śarīre tiṣṭhan 
tadavyabhicāraviṣayasaṃbandhavaśāt rāgadveṣādijaṃ 
saṃrambhamātmaikaniṣṭhatayā soḍhum avadhārayituṃ 
dehatyāgādarvāgeva śaknoti prabhavati sa yogī samāhitaḥ | sa ca sukhī 
niruttarānandalakṣaṇasamādhiphalavān | yatastattvavido'nye hi 
kevalajñānavādino viṣayebhyaḥ palāyamānā yogamabhyasyanti | 
teṣāmantaraprepsuṣu teṣu ātatāyiṣu satsu ihaiva dehe durlabho 

p. 131) yogastatphalaṃ ceti yadi paraṃ te dehatyāge tadavāpsyanti | yastu 
karmayogī satsu eva viṣayeṣu aviluptātmasthitiḥ; sa jīvanneva mukta iti sa 
eva yogī, sa eva sukhīti uktam || 22 ||

ata eva tatsvarūpamāha �

antaḥsukho'ntarārāmastathāntarjyotireva yaḥ | 
sa pārtha paramaṃ yogaṃ brahmabhūto'dhigacchati || 23 ||

sukhaṃ tattatkriyāphalabhūtā nirvṛtiḥ, āramyate'sminniti 
ārāmaḥ sukhānubhavādhikaraṇaṃ, jyotistadubhayavyaktihetuḥ 
prakāśaḥ, tat tritayamapi antarātmani eva yasya sa tādṛśaḥ sarvatra 
saṃvedyamāne vastuni vigalitānātmapratyayo'ta eva brahmabhūto 
brahmaparamātmasvarūpaṃ prāptaḥ paramaṃ yogaṃ niruttaraṃ 
samādhim adhigacchati prāpnoti || 23 ||

yasmāt �

labhante brahma nirvāṇamṛṣayaḥ kṣīṇakalmaṣāḥ |
chinnadvaidhā yatātmānaḥ sarvabhūtahite ratāḥ || 24 ||

ye mahātmānaḥ chinnadvaidhāḥ 
truṭitadvaitapratyayātmakasaṃsārabandhaśṛṅkhalā yataḥ 
kṣīṇakalmaṣāḥ vinaṣṭākhilājñānamayamahāpātakā ṛṣayaḥ 
samyagdarśinaḥ, ata eva svarūpavyatiriktaṃ vastujātamapaśyantaḥ, 
sarvabhūtānāmātmana iva hite'nukūle vastuni ratāḥ abhiniviṣṭāste 
nirvāṇaṃ tāpatrayeṇa asaṃspṛṣṭatvāt praśāntaṃ brahmaparaṃ tattvaṃ 
labhante prāpnuvanti, natu ye vyatiriktatvena viṣayān manyamānāstebhyaḥ 
palāyante | te hi dvaitabandhādanirmuktā eva || 24 ||

etadeva viśinaṣṭi �

kāmakrodhavimuktānāṃ yatīnāṃ yatacetasām |
abhito brahma nirvāṇaṃ vartate viditātmanām || 25 ||

p. 132) ye rāgadveṣādidoṣarahitatvena yatacetaso nigṛhītāntaḥkaraṇāḥ, 
natu viṣayaviyuktatvena; teṣāṃ viditātmanāṃ 
vijñātaparamakāraṇasvarūpāṇāṃ mukhyalakṣaṇayuktānāṃ 
yatīnāṃ paramayogināṃ yathoktaṃ nirvāṇaṃ brahma abhitaḥ 
sāṃmukhyena vartate || 25 ||

	tato naiṣkarmyayoginaḥ sakāśāt karmayogino viśeṣaṃ 
pratipādayitumāha �

sparśān kṛtvā bahirbāhyāṃścakṣuścaivāntare bhruvoḥ |
prāṇāpānau samau kṛtvā nāsābhyantaracāriṇau || 26 ||

yatendriyamanobuddhirmunirmokṣaparāyaṇaḥ |
vigatecchābhayakrodho yaḥ sadā mukta eva saḥ || 27 ||

	yaḥ evaṃvidho muniḥ yatiḥ san mokṣaparāyaṇaḥ mokṣaṃ parameti 
tatprāptikāmaḥ, natu mukto heyopādeyavastvālambanacittatayā 
rāgadveṣādidoṣāviyogāt; yasya tu icchābhayakrodhāḥ 
pūrvavyākhyātā durvārāścittadoṣāḥ sadā nityameva 
nisarganirdoṣasvātmamātrapratiṣṭhatvāt viśeṣeṇa apunarāvṛttilakṣaṇena 
gatāḥ pradhvastāḥ, sa tādṛśaḥ karmayogī mukta eva yato'sya ātmanaḥ 
sāmānyasaṃvillakṣaṇā svabhāvaśuddhā śaktiryadā māyāvaśāt 
svarūpāvimarśinī satī nādehādyahaṃpratyayaniruddhā 
heyopādeyavastvanusāriṇī viṣayitvamanubhavati, tadā 
rāgādidoṣoparaktaṃ cittamiti saiva ucyate; yadā tu tairdoṣairvidyāvaśāt 
viyujyate, tadā nityānantanirupamanairmalyaikalakṣaṇaṃ svarūpaṃ 
pratipadyamānā saṃvinmātrarūpaiva avaśiṣyate | taduktaṃ 

cittameva hi saṃsāro rāgādikleśadūṣitam |
tadeva tairvinirmuktaṃ bhavānta iti kathyate ||

iti | tadevaṃ satsveva viṣayeṣu rāgādidoṣonmeṣaśūnyatvāt 
paramātmasamāpannaḥ karmayogī jīvanneva muktaḥ | yastu 
etadvyatiriktalakṣaṇa 

p. 133) evaṃvidho yogī, sa yadi paraṃ mokṣapravaṇa eva; natu muktaḥ | 
kīdṛśaḥ yatendriyamanobuddhiḥ yatāni nigṛhītāni indriyāṇi karaṇāni 
manaścittaṃ saṃkalpātmakaṃ buddhiradhyavasāyātmikā yena sa 
tādṛśaḥ | kiṃ kṛtvā evaṃvidhaḥ | bāhyān grāhyatvena vyatiriktān 
śabdādīn sparśān indriyaiḥ spṛśyamānatvāt sparśaśabdavācyān 
viṣayān bahiḥ kṛtvā | indriyairagrahaṇena cetaso vyatiricyetyanena 
viśeṣaṇena indriyāṇāṃ viṣayebhyaḥ pratyāhāralakṣaṇaṃ yogāṅgaṃ 
pratipāditam | tena ca pūrvabhāvīni āsanaprāṇajayadīni aṅgāni 
ākṣiptāni | anyat kiṃ kṛtvā | bhruvorantare bhrūmadhye cakṣuḥ 
darśanaśaktiṃ kṛtvetyanenāpi dhāraṇālakṣaṇaṃ yogāṅgamuktam | 
bhrūmadhyalakṣaṇe deśe tejomātrālambanatayā cittasya bandho hi anena 
uktaḥ | kiṃca nāsābhyantaracāriṇau nāsāyāḥ 
savyāpasavyanāḍīdvayātmakasya mukhāvayavasya abhyantare carituṃ 
śīlaṃ yayostau tathāvidhau prāṇāpānasaṃjñau śārīrau mārutau 
samau yaugapadyena tulyarūpatayā vahanāt samānau saṃpādyetyaena 
ekāgrabuddhisaṃpādyaṃ dhyānalakṣaṇaṃ yogāṅgamuktam | 
ekavastvālambane hi antaḥkaraṇe dhyānasaṃpattiḥ, tadbalāt viṣuvadgatiṃ 
prāṇāpānau samāśrayata iti trividhena karmaṇā 
yatendriyamanobuddhiryogī pratyāhāradhāraṇādhyānaparyantaṃ 
yogāṅgaṃ karma prāpto bhavati, natu buddheratyantatirodhānābhāvāt 
samādhimasāvadhirūḍhaḥ | sa hi asya dehavimuktiṃ vinā na sidhyati 
ādehasthiterbuddhivṛttisaṃskāraśeṣasya sattvābhyupagamāt | evaṃ 
cittavṛttinirodhalakṣaṇayogābhyāsaniṣṭhasya yoginaḥ sakāśāt sadā 
svarūpapratiṣṭhasaṃvitkarmayogī viśiṣṭaḥ pratipāditaḥ || 27 ||

sa hi �

bhoktāraṃ yajñatapasāṃ sarvalokamaheśvaram |
suhṛdaṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ jñātvā māṃ śāntimṛcchati || 28 ||

p. 134) māṃ nirvāṇaṃ brahmaśabdena uktamanuttaramupeyaṃ 
paramātmānaṃ jñātvā ātmatvena pratipadya śāntiṃ 
niḥśeṣavikalpakṣobhaviratilakṣaṇāṃ nirvṛtim ṛcchati gacchati 
matsvarūpajñānādeva mucyata ityarthaḥ | kīdṛśaṃ māṃ jñātvā | 
yajñatapasāṃ bhoktāram iha nānāyajñatapaḥ prabhṛtayaḥ kriyāḥ 
śāstreṇa anuṣṭheyatayā coditāḥ | tāsāmajñānamohitā anuṣṭhātāraḥ 
parasparabhinnamātmānaṃ tattatphalabhoktāraṃ manyante, 
paramārthavidastu kartṛbhedābhāvāt sarvakriyāphalānāmahamevaikaḥ 
kartā ca bhoktā ceti yajñatapasāṃ bhoktārm | tathā 
sarvalokamaheśvaraṃ sarvasya kasyacit jantujātasya 
krīḍāmātraprayojanatayā svecchāmātreṇaiva nānātvena avabhāsitasya 
mehśvaraṃ tāsu tāsu kriyāsu ekaviniyoktṛtvāt paramaṃ prabhuṃ, tathā 
paramārthato bhedābhāvāt sarveṣāṃ bhūtānāṃ suhṛdaṃ sarvadā 
nisarganirdoṣasvātmamātrapratiṣṭhitatvāt na kasyacit dviṣṭaṃ, nāpi 
anuraktam | evaṃvidhaṃ māṃ jñātvā madvadeva karmasu pravartamānaḥ 
karmayogī śāntiṃ muktimāpnotīti pūrvasmāt yogino viśiṣṭatvameva 
asya pratipāditamiti || 28 ||

yatraikatāṃ yamunayā gaganāpageva
saṃnyāsadhīrupagatā saha yogabuddhyā |
adhyāyamenamabhigacchata sarvatīrtha-
sāraṃ prayāgamiva pañcamamātmasiddhyai ||

iti śrīrājānakarāmakaṇṭhaviracite vākyārthānvayamātre 
sarvatobhadranāmni bhagavadgītāvivaraṇe pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ || 5 ||


atha ṣaṣṭho'dhyāyaḥ |

	ātmajñānavihitakarmasamuccayānuṣṭhānarūpasya 
sakalaprakaraṇasiddhāntasya prathamena adhyāyena 
prastāvanāmātramupakrāntaṃ, dvitīyena jñānakarmaṇoḥ 
svarūpamupadarśitaṃ, tṛtīyena tayoreva 
jñānakarmaṇorbhinnanuṣṭhātṛviṣayatvabhrāntirapākṛtā, caturthena 
evaṃvidhasamuccayānuṣṭhānarūpasya yogasya parasmāt kāraṇāt 
pravṛttirupadarśitā, karmaṇaśca nānārūpatvaṃ; pañcamena te eva 
jñānakarmaṇī saṃnyāsayogāviti pradarśitam | idānīṃ 
tayorjñānakarmaṇoḥ samuccayena anuṣṭhātavyatayā upadiṣṭayoḥ kiṃ 
sarvavarṇāḥ sarvāśramiṇaśca adhikriyante, uta niyatāḥ kecaneti 
saṃśayanirāsārthaṃ sarvasādhāraṇyamasya 
pratipādayitumadhyāyāntareṇa āha �

śrībhagavānuvāca

anāśritaḥ karmaphalaṃ kāryaṃ karma karoti yaḥ |
sa saṃnyāsī ca yogī ca na niragnirna cākriyaḥ || 1 ||

	karmaphalaṃ tasya tasya karmaṇo yajñādeḥ kriyamāṇasya phalaṃ 
svargādibhogātmakam anāśritaḥ anabhisandhānādanālambitaḥ san yaḥ 
pumān kāryaṃ śāstreṇa kartavyamiti varṇāśramaniyamena coditaṃ 
karma karoti anutiṣṭhati sa saṃnyāsī tyāgavān yogī ca samāhita ucyate | 
tena sarvavarṇāḥ sarvāśramiṇaśca atra 
mokṣaphalasamuccayānuṣṭhāne'dhikṛtā iti uktam | 
karmaphalānabhisandhānamātraṃ ca saṃnyāsalakṣaṇaṃ, tadvacca 
vihitakarmānuṣṭhānaṃ ca yoga iti saṃgraheṇa 
sakalaprakaraṇapratipādyo'rthaḥ siddhāntīkṛtaḥ | tataśca 
yathāpratipāditātmajñānaikatānāntaḥkaraṇatayā 
vyatiriktasukhātmakaphalanirabhikāṅkṣaḥ san 
rāgādidoṣanirmuktasvabhāvatvādadhikārakālāt 

p. 136) prabhṛti prastutaṃ karma yo'nutiṣṭhati, sa brāhmaṇo'stu kṣattriyo 
vaiśyaḥ śūdro vā brahmacārī gṛhastho vānaprastho yatirvā, sa eva 
saṃnyāsī yogī ca bhavati; na punaḥ niragniḥ eva akriyaḥ eva vā | 
niragnyakriyaśabdābhyāṃ brahmacārī yatiśca lakṣyate | yataḥ kecit 
anayoreva āśramayoraparyālocitaparamārthatvāt naiṣkarmyābhimānino 
hetutvaṃ manyamānā brahmacāriyatyoreva saṃnyāsitāṃ yogitāṃ ca 
pratijānate | yo hi brahmacaryādeva parivrājakadharmamāśritaḥ, sa 
dāraparigrahābhāvādanāhitāgniḥ san mūlādeva parihṛtayajñādikriyo 
brahmacārī ekaḥ saṃnyāsī ca yogī ca teṣāṃ mataḥ; yo vā 
āśramatrayānuṣṭhitāḥ 
svādhyāyayāgadānādhyāpanayājanapratigrahādikāḥ sakalāḥ kriyāḥ 
parityajya nirāśiṣamāśramamāśritaḥ, sa yatirdvitīyaḥ saṃnyāsī yogī 
ca teṣāṃ mato dvayorapi anayoreva bandhahetūnāṃ kriyāṇāṃ tyāge sati 
mokṣaphalabhāgitvāt || 1 ||

tadetat kevalajñānavādimatamanupapannameveti pratipādayitumāha �

yaṃ saṃnyāsamiti prāhuryogaṃ taṃ viddhi pāṇḍava |
na hyasaṃnyastasaṃkalpo yogī bhavati kaścana || 2 ||

	ete jñānamātravādinaḥ saṃnyāsasaṃjñayā naiṣkarmyarūpaṃ 
yam arthaṃ kathayanti taṃyogaṃ karmānuṣṭhānameva tvaṃ jānīhi | 
yasmāt saṃnyāsaṃ vinā yogasteṣu nāsti, kintu sa saṃnyāsaḥ 
saṃkalpasya phalābhilāṣasya eva upapadyate, natu karmaṇaḥ sarvavarṇeṣu 
sarvāśrameṣu ca karmāvirahāt | yau kila yatibrahmacāriṇau paritrājakau 
karmatyāgitvena abhimatau, tau gṛhasthavānaprasthocitaṃ karma tyajataḥ; 
svaṃ tu yatitvādisiddhikāraṇaṃ kaṃcit karmaviśeṣamanutiṣṭhata eva | 
yatheṣṭācaraṇe hi tayorvikarmasthataiva paryavasyati | tasmāt 
saṃkalpamātrasaṃnyāsitvāt saṃnyāsitvaṃ sidhyatīti yogī yaḥ sarvadā 
sarvathā svānandāmṛtanityatṛptaparamātmalakṣaṇe 

p. 137) brahmaṇi prarūḍhāhaṃpratyayatayā 
saṃnyastakarmaphalasaṃkalpaḥ, sa yogī karmaṇi samādhimān bhavati, na 
anya iti || 

	yathoktayoḥ saṃnyāsayogayorekapuruṣānuṣṭhātṛniṣṭhayoḥ sarve 
varṇāḥ sarvāśramiṇaśca adhikṛtā anena ślokena pratipāditāḥ, kintu 
anuṣṭhāturyathoktayogaviṣayā ārurukṣāvasthā ca saṃbhavatīti tatkṛtaṃ 
tasya bhedaṃ pratipādayitumāha �

ārurukṣormuneryogaṃ karma kāraṇamucyate |
yogārūḍhasya tasyaiva śamaḥ kāraṇamucyate || 3 ||

	muneḥ jhagiti prāptamātrajñānatvāt manananiṣṭhasya 
anuṣṭhāturyathoktaṃ yogamārurukṣoḥ āroḍhukāmasya tatra nirviplavāṃ 
pratiṣṭhāṃ lipsamānasya karma varṇāśramakramāgatā sā sā kriyā 
kāraṇaṃ yogārohahetuḥ | tasyaiva tathāvidhasyaiva kāraṇabhūtaṃ karma 
ajahata eva 

paśyañśṛṇvan spṛśañjighran ----------------------- | (5|8)

	ityādinā pūrvopadiṣṭanirvyavadhānanityābhyāsavaśāt 
yogārūḍhasya yoge labdhapratiṣṭhacittatve paramātmasamāpannasya 
śamaḥ saṃkalpavikalpakṣobhakṣayaḥ kāraṇaṃ prayojakaṃ nimittam | 
kimuktaṃ bhavati | āgamamātreṇa prāptopadeśasya prāthamakalpikasya 
yogino yogapratiṣṭhālābhotsukasya api tatprāptikāraṇaṃ karmaiva 
upeyatayā vartate | tasmiṃśca śraddhādarābhyāṃ samyaganuṣṭhite, 
jñāne ca prasādakāṣṭhāṃ prāpte tatkarma atyajata eva śamaḥ 
paramopeyatvāt kāraṇaṃ saṃpadyate || 3 ||

	ata eva praśāntasya yogārūḍhasya lakṣaṇamāha �

yadā hi nendriyārtheṣu na karmasvanuṣajjate |
sarvasaṃkalpasaṃnyāsī yogārūḍhastadocyate || 4 ||

	yadā hi yasyāmeva avasthāyāṃ indriyārtheṣu aviruddheṣu 
viṣayeṣu indriyaiḥ spṛśyamāneṣu api karmasu vā tatprāptihetuṣu 
yajñatapaḥ prabhṛtiṣu 

p. 138) kriyamāṇeṣu api nānuṣajjate sahajānandatṛptatvāt na āsaktiṃ 
bhajate, kevalaṃ pavana iva padārthajātaṃ spṛśannapi 
svabhāvavyomapratiṣṭha eva bhavati; tadā sarveṣāṃ saṃkalpānāṃ 
phalābhisandhīnāṃ saṃnyāsitvāt tyāgitvāt yogārūḍhaḥ yoge 
karmasamādhāvārūḍho labdhapratiṣṭhaḥ ucyate | tadevaṃvidhasya 
paramapraśāntabrahmasamāpattikāraṇasya yogārūḍhatvasya na 
ātmavyatirekeṇa bāhyaṃ kiñcit sādhanaṃ bhavitumarhati || 4 ||

ataḥ �

uddharedātmanātmānaṃ nātmānamavasādayet |
ātmaiva hyātmano bandhurātmaiva ripurātmanaḥ || 5 ||

bandhurātmātmanastasya yenātmaivātmanā jitaḥ | 
ajitātmanastu śatrutve vartetātmaiva śatruvat  || 6 ||

jitātmanaḥ praśāntasya parātmasu samā matiḥ | 
śītoṣṇasukhaduḥkheṣu tathā mānāvamānayoḥ || 7 ||

	durvāradvaitapratyayapavanaparikṣobhitabhīṣaṇavikalpakallolasahasra
saṃkulāpāragambhīrabhavasāgaranimagnam ātmānaṃ svabhāvam 
ātmanaiva yathoktajñānopadeśapratyuditotsāhena uddharet 
yathoktayogalakṣaṇaṃ tīradeśamāropayet, natu enaṃ vismṛtasvabalatayā 
upekṣitaṃ tatraiva avasādayet majjayet | yato'sya 
uddharaṇāvasādahetutayā upakārī pratikūlaśca ātmaiva na anyaḥ | 
kathamityāha tasya puruṣasya saṃbandhinaḥ ātmanaḥ ātmaiva svabhāva 
eva bandhuḥ sarvāpatpratīkārakṛt svajanaḥ | yaduktaṃ 

ātmaiva yadi nātmānamahitebhyo nivārayet |
ko'nyo hitatarastasmādya enaṃ vārayiṣyati ||

	iti | kasya ātmaiva bandhuḥ | yena puruṣeṇa sa ātmā svasvabhāvaḥ 

p. 139) ātmanaiva jñānāmṛtābhyavahārābhyāsalabdhabalena jitaḥ 
pravimarśagocarāpādānāt vaśīkṛtaḥ | yena tu asāvātmā 
pramātṛpareṇa satā upekṣitaḥ san na jito na vaśīkṛtaḥ, tasya tathāvidhasya 
ayaṃ śatrutve sarvecchāvyāghātakāritve vartate | tathā ca uktaṃ 

na tatkuryādasistīkṣṇaḥ sarpo vātha viṣolvaṇaḥ |
arirvā nityasaṃkruddho yadātmā duradhiṣṭhitaḥ ||

	iti | yathoktasya ātmajayasya phalamāha � jito yathoktena prakāreṇa 
vaśīkṛta ātmā yena tasya jitātmanaḥ ata eva praśāntasya 
svasvabhāvanirdoṣaparamātmasamāpattivaśāt nivṛttavikalpaviplavasya 
pareṣu vyatiriktatvena pratipanneṣu sarvadehiṣu tathā ātmani 
ahantāpratīyamāne tulyā sāmānyasaṃvidekasvarūpagrāhiṇī matiḥ 
bhavati, tathā śītoṣṇasukhaduḥkhādiṣu bāhyābhyantaranimittajeṣu 
dvandvopaplaveṣu, tathā pareṇa prayujyamāne māne pūjāyāṃ, tathā 
avamāne parābhave tathaiva samānā matirbhavati | na tasya 
sarvagatādekasmāt svabhāvanirvikārāt 
paramātmano'haṃpratītirvyatiricyate, nānāvidheṣu vikārakāraṇeṣu 
upasthiteṣu api tasya satye svātmani anusandhānaṃ lokavat na vicchidyate 
ityarthaḥ || 7 ||

	sa ca evaṃvidho yogārūḍha ucyate ityāha �

jñānavijñānatṛptātmā kūṭastho vijitendriyaḥ |
yukta ityucyate yogī samaloṣṭāśmakāñcanaḥ ||

	jñānaṃ śāstrataḥ svarūpāvagamamātraṃ, vijñānaṃ 
śāstrāvagatasya tattvasya, sākṣātkāraḥ, tābhyāṃ tṛpto nirākāṅkṣaḥ 
ātmā svabhāvo yasya sa tathā, ata eva kūṭasthaḥ 
prāptaparamārthasattvanityātmapratyayatvāt kūṭasthaḥ śailādivat 
niṣprakampo yato vijitendriyaḥ vaśīkṛtabāhyāntaḥkaraṇaḥ, natu 
tadvaśaḥ; evaṃ lobhādidoṣarahitatvāt 

p. 140) samau loṣṭāśmānau mṛtpiṇḍapāṣāṇau kāñcanaṃ ca hema 
yasya, sa tathāvidho yuktaḥ yogārūḍha ucyate || 8 ||

suhṛnmitrāryudāsīnamadhyasthadveṣyabandhuṣu |
sādhuṣvapi ca pāpeṣu samabuddhirviśiṣyate || 9 ||

	yasya suhṛdariprabhṛtiṣu prītidveṣahetuṣu nānāvidheṣu 
anyonyabhinnarūpeṣu jantuṣu samā 
brahmalakṣaṇanirviśeṣaikatattvadarśitvādekarūpā buddhiḥ, sa viśiṣyate 
naiṣkarmyayoginaḥ sakāśāt prakarṣaṃ labhate | sa hi yat vastu 
samādhipratipakṣatvāt pariharati, yacca tadānuguṇyāt svīkurute, 
tābhyāṃ dveṣapriyābhyāṃ dveṣarāgadoṣai prakāśyete | yastu 
sarvasāmyaṃ prāptaḥ, sa kriyāsu vartamāno'pi samabuddhitvāt 
brahmabhūta eveti viśiṣyata iti uktam | suhṛdādīnāṃ bhedo vyākhyāyate-
suhṛt yaḥ svabhāvataḥ śobhanahṛdayaḥ, mitraṃ 
maitrīkaraṇanirvṛttasauhṛdam, ariḥ śatruḥ kāraṇavaśāt dveṣṭā, 
dveṣyastu vināpi kāraṇamapriyaḥ, udāsīnaḥ na mitraṃ na śatruḥ, 
madhyastho yaḥ kenāpi prakāreṇa mitratvamamitratvaṃ ca āśrayitumarhati, 
bandhavo jñātayaḥ, sādhavaḥ śāstroditasadācāraniyatāḥ, 
pāpāstadviparītā duṣṭācārasaṃkalpāḥ | teṣu sarveṣu 
samabuddhitvamasya karmayogina eva boddhavyam � yat śatrubhāvaṃ 
pratipanneṣu śāstracoditatvāt nyāyyavadhādikarmapravṛttāvapi 
nirvikāraikātmaniṣṭhatvam, natu śatrau śāstrāvihitatvādanyāyyā 
mitrapravṛttiḥ | yadyevamabhaviṣyat, tat tatra ātmajñānamarjunasya 
upadiśya tasmādyudhyasva iti upasaṃhāre na abhyadhāsyata | tasmāt 
karmayoginastatra tadvarṇāśramakramāgatāṃ tāṃ tāṃ śāstracoditāṃ 
kriyāmanatikramata eva sarvabhāvasāmyabhāvanā atra vivakṣitā || 9 ||

	ata eva āha �

ihaiva tairjitaḥ sargo yeṣāṃ sāmye sthitaṃ manaḥ |
nirdoṣaṃ hi samaṃ brahma tasmādbrahmaṇi te sthitāḥ || 10 ||

p. 141) ihaiva asminnevaṃvidhakriyākāriṇi śarīre vartamānaiḥ taiḥ sargaḥ 
punarbhavo jitaḥ adhaḥkṛtaḥ, yeṣāṃ yathāpratipādite sāmye 
sarvabhāvābhede manaḥ cittaṃ sthitaṃ labdhapratiṣṭham | brahma ekaṃ hi 
vaiṣamyakāraṇabhūtarāgādirahitatvāt nirdoṣam atastadeva samaṃ 
sarvathā sarvadā sarvatra viśuddhasaṃvinmātraikasvarūpam | tasmāt ye 
sāmye sthitāḥ te brahmaṇi eva sthitāḥ iti uktam || 10 ||

	yathoktasāmyānatikramasamāhitasya cittavṛttinirodhalakṣaṇamapi 
yogamitarakriyāvadahorātramdhye vyavasthitābhyāsakālaṃ 
viśiṣṭetikartavyatānvitamapi karmayoginaḥ ślokaṣaṭkena 
abhyanujānannāha �

yogī yuñjīta satatamātmānaṃ rahasi sthitaḥ | 
ekākī yatacittātmā nirāśīraparigrahaḥ || 11 ||

śucau deśe pratiṣṭhāpya sthiramāsanamātmanaḥ |
nātyucchritaṃ nātinīcaṃ cailājinakuśottaram || 12 ||

tatraikāgraṃ manaḥ kṛtvā yatacittendriyakriyaḥ |
upaviśyāsane yuñjyādyogamātmaviśuddhaye || 13 ||

samaṃ kāyaśirogrīvaṃ dhārayannacalaḥ sthiraḥ | 
saṃpaśyan nāsikāgraṃ svaṃ diśaścānavalokayan || 14 ||

praśāntātmā vigatabhīrbrahmacārivrate sthitaḥ |
manaḥ saṃyamya maccitto yukta āsīta matparaḥ || 15 ||

yuñjannevaṃ sadātmānaṃ madbhakto'nanyamānasaḥ |
śāntiṃ nirvāṇaparamāṃ matsaṃsthāmadhigacchati || 16 ||

	krameṇa etat vyākhyāyate | yogī 
prabandhapratipāditapratipādayiṣyamāṇasvarūpeṇa 
prastutavyākhyānayogāntaravilakṣaṇaparikarmaṇārva 
kāladeśāvirodhinā samādhiviśeṣanirapekṣeṇa 
samastāntarbahiṣkaraṇaprāṇavṛttiṣu 

p. 142) api aniruddhaprasareṇa karmayogena yogavān śāstravihitatvāt 
ātmānam antaḥkaraṇaṃ jīvātmānaṃ vā satataṃ pratyahaṃ 
vyavasthāpitaniyatakālānatikrameṇa rahasi aikāgryabādhakarahite deśe 
sthitaḥ yata ekākī kevalaḥ yatacittātmā yataṃ vinigṛhītaṃ cittaṃ mano 
yena tādṛśa ātmā kṣetrajño yasya, sa tathāvidhaḥ san paramātmani 
samādadhīta cittavṛttinirodhalakṣaṇamapi yogamanutiṣṭhedityarthaḥ; kintu 
irāśīḥ āśiṣaḥ phalāśaṃsāto niṣkrānto mokṣalakṣaṇo'pi phale 
prārthanā dainyarahitaḥ, tathā aparigrahaḥ sarvatra 
mamatvābhimānarahitaḥ, tathā maccittatvādivakṣyamāṇadharmayukto 
yuñjīta ityanena karmayogasamādhānāpramattena ayaṃ yogo'bhyasanīya 
iti uktam | asya idānīṃ parikarma abhidhīyate | kiṃ kṛtvā yuñjyāt | 
ātmanaḥ śucau deśe yatyādyapekṣayā puṇyasaritparisaragiriguhādau, 
gṛhasthāpekṣayā sūpaliptaviviktasadanādau viśuddhe sthāne āsanaṃ 
samādhiviṣṭaraṃ pratiṣṭhāpya sthitiṃ prāpayya | kīdṛśam | sthiraṃ 
niścalaṃ tathā sukhitvārthamucchrāyanimnatvātiśayarahitaṃ, tathā 
cailaṃ vastraṃ, ajinaṃ mṛgādicarma, kuśā darbhāḥ, taduttaraṃ 
tatpradhānam | tena gṛhasthasya cailājinottaram, yatyādeḥ kuśottaram, evaṃ 
tatra sthirasukhe āsana upaviśya ekāgraṃ dhyeyatvena 
parigṛhītaikavastuniṣṭhaṃ manaḥ cittaṃ kṛtvā ata eva yatacittendriyakriyaḥ 
yatā niṣiddhabahiṣprasarāścittendriyāṇāṃ manaso'kṣāṇāṃ ca 
kriyāḥ saṃkalpavikalpaśabdādigrahaṇarūpāṇi karmāṇi yena, 
tathāvidhaḥ san; yogaṃ cittavṛttinirodhātmakaṃ yuñjyāt samādadhīta | 
kimartham | ātmaviśuddhaye ātmano'ntaḥkaraṇasya 
bāhyoparāgalakṣaṇamalakṣālanāya kṣetrajñasya vā 
dehādyahaṃpratyayarūpakalaṅkaparimārjanāya | evaṃ 
cittasamādhānamabhidhāya 

p. 143) tatsādhanabhūtaṃ kāyasaṃsthānamāha | kāyaḥ kaṇṭhādadhaḥ 
śarīraṃ śiro mūrdhā grīvā kandharā kāyaśirogrīvaṃ tat samam 
avaktramṛju dhārayan bibhrat, tathārūpo'pi acalaḥ 
niṣprakampo'ṅgamejayatvādicittavikṣepakāraṇarahitaḥ sthiraḥ tathā 
svaṃnāsikāgraṃ ghrāṇaprāntaṃ saṃpaśyan ata evaca diśaḥ kāṣṭhā 
anavalokayan aprekṣamāṇaḥ | dikṣu dṛgvikṣepaparihārārthaṃ 
nāsikāgrasaṃdarśanamuktam, natu tat lakṣyīkartavyamiti abhiprāyaḥ | 
itthaṃ vihitasamastasamādhiparikarasya 
paramātmasamāpattilakṣaṇamokṣaprātpihetuṃ viśeṣaṇaṃ 
pratipādayitumāha praśāntātmā praśāntaḥ 
sandehādivikalpakṣobharahitaṃ ātmā antaḥkaraṇaṃ yasya sa, tathā 
sarvātmatvamavagamya sarvataścarācrabhūtajātāt vigatabhīḥ 
nivṛttasādhvasaḥ; tathā brahmacārivrate sthitaḥ brahmacāriṇaḥ 
kāmasaṃkalpavarjanaṃ vrataṃ tasmin sthitaḥ, iti 
sarvārthaviṣayaprārthanārahitatvamanena viśeṣaṇena utkam | itthaṃ 
manaḥ sa.yamya sarvataścittaṃ nigṛhya mayyeva paramātmani ekasmin tattve 
cittaṃ saṃvedanaṃ yasya sa tathāvidho yuktaḥ samāhitaḥ san matparaḥ 
tathāvidhāt yogadananyaviṣayamanā āsīta tiṣṭhet | evam 
etadviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭaḥ sadā pratyaham ātmānam antaḥkaraṇaṃ yuñjan 
samādadhānaḥ madbhaktaḥ māmeva ātmatvena samāśrito'ta eva 
ananyamānasaḥ anyasmin viṣayāntare na yasya mānasaṃ madhukta iti 
pāṭhe tu mayyeva yukto matsamāpattimeva samādhiphalatvena pratipannaḥ 
san matsaṃsthāṃ mayi vartamānāṃ śāntiṃ 
svābhāvikasarvavikārarahitatvalakṣaṇaṃ praśamam adhigacchati svīkurute 
| kīdṛśīṃ śāntim | nirvāṇaparamāṃ nirvāṇaṃ śarīrādāvanātmani 
ātmapratyayādikṣobhaviratirūpā nirvṛtiḥ paramaṃ prakṛṣṭaphalatayā 
sthitaṃ yasyāṃ sā tathoktā tām || 16 ||

p. 144) itthamasya yogasya karmasamādhilakṣaṇamahāyogāt nirvartino 
yathoktādvaitātmajñānaniṣṭhatayā anuṣṭheyatayā abhyanujñātasya 
anyadapi parikarma pratipādayitumāha �

nātyaśnatastu yogo'sti na caikāntamanaśnataḥ |
na cātisvapnaśīlasya jāgrato naiva cārjuna || 17 ||

yuktāhāravihārasya yuktaceṣṭasya karmasu |
yuktasvapnāvabodhasya yogo bhavati duḥkhahā || 18 ||

	eṣa yathokto yogaḥ evaṃvidhasya anuṣṭhātuḥ naiva asti naiva 
saṃbhavati | kīdṛśasya | atyarthamaśnataḥ sauhityaparyantaṃ 
bhuñjānasya, tathā ekāntaṃ sarvathānaśnataḥ api nāsti tathā 
atisvapnaśīlasya nityaṃ nidrāratasya, tathaiva sadā jāgrataḥ unnidrasya api | 
atyaśanāt hi kāyagauravaśūlābhiṣyandādayo doṣāścittamakarmaṇyaṃ 
saṃpādayanti, atyantānaśanācca dhātukṣayādayaḥ 
śaktihānimāvahanti, atisvapnādālasyādaya 
udyogabhaṅgamatyantanidrāpahārādajīrṇādayo 
daurmanasyādikamāpādayanti || ato yktāhāravihārasya yukto 
hitamātrakālāvacchinnatvādupapanna āhāro bhojanaṃ 
vihāraścaṅkramaṇādiryasya tādṛśasya, tathā karmasu 
dṛṣṭādṛṣṭaviṣayeṣu vyāpāreṣu yuktā 
cittaśarīrānavasāditvaparyavasāyinī ceṣṭā manaḥkāyavyāpāro yasya 
tasya tādṛśasya, tathā yuktau yathākālabhāvitvāt nyāyyau svapnabodhau 
nidrājāgarau yasya, tasya tādṛśasya yogina eṣa yogo duḥkhahā bhavati 
abhyasyamānaḥ kleśaṃ hanti sukhena saṃpadyate ityarthaḥ || 18 ||

	evamāhāravihāraniyamamuktvā siddhilakṣaṇaṃ yogamāha �

yadā viniyataṃ cittamātmanyevāvatiṣṭhate |
niḥspṛhaḥ sarvakāmebhyo yukta ityucyate tadā || 19 ||

	yadā, asya yoginaḥ samādhyabhyāsakāṣṭhāṃ prāptasya cittaṃ 
viniyataṃ 

p. 145) nigṛhītaṃ sat samprajñātasamādhibhūmikā 
vitarkavicārānandāsmitānugatā atikrāntaṃ sat sthūlasūkṣmeṣu 
dhyeyeṣu labdhasaṃyamatvāt tāni tyaktvā ekasmin dhyeye ātmanyeva 
svabhāva eva avatiṣṭhate, avasthitiṃ pratiṣṭhāṃ saṃyamaṃ labhate, tadā 
tasyāṃ daśāyāmātmaikārāmatvāt sarvebhyo vyatiriktebhyo bhogebhyaḥ 
spṛhārahito nirabhikāṅkṣaḥ sannasau yuktaḥ sampannanirbījasamādhiḥ iti 
ucyate || 19 ||

	tasya ātmadhyānaniṣṭhasya yogicittasyaiva yogaśabdavācyasya 
upamānamāha �

yathā dīpo nivātastho neṅgate sopamā smṛtā |
yogino yatacittasya yuñjato yogamātmani || 20 ||

	ātmani riruttare dhyeye yogaṃ samādhiṃ yuñjānasya yoginaḥ 
sarvataḥ pratyāhṛtatvāt yataṃ nigṛhītaṃ cittaṃ yena tādṛśasya, sā 
upamā tadupamānaṃ tattvajñaiḥ smṛtā | yathā dīpaḥ pradyotako 
nivṛttavātadeśe sthitaḥ san neṅgate na prakampate, tathaiva ātmālambane 
samādhau sthitaṃ yogicittaṃ na prakampate iti 
upameyavākyamadhyāhāryam | anena dīpopamānena 
pratyāsannaprabalavipakṣatvāt yatnasaṃrakṣaṇīyatvaṃ yogicittasya 
pratipāditam || 20 ||

	athāsya parasya yogasya lakṣaṇamāha �

yatroparamate cittaṃ niruddhaṃ yogasevayā |
yatra caivātmanātmānaṃ paśyannātmani tuṣyati || 21 ||

sukhamātyantikaṃ yatra buddhigrahyamatīndriyam |
vetti yatra na caivāyaṃ sthitaścyavati tattvataḥ || 22 ||

yaṃ labdhvā cāparaṃ lābhaṃ manyate nādhikaṃ tataḥ |
yasmin sthito na duḥkhena guruṇāpi vicālyate || 23 ||

p. 146) taṃ vidyād duḥkhasaṃyogaviyogaṃ yogasaṃjñitam |
sa niścayena yoktavyo yogo'nirviṇṇacetasā || 24 ||

taṃ yogasaṃjñitaṃ samādhyākhyamarthaṃ vidyāt jānīyāt | 
kaṃ tam | yatra yasmin niruddhaṃ nivṛttabahiṣpracāratayā niyamitaṃ yat 
cittaṃ yogasevayā yogasya samādheḥ prāpyasya anyasya sevayā 
sevanādāśrayaṇādupalabdhaparamopeyatvāt tatraiva viśrāntaṃ sat 
yogāntaraviṣayaṃ prayatnamutsṛjati, tasyaiva yogasya niruttaratvāt | ata eva 
tasyāmavasthāyāṃ yogī yatra ātmanaivātmānaṃ paśyan 
svasaṃvedanena svarūpamanubhavan ātmani svabhāve eva 
adhikaraṇabhūte tuṣyati parāṃ prītiṃ pratipadyate, taṃ yogaṃ vidyāditi 
sambandhaḥ | tadānīṃ hi nivṛttasakalabāhyoparāgaṃ 
cittamātmasamāpannaṃ sat śuddhasaṃvedanalakṣaṇataddharmatvena 
avatiṣṭhate | tathā yatra ātyantikam anantaṃ 
nirupamasvarūpānubhavacamatkāramayaṃ sukhaṃ sahaja 
ānando'bhivyajyate | kīdṛśaṃ sukham | atīndriyaṃ śrotrādīni 
bāhyendriyāṇi atītamatikrāntam, ata eva buddhigrāhyaṃ buddhyā 
tātkālikyā bodhalakṣaṇayā, ātmanaḥ svaśaktyaiva grāhyaṃ saṃvedyam 
ayaṃ yogī vetti jānāti, taṃ yogaṃ vidyāt | tathā yatra sthitaḥ yasmin 
labdhaviśrāntirayaṃ yogī tattvataḥ paramātmarūpāt paramārthāt na 
cyavate na viyujyate | tathā yaṃ yogamāsādya tato'dhikaṃ 
tasmādutkṛṣṭam aparam anyaṃ lābham iṣṭaprāptiṃ yogī na manyate na 
buddhyate | ata eva prāguktaṃ yatra sthitastattvato na cyavata iti | ata eva 
yasmin sthitaḥ saṃrūḍho nivṛttetaravyāpṛtiḥ san yogī guruṇāpi 
prabalenāpi kenacin mānasena śārīreṇa vā duḥkhena na vicālyate na tato 
vyutthāpyate, taṃ yogasaṃjñitamarthaṃ vidyāt | kīdṛśam | 
duḥkhasaṃyogaviyogaṃ duḥkhaṃ viṣayeṣu indriyapravṛttijanito 

p. 147) rājasaḥ kleśaḥ, tena saṃyogaḥ saṃsāridaśāyāṃ sambandhaḥ, 
tasmāt viyogo virahastaṃ, heyavastuprārthanājanmanā duḥkhena 
sambandhābhāva eva viyoga ityarthaḥ | sa evaṃlakṣaṇo yogaḥ paraḥ 
samādhirnaruttarānandalābhalakṣaṇamahābalahetuḥ niścayena 
niḥsandehatvena anirviṇṇacetasā anudvignamanasā yoktavyaḥ 
samādhātavyaḥ | alabdhabhūmikasya hi yoginaḥ prathamaṃ sandehaḥ, 
ārurukṣataśca abhyāsadaśāyāṃ kleśaḥ sambhāvyate iti niścayena 
anirviṇṇacetasā sa yoktavya iti uktam || 21-24 ||

	evaṃ bāhyaviṣayaparihārakrameṇa cittavṛttinirodhalakṣaṇāṃ

paramātmani 

p. 148) ahaṃkārāvaṣṭambhalakṣaṇaṃ dhairyaṃ tayā gṛhītā svīkṛtā 
yā buddhistayā tādṛśyā | sāttvikī hi dhṛtiryāṃ buddhiṃ dhārayati, sā 
atra yoge sādhanatvamarhati, yasyā dhṛterlakṣaṇaṃ vakṣyati �

dhṛtyā yayā dhārayate manaḥ prāṇendriyakriyāḥ |

	yogenāvyabhicāriṇyā dhṛtiḥ sā pārtha sāttvikī || (18|33)

	iti | pūrvaṃ kiṃ kṛtvā uparamet | manasaiva pareṇa 
mokṣaviṣayeṇaiva cetasā prāk sthāvarasaṃjñayā uktena 
saṃkalpaprabhavān viṣayagrahaṇavyagrajaṅgamamanaḥsamutthān sarvān 
dṛṣṭānuśravikabhedena samastān tathā 
pratyekamaśeṣānavidyamānasaṃskāramātrān kāmān bhogān tyaktvā 
parihṛtya tathā tenaiva manasā, natu viṣayaviṣayeṇa indriyagrāmaṃ 
karaṇavargaṃ samantataḥ sarvebhyo vihitebhyo'pi śabdādibhyo viniyamya 
rāgādidoṣaparihārātmanā viśeṣeṇa vigṛhya, ityanena 
prastutasamuccayānuṣṭhānasādhyakarmayogaviṣayaṃ kāmatyāgasya 
indriyaniyamasya ca pūrvoktaṃ kramameva smārayati | evamanena krameṇa 
ātmasaṃsthaṃ mukhye ātmani brahmāparanāmni samyak sthitaṃ 
labdhaviśrānti manaḥ antaḥkaraṇaṃ kṛtvā sampādya tadvatiriktaṃ 
vstvantaraṃ na kiñcida dhyāyet | tadānīṃ hi sarvaṃ brahmarūpatayaiva 
prakāśate | evaṃ vidhena abhyāsakrameṇa yato yato yasmāt yasmāt 
kutaścidbandhanabhūtāt ābhyantarād bāhyācca manaḥ ceto niścarati 
nirgacchati, tatastataḥ tasmāt tasmāt niyamya pratiṣidhya ātmani 
paramakāraṇa eva vaśaṃ nayet tatraiva nibadhnīyāt | yataḥ svabhāvata etat 
cañcalaṃ capalaṃ tathā prārthite'pi viṣaye na ciramavatiṣṭhate iti asthiram 
yasmāt tasminnātmavaśaṃ nīte sati enaṃ yoginaṃ praśāntamanasaṃ 
viṣayasukhaunmukhyakṣobhaviratinirvṛtacetasaṃ santaṃ svayameva uttamaṃ 
nityaniravadyasvabhāvatvādutkṛṣṭaṃ sukhaṃ sahaja ānandaḥ upaiti 

p. 149) upagacchati | kīdṛśaṃ yoginam | śāntarajasaṃ śāntaṃ vilīnaṃ 
rajo viṣayapravṛttiheturguṇo yasya, ata eva brahmabhūtaṃ 
paramātmasamāpannaṃ yasmāt akalmaṣam 
avidyamānājñānalakṣaṇakilviṣakalaṅkam | evamanena yathoktena 
krameṇa pūrvameva prabandhapratipāditena niyatamānasaḥ 
samprayatāntaḥkaraṇo yogī sadā sarvāsu avasthāsu paśyan śṛṇvan (5|8) 
ityādinā pratipāditāsu ātmānaṃ yuñjan samādadhānaḥ sukhena 
āsanaprāṇāyāmapratyāhāradhyānadhāraṇādikleśābhāvādanāyāse
na brahmaṇā paramātmanā saṃyogaṃ abhedam atyantaṃ nityameva 
adhigacchati prāpnoti || 25-29 ||

	itthaṃ siddhaniruttarasamādheḥ karmayogino lokottaraṃ vyavahāraṃ 
pratipādayitumāha �

sarvabhūtasthamātmānaṃ sarvabhūtāni cātmani |
īkṣate yogayuktātmā sarvatra samadarśanaḥ || 30 ||

yo māṃ paśyati sarvatra sarvaṃ ca mayi paśyati |
tasyāhaṃ na praṇaśyāmi sa ca me na praṇaśyati || 31 ||

sarvabhūtasthitaṃ yo māṃ bhajatyekatvamāsthitaḥ |
sarvathā vartamāno'pi sa yogī mayi vartate || 32 ||

ātmaupamyena sarvatra samaṃ paśyati yor'juna |
sukhaṃ vā yadi vā duḥkhaṃ sa yogī paramo mataḥ || 33 ||

	yathokte'smin karmayoge yuktātmā samāhitaḥ sarvatra sarvadā 
sarvathā avahitacitto yogī ātmānam advayacinmātraikalakṣaṇatayā 
uktasvarūpaṃ sarvabhūteṣu parasparaṃ bhinnatvena avasthiteṣu carācareṣu 
prāṇiṣu sthitaṃ paśyati, yadātmanaḥ sāmānyacinmātraṃ rūpamekaṃ 
tadātmakānyeva bhūtāni prekṣate ityarthaḥ | tathoktasvarūpe svātmani 
sarvabhūtāni vigalitānyonyabhedāni paśyati, ityātmanaḥ 
sarvabhūtānāṃ 

p. 150) cābhedadarśanaṃ pratipāditameva | 
abhedadarśanapratipādanaparametat | yathā caitat sambhavati tathā 
pratipāditameva prāk, tathāpi smṛtyarthaṃ punarucyate | ātmā nāmāyaṃ 
sukhaduḥkhagrāhyagrāhakamūḍhatvaśūnyatvādisakalavikalpavirahaviśu
ddhasamvitprakāśaikalakṣaṇaḥ, tasya saṃveditṛtvaṃ nāmāvyabhicārī 
guṇaḥ | tena yadyat saṃvetti tattat saṃvedyamānameva tena tena rūpeṇa 
ātmānaṃ labhate | saṃvedyamānatā ca nirvikalpāvasthāpūrvikā bhavati 
| tena yatakiñcit yadā yadā yatra yena yena pramātrā saṃvedyate, 
tattadadvayānantacinmātrasvarūpamiti ātmanaḥ saṃvediturvedyānāṃ ca 
sarvabhūtānāṃ nāsti vāstavaḥ kaścidbhedaḥ, 
devalamacintyaparameśvarmāyodbhāvito vedyavedakavikalpaḥ 
sarvamidamanyonyaṃ vibhinnatayā avabhāsayati | sa ca 
parameśvaraprasādādunmiṣitayā vidyākhyayā divyayā dṛśā 
tattvavidbhistādṛśo vibhāvyamāno yadā vigalati, tadā 
bhinnasetusāgarasalilavat sarvamidamātmatayā aikyenāvabhāsate | 
jñānakarmasamuccayānuṣṭhānātmani cāsmin mahāyoge 
pratyuditavidyāśaktirevādhikṛtā iti | karmayogī sarvatra sthāvare jaṅgame 
ca bhūtagrāme samadarśanaḥ advaitātmatattvaikadarśī ityuktaḥ | yaśca 
ātmā so'hamevaṃ ityabhedaṃ bhagavān pratipādayitumāha- yo yogī 
sarvatra bhāvajāte māṃ yathoktātmatattvaikarūpamuktayā upapattyā 
paśyati mayi ca sarvaṃ tathaiva paśyati tasya tattvadarśinaḥ ahaṃ na 
praṇaśyāmi nādṛśyo bhavāmi, so'pi me svarūpabhūtatvāt na 
praṇaśyati nādṛśyatvamāpadyate, iti karmayogasiddhasya yoginaḥ 
svātmanaśca abhedapratipādanaparametad bhagavadvacanam | kiñca yo yogī 
sarvatra sarvasmin dehaprabhṛti sukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ vā anyaṃ vā 
kaṃcitprāṇidharmamuktayā 

p. 151) upapattyā saṃvedyamānasya saṃvidātmakatvāpattirūpatayā 
samaṃ saṃvinmātraikasvabhāvaṃ paśyati budhyate | katham | 
ātmaupamyena ātmānamupamānīkṛtya svānubhavavadityarthaḥ | sa 
sarvathā sarveṇa kenacit sādhāraṇena prakāreṇa vartamāno'pi mayi 
vartate madabhinno'hamevāsāvityarthaḥ | sa evaṃvidho yogī sarvāsu 
avasthāsu aviliptaniruttarasamādhiphalāsvādo 
durvāravighnavṛndānubandhaduḥsādhayogāṅgānuṣṭhānaniṣṭhopalabhy
asamādhiphalapūrvoktayogyapekṣayā paramo niruttarātiśayayuktaḥ 
īśvaravat sarvasāmyadaśādhirūḍhatvāt jīvanmukta ityarthaḥ || 30-33 ||

	atha samprati ananubhūtatvādasya sarvasāmyapratipattiphalasya 
samādherasambhāvyatāṃ manyamāno'rjuna āha �

yo'yaṃ yogastvayā proktaḥ sāmyena madhusūdana |

tasyāhaṃ nigrahaṃ manye vāyoriva suduṣkaram || 35 ||

	he kṛṣna yo'yaṃ samanantaraṃ 
karmayogalakṣaṇo'nyayogavilakṣaṇo yogastvayā proktaḥ etasya parāṃ 
sthitiṃ prakṛṣṭāṃ pratiṣṭhāṃ na paśyāmi na tarkayāmi cañcalatvāt 
anavasthitatvāt | yasmāt tatsādhanaṃ manaścañcalaṃ prakṛticapalamataḥ 
pramāthi pramathanaśīlaṃ, yataḥ kṛcchralabdhāmapi 
bhūmikāmunmārgapravaṇatvādāśu vināśayati | na ca tadvyāvartayituṃ 
śakyaṃ yato balavat asya viṣayān pratyabhidhāvataḥ

evaṃvidhasyāsya manaso nigraham utpathāt pratiṣedhanaṃ sutarāṃ 

p. 152) duṣkaraṃ dussādhaṃ sambhāvayāmi, kasyeva vāyoriva yathā

p. 153) atyantaduḥsādhatvādātmanaśca saṃsāridharmānuparamād 
yogasya duravāpatvaṃ manyamānaḥ, śraddhāmātrasya ekasya kāraṇasya 
yogaṃ prati upāyatvaṃ syānna vā iti saṃśayāno'rjuna uvāca �

ayataḥ śraddhayopeto yogāccalitamānasaḥ |

aprāpya yogasaṃsiddhiṃ kāṃ gatiṃ kṛṣṇa gacchati || 39 ||

	āptopadiṣṭādupāyāt niḥsaṃśayatayā phalasya sambhāvanā

satpuruṣāṇāmuttamaphalārthināṃ puṃsāṃ mārga panthānaṃ

mūḍhaḥ samyagjñānarahitaḥ | ataścopāyāniścayāt anekacittaḥ 
vikalpākulatvāt pratikṣaṇaṃ cañcalatvād bahuvidhamanovṛttirata eva 
vibhrāntaḥ na kvacillabdhasthitiḥ, yato mohasyaiva ajñanasya vaśaṃ gataḥ 
vidheyatāṃ prāptaḥ syāt | tadeva doṣasāmagryā yogasaṃsiddhiṃ 
samādhilābhamaprāptaḥ san kāṃ gatiṃ gacchati tasyaivaṃvidhasya

p. 154) kaccit iti abhīṣṭapraśne | api nāma evaṃ śraddadhānaḥ puruṣaḥ

na hi kalyāṇakṛt kaścid durgatiṃ tāta gacchati || 42 ||

	tasya evaṃvidhasya

svakāryaṃ nirvartayati || 42 ||

p. 155) yata evamataḥ �

prāpya puṇyakṛtāṃ lokānuṣitvā śāśvatīḥ samāḥ |
śucīnāṃ śrīmatāṃ gehe yogabhraṣṭo'bhijāyate || 43 ||

athavā yogināmeva jāyate śrīmatāṃ kule |
etaddhi durlabhataraṃ loke janma yadīdṛśam || 44 ||

tatra taṃ buddhisaṃyogaṃ labhate paurvadaihikam |
tato bhūyo'pi yatate saṃsiddhau kurunandana || 45 ||

pūrvābhyāsena tenaiva hriyate hyavaśo'pi san |
jijñāsurapi yogasya śabdabrahmātivartate || 46 ||

prayatnād yatamānastu yogī saṃśuddhakilviṣaḥ |
anekajanmasaṃsiddhastato yāti parāṃ gatim || 47 ||

	yogabhraṣṭaḥ yogādyathoktajñānaniṣṭhatayā 
śāstravihitasvakarmānuṣṭhānalakṣaṇāt samādheḥ bhraṣṭaḥ 
parameśvarasamāpattilakṣaṇaṃ phalamaprāpyaiva vicyutaḥ san 
śraddhābīje satyapi īśvarecchāvaśādeva samprati tīvrasaṃvegābhāvād 
yatnādisahakārisāmagrīsākalyāddhikāryā(lā)ntarāpekṣiphalaḥ san

prāpya āsādya tatra ca tatkarmānurūpāḥ śāśvatīḥ samāḥ 
sahasrasaṃkhyāni varṣāṇi uṣitvā nivāsamnubhūya 
tatastādṛśakarmaphalopabhogānte karmabhūmau asyāṃ śucīnāṃ 
śrīmatāṃ śuddhābhijanakarmaṇāṃ lakṣmīvatāṃ kule anvaye 
abhijāyate janma labhate | yena yogābhivāñchāleśakaluṣitacittatvāt 
asamyaganuṣṭhitāt karmayogād bhraṃśaṃ prāptastasya eṣā 
gatirnirdiṣṭā | yasya tu apavargapravaṇatveṣpi sati kutaścit pratibandhāt 
karmayogaḥ samyak na nirvṛttaḥ, sa imāṃ gatimāpnoti iti 
pakṣāntaropanāsena 

p. 156) āha � athavā yogabhraṣṭo yogināṃ 
yathoktakarmasamādhiniṣṭhānāmata eva dhīmatāṃ

samutpadyate, īdṛśametat evaṃvidhānāṃ yogināṃ kule janma

	evaṃvidhasya karmayoginaḥ praśaṃsārthamāha �

p. 157) tapasvibhyo'dhiko yogī jñānibhyo'pi mato'dhikaḥ |
karmibhyaścādhiko yogī tasmādyogī bhavārjuna || 48 ||

	sutīvrasyāpi tapomātrasya antavatphalatvāt tapasvibhyaḥ yogī 
nityaniruttarānandamayasamādhimān adhikaḥ prakṛṣṭaḥ, jñānibhyo'pi 
kevalajñānamātraśaraṇebhyaḥ, tathā karmibhyaḥ 
yajñādikevalakarmavadbhyaḥ adhikaḥ tasmāt tvaṃ yathopadiṣṭaṃ yogaṃ 
svīkṛtya sarvotkṛṣṭo yogī bhava || 48 ||

	kecana yogino darśanabhedāt sambhavanti, teṣāmapi ayamevaṃvidho 
yogī prakṛṣṭatama iti pratipādayannadhyāyamupasaṃharati �

yogināmapi sarveṣāṃ madgatenāntarātmanā |
śraddhāvān bhajate yo māṃ sa me yuktatamo mataḥ || 49 ||

	samastānāṃ yogināṃ madhye sa yogī yuktatamaḥ atiśayena 
yuktaḥ samāhito mama mataḥ, ko'sau yaḥ śraddhāvān 
sarvasiddhibhūtayā śraddhayā yuktaḥ san madgatena antarātmanā, yathā 
pratipāditajñānagatyā māṃ paramātmānamīśvaramekaṃ tattvaṃ 
paramopeyatayā gatena prapannena antarātmanā antaḥkaraṇena bhajate 
vāṅmanaḥkāyavyāpārān matparāneva kurvan madbhakto bhavati,

upāste, sa eva samāhitatama iti || 49 ||

yātā yasyāṅgabhāvaṃ dadhati yadi paraṃ yogatāmanyayogā-
staṃ yogaṃ jñānakarmavyatikarasukarābhyāsavairāgyagamyam |

ṣaṣṭho'dhyāyo'yamāryairavahitamatibhistyaktasarvānyakāryaiḥ ||

atha saptamo'dhyāyaḥ |

	evaṃ yadaṅgabhāvamāpannānāṃ yogāntarāṇāṃ

eva ityātmana eva jagatkāraṇatvādi prāksūcitaprāyaṃ jñeyāntaraṃ 
sphuṭatayā pratipādayituṃ bhagavānuvāca �

mayyāsaktamanāḥ pārtha yogaṃ yuñjan madāśritaḥ |
asaṃśayaṃ samagraṃ māṃ yathā jñāsyasi tacchṛṇu || 1 ||

	ṣaṣṭhādhyāyaparisamāptau madgatenāntarātmanā yo māṃ 
bhajate sa yuktatamaḥ iti yaduktaṃ, tadeva mayyāsaktamanā madāśritaśca 
ityanena paddvayena iha anūditam, tataśca matsvarūpaṃ jñātvā māṃ 
bhajaṃstvaṃ yathā yena prakāreṇa sandeharahitaṃ kṛtvā samagraṃ 
saṃpūrṇasvarūpaṃ māṃ jñāsyasi pratipatsyase, tadidānīṃ śṛṇu | 
ayaṃ hi jagatprapañcaḥ svataḥsiddhanityapradhānapariṇāmatayā kaiścit 
pratipannaḥ, anyaiḥ paramāṇupākaprabhavatvena, itaraiḥ 
kṣaṇikajñānasantānabhramajanyatayā, paraiśca anyathā ceti, kimatra

vakṣyamāṇamavadhāraya, tadavadhāraṇādeva ca samagraṃ 
matsvarūpajñānaṃ bhaviṣyati iti || 1 ||

kimucyate ityatra uttaram � adhibhūtaṃ kṣaro bhāvaḥ iti | kṣaraḥ 
pariṇamadrūpatayā svarūpasaṃcalanaparo yo bhāvaḥ pratipuruṣaṃ 
vibhaktakāryakāraṇakalāpātmakaḥ 

p. 178) padārthaḥ, so'dhibhūtamucyate tasya bhūtādhīnatvāt | 
bhūtarūpā prakṛtiḥ kāryakāraṇātmanā vipariṇamate iti kṣaro 
bhāvo'dhibhūtamiti caturthapraśnasya uttaram | atha adhidaivaṃ kimucyate 
ityatra uttaram puruṣo'dhidaivatam iti | puruṣaḥ kṣetrajña iti 
jātāvekavacanam | ataste te puruṣāstattadvāsanānuguṇyena 
pariṇatapravibhaktatattatkāryakāraṇātmakakṣarabhāvarūpe'rthe-
'dhiṣṭhātṛtayā avatiṣṭhamānā adhidaivatatvaṃ bhajante, iti 
puruṣo'dhidaivatamiti pañcamapraśnasya uttaram | atha atra dehe kathaṃ 
kena prakāreṇa kaścādhiyajñaḥ ityatra uttaramāha adhiyajño'hamevātra 
iti | parasparavibhaktakartrabhimānāviṣṭanānākṣetrajñādhiṣṭhite'smin 
dehe yadyapi kṣetrajñā eva adhiyajñatvamarhanti tattatphalābhisandhinā 
taṃ taṃ yāgavidhimanutiṣṭhantastādṛśamavacchinnamātmānaṃ 
kartṛtvena abhimayante, tathāpi  pāramārthikasya kartṛtvasya 
ananyasādhāraṇatvādahameva mukhyaḥ kartā sarvayajñānāmiti ahameva 
adhiyajño, na anyaḥ | kathamiti prakārapraśne'pi etadeva uttaram ahameva | 
ahamityanenaiva prakāreṇa ahamadhiyajñaḥ | idamatra tātparyam yo'pi 
kṣetrajñānāṃ śarīrādyālambananimittāhaṃkāraḥ, yo'pi 
parasvarūpaniṣṭho mama, tayorahaṃkāratve nāsti kaścit bhedaḥ, kevalaṃ 
māyāvaśāt kṣetrajñānāmavacchedyālambananiṣṭhatvādavacchinna iva 
asau, mama tu svabhāvaniṣṭhatvādanavacchinnaḥ iti ahameva, na anyo 
mukhyāhaṃkāraniṣṭhatayā adhiyajño, na prakārāntareṇeti 
sarvayajñeṣu adhikṛtaḥ prabhurahameva | yadvakṣyati 

ahaṃ hi sarvayajñānāṃ bhoktā ca prabhurevaca | (9|25) 

p. 179) iti ṣaṣṭhasya praśnasya uttaram | anenaiva nyāyena 
adhibhūtamahameveti boddhavyam, bhūtabhāvena paramārthato'hameva 
sthita ityarthaḥ | tathaiva adhidaivatatvena puruṣākhyo yaḥ prakāra uktaḥ, 
tatra ahameva adhikṛta iti praśnaṣaṭkasya saṃkṣepato vyākaraṇamevaṃ 
veditavyam | yaduta brahmātmakarmasaṃjñaṃ 
paramātmakṣetrajñaprakṛtipariṇāmarūpaṃ saptame tattvatrayaṃ 
nirṇītam, tatra yathākramamadhiyajñādhidaivatādhibhūtabhāvena 
ahameva tattvato vyavasthito, na madvyatiriktaṃ kiṃcit saṃbhavatīti 
tātparyam | tathā 

te brahma tadviduḥ kṛtsnam ----------------- | (7/29)

ityādinā tattvatrayametaduktvā 

sādhibhūtādhidaivaṃ māṃ sādhiyajñaṃ ca te viduḥ | (7/30)

	iti tadvyāpakatvena ātmānaṃ bhagavān nirdiṣṭavān | atha 
prayāṇakāle dehāt dehāntarasaṃkramaṇasamaye 
niyatātmabhirnigṛhītāntaḥkaraṇaiḥ kathaṃ jñeyo'sītyatra uttaraṃ 
antakāle'pi māmeva ityādi | antakāle'pi tattatkarmakṣayāt tasya tasya 
śarīrasya prakṣayasamaye māṃ yathoktaviśeṣaṇaṃ paramātmānaṃ 
smṛtvā saṃcintya matsvarūpapratyavamarśapūrvaṃ kalevaraṃ śarīraṃ 
tyaktvā svātantryeṇa vimucya yaḥ prayāti tato dehādutkrāmati, sa 
madbhāvaṃ madīyāṃ sattāṃ yāti ahameva saṃpadyate iti | atra na 
saṃśayaḥ sandeho nāsti || 3-5 ||

tathā yuktiṃ pradarśayitumāha �

yaṃ yaṃ vāpi smaran bhāvaṃ tyajatyante kalevaram |
taṃ tamevaiti kaunteya sadā tadbhāvabhāvitaḥ || 6 ||

	yaṃ yaṃ sāttvikaṃ rājasaṃ tāmasaṃ vā bhāvaṃ padārthaṃ 
smaran sāttvikyā rājasyā tāmasyā vā buddhyā dhyāyan puruṣaḥ ante 
avasāne dehaṃ jahāti, taṃ tameva dehāntaraṃ prapadyate | atra 
hetugarbhaṃ

p. 180) viśeṣaṇamāha sadā tadbhāvabhāvitaḥ sadā nityakālaṃ tasmin 
tādṛśi sāttvikādau bhāve bhāvitaḥ saṃjātabhāvano yataḥ | kimuktaṃ 
bhavati | vartamāne dehe yadeva sāttvikādikaṃ vastu puruṣo'bhyāsena 
bhāvayati, tat tadbhāvanābhyāsaprakarṣāhitasaṃskārastameva 
ante'vaśyaṃ smarati | yameva ca smaran tasmāt dehādutkrāmati, tameva 
samanantare dehāntare labhate, yat vakṣyati 

yadā sattve pravṛddhe tu pralayaṃ yāti dehabhṛt | 
tadottamavidāṃ lokānamalānpratipadyate ||
rajasi pralayaṃ gatvā karmasaṅgiṣu jāyate |
tathā pralīnastamasi mūḍhayoniṣu jāyate || (14/15)

iti || 6 || 

yata evaṃ �

tasmāt sarveṣu kāleṣu māmanusmara yudhya ca |
mayyarpitamanobuddhirmāmevaiṣyasyasaṃśayam || 7 ||

	tasmāt uktāt kāraṇādāptopadeśaprāptikṣaṇāt prabhṛti sarveṣu 
kāleṣu kālaśabda upalakṣaṇārthastena sarvesu deśeṣu sarveṣu ākāreṣu 
sarvāsu avasthāsu yathāpratipāditasvarūpaṃ māṃ paramātmānaṃ 
parameśvaraṃ anusmara saṃcintaya 
matsvarūpadhyānānuparataniviḍāvadhāno bhava, yuddhya ca svakarma 
anutiṣṭha ca yena mayi yathoktena prakāreṇa nityaviniveśitāntaḥkaraṇo 
niḥsaṃśayaṃ māmevaiṣyasi madabhedaṃ prāpsyasi ahameva 
bhaviṣyasītyarthaḥ || 7 ||

pratipattidārḍhyārthamenameva arthaṃ vivarītumāha �

abhyāsayogayuktena cetasānanyagāminā |
paramaṃ puruṣaṃ divyaṃ yāti pārthānucintayan || 8 ||

p. 181) 

	nityānusmaraṇayatnalakṣaṇe samādhau yuktena saṃbaddhena, ata 
eva ananyagāminā yathoktatattvavyatiriktavastvantaraviratagatinā 
antaḥkaraṇena anucintayan sadā dhyāyan saṃskārabalāt paramaṃ 
sarvebhyaḥ kṣetrajñebhyaḥ utkṛṣṭamekaṃ puruṣaṃ ātmānaṃ divyaṃ 
divi paramavyomni svabhāvamaye bhavaṃ yāti pratipadyate 
tādātmyamupaiti || 8 ||

	evamabādhitopapattyupanyāsapūrvaṃ 
yathāpratipāditānuttarajñānamātrasādhanaṃ paramutkrāntikramaṃ 
pratipādya idānīṃ prāṇajayādisādhyayogasādhanamaparaṃ taṃ 
pratipādayitumāha �

kaviṃ purāṇamanuśāsitāramaṇoraṇīyāṃsamanusmaredyaḥ |
sarvasya dhātāramacintyarūpamādityavarṇaṃ tamasaḥ parastāt || 9 ||

prayāṇakāle manasācalena bhaktyā yukto yogabalena caiva |
bhrūvormadhye prāṇamāveśya samyak sa taṃ paraṃ puruṣamupaiti 
divyam || 10 ||

	sa evaṃvidho yogī taṃ divyaṃ divi paramavyomni bhavam, ata eva 
paraṃ sarvotkṛṣṭaṃ puruṣam īśvaramātmānam upaiti prāpnoti | ko'sau | 
yo bhaktyā bhagavadanugrahadivasālokaprabhātasaṃdhyayā 
sakalapuruṣārthasiddhibījabhūtayā tathā 
prastutasvarūpanirūpaṇasamādhisāmarthyena yuktaḥ san 
tasyāmavasthāyām acalena sthirasamādhānena manasā antaḥkaraṇena 
sarvasya ghātāraṃ paramakāraṇam anusmaret antakāle'nucintayet | 
kathaṃ, kīdṛśaṃ ca bhruvormadhye abhyāsasvatantrīkṛtaṃ prāṇam 
āntaraṃ mārutaṃ samyak madhyamena mārgeṇa āveśya saṃdhāya 
ādityavarṇaṃ sarvatejovyatiriktajyotīrūpam, ata eva tamasaḥ parastāt

p. 182) ajñānasya pṛthaktayā vartamānam | kīdṛśaṃ sarvasya 
ghātāramanusmaret parāvasthāyāṃ kavimityādiviśeṣaṇāviśiṣṭatvāt 
acintyaṃ pāramārthikajñātṛsvabhāvatvāt cintanakriyāyāḥ karmatvaṃ 
netumaśakyam, yataḥ purāṇaṃ kavim anādiṃ draṣṭāraṃ, tathā 
anuśāsitāraṃ sarvasya carācarasya sargasya tena tena niyamena niyantāram, 
ataḥ sarvajñaṃ sarvakartāraṃ cetyuktaṃ bhavati; tathā svarūpato'ṇuḥ 
sūkṣmaḥ kṣetrajñādiḥ, tasmādapi aṇīyāṃsam atiśayena aṇum | 
idamatra tātparyam yaḥ parāvasthāyāmadvayajñānaikaviṣayatvāt 
smaraṇavyāpārāviṣayabhūtastatsaṃyogarūpāyāmaparāvasthāyāṃ 
niyatadeśatvena niyatākāratvena ca yo yogī yathoktena krameṇa smarati, sa 
tameva puruṣamupaiti tatsamīpaṃ gacchati || 10 ||

	yogakrama eva paramapadaprāptiphalo yathā bhavati, tathā samyak 
vaktuṃ pratijānīte �

yadakṣaraṃ vedavido vadanti viśanti yadyatayo vītarāgāḥ |
yadicchanto brahmacaryaṃ caranti tatte padaṃ saṃgraheṇābhidhāsye || 11 ||

	tat padaṃ yogibhiḥ prāpyaṃ dhāma saṃgraheṇa saṃkṣepeṇa te 
tubhyam abhidhāsye kathayiṣyāmi | kiṃ tat dhāma | yad vedavidaḥ 
vedārthaviśāradāḥ akṣaraṃ nityaṃ vidvāṃso vadanti pravacanena 
pratipādayanti | yat ca yatayaḥ jñānaikaniṣṭhāḥ viśanti samāpadyante | 
yat ca icchantaḥ prāptukāmāḥ brahmacaryaṃ brahmaṇo vedasya 
adhyayanaśravaṇādinā caryamācāraṃ caranti gacchanti samāśrayante | 
kecit vidanti iti pāṭhaṃ paṭhantaḥ evametat vyācakṣate yathā 
vedavidgrahaṇena gṛhasthavānaprasthāḥ pratipāditā yataste 
śāstroktakriyānuṣṭhānaniṣṇātāḥ santastat padaṃ vidanti jānanti

p. 183) prāpyatayā adhyavasyanti, yatayastu kṛtakṛtyatvāt viśanti 
tādātmyamāpnuvanti, brahmacāriṇastu tatprāptukāmāḥ 
svādhyāyaśāstrārthabodhādiniṣṭhā vrataviśeṣaṃ bhajante iti || 11 ||

	yathāpratijñātamabhidhātumāha �

sarvadvārāṇi saṃyamya mano hṛdi nirudhya ca |
mūrdhnyādhāyātmanaḥ prāṇamāsthito yogadhāraṇām || 12 ||

omityekākṣaraṃ brahma vyāharan māmanusmaran |
yaḥ prayāti tyajan dehaṃ sa yāti paramāṃ gatim || 13 ||

	yogī paramāṃ gatiṃ pūrvoktaṃ prakṛṣṭaṃ padaṃ yāti 
pratipadyate | ko'sau | yaḥ evaṃ dehaṃ śarīraṃ tyajan vimuñcan prayāti 
utkrāmati | kīdṛśaḥ | māmanusmaran yogadhāraṇāmāsthitaḥ māṃ 
yathoktasvarūpaṃ paramakāraṇamoṃkāravācyaṃ 
brahmatattvamanudhyāyan yogasya kasyacit samādherālambanībhūte'rthe 
cittaniyamarūpāṃ dhāraṇāṃ bhūmikāmāsthita āśritaḥ | 
kathamāsthitaḥ | prathamataraṃ sarvadvārāṇi cakṣurādīni 
cittavṛttiprasaravartmāni saṃyamya yathāsvaṃ viṣayebhyaḥ pratyāhṛtya, 
manaśca upadeśādhigate hṛdi sarvasaṃvidadhikaraṇabhūte dehaikadeśe 
nirudhya pratiṣiddhendriyaviṣayapracāraṃ kṛtvā, tathā prāṇam 
antaścaraṃ mārutaṃ mūrdhni brahmarandhre 
brahmapurārgalabhūtabhrumadhyagranthibhedāt labdha (mārgaṃ santam 
ādhāya kṛtvā ), tahthā omitiprasiddhaikākṣararūpaṃ brahma 
śabdatattvaṃ vyāharan uccārayan | evaṃ prāṇasya mūrdhni preraṇaṃ, 
praṇavasya uccāraṇaṃ, praṇavavācyasya ca matsvarūpasya anusmaraṇaṃ 
ceti sarvendriyamanoniyamapūrvakaṃ yugapat

p. 184) tritayametat saṃpādayan yo dehavisargaṃ karoti, sa yogī paramaṃ 
padamāpnotīti tātparyam || 13 ||

	tasmin prayāṇakāle tātkālikāvasthāvisaṃsthulacitto yogī 
kathamatra samādhiṃ sādhayedityatra pūrvoktaṃ kāraṇamāha �

ananyacetāḥ satataṃ yo māṃ samarati nityaśaḥ | 
tasyāhaṃ sulabhaḥ pārtha nityayuktasya yoginaḥ || 14 ||

	tasya nityayuktasya avicchinnasamādheḥ yogino'haṃ nityaśaḥ 
sarvāsu avasthāsu sukhātmikāsu duḥkhātmikāsu vā sulabhaḥ suprāpaḥ | 
kasya | yaḥ satatam avyavadhānena māṃ yathoktayogābhyastarūpatayā 
ekaṃ tattvaṃ paramātmānam ananyacetāḥ anyatomadvyattiriktāt vastuno 
nivṛttāntaḥkaraṇaḥ smarati ātmatvena pratyavamṛśati |

yaṃ yaṃ bhāvaṃ smaranvāpi -------------------- | (8|6)

	ityādinā uktopapattyā 
sarvātmakamatsvarūpapratyavamarśābhyāsasudṛḍhasaṃskārasya
