tātparyam || 14 ||

	ye ca anena krameṇa māṃ prapannāste �

māmupetya punarjanma duḥkhālayamaśāśvatam |
nāpnuvanti mahātmānaḥ saṃsiddhiṃ paramāṃ gatāḥ || 15 ||

	mahātmānaḥ yathoktajñānapariśīlanabhāvāt mahān 
sarvātiśāyī parameśvara eva ātmā yeṣāṃ, te tathā, ata eva māṃ 
yathoktayā bhaktyā upetya samāśritya paramāṃ saṃsiddhim 
abhedasamāpattilakṣaṇāṃ paramāṃ phalasaṃpattimitaḥ saṃsārāt 
gatāḥ santaḥ

p. 185) punaḥ bhūyo janma dehāntaraprāptiṃ nāpnuvanti na labhante | 
kīdṛśaṃ janma | duḥkhānāmādhyātmikādīnāṃ saṃgrahāt 
triprakārāṇāṃ vistārato'nantabhedānāmālayamāspadam, tathā 
aśāśvatam avaśyamanuṣaktaśarīrendriyādisarvasvavināśam | anātmani 
ātmapratyayanibandhanā sarvaduḥkhayogitā 
janmavināśādivikārasaṃbaddhatā ca jīvānāṃ sāmānyaṃ 
paramakāraṇamekamātmānaṃ māmahantayā pratipannānāṃ punarna 
saṃbhavatīti tātparyam || 15 ||

	evaṃ ca yathoktena nayena matsamāśrayaṇamekamupāyaṃ 
varjayitvā na anyat kiṃcidapunarāvṛttikāraṇaṃ saṃbhavatītyāha �

ā brahmabhuvanāllokāḥ punarāvartino'rjuna |
māmupetya tu kaunteya punarjanma na vidyate || 16 ||

	paramaprakṛṣṭatapoyajñadānādipuṇyakarmaprāpyo brahmaṇaḥ 
prajāpaterloko brahmalokaḥ, tamavadhiṃ kṛtvā yāni kānicit 
puṇyakarmaprāpyāṇi lokāntarāṇi saṃbhavanti, teṣu ye lokāḥ 
tattatkriyānuṣṭhātṛtvāt labdhopapattayo janāḥ, te 
yathoktajñānaśūnyatvāt tāsāṃ kriyāṇāmanuṣṭhitānāmantavattve 
sati punarāvartinaḥ bhūyaḥ saṃsāravartmani prādurbhāvinaḥ | māṃ 
punaryathoktajñānanirmalabhaktimātraikopāyagamyaṃ 
parabrahmasvarūpaṃ parameśvaram upetya 
tādātmyapratipattiparyantamāśritya punarjanma saṃsārabhūmāvutpattiḥ 
na vidyate eva | māmupetya bhūyo na jāyante iti 
upāyajananakriyayorekakartṛtvamatra avagantavyam || 16 ||

	atha kathaṃ brahmalokavāsino'pi punarāvartante, na 
punarmatprapannā ityatra krameṇa kāraṇamāha -

sahasrayugaparyantamaharye brahmaṇo viduḥ |
rātriṃ yugasahasrāntāṃ te'horātravido janāḥ || 17 ||

p. 186) ye 
jāgarasvapnādyavasthāhetubhūtasūryodayāstamayopalakṣitameva 
kālaviśeṣamahorātratayā manyante, na te tattvato'horātrasvarūpajñāḥ 
ye tu brahmaṇaḥ prajāpaterdivyayā saṃkhyayā caturyugānāṃ sahasraṃ 
paryanto'vadhiryasya, tāvatpramāṇamaharvāsaraṃ viduḥ jānate, 
tāvatparimāṇāmeva ca rātriṃ te tattvajñāḥ ahorātravidaḥ ucyante | 
kimanena uktaṃ bhavati | yathā martyānāṃ nālikāṣaṣṭyavacchinnaḥ 
kālo'horātraḥ, tathā brahmalokanivāsināṃ 
caturyugasahasradvayāvacchinnaḥ kālo'horātraḥ | tataśca yathā martyāḥ 
svāhorātraparisaṃkhyayā parikalitaṃ varṣaśataṃ puruṣāyuṣamativāhya 
punardehāntaraṃ bhajante iti iha vyavasthā, tathā brahmalokanivāsino'pi 
yathoktaparimāṇāvacchinnāhorātrakalpanayā varṣaśataṃ 
puruṣāyuṣamativāhya punardehāntaraṃ bhajanti, iti karmabhūmibhājāṃ 
martyānāṃ brahmalokanivāsināṃ ca puṇyakarmaṇāmahorātrādeḥ 
puruṣāyuṣaparyantasya kālasya hrāsadairghyakṛto viśeṣ'h, natu 
saṃsāritve teṣāṃ kaścit viśeṣaḥ | tato yathā martyāḥ punarāvartante, 
tathā brahmalokāntaranivāsino'pi svakālaparimāṇena 
puruṣāyuṣamativāhya punarāvartante iti 

ā brahmabhuvanāllokāḥ punarāvartinaḥ ( 8|16) 

	iti uktam | tena idamatra tātparyam � 
yathoktajñānaśūnyapuṇyakriyāviśeṣānuṣṭhānalabhye 
kālāvacchedatucchabhogādhikaraṇe brahmalokādau vivekinā na 
sthairyāsthāvipralabdhena bhavitavyam | so'pi 
viśiṣṭajātyāyurbhogasaṃbandhāt saṃsāre eva, 
yugasahasraparimāṇamapi kāladairghyamantavattvāt martyāhorātrāt na 
vyatiricyate iti yathoktaṃ yugasahasradvayaparimāṇaṃ kālamahorātratvena 
ye manyante, te'horātravido janāḥ iti uktam || 17 ||

p. 187) idānīṃ tu tasya brāhmasya ahorātrasya 
svarūpapratipādanadvāreṇa 
martyāhorātratulyavṛttāntatāmupadeśyānāṃ vivekanairmalyāya 
upadeṣṭumāha �

avyaktād vyaktayaḥ sarvāḥ prabhavantyaharāgame |
rātryāgame pralīyante tatraivāvyaktasaṃjñake || 18 ||

bhūtagrāmaḥ sa evāyaṃ bhūtvā bhūtvā pralīyate |
rātryāgame'vaśaḥ pārtha prabhavatyaharāgame || 19 ||

	avyaktāt īśvarodbhāvitajagatkāraṇabhāvāt guṇasāmyātmanaḥ 
pradhānāparābhidhānāt padārthāt 
yugasahasraparimāṇabrahmadivasāvirbhāve sati sarvā vyaktayaḥ 
sarvabhāvānāṃ sarve viśeṣāḥ prabhavanti prādurbhavanti | yathā 
sūryodayāt pravṛtte divase sarvaprāṇināṃ sarvakriyāviśeṣāḥ 
prabhavanti, tathaiva yugasahasraparimāṇe brahmaniśāgame tatraiva 
yathokte'vyaktasaṃjñatattve pralīyante vyaktarūpatāṃ parityajya 
śaktimātratvena avatiṣṭhante yathā iha sarvaprāṇabhṛtāṃ 
sūryāstamayāt saṃjāte niśāgame sarvendriyapravṛttayo'ntareva 
pralīyante | kimatra tātparyam iha udayāstamayayoḥ sargapralayayośca 
paryāyeṇa pravahatoranityatve nāsti kaścit bhedaḥ, kevalaṃ 
kalevarādivinaśvarabhāvārhābhāvasaṃkalitanisarganiravacchedasaṃvi-
dāṃ māyāśaktimohitānāṃ hrāsadairghyakalpanākṛto 
viśeṣavibhramaḥ, na tadā yathoktajñānaniṣṇātatayā kālānavacchinne 
satye svātmani ahaṃpratipattirvivekibhirdṛḍhīkāryeti | etadeva nigamayati 
bhūtagrāmaḥ sa evāyam iti | sa eva ayaṃ caturdaśavidhena prapañcena 
pravibhakto bhūtagrāmaḥ carācaraprāṇivargo yathoktena krameṇa 
sargādau bhūtvā

p. 188) bhūtvā punaḥ punarabhivyaktimāsādya rātryāgame 
brahmarātrisaṃjñe pralayakāle pralīyate nyagbhavati, aharāgame sargādau 
prabhavati vyaktimāsādayatīti || 19 ||

	evam ā brahmabhuvanāllokāḥ punarāvartante 
ityetadarthapratipādanaprasaṅgena sargapralayasvarūpaṃ pratipādya 
idānīṃ matpratipannā yena kāraṇena na punarāvartante, tat 
pratipādayitumāha �

parastasmāttu bhāvo'nyo vyaktāvyaktaḥ sanātanaḥ |
yaḥ sa sarveṣu bhūteṣu naśyatsu na vinaśyati || 20 ||

avyakto'kṣara ityuktastamāhuḥ paramāṃ gatim |
yaṃ prāpya na nivartante taddhāma paramaṃ mama || 21 ||

	tasmāt avyaktasaṃjñāt jagatkāraṇatvena avabhāsitāt padārthāt 
pradhānākhyāt paraḥ punaranyo vilakṣaṇaḥ bhāvaḥ paramārthaḥ 
satpadārtho vidyate iti śeṣaḥ | kīdṛśaḥ | vyaktāvyaktaḥ vyaktaṃ 
kāraṇatvena pradhā(natā)nāpannamavyaktaṃ pradhānātmakaṃ vastu 
yasmāt, tathāvidhaḥ, vivakṣiteśvaralakṣaṇabhāvavaśāt kila 
avyaktavyavahāro'yaṃ pratīyate; yadivā vyaktaśca avyaktaśceti 
viśeṣaṇasamāsāśrayaṇena vyākhyeyametatpadam | sa hi bhāvo 
nityoditaprakāśātmakatayaiva sarvamidaṃ prakāśayan vyakta eva, 
māyāvaśāt tu sarvasya tathāpratipattyabhāvādavyaktaḥ | tathā 
sanātanaḥ sadā sarvakālaṃ bhavatīti sanātano nityaḥ | kīdṛśo'sau | 
samasteṣu jaḍacetanabhedena dviprakāreṣu 
avāntarabhedādaparyantaprapañceṣu bhūteṣu prāṇiṣu naśyatsu uktena 
prakāreṇa pradhāne pralayamanubhavatsu na vinaśyati 
nityoditādvaitacitprakāśātmakatvāt pralīnatvādivyapadeśaṃ na yāti, 
kuto'tyantābhāvatā | tat kasmāt na upalabhyate ityāha 
acintyasvamāyāśaktimohitān prati yathāsthitena nityena

p. 189) rūpeṇa aprathamānatvāt avyaktaḥ paramārthataḥ punaḥ akṣaraḥ 
na kṣarati svasmāt svarūpāt na saṃcalati | yaśca evaṃ vidho bhāvastaṃ 
paramāṃ gatim anuttarāṃ prāptiṃ āhuḥ tattvavidaḥ | ata eva kīdṛśaṃ 
tam | yaṃ prāpya āsādya ātmatvena pratipadya 
praśāntaprāpyāntaraspṛhā yathoktā yogino na nivartante 
saṃsārasaraṇiṃ na avataranti yatastathāvidho yo bhāvaḥ tat mama parasya 
brahmaṇaḥ paramakāraṇasya parameśvarasya dhāma nityoditaprakāśo 
niruttarasthānaṃ vā | tadevaṃvidhaṃ māṃ ye prapannāste na 
punarāvartante iti tathā vidhānāṃ yogināmapunarāvṛttikāraṇaṃ 
pratipāditam || 21 ||

	tasyaiva yathoktasya bhāvasya svarūpaṃ nigamayan 
tatprāptyupāyaṃ smārayannāha �

puruṣaḥ sa paraḥ pārtha bhaktyā labhyastvananyayā |
yaṃ prāpya na punarjanma labhante yogino'rjuna || 22 ||

yasyāntaḥ sthāni bhūtāni yatra sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitam |

	ya eṣa yathāpratipāditasvarūpo bhāvaḥ sa paraḥ 
sarvakṣetrajñātiriktaprabhāvatvāt niratiśayaḥ puruṣaśabdavācyaḥ | 
sarvaśarīrāntaḥśāyitvāt puruṣaḥ paramātmā | kīdṛśaḥ | 
uktavakṣyamāṇaprakāreṇa yaṃ prāpya ātmatvena pratilabhya yoginaḥ 
svīkṛtanānāvidhasamādhibhedāḥ punaḥ bhūyo janma saṃsāritayā 
utpattiṃ na labhante na āpnuvanti, tathā yasya ekasya antaḥ 
sāmānyasaṃvillakṣaṇe hṛdaye sthitāni sarvāṇi carācarabhūtāni | nahi 
teṣāmevamanyonyavibhinnarūpatayā 
sphuratāmadvayaikacinmātrānupraveśamantareṇa sattāsaṃbhavaḥ, ata eva 
yatra yasmin sakalajagatpadārthasārthasāmānyādhāre

p. 190) sarvam idaṃ pratiṣṭhitaṃ kṛtavyavasthānam | sa punaritthaṃbhūto 
bhaktyā 
pratipāditottarottaraprakṛṣṭārtatvādijñāniparyantacaturavasthārūpayā 
sevayā labhyaḥ prāpyaḥ | kīdṛśyā | ananyayā tatsvarūpavyatirekeṇa 
sarvāsu avasthāsu avidyamānamanyadālambanaṃ yasyāstayā 
tathāvidhayeti ||

	atha anyamutkrāntiviṣayaṃ yogopadeśaṃ prastāvayannāha �

yatra kāle tvanāvṛttimāvṛttiṃ caiva yoginaḥ || 23 ||
prayātā yānti taṃ kālaṃ vakṣyāmi bharatarṣabha |

	yatra yasmin vakṣyamāṇe kāle prayātāḥ saṃnyastadehāḥ santo 
yogino'nāvṛttiṃ tathā āvṛttiṃ 
viśiṣṭabhogādhikaraṇayogyaśarīrādisaṃbandhaṃ yānti svātantryeṇa 
bhajante, taṃ tādṛśaṃ vakṣyamāṇakrameṇa yogināmeva sulabhaṃ 
kālam upadekṣyāmi || 23 ||

tamāha �

agnirjyotirahaḥ śuklaḥ ṣaṇmāsā uttarāyaṇam || 24 ||

tatra prayātā gacchanti brahma brahmavido janāḥ |
dhūmaḥ kṛṣṇastathā rātriḥ ṣaṇmāsā dakṣiṇāyanam || 25 ||

tatra cāndramasaṃ jyotiryogī prāpya nivartate |

	iha agnīṣomau prakāśatamasī, tadupalakṣite dinarātrī; 
śuklakṛṣṇau pakṣau; tathā ṣaṇmāsaparimāṇe uttaradakṣiṇe ayane, iti 
sthūladṛṣṭyā kālavibhāgamācakṣate tasya devapitṛmartyaviṣayatvena 
prakṛṣṭamadhyamanikṛṣṭaparimāṇatāṃ ca | itthaṃsthite agnirjyotiḥ 
sūryaprakāśastadupalakṣitam ahaḥ triṃśannālikāvacchinnaparimāṇo 
divasākhyaḥ kālāṃśaḥ | āpūryamāṇasomopalakṣitāḥ pañcadaśa 
ahorātrāḥ 

p. 191) śuklapakṣaḥ śuklaśabdena nirdiṣṭaḥ | tathā 
makarādirāśiṣaṭkasaṃcārivirocanacāropalakṣitāḥ ṣaṇmāsāḥ 
uttarāyaṇam ityete kālaviśeṣāḥ prasiddhāḥ | tatra evaṃ vidhāyāṃ 
kālaviśeṣasāmagryāṃ ye brahmavidaḥ vijñātaparamakāraṇasvarūpāḥ 
prayānti utkrāmanti śarīramutsṛjanti te brahma paraṃ tattvaṃ gacchanti 
apunarāvṛttiṃ mokṣagatiṃ prāpnuvantītyarthaḥ | tathā dhūmaḥ 
jalātmakatvāt somaḥ, tathā tadupalakṣitā rātriḥ prasiddhā | kṛṣṇaḥ 
kṣīyamāṇasomopalakṣitāḥ pañcadaśa ahorātrāḥ kṛṣṇaḥ pakṣaḥ | sa 
ca kramāpekṣayā yathāpāṭhaṃ na vyākhyātaḥ | tathā 
karkaṭādirāśiṣaṭkasaṃcārisūryopalakṣitāḥ ṣaṇmāsā dakṣiṇāyanam 
| tatra evaṃ vidhāyāṃ kālasāmagryāṃ punarjanmahetubhūtaṃ 
candramasaṃ jyotiḥ tejaḥ prāpya yogī samādhimān āvartate punaḥ 
saṃsāramārgabhavatarati | atra kecit vicakṣaṇā evaṃ vicāramārabhante 
iha tāvat brahmaṇo yathoktasya paramasya kāraṇasya dvividhaṃ vedanam 
svasaṃvedanena sākṣātkāraḥ, āptopadiṣṭasya vā 
svarūpāvagamamātram | tatra ādyena prakāreṇa ye brahmavidasteṣāṃ 
prāptaprāpaṇīyatvāduktakālasāmagrī na brahmaprāptihetutvamarhati, 
dvitīye'pi pakṣe svarūpāvagamamātreṇa ye 
brahmavidasteṣāmasākṣātkṛtatattvānāṃ 
yathoktakālaviśeṣaprāptimātramapunarāvṛttihetorbrahmaṇaḥ 
prāptikāraṇamiti na śakyate vaktum | evamubhayarūpāṇāmapi 
brahmavidāmantakāle yathoktakālaprāptyadhīnā brahmaprāptiriti na 
yuktyā saṃgacchate | yo'pi yogī svīkṛtasamādhiviśeṣastasya 
tadbalānapohena yathoktakālaviśeṣaprāptimātrādeva 
punarāvartanamayuktimat 

yaṃ yaṃ vāpi smaran bhāvaṃ tyajatyante kalevaram | (8|6)

p. 192) ityādinā pratipāditopapattyā sudṛḍhabhāvanābhāvitasya 
bhāvasya avyabhicārāt mantharaṃ bhāvitasya tatkālamasaṃsmaraṇe sati 
punarāvṛttiḥ sthitaiva, kimuktaḥ kālaḥ kariṣyati | tadevaṃ yogī 
yathoktakālaviśeṣaprāpteḥ punarāvartate ityetadapi na hṛdayāvarjakam | 
yadapyucyate yadyeṣa yathoktaḥ kālo brahmaprāptyādiheturna bhavet, tat 
kathaṃ brahmavido'pi śāntanavasya 
tanusaṃnyāsārthamuttarāyaṇapratīkṣā śrūyate | satyaṃ śrūyate, kintu 
brahmaprāptihetutvena tena asau kālo na pratīkṣitaḥ | yadi hi sa eva 
brahmaprāptihetuḥ syāt, tadā sarvairbrahmavidbhirdehotsarge sa eva 
apekṣyeta, tatkālotsṛṣṭadehāśca sarva eva anāvṛttibhāvaṃ bhajeyuḥ | na 
ca evamasti | naca evaṃvidhe kāle mriyeteti vidhirupapadyate sarvasya maraṇe 
svātantryābhāvāt | bhīṣmasya tu svatantramṛtyutvāt 
tādṛkkālapratīkṣaṇe nimittāntaramanumeyam | tat ca idam yat kila 
sthūlayā dṛśā yathoktaḥ kālaḥ kalevarasaṃnyāsāvasare samupanato 
gativiśeṣādhānaheturbhavati, ityāgamavacanapramāṇīkaraṇārthaṃ 
bhīṣmasya tādṛkkālapratīkṣodyamaḥ, tasmāt yathoktakālasya 
mukhyatayā na ahorātrādirūpatvam | tataśca brahmavidāṃ yogināṃ ca 
yathākramaṃ brahmaprāpteḥ punarāvṛtteśca kāraṇabhūtaṃ yadetat 
kālagatidvayamuktam, tatra avaśyaṃ kaścit viśeṣo boddhavyatvena vartate | 
vakṣyati hi 

naite sṛtī pārtha jānan yogī muhyati kaścana | (8|27) 

	iti | naca etayoḥ 
sṛtyorbāhyakālaviśeṣarūpayordevayānapitṛyānasaṃjñāmātrasya 
jñānaṃ brahmādiprāptiheturbhavet | ataḥ kaścit viśeṣo 
yathoktārthasaṃgatiheturatrānumeyaḥ, ityācakṣate | kālasya hi 
bāhyābhyantarabhedena rahasyaśāstreṣu dviprakāratā pratipāditā | tatra 

p. 193) prasiddhasūryenducāropalakṣito bāhyaprakāraḥ, 
prāṇāpānalakṣaṇasūryenducāropalakṣitastu ābhyantaraḥ | tatra 
mukhyasya bāhyasya pratipāditabādhakasadbhāvāt gauṇa ābhyantaraḥ 
samāśrīyate, tena agnyādaya uttarāyaṇaparyantāḥ śabdāḥ 
pāṇagatipratipāditatvena atra prayuktāḥ, yataḥ prāṇagatireva 
divasādyuttarāyaṇāntakālāvayavatvena tatra tatra rahasyaśāstreṣu 
tattadviśiṣṭaśarīrādyapekṣayā prasiddhā | itthaṃsthite yeṣāṃ 
yathoktajñānaniṣṭhatayā brahmavidāṃ satāmantakāle svarasena 
mūrdhacchidrākhyabrahmadvāravāhini prāṇacāre niyamitaṃ 
saṃvitsrotaḥ prāṇopaśāntikṣaṇe sarvato 
nistaraṅganisarganirmalasāmānyasaṃvinmahodadhau praviśya 
samarasībhāvamāpadyate, teṣāṃ brahmaprāptiravyavadhānena upapannā 
| sa hi brahmavidāṃ devayānaḥ panthāḥ | eṣā ekā tadviṣayā śāśvatikī 
gatiḥ | yastu yathoktatattvajñānavyatirekeṇa abhyāsasvīkṛtakevalayogaḥ, 
tasya yogino yathoktaprāṇagatiniyamitamapi saṃvitsrotaḥ 
prāṇopaśāntikṣaṇe brahmaveditvābhāvāt 
dehādyahaṃpratyayaparvatapratihataprasaraṃ sat parāvartate | 
parāvartamānaṃ ca 
dhūmādidakṣiṇāyanaśabdāntapratipāditāmapānavṛttimeva 
pitṛyāṇalakṣaṇāṃ gatiṃ samāśritya yogābhyāsaprakarṣānusārocitaṃ 
viśiṣṭaṃ śarīrāntaramāviśati, yatra pūrvābhyāsahniyamāṇacittatayā 
prakṛṣṭatareṇa utsāhena yatamānaḥ parāṃ siddhiṃ prāpsyatītyuktam | 
tadayamatra saṃkṣepārthaḥ ye jñānitvāt brahmavidaḥ santo'ntakāle 
yathāpratipāditena krameṇa 
prāṇaśaktyālambanacittavṛttayastatpraśāntikṣaṇe'pi 
aviluptaparatattvasmṛtayo dehamutsṛjanti, te 
nirvyavadhānabrahmaprāptivaśāt na punarāvarttante | ye tu kevalayoginaḥ 
santaḥ prāṇavṛttimeva āśritya dehamutsṛjanto jñānitvābhāvāt 
paratattvasmṛtyabhāve sati utkrāmanti,

p. 194) te dehādyanuparatāhaṃpratyayatvādapānavṛttiṃ 
saṃsāramārgamāśritya viśiṣṭadehāntaraṃ prāpnuvanti, yataḥ sarvasya 
kasyacit śarīriṇaḥ sarvasaṃsārisādhāraṇaprāṇavṛttyāśrayaṇena 
vartamānāt dehādutkrāntasya apānavṛttyāśrayaṇena 
bhaviṣyaddehasaṃkrāntirbhavati | brahmavidāṃ yogināṃ ca iha prakaraṇe 
viśeṣa eva pratipāditaḥ || 25 ||

	ata eva prāṇāpānalakṣaṇāntaraviśiṣṭamārgadvayatvena etat 
saṃgrahītumāha �

śuklakṛṣṇe gatī hyete jagataḥ śāśvate mate |
anayoryātyanāvṛttimekayāvartate'nyayā || 26 ||

	ete yathokte dve gatī prāṇāpānavṛttirūpau 
devayānapitṛyāṇasaṃjñau dvau mārgau jagataḥ sarvalokasya śāśvate 
nitye | tena na sarvaḥ kaścit janaḥ prāṇamārgāśrayaṇena 
dehādutkrāmati, kintu apānamārgāśrayaṇena dehāntare saṃkrāmati | 
brahmavido yoginaśca ayaṃ viśeṣo yat anayoḥ gatyormadhyāt ekayā 
ūrdhvavāhaprāṇarūpayā śuklayā gatā yathāpratipāditena prakāreṇa 
antakāle samāśritayā brahmavit anāvṛttiṃ yāti brahma prāpnoti | yogī tu 
tayaiva utkrāman anyayā apānarūpayā kṛṣṇayā uktena hetunā āvartate 
viśiṣṭadehāntare saṃkrāmati || 26 ||

	yataḥ �

naite sṛtī pārtha jānan yogī muhyati kaścana |
tasmāt sarveṣu kāleṣu yogayukto bhavārjuna || 27 ||

	ete yathāpratipādite sṛtī prāṇāpānarūpe gatī jānan

p. 195) yathoktayā mahattayā pratipadyamāno yogī 
prākpratipāditasvīkṛtamukhyayogo na kaścit muhyati sarva eva tadgatijñaḥ 
san brahmaprāptihetugatisamāśrayaṇena brahmaiva prāpnoti, natu 
saṃsāramitaragatiparihārāt | etayośca gatyorevaṃvidhāṃ mahattāṃ 
brahmavidāṃ jñeyatvena sthitāṃ tattvavido vadanti | manaḥ 
prasarasahacaro'yaṃ prāṇaprasaro yata udeti, yatra ca astameti; tat 
kālakalanādisakalaprapañcavirahitabrahmatattvopalabdhipadam | tataśca 
yathoktaprāṇaprasaranibaddhaḥ sakalaḥ kālādiḥ 
prapañcastadudayāstamayadaśāvimarśakṣamasaṃvidāṃ brahmavidāṃ 
pralīyate | tādṛśīṃ ca daśāmāsādya yo brahmavit paramayogī 
dehamutsṛjati, sa brahmaṇyeva pariṇato na punarāvartate | yastu kevalayogī 
tāṃ daśāṃ pratyavamaṣṭuṃ (mraṣṭuṃ) na kuśalaḥ, sa cāndramasaṃ 
jyotirapānaprasararūpamāśritya utkrāman dehāntare saṃkrāmati, yatra 
krameṇa siddhimāpsyatīti | evamanayorgatyoḥ siddhihetuvibhāgavijñānam 
| ata eva uktaṃ 

naite sṛtī pārtha jānan yogī muhyati --------------- |

	ityādi | yata evaṃ, tasmāt sarveṣu kāleṣu bāhyadṛṣṭyā 
uttarāyaṇadakṣiṇāyanādibahubhedapratipāditeṣu yogayukto bhava 
yathoktena prakāreṇa prāṇāpānagatijñānātmake yoge samādhau 
yukto'vahito bhava || 27 ||

	yato'sya yogasya ayaṃ prabhāvaḥ �

vedeṣu yajñeṣu tapaḥsu caiva 
dāneṣu yat puṇyaphalaṃ pradiṣṭam |
p. 196) atyeti tatsarvamidaṃ viditvā 
yogī paraṃ sthānamupaiti cādyam || 28 ||

	idaṃ saptapraśnātmakavyākaraṇarūpaṃ vastu viditvā jñātvā 
yogī yathoktasamādhiḥ tat sarvaṃ vedādhyayanayajñatapodānādi antavat 
puṇyakriyāphalam atyeti atikrāmati, yataḥ ādyam anuttaramata eva param 
anantānandamayatvādatyutkṛṣṭaṃ sthānaṃ dhāma upaiti prāpnoti 
paramātmani svābhāvikīṃ pratiṣṭhāṃ labhate iti | yadyapi evaṃ 
kaiścidayamartho vyākhyātaḥ yathāmuyā eva vṛttyā 
uttarāyaṇasāmagryā prayātā 
brahmavido'rcirādisaṃjñalokāntarākramaṇakrameṇa brahma gacchanti, 
yoginaśca ata eva yogiśabdena uktā dakṣiṇāyanakālasāmagryāṃ 
prayātāḥ santaḥ cāndramasaṃ jyotiḥ prāpya 
dhūmādisaṃjñalokāntarākramaṇakrameṇa saṃsāra āvartante iti | 
pūrvoktayuktyā ca etadapi vyākhyānaṃ na saṃgacchate | yathoktayuktyā 
ca ayamapi kramo'nayoreva gatyorantarbhavatīti kecit manyante | tadatra 
paramārthavedinaḥ pramāṇamiti om || 28 ||

gambhīrairvipulaiḥ svabhāvavimalaiḥ śrīmatpadārthojvalaiḥ 
sphītāṃ saptabhiramburāśibhiriva brahmādibhirvastubhiḥ |
adhyāyasya naro'ṣṭamasya vivṛtiṃ pṛthvīmimāmañjasā 
yaḥ svīkartumalaṃ sa eva viduṣāṃ sāmrājyameko'rhati || 

iti śrīrājānakarāmakaṇṭhaviracite vākyārthānvayamātre 
sarvatobhadranāmni bhagavadgītāvivaraṇe'ṣṭamo'dhyāyaḥ || 8 ||


atha navamo'dhyāyaḥ |

	aṣṭamādhyāyaparisamāptau saptamapraśnanirṇaye'ntakāle 
śuklakṛṣṇagatidvayajñānaṃ brahmaprāptyupāyatvena upadiṣṭam, 
atastāvanmātra eva brahmaprāptyupāyastvayā mā vedi, iti 
arjunamaṣṭamādhyāye pratipāditameva upadeśaṃ smārayan saṃgraheṇa 
bhagavānuvāca �

idaṃ tu te guhyatamaṃ pravakṣyāmyanasūyave |
jñānaṃ vijñānasahitaṃ yajjñātvā mokṣyase'śubhāt || 1 ||

	tuśabdo vyatireke | aṣṭamādhyāyoktalakṣaṇopāyavyatirekeṇa idaṃ 
svabuddhinityasaṃnihitaṃ jñānaṃ brahmaprāptyupāyabhūtaṃ 
vijñānena svasaṃvitsākṣātkāraparyantena viśiṣṭena jñānena bodhena 
saṃyuktaṃ tubhyamahaṃ prakarṣeṇa saṃkṣepataḥ 
sārapratipādanarūpeṇa atiśayena vakṣyāmi kathayiṣyāmi | kīdṛśāya | 
anasūyave śuddhātiśuddhabuddhitvāt yato'smai jñānāya anasūyasi 
yathoktasya phalasya asaṃbhāvanāvaśādajñavat na garhaṇamārabhase | 
kīdṛśaṃ jñānaṃ vakṣyāmi | guhyatamaṃ guhyaṃ guhyataraṃ ca 
pūrveṣu adhyāyeṣu jñānamuktam, idaṃ tu tadapekṣayā atiguhyaṃ, 
anāvirbhūtavivekajajñānānāṃ guhāyāṃ dūratare'dhvani sthitaṃ, 
rahasyatayā vā guhyam | ata eva tvaṃ yat viditvā 
janmajarāmaraṇacakraparivartanalakṣaṇāt aśubhāt aśreyasaḥ mokṣyase 
svayameva mokṣaṃ prāpsyasi || 1 ||

	yato vakṣyamāṇajñānopāyaniṣṭhasya antakāle 
gatidvayajñānābhyupāyavyatirekeṇāpi brahmaprāptirnirdiṣṭaiva, ata eva 
asya jñānasya praśaṃsārthamāha �

p. 198) rājavidyā rājaguhyaṃ pavitramidamuttamam |
pratyakṣāvagamaṃ dharmyaṃ susukhaṃ kartumavyayam || 2 ||

	idaṃ vakṣyamāṇaṃ jñānam uttamam ananyasāmānyaṃ pavitraṃ 
pāvanaṃ sarvakilviṣakalaṅkapaṅkasaṃkṣālanahetuḥ | santi hi pavitrāṇi 
tīrthamantratapoyajñadānādīni | tāni tu jñānarahitatvādantavanti yadi; 
parametajjñānasaṃskṛtāni anantaphalatvamaśnuvīran, ata eva uttamaṃ 
pavitramiti uktam | yato rājavidyā eṣā rājñāṃ 
janakāśvapatiprabhṛtīnāmugraṃ svakarmāpi anutiṣṭhatāmeṣā vidyā 
jñānamapavargaphalatvena śrūyate | tataśca teṣāṃ rājñāṃ guhyaṃ 
rahasyametat, yataḥ pratyakṣāvagamaṃ pratyakṣaḥ 
svasaṃvedanavedyo'vagamo bodho yasya tat tathāvidhaṃ, anyeṣāṃ hi 
pavitrāṇāṃ kālāntare dehāntare lokāntare ca phalāvagamo bhaviṣyati; 
tathā dharmyaṃ dharmāt śāstroditāt varṇāśramakramavibhaktāt 
sadācārādanapitam, ata eva kartuṃ saṃpādayituṃ, susukham 
atyantasukhakaraṃ sahajasvabhāvakarmamātrasādhanatvāt, tathāvidhaṃ 
sadapi avyayam avidyamānakṣayaṃ tatprāpyasya phalasya 
matsamāpattilakṣaṇasya nityatvāt || 2 ||

	etacca madanugrahotthitabhaktiśraddhādiguṇasaṃpadbhireva 
sulabhaṃ yataḥ �

aśraddadhānāḥ puruṣā dharmasyāsya parantapa |
aprāpya māṃ nivartante mṛtyusaṃsāravartmani || 3 ||

	asya uktasya vakṣyamāṇasya ca jñānarūpasya dharmasya 
aśraddadhānāḥ yathoktaphalāsaṃbhāvanāparāḥ puruṣā māṃ 
yathoktasvarūpaṃ paraṃ brahma aprāpya matsamāpattimanāsādyaiva 
mṛtyusaṃsārayorbhūteṣu nityaparivarttinormaraṇajanmanorvartmani 
nivartante punaḥ punarāpatanti |

p. 199) kimuktaṃ bhavati | 
madanugrahasudhāsāgarasamudgataśraddhāsuratarumañjarī 
sakalasiddhiprasavaikaheturyairupāsyate, taireva anena jñānena sulabho na 
anyairiti || 3 ||

	tadidānīṃ jñānaṃ pratipādayitumāha �

mayā tatamidaṃ sarvaṃ jagadavyaktamūrtinā |
matsthāni sarvabhūtāni na cāhaṃ teṣvavasthitaḥ || 4 ||

na ca matsthāni bhūtāni paśya me yogamaiśvaram |
bhūtabhṛnna ca bhūtastho mamātmā bhūtabhāvanaḥ || 5 ||

	mama saṃbandhinam aiśvaram īśvare sarvathā svāmini ekasmin 
bhavaṃ yogam ananyādhikaraṇaṃ samādhiṃ paśya pratipadyasva | yataḥ 
idaṃ vedyatvena sarvapramātṝṇāṃ sarvaṃ 
saṃkṣepataścarācarabhāvarūpamavāntarabhedaparikalpanayā 
niṣparyantaṃ vastu mayā upādānasahakāryādikāraṇāntaranirapekṣeṇa 
nityanirvikārasāmānyasaṃvinmātrasvabhāvāpracyutena 
ananyānukāryaniratiśayanijakrīḍāmātraikaprayojanena 
svecchaikasādhanena ekena kartrā tatam 
itthamanantāścaryāvabhāsarūpatayā vistāritaṃ jagat bhuvanam 
avyaktamūrtinā manmāyotthāpitaiḥ sarvaiḥ pramātṛbhiridantayā 
paricchettumaśakyatvādavyaktā vyapadeśyavyaktivirahitā mūrtirākṛtiḥ 
svarūpaṃ yasya, sa tathā tena tādṛśena | kimuktaṃ bhavati | 
brahmāderārabhya paramakṣudrajantuparyantasya sarvasya pramāturyat 
saṃvedyaṃ vastu yena yena prakāreṇa yadā yadā yatra yatra prakāśate, tat 
saṃvedyamānaṃ santamātmānaṃ labhate, saṃvedyamānatā ca 
saṃvedituḥ karmabhāvāpattiḥ, saṃveditā ca paramārthataḥ sadā eka eva 
bhavitumarhati yo na kathaṃcit na kadācit

p. 200) na kutracit saṃvedyatvalakṣaṇaviśeṣaṇasaṃparkaṃ sahate | tato 
matsthāni mayi ekasmin saṃveditari sthitāni sarvāṇi nikhilāni sthāvarāṇi 
jaṅgamāni ca saṃvedyāni bhūtāni | evaṃ sarvakartā sarvaśaktihetuḥ 
ahaṃ na ca teṣu bhūteṣu vyavasthitaḥ yathā 
nityasaṃveditṛtvamātrasvabhāvāvyabhicāriṇo mama vyavasthā 
saṃvedyānāṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ vyavasthitihetuḥ, na tathā 
sarvabhūtavyavasthā madvyavasthitiheturityarthaḥ | sarvāṇi hi bhūtāni 
saṃvedyatvādeva utpādavināśādidharmasaṃbandhādanityāni, mama tu 
saṃveditṛtvamekaṃ lakṣaṇamiti kathaṃ mama bhūtasthityadhīnā 
avasthitirbhavet | itthaṃ madadhīnasthitikāni api bhūtāni na matsthāni mayi 
saṃveditari madrūpatayaiva na sthitiṃ bhajante vedyatayā sphuraṇāt; 
bhūtasthitiheturahaṃ yathā saṃveditṛtayaiva nityaṃ sphurāmi, na tathā 
anyāni api bhūtāni saṃvedyatvenaiva teṣāṃ pratīteḥ | evaṃ yathoktena 
prakāreṇa mamātmā svabhāvo bhūtabhṛt bhūtāni bibharti dhārayati tena 
tena rūpeṇa avasthāpayati naca bhūtasthaḥ na bhūtasthityadhīnasthitikaḥ, 
ityuktameva | ataśca anena krameṇa bhūtabhāvanaḥ bhūtāni bhāvayati 
sṛjatītyarthaḥ || 5 ||

	atra kenacit sādṛśyāṃśena dṛṣṭāntamāha �

yathākāśasthito nityaṃ vāyuḥ sarvatrago mahān |
tathā sarvāṇi bhūtāni matsthānītyupadhāraya || 6 ||

	evamanena prakāreṇa mayi anavacchinnamahimni sthitāni 
ādheyatvamāpannāni tattaddeśagatāni sūkṣmāṇi mahānti ca bhūtāni 
upadhāraya budhyasva | katham | yathā ākāśe niravacchedavyāptau nityaṃ 
sadaiva sthitaḥ vāyuḥ | kīdṛśaḥ | sarvatragaḥ tatra tatra deśe gataḥ 
kṛtavyāptiḥ mahān ca anyāpekṣayā vyāpakaḥ | evaṃ

p. 201) matsthāni bhūtāni budhyasva | atra idaṃ tātparyam yathā vāyoḥ 
sarvatra sarvagatatvamahattvādayo dharmā 
aparyantākāśāvakāśaikanibandhanāḥ, tathā sarvabhūtānāṃ sarve 
dharmā matsvabhāvānupraveśaikanibandhanā iti || 6 ||

	tadetat nigamayitumāha �

evaṃ hi sarvabhūteṣu carāmyanabhilakṣitaḥ |
bhūtaprakṛtimāsthāya sahaiva vinaiva ca || 7 ||

	evam anena vāyvākāśasaṃbandhopamena prakāreṇa sarvabhūteṣu 
carāmi tāni vyāpnomi anabhilakṣitaḥ idantayā kenacidapi 
anavadhāritasvarūpaḥ | kiṃ kṛtvā carāmi | bhūtaprakṛtiṃ 
prākpratipāditacetanātmakabhūtalakṣaṇāṃ prakṛtiṃ svabhāvam 
āsthāya adhiṣṭhāya | kathaṃ carāmi | saha caiva vinaiva ca 
nityamanyonyasaṃbaddhatvāt saha vedyavedakalakṣaṇasvarūpābhedāt, 
vinaiva anyonyapṛthaktayā eva | anyo hi vedakasvarūpo mama 
svabhāvo'nyacca madicchodbhāvitaṃ vedyasvarūpam || 7 ||

madicchodbhāvitatvameṣāṃ pratipādayitumāha �

sarvabhūtāni kaunteya prakṛtiṃ yānti māmakīm |
kalpakṣaye punastāni kalpādau visṛjāmyaham || 8 ||

	etāni cetanācetanāni sarvabhūtāni mahākalpāvasāne madīyāṃ 
prakṛtiṃ svabhāvaṃ yānti madicchayaiva mayi pralīyante ityarthaḥ | tāni ca 
kalpādau kalpāntarārambhe'hameva visṛjāmi caturdaśavidhenaiva bhedena 
avabhāsayāmi, natu yāni eva pralīnāni tāni eva punaḥ sṛjāmīti atra 
arthaḥ | īśvarecchāyā aparyanuyojyatvāt svātantrye'pi 
caturdaśavidhameva bhūtasargaṃ pralayotpādāvasthayoḥ saṃharati sṛjati 
ca parameśvara iti tātparyam || 8 ||

p. 202) etadeva nigamayati �

prakrtiṃ svāmavaṣṭamya visṛjāmi punaḥ punaḥ |
bhūtagrāmamimaṃ kṛtsnamavaśaṃ prakṛtervaśāt || 9 ||

	svāṃ prakṛtiṃ nijaṃ svabhāvaṃ, na anyat kiñcit kāraṇāntaram 
avaṣṭabhya avalambya imaṃ sarvaṃ bhūtagrāmaṃ prāṇisamūhaṃ 
punaḥ punaḥ bhūyo bhūyo'nādiprabandhapravṛttakalpādikālakalpanayā 
sṛjāmi evamudbhāvayāmi prakṛteḥ eva vaśāt 
matsvabhāvapāratantryādeva avaśam asvatantram | kalpānte ca tathaiva 
saṃharāmīti atra anuvartanīyam || 9 ||

	kiṃca �

naca māṃ tāni karmāṇi nibadhnanti dhanañjaya |
udāsīnavadāsīnamasaktaṃ teṣu karmasu || 10 ||

	tāni sargasaṃhārādirūpāṇi karmāṇi na punarmāṃ badhnanti 
manmāyodbhāvitanānākṣetrajñavat niyamitavipākārambhakatvādinā na 
paratantrīkurvanti | yathā kṣetrajñānāṃ karmāṇi 
jātyāyurbhogalakṣaṇavipākārambhakatvena 
saṃsārabandhakāraṇatvamāpadyante, na tathā sargādikarmāṇi 
mametyarthaḥ | kīdṛśaṃ santaṃ māṃ na nibadhnanti, iti hetugarbhaṃ 
viśeṣaṇamāha teṣu karmasu asaktatvān mamatvādigrahābhāvena 
animagracittatvāt udāsīnavat taṭasthavat āsīnaṃ sthitam | yathā 
parakīyakarmasu paro naitāni mama tattatphaladāni iti 
manyamānastāṭasthyamanubhavaṃstaistaiḥ karmabhiḥ 
svaphalabhoktṛtvādinā na niyamyate, tathā sargādibhiḥ karmabhirna 
nibadhyaḥ, | yatastāni sargādīni karmāṇi 
acintyavaibhavasvamāyodbhāvitānyonyabhedān

p. 203) ksetrajñān prati, mama sarvataḥ svecchāmātreṇa 
tathāvabhāsakatvameva kartṛtvam | kṣetrajñā eva hi manmāyāmohitāḥ 
santo'nyonyabhedena avabhāsamānānāṃ bhāvānāṃ 
sargādyavasthāyogitvaṃ pratipadyante, mama punarete sarve 
bhāvāstattatsargādyavasthājuṣo'pi advayacinmātralakṣaṇāt svabhāvāt 
na bhidyante, iti | anena teṣu karmasu udāsīnavadāsīnaṃ māmetāni na 
nibadhnantīti uktam | eva ca arthaḥ prāgeva punaḥ punaḥ 

	tasya kartāramapi māṃ viddhyakartāramavyayam | (4|13)

ityādinā 

na māṃ karmāṇi limpanti na me kāmaḥ phaleṣvapi | (4|14)

ityādinā ca prtipāditaḥ || 10 ||

	yadi evaṃ vastusthityā parameśvaro na, idaṃ jagat sākṣāt 
sargādikarmaṇā niyunakti, tat kena idaṃ jagat sṛjyate ityāha �

mayādhyakṣeṇa prakṛtiḥ sūyate sacarācaram |
hetunānena kaunteya jagadviparivartate || 11 ||

	mayā parameśvareṇa nityaṃ svabhāvamātrāvasthitenaiva 
adhyakṣeṇa adhiṣṭhātrā hetubhūtena prakṛtiḥ saptame'dhyāye 
cetanācetanavibhāgena pratipāditaparāparasvarūpā svabhāvavṛttireva 
aparyantasthāvarajaṅgamabhedayuktaṃ jagat sūyate janayati | anena hetunā 
nimittena idaṃ jagad viparivartate sargasthitisaṃhāraprabandhacakre 
paribhramati || 11 ||

	evamadbhutaiśvaryaikāspadam �

avajānanti māṃ mūḍhā mānuṣīṃ tanumāsthitam |
paraṃ bhāvamajānanto mamāvyayamanuttamam || 12 ||

	mūḍhāḥ māyāmuṣitatattvajñānāḥ māṃ 
yathāpratipāditasvabhāvam

p. 204) avajānanti nityoditaniratiśayaprakāśapradyotamānasvabhāvamapi 
mohabalotsāhavicchedāt majjijñāsāyāmapi avajñāmāśrayanti | 
kīdṛśaṃ santaṃ māmavajānanti | mānuṣīṃ tanumāsthitam 
mānuṣīgrahaṇamupalakṣaṇārtham, tena manuṣyādibhedena 
anyonyabhinnānantākṛtiviśeṣarūpāṃ 
pāramārthikasvarūpatirodhānatiraskariṇīmāśritam | ata eva te mūḍhāḥ | 
kīdṛśāḥ santo māmavajānanti | evaṃvidhayā manmāyayā madīyaṃ 
paraṃ etasmāt nānācetanācetanabhāvalakṣaṇāt prapañcādanyaṃ 
vilakṣaṇam, tathā avyayam avinaśvaram, ata eva anuttamam 
uttarottaravicāryamāṇaprakarṣe sati avidyamāno'nya uttamaḥ prakṛṣṭo 
yasmāt, taṃ tathāvidhaṃ bhāvaṃ pāramārthikīṃ sattām ajānantaḥ 
santo māmevaṃ vimarśābhāvādabuddhyamānāḥ || 12 ||

yataste mūḍhāḥ santaḥ �

moghāśā moghakarmāṇo moghajñānā vicetasaḥ |
āsurīṃ rākṣasīṃ caiva prakṛtiṃ mohinīṃ śritāḥ || 13 ||

	mohinīṃ svabhāvājñānakāriṇīṃ prakṛtiṃ guṇamayaṃ 
svabhāvaṃ śritāḥ dehātmakāhaṅkāranibandhanasthitayaḥ | ata eva 
kīdṛśīṃ prakṛtim | āsurīṃ rajoleśānugatatamaḥ pradhānāṃ, tathā 
rākṣasīṃ kevalatamaḥpradhānām, uttamādhamāsurasargabhedatvena 
prāk prastāvanāyāṃ pratipāditasvarūpām | yata evaṃvidhāṃ prakṛtiṃ 
śritāstato moghāśāḥ moghāḥ phalaśūnyā āśā yeṣāṃ te 
tathāvidhāḥ | te hi āsurarākṣasaprakṛtitvāt rajastamomayatve sati 
kāmātmāno bhogaviṣayaṃ saṃkalpameva kurvanti, natu 
tatphalaprāptihetuṃ śāstroditaṃ

p. 205) karma ārabhante, tato moghāśā iti uktam | tathā moghakarmāṇaḥ 
yadapi karma ārabhante, tat śāstrīyopāyaniyamābhāvāt moghaṃ 
niṣphalaṃ yeṣāṃ, te tathāvidhāḥ | vakṣyati hi rājasatāmasayoḥ 
karmaṇorlakṣaṇam | tadyathā

yattu kāmepsunā karma sāhaṅkāreṇa vā punaḥ |
kriyate kleśabahulaṃ tadrājasamiti smṛtam ||

anubandhaṃ kṣayaṃ hiṃsāmanavekṣya ca pauruṣam |
mohādārabhyate karma yattattāmasamucyate || (18|25)

iti | tathā evaṃvidhakartṛlakṣaṇamapi 

rāgī karmaphalaprepsurlubdho hiṃsātmako'śuciḥ |
harṣaśokānvitaḥ kartā rājasaḥ parikīrtyate || (18|27)

tathā

ayuktaḥ prākṛtaḥ stabdhaḥ śaṭho naikṛtiko'lasaḥ |
viṣādī dīrghasūtraśca kartā tāmasa ucyate || (18|28)

	iti | tathā moghajñānāḥ cikīrṣitakarmaniṣpattihetutvāt 
karmārambhapūrvabhāvijñānamapi moghaṃ niṣphalaṃ yeṣāṃ, te 
tathoktāḥ | jñānapūrvakaṃ hi karmaṃ, jñānasyaiva ca 
āsurādiprakṛtyāśritajñātṛsaṃbandhino 
viparyayapratipattidoṣasaṃbandhāt karmaphalena visaṃvādāt 
niṣphalatāsthitaiva | tathāca evaṃ vidhajñānalakṣaṇamapi vakṣyati 

pṛthaktvena tu yajjñānaṃ nānābhāvān pṛthagvidhān |
vetti sarveṣu bhūteṣu tadrājasamiti smṛtam || (18|21)

tathā 

yattvakṛtsnavidekasmin kārye saktamahaitukam |
atattvārthavadalpaṃ ca tattāmasamudāhṛtam || (18|22)

p. 206) iti | moghajñānatvāt vicetasaḥ tattvārthaniṣṭhatvābhāvāt sadapi 
vigataṃ samyagjñānalakṣaṇasvakāryākaraṇādavidyamānamiva 
ceto'ntaḥkaraṇaṃ yeṣāṃ, te tathāvidhāḥ || 13 ||

	evamekāntato māyākroḍīkṛtānāmāsuraprakṛtyāśritānāṃ 
vṛttiṃ pratipādya, vidyānugṛhītānāṃ 
pravṛttinivṛttiviṣayaśāstropadeśapātrabhūtānāṃ 
daivaprakṛtyāśritānāṃ pratipipādayiṣuḥ paradaivaprakṛtyāśritānāṃ 
tāvattāṃ pratipādayitumāha �

mahātmānastu māṃ pārtha daivīṃ prakṛtimāśritāḥ |
bhajantyananyamanaso jñātvā bhūtādimavyayam || 14 ||

	prastāvanāpratipāditaparāparasvarūpamayyāḥ parasyā devyāḥ 
prakṛtiṃ daivīṃ samyak jñānavivekadyotanaśīlatvāt 
devaśabdavācyānāṃ saṃbandhinīṃ prakṛtiṃ svabhāvam āśritāstu ata 
eva mahātmānaḥ utkṛṣṭasvabhāvāḥ māṃ paramātmānaṃ bhajante 
caturvidhayā sevayā ananyamanasaḥ 
madvyatiriktavastvantarābhāvaprarūḍhapratipattitvāt mayi evaikasmin tattve 
mano yeṣāṃ, te tathāvidhāḥ jñātvā ca anuṣṭhānam iti sthiteḥ | 
kīdṛśaṃ māṃ viditvā bhajante | bhūtādiṃ bhūtānāṃ 
cetanācetanānāṃ madhye ādiṃ prathamaṃ, tathā avyayaṃ nityatvāt na 
mama kṣayo vidyate ityarthaḥ | sarvabhūtāni ādyantavanti, ahamādireva, na 
antavāniti atra tātparyam || 14 ||

kiṃca �

satataṃ kīrtayantaśca yatantaśca dṛḍhavratāḥ |
namasyantaśca māṃ bhaktyā nityayuktā upāsate || 15 ||

	te evaṃ daivaprakṛtyāśritāḥ prabuddhāḥ satatam avyavadhānena 
kīrtayantaḥ mantrajapastutyādinā udghoṣayantaḥ, tathā dhyānādinā

p. 207) yatamānāḥ, tathā namasyantaḥ grahībhavanto māṃ bhaktyā 
hetubhūtayā nityayuktāḥ santaḥ upāsate vāṅmanaḥkāyavyāpārān 
madekaviṣayān kurvāṇāḥ paricarantītyarthaḥ || 15 ||

kiṃca �

jñānayajñena cāpyanye yajanto māmupāsate |
ekatvena pṛthaktvena bahudhā viśvatomukham || 16 ||

pūrvebhyaḥ prasiddhabhaktiprakārāśrayaṇena madyajanaparebhyaḥ anye 
vilakṣaṇā jñānena viśuddhasaṃvinmātramatsvarūpacintanaikalakṣaṇena 
yajñena yāgavidhinā yajantaḥ pūjayantaśca māmupāsate sevante | 
kathaṃ māṃ jñānayajñena upāsate | ekatvena 
sakalaprapañcaparihārādabhinnaikasaṃvinmātramatsvarūpatayā, tathā 
pṛthaktvena nānātvena, ata eva bahudhā anekena jñānena 
kriyādyupacaraṇīyavicitrākṛtyādibhinnarūpatayā | vastutaḥ kīdṛśam | 
viśvatomukhaṃ sarvataḥ sarva eva upāsanīyābhedāt mukhaṃ 
pratyabhijñākāraṇaṃ yasya, taṃ tādṛśam | idamatra tātparyam 
dvividhaḥ pāramārthiko matsvarūpopāsākramaḥ | ekaḥ 
saṃvinmātraikatattvaviṣayajñānamātrasādhanaḥ, apara ekasyaiva 
tattvasya nānājagadbhāvanānāśaktisamūhatvāt taddvāreṇa tasya tasya 
upāsanamiti || 16 ||

tadeva ekasya tattvasya sarvaśaktitvaṃ pratipādayitumāha �

ahaṃ kraturahaṃ yajñaḥ svadhāhamahamauṣadham | 
mantro'hamahamevājyamahamagnirahaṃ hutam || 17 ||

	kratuyajñayoryadyapi paryāyatvena vyavahāraḥ, tathāpi 
yajñaśabdasannidhāne paśusomādisādhanaviśiṣṭa eva yajñaḥ 
kratuśabdena atra

p. 208) uktaḥ | yajñaśabena pākayajñānāṃ grahaṇaṃ, tato 
yathāpratipāditabrahmādvaitasthityā sarvo 
yāgavidhirnānādevatāsaṃpradānaḥ aham eva | tathā svadhā 
pitṛtarpaṇamantro'pi aham eva | tathā nānauṣadhivikārātmakam 
auṣadhaṃ yāgopakaraṇadravyam aham eva | tathā 
saṃpradānabhūtadevatāsaṃmukhīkārādivividhārthavācakaśabda-
viśeṣarūpaḥ mantraḥ yajñopakaraṇaviśeṣo'pi aham | tathā ājyaṃ 
ghṛtaṃ tarpaṇadravyam | tathā agniḥ āhavanīyādiryāgavidhyadhikaraṇam 
| tathā hutaṃ havanaṃ homakarma, iti yajñādirūpatayā 
brahmārpaṇavidhinyāyena aham eva sthitaḥ, iti atra tātparyam || 17 ||

kiṃca-

pitāhamasya jagato mātā dhātā pitāmahaḥ |
vedyaṃ pavitramoṅkāra ṛk sāma yajureva ca || 18 ||

gatirbhartā prabhuḥ sākṣī nivāsaḥ śaraṇaṃ suhṛt |
prabhavaḥ pralayaḥ sthānaṃ nidhānaṃ bījamavyayam || 19 ||

	asya jagataḥ samastasya carācarasya viśvasya utpattimūlakāraṇatvāt 
pitā mātā cāham | yadivā pitarau yathā 
apatyasyasamastayogakṣemodvahane nityāvahitau, tathā ahamasya jagataḥ | 
tathā dhātā dhartā sthitihetuḥ | tathā pitāmahaḥ brahmādīnāṃ jagataḥ 
sraṣṭṭatvena sthitānāṃ janakatvāt pitāmahaḥ | pavitraṃ paramaṃ 
pāvanaṃ yat vedyaṃ jñeyaṃ vastu | tathā oṃkāraḥ parabrahmavācakaḥ 
praṇavākhyo vedādiḥ śabdaviśeṣaḥ, tatprabhavāṇi ca ṛksāmayajūṃṣi | 
tathā gamyate jñāyate vā paraṃ tattvamanayeti gatiḥ upāyaḥ | tathā 
bhartā poṣṭā | tathā prabhuḥ yatheṣṭaviniyogaikasvāmī | tathā sākṣī 
śubhāśubhakarmādhyakṣaḥ | tathā nivāsaḥ 
sarvabhūtānāmekamāvasatham |

p. 209) tathā śaraṇaṃ sarvopadravapraśamāya āśrayaḥ | tathā suhṛt 
sarvabhāvena upakārī | tathā janmavināśasthitihetutvāt prabhavaḥ 
pralayaḥ sthānaṃ ca | tathā pralīnaṃ sat yatra avatiṣṭhate, tat 
pradhānātmakaṃ nidhānam | tathā yataḥ prabhavati, tat pradhānasyāpi 
avyayam akṣayaṃ bījaṃ kāraṇamiti | na tat kiñcidasti, ydahamasya jagato 
na bhavāmītyarthaḥ || 19 ||


tathāca �

tapāmyahamahaṃ varṣaṃ nigṛhṇāmyutsṛjāmi ca |
amṛtaṃ caiva mṛtyuśca sadasaccāhamarjuna || 20 ||

	sūryo bhūtvā grīṣmādau rasamādadānaḥ ahaṃ tapāmi yataḥ, 
tadā hi ahameva varṣaṃ nigṛhṇāmi niruṇadhmi, varṣākāle ca ahameva 
parjanyo bhūtvā utsṛjāmi tapanavarṣaṇakriyayā annādyutpādanena 
ahameva jagat pravartayāmītyarthaḥ | tathā amṛtam avināśi yat vastu 
mṛtyuśca vināśaheturahameva | kiṃ bahunā uktena yat kiñcit sat 
vidyamānaṃ śobhanaṃ vā, yat asat tadviparītaṃ kiñcit jagati saṃbhavati; 
tat sarvam ahaṃ macchaktireva ekā sadasadbhāvena avabhāsate iti || 20 ||

	evaṃ paradaivaprakṛtyāśritasargabhedapratipādanaprasaṅgena 
brahmasvarūpaṃ saṃgraheṇa uktvā, 
idānīmaparadaivaprakṛtyāśritānāṃ puruṣāṇāṃ pravṛttiṃ 
pratipādayitumāha �

traividyā māṃ somapāḥ pūtapāpā
yajñairiṣṭvā svargatiṃ prārthayante |
te puṇyamāsādya surendraloka-
maśnanti divyān divi devabhogān || 21 ||

p. 210) te taṃ bhuktvā svargalokaṃ viśālaṃ 
kṣīṇe puṇye martyalokaṃ viśanti |
evaṃ trayīdharmamanuprapannā 
gatāgataṃ kāmakāmā labhante || 22 ||

	jñānarahitakriyāmātropadeśaparyavasitārthā 
ṛgyajuḥsāmalakṣaṇāstisro vidyā adhīyate vidanti vā traividyāḥ 
mukhyatayā brāhmaṇādayastraivarṇikāḥ pūtapāpāḥ āniṣekāt 
śāstravihitasaṃskārasaṃpannatvāt hatakalmaṣāḥ, tathā 
nirvṛttayathoktabrahmacaryādyāśramaniyamāḥ santaḥ somapāḥ 
nityanaimittikasvakarmānuṣṭhānaniṣṭhā ityarthaḥ, tathāvidhāḥ santo 
devatāntarayajanavidhirūpaiḥ yajñaiḥ taistaiḥ 
śāstracoditairyāgaviśeṣaiḥ samyagjñānābhāvādavidanto'pi māmeva 
iṣṭvā pūjayitvā svargatiṃ devalokaprāptiṃ kāmātmānaḥ santaḥ 
prārthayante upayācante, yatastaistairyajñavidhibhistāṃstān devaviśeṣān 
yajante, iti viśvatomukhamekaṃ tattvaṃ māmeva yajante | kevalaṃ saṃprati 
vidyāśaktyānanugṛhītāḥ paraṃ madīyaṃ bhāvamavidanto 
bhogamātraṃ yajñādikriyāphalaṃ manyamānāḥ 
svargamabhikāṅkṣanti | te tathāvidhāstraividyā dehānte svakarmopanataṃ 
svargalokaṃ viśālaṃ vipulaṃ devalokaṃ gatvā tadgatān bhogān bhuktvā 
upasevya svakarmopārjite puṇye tadanurūpakālātivāhanāt kṣīṇe antaṃ 
gate martyalokaṃ karmabhūmimimāṃ 
svakarmānurūpajātiviśeṣāśrayaṇena viśanti pratipadyante | evam anena 
krameṇa trayīdharmaṃ vedoktaṃ svaṃ svamācāram anuprapannāḥ 
samāśritāḥ santaḥ kāmakāmāḥ 
svargādibhogābhikāṅkṣiṇaścāturvarṇikā api gatāgataṃ gataṃ 
svargādibhūmau gamanaṃ, āgataṃ tato bhūyaḥ 
karmabhūmāvāgamanaṃ labhante prāpnuvanti saṃsārabandhabhāginaḥ 
punaḥ punarbhavantītyarthaḥ || 21 || 22 ||

p. 211) etadantena granthena jñānamuktvā vijñānamāha �

ananyāścintayanto māṃ ye janāḥ paryupāsate |
teṣāṃ nityābhiyuktānāṃ yogakṣemaṃ vahāmyaham || 23 ||

	ye janāḥ parāṃ daivīṃ prakṛtimāśritā 
dṛṣṭānuśravikaguṇātmakebhyo 
bhogebhyo'śuddhyatiśayakṣayādidoṣavadbhyo viraktāḥ santo 
nityanirmalaniratiśayānantānandasudhāsāgaraṃ māṃ paramakāraṇaṃ 
brahmatattvaṃ paryupāsate yathāpratipāditajñānaniṣṭhatayā 
svakarmāṇi ajahataḥ samantāt sevante | kīdṛśāḥ santaḥ | ananyāḥ 
avidyamānamanyat madvyatiriktaṃ vastu yeṣāṃ te, tathāvidhā 
manmayameva sarvamidamanubhavanta ityarthaḥ | teṣāṃ tādṛśānāṃ 
nityābhiyuktānāṃ yathoktajñānaikatānatayā satatodyuktānāṃ 
sarvāsvavasthāsu yathoktamatsvarūpasmaraṇaikaparāyaṇānāmityarthaḥ 
ahaṃ parameśvaraḥ yogakṣemaṃ vahāmi yogaṃ prāganāsāditena 
matsvarūpasaṃvedanātmakena phalena saṃbandhaṃ, kṣemamāsāditasya 
punaraparilopalakṣaṇaṃ dhārayāmi | ye nityaṃ manmayāsteṣāṃ kuto 
yogakṣemakṣatirityarthaḥ | evaṃ 
yathoktopāyanirapāyaniveśitāntaḥkaraṇatayā 
matsvarūpānubhavarūpāṃ parāṃ kāṣṭhāmadhirūḍhā bhaktireva 
vijñānamiti tātparyam || 23 ||

	nanu viśvatomukhastvamevaikaṃ tattvamiti devatāntarayājino'pi 
ananyā eva, tat kathamuktam ananyā ye māmupāsate, teṣāṃ yogakṣemaṃ 
vahāmīti | atra āha �

ye'pyanyadevatā bhaktā yajante śraddhayānvitāḥ |
te'pi māmeva kaunteya yajantyavidhipūrvakam || 24 ||

	satyam ye'pi janāḥ anyadevatā bhaktāḥ parasparabhinnasvarūpān 

p. 212) tāṃstān sūryendrādipramukhān devaviśeṣān yajante, te'pi 
paramārthato madvyatiriktadvitīyavastvabhāvāt māmeva śraddhayā 
tattadbinnaphalasaṃbhāvanayā yajanti pūjayanti, kintu avidhipūrvakam | 
madyajane evaṃvidho vidhirukto vakṣyate ca | yathoktajñānaniṣṭhatayā 
sarveṣu yaṣṭavyeṣu devatāviśeṣeṣu tāsu tāsu yāgakriyāsu yaṣṭari ca 
svātmani advayacinmātralakṣaṇamatsvarūpānubhavaḥ, sa ca teṣāṃ 
jñānaśūnyakevalakriyāmātraśaraṇānāṃ vidhirna saṃbhavati, 
tasmādavidhipūrvakaṃ yajanti, ityuktam || 24 ||

etadeva prakaṭayitumāha �

ahaṃ hi sarvayajñānāṃ bhoktā ca prabhureva ca |
natu māmabhijānanti tattvenātaścyavanti te || 25 ||

	sarvayajñānāṃ nānābhedānāṃ yāgānām ahameva bhoktā 
eka eva parameśvaraḥ phalasya svīkartā | tathā prabhuḥ 
adhiṣṭhānakriyādhiṣṭhitaḥ | sarve yajñā ahameveti na punastathāvidhaṃ 
māṃ tattvena paramārthena yathoktena abhijānanti ekatvena na pratipadyante 
| ato hetoḥ te tādṛśā anuṣṭhātāraḥ cyavanti matto viyujyante nānātvena 
sarvaṃ paśyantītyarthaḥ || 25 ||

tataśca 

yānti devavratā devān pitṛn yānti pitṛvratāḥ |
bhūtāni yānti bhūtejyā yānti madyājino'pi mām || 26 ||

	devebhyo devārthaṃ vrataṃ niyamo yeṣāṃ, te 
śakrādidevārādhakāḥ devavratāḥ tatsaparyāsiddhau devān eva yānti 
tallokabhājo bhavantītyarthaḥ | evaṃ pitṛvratāḥ pitṝn yānti | tathā 
bhūtebhyo

p. 213) yakṣarakṣaḥpiśācādibhyastadarthamijyā yāgo yeṣāṃ, te 
tādṛśāstāni eva pūrvavat yānti | devapitṛyājinaḥ sāttvikāḥ, 
bhūtayājinastu rājasāstāmasāśca | yaduktaṃ

yajante sāttvikā devān yakṣarakṣāṃsi rājasāḥ |
pretān bhūtapiśācāṃśca yajante tāmasā janāḥ || (17|4)

	iti | ye tu nistraiguṇyāste madyājinaḥ māmeva 
traiguṇyātītasvarūpaṃ yaṣṭuṃ śīlaṃ yeṣāṃ te tathāvidhameva 
matsvarūpaṃ parabrahma yānti samāpadyante || 26 ||

	sa ca madyāgo devavratādiyāgavat 
tattannānādravyasaṃbhārasādhano naivetyāha �

patraṃ puṣpaṃ phalaṃ toyaṃ yo me bhaktyā prayacchati | 
tadahaṃ bhaktyupahṛtamaśnāmi prayatātmanaḥ || 27 ||

	bhaktiradvayacinmātrasvarūpabrahmatattvabhāvanayā ātmana eva, 
tasya tasya vā nānādevatāviśeṣasya upāsanam | tayā sādhanabhūtayā 
yastattvavit patrādyapi yatkiñcit mahyaṃ prayacchati, tat tasya 
prayatātmanaḥ tathāvidhādvayavijñānasamāhitāntaḥkaraṇasya 
tādṛśabhaktyupahṛtam aśnāmi upabhuñje yādṛśabhāvanāviśeṣeṇa 
upahṛtaṃ, tathaiva tat svīkaromītyarthaḥ || 27 ||

	yata evaṃ, tataḥ

yat karoṣi yadaśnāsi yajjuhoṣi dadāsi yat |
yat tapasyasi kaunteya tat kuruṣva madarpaṇam || 28 ||

	yat karoṣi tattatphalārthitayā yat karma anutiṣṭhasi, yacca aśnāsi 
tattatkarmaphalabhūtamupabhuñje, tat sarvaṃ madarpaṇaṃ kuru mayi 
brahmasvarūpe'rpaṇaṃ madātmakapratipattyā nivedanaṃ yasya, tat

p. 214) tādṛśaṃ kuru saṃpādaya | tacca kāryaṃ jñānarahitānāṃ 
nānāphalahetutvena vyavasthitaṃ vivṛṇoti yat juhoṣi teṣu teṣu yajñeṣu 
tattaddevatātarpaṇārthaṃ yadagnau havanakriyayā kṣipasi, yacca 
anatarvedyādau tadāsi pātrebhyaḥ prayacchasi yat taponiyamaviśeṣaṃ 
carasi; tat sarvaṃ madarpaṇaṃ kuru | yajñadānatapolakṣaṇāsu kriyāsu 
avaśyakartavyāsu tattatphalābhikāṅkṣākārpaṇyaparihāreṇa 
matsvarūpānubhavameva ekaṃ phalamavalambasvetyarthaḥ || 28 ||

asyā bhāvanāyāḥ phalamāha �

śubhāśubhaphalairevaṃ mokṣyase karmabandhanaiḥ |
saṃnyāsayogayuktātmā vimukto māmupaiṣyasi || 29 ||

	evam anena prakāreṇa yajñādikāḥ svāḥ kriyā anutiṣṭhannapi 
śubhāśubhaphalaiḥ śubhaiḥ sukhātmakairaśubhairduḥkhātmakaiḥ 
kriyāṇāṃ phalaiḥ mokṣyasetyakṣyase tāni te bandhanāya na 
kalpayiṣyante ityarthaḥ | kīdṛśaiḥ | karmabandhanaiḥ karmāṇi 
jñānaśunyāni bandhanaṃ niyamaheturyeṣāṃ, tathāvidhaiḥ | 
karmanibandhanāni hi phalāni jñānaśūnyaṃ badhnanti, na 
yathoktajñānasaṃpannam | atastvaṃ saṃnyāsayogena 
phalāsaṃkalpasamādhinā yuktātmā saṃbaddhasvabhāvaḥ, ata eva 
vimuktaḥ sarvabandhanebhyo viniṣkrānto mām ekaṃ paramakāraṇam 
upaiṣyasi ahameva saṃpatsyase ityarthaḥ || 29 ||

	nanu keṣāṃcit yajñādikriyābhirapi upasevamānānāṃ 
bandhanaheturbhavān, anyeṣāṃ tu patrādi upaharatāṃ mokṣaheturiti kiṃ 
tavāpi rāgadveṣānuṣaṅga iti | atra āha �

samo'haṃ sarvabhūteṣu na me dveṣyo'sti na priyaḥ | 
ye bhajanti tu māṃ bhaktyā mayi te teṣu cāpyaham || 30 ||

p. 215) sarvabhūteṣu caturdaśavidhena prapañcena prasṛteṣu 
paramārthatastattvasya ekatvāt paratattvātmakaḥ ahaṃ samaḥ 
nirviśeṣavṛttiḥ; ato vyatiriktatvābhāvāt na me kaścit dveṣyo 
dveṣadoṣahetuḥ, nāpi priyaḥ rāgadoṣahetuḥ saṃbhavati | etau 
bhedadarśināmeva anuṣaktau | tataśca ye punarmahātmāno madicchayaiva 
vidyāśaktyanugrahādekatattvadarśinaḥ santo māṃ yathoktasvarūpaṃ 
yathoktayā bhaktyā bhajanti sevant yathoktayā bhāvanayā upāsate te mayi 
tiṣṭhanti teṣu cāham abheda eva tiṣṭhāmi mattasteṣāṃ 
durbhedabhedapratyayasetuvidalanāt brahmāmṛtasāgarasamarasībhūtatvāt 
tattadbhedasya || 30 ||

	evaṃ yathoktayā bhāvanayā bhagavadbhakteḥ stutimāha �

api cet sudurācāro bhajate māmananyabhāk |
sādhureva sa mantavyaḥ samyagvyavasito hi saḥ || 31 ||

yadyapi yuṣmatpratipāditadṛṣṭyā yuddhādighorakarmānuṣṭhānāt 
sutarāṃ duṣṭaceṣṭitaḥ san ananyabhāk madekālambanaḥ māṃ bhajate 
upāste sa tādṛśaḥ sādhuḥ sadācāra eva mantavyaḥ boddhavyaḥ, yataḥ 
samyak aviparyastajñānatvāt yathāvat sa tādṛk bhakto vyavasitaḥ 
gṛhītavyavasāyaḥ | etāvadeva sadācāradurācārayorlakṣaṇam 
yo'nanyatayā madbhaktaḥ, sa sadācāraḥ; tadvyatiriktastu durācāra iti | 
etāvadatra tātparyam aparyanuyojyecchasya hi bhagavataḥ śaktiravatīrṇā 
kvacidapi anugrāhye vinaṣṭaṃ ca nirmūlamakhilaṃ durācāratvam || 31 ||

p. 216) etadeva prakaṭayitumāha �

kṣipraṃ bhavati dharmātmā śaśvacchāntiṃ nigacchati |
kaunteya pratijāne'haṃ na madbhaktaḥ praṇaśyati || 32 ||

	kṣipram anugrahalakṣaṇasamakālameva dharmātmā bhavati 
pratipāditajñānayogalakṣaṇaḥ paramo dharma ātmā svabhāvo yasya, sa 
tādṛśaḥ saṃpadyate | tathāsaṃpannamātraṃ śaśvat nityakālaṃ 
śāntiṃ 
yathāpratipāditātmānātmādijñeyavastujñānabhāvalakṣaṇakṣobhakṣa
yarūpaṃ praśamaṃ yathoktatattvasamāpattirūpaṃ nitarāṃ gacchati 
prāpnoti | niyacchatīti pāṭhe ātmani tāṃ niyacchati 
niyatāmavyabhicāriṇīṃ karoti | tataśca hetoste tubhyaṃ pratijñāṃ 
karomi yathā madbhaktaḥ yathoktena rūpeṇa madupāsako na praṇaśyati 
matsvarūpavyatirekapratipattilakṣaṇaṃ nāśaṃ na prāpnoti || 32 ||

ata eva āha �

māṃ hi pārtha vyapāśritya ye'pi syuḥ pāpayonayaḥ |
striyo vaiśyāstathā śūdrāste'pi yānti parāṃ gatim || 33 ||

kiṃ punarbrāhmaṇāḥ puṇyā bhaktā rājarṣayastathā |
anityamasukhaṃ lokamimaṃ prāpya bhajasva mām || 34 ||

	pāpā pāpakarmavipākajā yonirutpattikāraṇaṃ yeṣāṃ te 
strīśūdravaiśyādayaḥ ye'pi kecana bhaveyuḥ te māṃ vyapāśritya 
yathoktamadekaśaraṇā bhūtvā parām anuttarāṃ gatim 
apunarāvṛttipadavīmāpnuvanti | naca etadatiśayoktimātrameveti 
vyākhyātameva prāk | punaḥ kiṃ atra vācyaṃ, ye puṇyāḥ 
puṇyakarmāṇaḥ puṇyakarmavipākāt labdhotkṛṣṭayonayaḥ brāhmaṇā 
rājarṣayaśca rājatve

p. 217) sati apavargabhāgitvāt mukhyā ṛṣayo draṣṭāro bhaktāḥ santaste 
parāṃ gatiṃ yāsyanti | tadetena sarvalokasādhāraṇatvamātmajñānasya 
bhagavatā pratipāditam | yata evamutkṛṣṭaphalā madbhaktistataḥ imaṃ 
lokaṃ karmabhūmilakṣaṇaṃ bhuvanaṃ prāpya atra janma labdhvetyarthaḥ

bhūya eva mahābāho śṛṇu me paramaṃ vacaḥ |
yatte'haṃ prīyamāṇāya vakṣyāmi hitakāmyayā || 1 ||

	yadyapi prāyeṇa niravaśeṣamupadeśyaṃ vastu vitatya pratipāditaṃ, 
tathāpi tadatisūkṣmatvāt paramaprakṛṣṭaprajñānāmapi hṛdaye jhagiti 
pratiṣṭhāṃ na labhate, iti bhūyaḥ bahutarameva pūrvoktaṃ tat madīyaṃ 
paramam uttamārthapratipādakatvāt prakṛṣṭamidaṃ vacanamākarṇaya | 
bahutaravacanakathane kāraṇamāha yat vacanaṃ prīyamāṇāya paritoṣaṃ 
bhajate hitakāmyayā ātyantikānukūlyakaraṇecchayā tubhyaṃ ahaṃ 
idānīṃ kathayiṣyāmi | kimanena uktaṃ bhavati | yāvat 
prakārāntaradurharamohaviṣaviṣādahāriṇā 
pratipāditārthaparamārthāpurvapīyūṣaparamaleśasparśena 
anāhlāditaṃ hṛdayam, tāvat kutaḥ prīyamāṇatvam | atastathāvidhasya hi 
punaruktakathanādudvega evaṃ syāt | ataḥ prīyamāṇatvena 
āviṣkṛtaśiṣyaguṇaviśeṣāya taddhitacikīrṣuṇā dayāviṣṭahṛdayena 
deśikottamena vicitroktiprakāraistāvadupadeṣṭavyam, yāvadasau 
sugṛhītārthatayā nirākāṅkṣatāmāpadyate, iti bhūyovacanakathanena 
bhavatā na udvejanīyamiti bhagavato'bhipretam | athavā 

p. 220) nidrāṃ mohamayīṃ haratsu sahasaivāntaḥ prabodhaśriyaṃ

sūryāṃśuṣviva paunaruktyajanitodvegatvamāpatsyate ||

yataḥ-

na me viduḥ suragaṇāḥ prabhavaṃ na maharṣayaḥ |
ahamādirhi devānāṃ maharṣīṇāṃ ca sarvaśaḥ || 2 ||

utpādam, prabhāvamiti vā pāṭhe māhātmyam, suragaṇāḥ 
brahmendrasūryādayo devasamūhāḥ na viduḥ na jānate | maharṣayaḥ 
bhṛgvādayo'pi tathaiva | te hi 
manmāyāśaktyudbhāvitatattatsvarūpaśaktyādibhedāstaṃ taṃ 
svādhikāramanutiṣṭhantaḥ pratikalpaṃ 
sargapralayādyanityabhāvasaṃbandhāvasthājuṣaḥ | ahaṃ tu nityatvāt

vidanti || 2 ||

tataḥ �

yo māmajamanādiṃ ca vetti lokamaheśvaram |

p. 221)	yaḥ kaścit viralo mahātmā madanugrahādeva 
pratyuditasamyagjñānaśaktiḥ ajaṃ nityatvāt janmarahitamata eva anādim

janānāṃ mahāntaṃ 
sargasthitipralayādyavasthāvidhānasvatantraśaktitvāduttamaṃ svāminam;

kāraṇamityarthaḥ | evaṃvidhaṃ māṃ yo vetti, ātmatvena pratijānīte sa 
martyeṣu māyākroḍīkṛtatvāt maraṇadharmasaṃbaddheṣu devādiṣu

p. 222) prasaranto buddhyādayaḥ pṛthagvidhāḥ nānārūpāḥ bhāvāḥ

saṃmohātmakam | sa ca asaṃmohalakṣaṇo bhāvo mumukṣūṇāmeva

prajāsargahetutvāt te'pi prajāpataya iti vāhuḥ |

p. 224) catvāro manavaḥ brahmasāvarṇo rudrasāvarṇo dakṣasāvarṇo --- iti 
dakṣasya duhiturutpannāḥ prajāsarge prativarṇaṃ cāturvarṇyasya prathame

ca | ete madbhāvāḥ mattaḥ paramakāraṇādeva bhāva utpattiryeṣāṃ, te 
tathāvidhāḥ | kīdṛśāḥ santo madbhāvāḥ | mānasā jātāḥ saṃkalpajā 
ityarthaḥ | yeṣāṃ prajāpatīnāṃ satāṃ saṃbandhinyaḥ loke bhuvaneṣu

te madicchāmātrodbhāvitasraṣṭṭabhāvā ityarthaḥ || 6 ||

	evaṃvidhasvaiśvaryajñānaphalamāha �

etāṃ vibhūtiṃ yogaṃ ca mama yo vetti tattvataḥ |
so'vikampena yogena yujyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 7 ||

	etāṃ yathoktāṃ vibhūtiṃ jagatkartṛtvādirūpamaiśvaryaṃ, tathā 
yogaṃ nānābhāvaprapañcāvabhāsanarūpakartṛtvādisaṃbandhe'pi 
advayacinmātrasvabhāvāpracyutilakṣaṇamatyadbhutamananyasādhā-
raṇaṃ samādhiṃ mama yaḥ tattvataḥ paramārthato vetti jānāti, saḥ

madanugrahāvirbhūtasamyagjñānatayā mamaiva ekasya tattvasya

viśeṣaṇam | tathā paramam anuttamaṃ pavitraṃ pāvanaṃ bhavān 
mohalakṣaṇaṃmahāpātakavināśahetutvāt | uktārthāvagamasya 
āgamaṃ bhagavadvacanaṃ ca kāraṇaṃ pratipādayitumāha yasmāt sarve 
ṛṣayaḥ parāvaradraṣṭāro munayaḥ śāśvataṃ nityaṃ puruṣaṃ 
paramātmānaṃ tvāmāhuḥ kathayanti | kīdṛśam | divyaṃ 
paramavyomastham | tathā ādi sarveṣāṃ pūrvam | devaṃ 
paraprakāśātmatayā dyotanaśīlam, ata eva ajaṃ janmarahitam | vibhuṃ 
vyāpakam | na kevalaṃ sarvarṣayo, yāvat nāradāsitadevalābhidhānā 
maharṣayastvāmevaṃ vidhamāhuḥ | svayaṃ ca māṃ sākṣāt bravīṣi || 
12-13 ||

sarvametadṛtaṃ manye yanme vadasi keśava | 
nahi te bhagavan vyaktiṃ vidurdevā maharṣayaḥ || 14 ||

p. 228) svayamevātmanātmānaṃ vettha tvaṃ puruṣottama |
bhūtabhāvana bhūteśa devadeva jagatpate || 15 ||

	etatsarvaṃ yathāpratipāditasvavibhūtiyogādirūpaṃ vastu yat 
sākṣāt vadasi, tadahaṃ ṛtaṃ satyaṃ manye | yat devāḥ api tava vyaktiṃ 
pāramārthikīṃ mūrtiṃ na viduḥ maharṣayo'pi tathaiva na viduḥ | tato 
maharṣibhyaḥ śrute mama pratyayo nāsīt, idānīṃ tu bhagavadvacanādatra 
arthe niḥsaṃśayā pratītiḥ | kutastvāṃ devā ṛṣayaśca na vidurityatra 
hetumāha svayamevetyādi | jagatpate viśveśvara tathā bhūtabhāvana 
bhūtānāṃ carācarāṇāṃ sattvānāṃ bhāvana 
vedyatvāpādanamātreṇa utpādaka, tathā teṣāmeva 
yatheṣṭaviniyogaikasvatantratayā īśa svāmin svayameva ātmanā 
ātmānaṃ svarūpaṃ tvaṃ vettha vedakaikasvabhāvatvāt kathaṃ 
vedyapadavīmanyasya devāderavatarasi | sarvaṃ hi tava ekasya sarvajñasya 
vedyaṃ, natu tvaṃ kasyacit vedyaḥ || 14-15 ||

tato hetoḥ �

vaktumarhasyaśeṣeṇa vibhūtīrātmanaḥ śubhāḥ |
yābhirvibhūtibhirlokānimāṃstvaṃ vyāpya tiṣṭhati || 16 ||

	ātmano vibhūtīḥ svaiśvaryavijṛmbhāḥ aśeṣeṇa sākalyena 
vaktumarhasi kathayituṃ prasīdetyarthaḥ | kīdṛśīrvibhūtīḥ | śubhāḥ 
sarvasya sadasadvibhāgena vyavasthitasya māyāvabhāsitasya 
jagatpadārthajātasya śubhāśubhatve sati yatra yatra sarge yā yā 
śubharūpā vibhūtistāṃ tāmādiśetyarthaḥ | kāstā vibhūtayaḥ | 
yābhirimān lokān sapta bhuvanāni tvaṃ vyāpya ākramya vartase | atra

p. 229) divyā ātmavibhūtaya iti prathamāntapāṭhaṃ kecit paṭhanti | te ca 
yattacchabdādhyāhāreṇa cchāndasaprayogeṇa vā samarthayante || 16 ||

vibhūtiśravaṇe prayojanamāha �

kathaṃ vidyāṃ mahāyogiṃstvāmahaṃ paricintayan |
keṣu keṣu ca bhāveṣu cintyo'si bhagavan mayā || 17 ||

vistareṇātmano yogaṃ vibhūtiṃ ca janārdana |
bhūyaḥ kathaya tṛptirhi śṛṇvato nāsti me'mṛtam || 18 ||

	he bhagavan sarvajñatvādiguṇaikāśraya tathā mahāyogin 
ananyasādhāraṇasamādhyekaniṣṭha tvām evaṃrūpaṃ kathaṃ kena 
prakāreṇa ahaṃ paricintayan anusmaran vidyāṃ vijānīyām | keṣu keṣu ca 
bhāveṣu padārtheṣu mayā cintyo'si dhyātavyo'si | ataḥ 
prāguktamaiśvaryaṃ samādhiṃ vibhūtiṃ ca itthaṃ 
jagadābhāsalakṣaṇāṃ svairśvaryavijṛmbhāṃ vistareṇa vaitatyena 
mahyaṃ bhūyaḥ bahutaraṃ kathaya ādiśa | yasmādetat 
svarūpapratipādanalakṣaṇam amṛtaṃ sudhārasaṃ śṛṇvato me 
śravaṇābhyāṃ pivataḥ tṛptiḥ santoṣo nāsti || 17 || 18 ||

evamabhyarthito bhagavānuvāca �

hanta te kathamiṣyāmi vibhūtīrātmanaḥ śubhāḥ |
prādhānyataḥ kuruśreṣṭha nāstyanto vistarasya me || 19 ||

	hanta abhyupagame, bāḍhaṃ yathāśuśrūṣitāḥ śubhāḥ

padārtheṣu aparyantavargabhedavibhediteṣu yatra yatra varge yā yā kācit 
pradhānatvādatiśāyinī

p. 230) vibhūtistāṃ tāṃ vakṣyāmi | yato madvibhūtīnāṃ vistarasya 
vitatatvasya antaḥ niṣṭhā na vidyate || 19 ||

	tatra idānīṃ nānāvibhūtikathanaprastāvanasya ante'pi 
abhidhāyiṣyamāṇāṃ paramārthasvarūpalakṣaṇāṃ parāṃ vibhūtiṃ 
tāvat prathamameva pratipādayitumāha �

ahamātmā guḍākeśa sarvabhūtāśayasthitaḥ |
ahamādiśca madhyaṃ ca bhūtānāmanta eva ca || 20 ||

	sarveṣāṃ cetanānāmāśaye hṛdi sthitaḥ kṛtapratiṣṭhaḥ ātmā 
aham sakalakṣetrajñahṛdayābhivyaktasāmānyasaṃvidrūpa eka eva 
paramārthata ātmā | saiva parā vibhūtiḥ, yatastannibandhana eva ayaṃ 
svamāyāśaktiprakāśitaviśvabhāvātmakanānāvibhūtiprapañcaḥ | 
tadrūpatayā cintito'hamanantavibhūtivistaraikakāraṇatayā jñāto 
bhavāmi yataḥ sarvabhūtānām ādiḥ prāgavasthā sargarūpā antaśca 
nāśāvasthā madhyaṃ sthityavasthā ahameva ekaḥ | yato manmāyāvaśāt 
sargasthitivināśāvasthāyogitvena parasparamavabhāsamāneṣu 
sarvabhūteṣu nityanirviśeṣaikacinmātrasvabhāvo'hameva sthitaḥ | yaistu 
tādṛśyā mahāvyāptyā ahaṃ cintayitumaśakyaḥ, 
taistadadhigamābhyāsāya yatra yatra varge yat yadutkṛṣṭaṃ tat tat 
madvibhūtyaṃśatayā cintayadbhirniruttarā sarvavyāpinī 
sāmānyasaṃvidātmikā parā madīyā vibhūtiradhigantuṃ śakyate yāṃ 
nānāvibhūtipratipādanaprakaraṇasya ante'pi 
sāmānyarūpajaḍāṃśavyatirekiṇīṃ

	iti vakṣyati | tadevaṃvidhe caturdaśavidhe bhūtasargaprapañce,

madīyaparamaprakāśaleśatayā

pratipādayitumāha �

ādityānāmahaṃ viṣṇurjyotiṣāṃ raviraṃśumān |

	ādityapatyatvāt sarvadevānāmādityatve prāpte rūḍhyā

sūryo'ham | kīdṛśaḥ | aṃśumān

gaṇanaṃ, tasya ca āpyāyanahetutvādisāmarthyayogāt

tayaiva dṛṣṭyā anyānapi āha �

kiṃca �

rudrāṇāṃ śaṅkaraścāsmi vitteśo yakṣarakṣasām |

vasūnāṃ dharādisaṃjñābhiḥ prasiddhānāmaṣṭānāṃ madhye

p. 233) pāvakākhyo vasuraham, tasya

maharṣīṇāṃ bhṛgurahaṃ girāmasmyekamakṣaram |

bhṛgurahaṃ tasya tadapekṣayā jñānādiprakarṣaśravaṇāt prādhānyam |

ekamakṣaram aham, tasya hi padabhūtasya

madhye japaḥ

p. 336) kiñcit padaṃ vyākhyāyate | tatra prajanaḥ prajananakriyākartā,

p. 237) rūpaṃ, tat nādātmakaṃ paraṃ śabdatattvamiti tattvavido vadanti |

svāvayavāpekṣayaiva etasya prādhānyam | avayavanirdeśastu atra arthāt 
kalpanīyaḥ | kalpanā tu kālasya kriyātvena, dravyatvena ca, ubhayathāpi 
pratipattisahiṣṇutvāt saṃbhavatyeva | saṃbhavāpekṣayaiva ca bhagavatā 
punaḥ kālanirdeśaḥ kṛtaḥ | yadivā yo'ham akṣayaḥ paramārtho 
nityatvādavinaśvaraḥ, sa eva kālaḥ ahameva | svamātmānaṃ kālatvena 
avabhāsayāmīti pūrvoktasya kālasya 
madicchāmātrakalpitasvarūpapratipādanārthaṃ punarvacanamiti; yadivā 
darśanabhedena kvacit kriyātvaṃ, kvacit dravyatvaṃ kālasya paśyatā 
bhagavatā dvirnirdeśaḥ kṛtaḥ | tathā yaḥ satataṃ 
jīvitendriyaśarīraviṣayādikaṃ yugapadeva harati, tasya 
mṛtyostatkālabhāvino maraṇahetorvastuno'nyeṣāṃ hantṝṇāṃ madhye 
prādhānyam | tathā cchalayatāṃ vañcayatāṃ madhye dyūtasya 
cchalayitṛtve'pi dharmādanapetatvāt prādhānyam | tathā 
dharmadyūtadhanaṃ śrāddhādikriyāsu śasyate | 
paravañcanābhisandhimūlatvāt chalayitṛtvaṃ sthitam | sattvavatāṃ 
guṇānāṃ sattvarajastamolakṣaṇānāṃ sattvaguṇasya prakāśātmakatvāt 
prādhānyam | tathā yena rājāno durvinītān damayanti, sa daṇḍaḥ 
śāstraniyamito nigrahaviśeṣo mukhyasādhanamiti sa eva damayitā, tena 
damayatāṃ madhye daṇḍasya

p. 238) prādhānyam | tathā jigīṣatāṃ pratidvandvino jetumicchatāṃ nītiḥ 
karmārambhopāyādikāryasiddhiparyantāṅgapañcakamayo naya eva 
mukhyaṃ sādhanamiti nītereva jigīṣatāṃ madhye prādhānyam | tathā 
guhyānāṃ rahasyopāyānāṃ madhye maunaṃ vāṅniyamarūḍhaṃ 
munitvamaham, nānārthaviṣayasya saṃkalpasya api anunmeṣo yatra | sa hi 
paramaḥ samādhiḥ, tasmiṃśca sati saṃkalpamūlāyā vāco 
niyamarūpasya maunasya siddhiriti | tadeva paramaṃ guhyamityuktam | tathā 
jñānavadgrahaṇena nānārthaviṣayāṇi bhinnāni jñānāni gṛhyante | 
teṣāṃ madhye paramārthaikalakṣaṇaikatattvaviṣayasya abhinnasya 
jñānasya prādhānyam | tato nānājñānānāṃ madhye ekasya jñānasya 
yathoktasya prādhānyamanena pratipāditam || 26-38 ||

vibhūtipratipādanamupasaṃharannāha �

yaccāpi sarvabhūtānāṃ bījaṃ tadahamarjuna |
na tadasti vinā yat syānmayā bhūtaṃ carācaram || 39 ||

	kiṃ bahunā vibhūtivistarakathanena | saṃgrahādiyaṃ parā mama 
vibhūtiḥ, yataḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ prākpratipāditaparāparaprakṛtibhedena 
prasṛtānāṃ yadvījam utpattikāraṇaṃ, so'ham | na hi bāhyaṃ kāraṇaṃ 
jagatāṃ kiñcit saṃbhavati manmāyāśaktimātramekaṃ varjayitvetyarthaḥ 
| yataḥ tat iha vastu cetanamacetanaṃ vā nāsti na sattāṃ bhajate, yat mayā 
vinā utpadyate | ataḥ sakalakāraṇābhāsavyatirekeṇa 
pāramārthikamahameva kāraṇaṃ sarvabhūtānāmiti || 39 ||

	mayā ca paramakāraṇena satā 
svamāyāmahimodbhāvitanānābhāvabhedāśritā yā vibhūtayo mama 
saṃbhavanti, tāsāṃ �

p. 239) nānto'sti mama divyānāṃ vibhūtīnāṃ parantapa |
eṣa tūddeśataḥ prokto vibhūtervistaro mayā || 40 ||

	mama saṃbandhinīnāṃ divo 
maddhṛdayākāśādudbhūtānāmaparyantasarvapadārthavyavasthitānāṃ 
vibhūtīnāṃ nijaśaktivijṛmbhamāṇānām antaḥ avadhirna vidyate | eṣa tu 
iha prakaraṇe uddeśataḥ kasyacit dharmasya kīrtanāt vibhūteḥ 
svavibhavasya vistaraḥ prasaraḥ kathitaḥ || 40 ||

	saṃgrahatastu idaṃ svavibhūtivijñānāya bravīmi �

yadyadvibhūtimat sattvaṃ śrīmadūrjitameva vā |
tattadevāvagacchestvaṃ mama tejoṃśasaṃbhavam || 41 ||

	iha sarvalokeṣu yat yat sattvaṃ yatkiñcid vastujātaṃ 
cetanamacetanaṃ vā vibhūtimat vibhūtiyuktam, ata eva śrīmat 
śobhātiśayasaṃpannam ūrjitaṃ tattadarthakriyāsāmarthyayuktaṃ tat tat 
sarvaṃ mama saṃbandhinastejasaḥ parasya prakāśasya ya aṃśo 
leśamātraṃ tatsaṃbhavaṃ tatsamudbhūtaṃ tvamavagaccheḥ 
avajānīyāḥ || 41 ||

prakārāntarāpekṣaṇena vibhūtiniṣṭhāṃ pratipādayitumāha �

athavā bahunoktena kiṃ jñānena tavārjuna |
viṣṭamyāhamidaṃ kṛtsnamekāṃśena sthito jagat || 42 ||

	atha etena tatra tatra varge tat tat vastu madvibhūtimattvena 
sthitamavagaccheti pūrvoktena bahunā prabhūtagranthapratipāditena 
jñānena tathāpratipannena satā kiṃ te prayojanaṃ, na kiñcit kṛtyaṃ 
yataḥ idam anavadhi vicitrabhāvamayaṃ kṛtsnaṃ samastaṃ

p. 240) jagat saptalokīmitaṃ viśvamekena sarvadā sarvatra 
vicitrasāmānyasaṃvidātmanā vedyāṃśavyatirekiṇā aṃśena bhāgena 
avaṣṭabhya ākramya vyāpya ahaṃ sthitaḥ | tadidamanena uktaṃ bhavati tatra 
tatra darśane tattatprakārabhedena pratipāditasya asyajagato dvāvaṃśau 
saṃkṣepataḥ svarūpam | eko vedakaikasvabhāvaḥ sarvadā sarvātmanā 
nirvikāracinmātrasvarūpaḥ, aparo nānārūpatve'pi vedyaikasvabhāvaḥ | 
tatra vedyāṃśaparihāreṇa 
tadābhāsaikakāraṇabhūtavedakāṃśaviśrāntasaṃvidā abhiyuktena 
bhavitavyam, yena madvibhūtiḥ paramā adhigamyate ityom || 42 ||

vibhūtīrā viṣṇoriha sakalasargātiśayagāḥ 
parabrahmaiśvaryāvadhi samadhigantuṃ vyavasitaiḥ |
iyaṃ sākṣādbhaktirbhavati tu viśuddhaiva tribudhai-
rnidhātavyā nityaṃ manasi daśamādhyāyavivṛtiḥ ||

iti śrīrājānakarāmakaṇṭhaviracite vākyārthānvayamātre 
sarvatobhadranāmni bhagavadgītāvivaraṇe daśamo'dhyāyaḥ || 10 ||


atha ekādaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||

	atha adhyāyadaśakena bhagavadupadiṣṭajñānavijñānapūrvaṃ 
samastābhidheyavastuśravaṇamātrādeva bhagavataḥ 
parameśvaratvāvagamāt tasya paramakāraṇatve tadukte vacasi 
yathoktaphalāvisaṃvādakatayā prakṛṣṭopāyatve 
nivṛttasandehamātmānamāvedayan 
śrutimātrapratipannatatsākṣāddarśanecchāpracodito'rjuna uvāca �

madanugrahāya paramaṃ guhyamadhyātmasaṃjñitam |
yat tvayoktaṃ vacastena moho'yaṃ vigato mama || 1 ||

	madanugrahāya māmanugrahītuṃ nijayā paraśaktyā 
prabodhayituṃ tvayā parameśvareṇa svayaṃpratipāditasvabhāvena 
yadvacaḥ vacanamadhyāyadaśakātmakaṃ vākyam adhyātmasaṃjñitam 
ātmaviṣayajñānamayam, ata eva paramaṃ guhyaṃ prakṛṣṭaṃ rahasyaṃ 
uktaṃ upadiṣṭam, tena hetubhūtena ayaṃ sarvajanakroḍīkāreṇa sthito 
mohaḥ iti pratipāditaparamārthapratipattyanyathāpratītilakṣaṇamajñānaṃ 
mama nivṛtto vyapagataḥ iti || 1 ||

tatra hetumāha �

bhavāpyayau hi bhūtānāṃ śrutau vistarato mayā |
tvattaḥ kamalapatrākṣa māhātmyamapi cāvyayam || 2 ||

	yasmāt tvattaḥ paramakāraṇādekasmāt samastānāṃ 
sthāvarajaṅgamānāṃ bhūtānāṃ bhavāpyayau utpattilayau vistarataḥ 
vaitatyena mayā śrutau śrutimātreṇa pratipāditau, natu avadhāritau | apica 
avyayaṃ nityaṃ māhātmyaṃ niratiśayaiśvaryalakṣaṇaṃ mahattvaṃ 
śrutam |

ahaṃ kṛtsnasya jagataḥ prabhavaḥ pralayastathā |
mattaḥ parataraṃ nānyat kiñcidasti -------------- || (7|7)

p. 242) ityāderavisaṃvādino bhagavadvacanādeva bhagavāneva

viśuddhacinmātrasvabhāvāpracyuteradbhūtasya aiśvaryasya ekāspadamiti 
ca mayā pratipannamityarthaḥ iti || 2 ||

	śrutimātreṇa etat mayā pratipannaṃ, natu sākṣādanubhūtamiti 
tadarthamāha �

evametad yathāttha tvamātmānaṃ parameśvaram |
draṣṭumicchāmi te rūpamaiśvaraṃ puruṣottama || 3 ||

	yathā yena prakāreṇa pūrvoktena tvayā ātmānaṃ svarūpaṃ 
parameśvaraṃ brahmādīnāmapi īśvarāṇāmīśitṛtvāt pravaraṃ 
prabhum ātthakathayasi | etat vastu evam anena prakāreṇa sthitam, na atra me 
bhrāntirityarthaḥ | tata eva te rūpaṃ divyaṃ vapuḥ aiśvaraṃ īśvarasya 
tava saṃbandhi draṣṭuṃ sākṣāt nirūpayitum icchāmi abhikāṅkṣāmi | 
anena hi adhunā dṛṣyamānena puruṣākṛtinā rūpeṇa tat paraṃ rūpaṃ 
vāṅmātreṇa mama kevalaṃ kathitaṃ yatra idamekasmin sarvameva sthitam, 
tadidānīṃ bhagavadvacanapratyayāt mayā satyatastathā vidyamānatayā 
pratipannam | ataḥ puruṣottama paramapuruṣatvatprasādāt pratyakṣataḥ 
prekṣitumabhikāṅkṣāmīti || 3 ||

kintu �

manyase yadi tacchakyaṃ mayā draṣṭumiti prabho |
yogīśvara tato me tvaṃ darśayātmānamavyayam || 4 ||

	he prabho parameśvara tat tathāvidhaṃ tāttvikaṃ rūpaṃ 
paramayogibhirapi prārthyamānadarśanaṃ mayā manuṣyamātreṇa 
draṣṭuṃ sākṣāt prekṣituṃ śakyaṃ sahyamiti yadi manyase jānāsi, yadi 
mama iyatīṃ yogyatāṃ saṃbhāvayasi, tatastvaṃ yogīśvara  
sahajasiddhaniratiśayayogatvāt

p. 243) prayatnasādhyayogānāṃ sarveṣāṃ yogyantarāṇāṃ prabho me 
mahyaṃ avyayam anādinidhanaṃ ātmānaṃ svarūpaṃ darśaya 
pratyakṣīkuru iti || 4 ||

itthamabhyarthitaḥ prasādābhimukho bhagavānuvāca �

paśya me pārtha rūpāṇi śataśo'tha sahasraśaḥ |
nānāvidhāni divyāni nānāvarṇākṛtīni ca || 5 ||

paśyādityān vasūn rudrānaśvinau marutastathā |
bahūnyadṛṣṭapūrvāṇi paśyāścaryāṇi pāṇḍava || 6 ||

ihaikasthaṃ jagat kṛtsnaṃ paśyādya sacarācaram |
mama dehe guḍākeśa yaccānyad draṣṭumicchasi || 7 ||

	he pārtha mama ekasyaiva paramārthasataḥ paramātmanaḥ 
svaiśvaryātiśayāt svaśaktitvena saṃbaddhāni asaṃkhyātāni rūpāṇi 
ākṛtiviśeṣātmakāni paśya avalokaya | kīdṛśāni | nānāvidhāni 
vicitrāṇi, ata eva nānāvarṇākṛtīni vividhāvabhāsasaṃsthānāni, tathā 
divyāni amānuṣīyāṇi, iti sāmānyena upakramya viśiṣṭāni 
uddeṣṭumāha ādityān dvādaśasaṃkhyān devaviśeṣān | tathā vasūn 
aṣṭasaṃkhyān rudrān ca ekādaśasaṃkhyān aśvinau dasrau marutaśca 
ekānnapañcāśataṃ devaviśeṣān paśya iti prādhānyāt devatārūpāṇi 
uddeśamātreṇa uktvā, saṃkṣepata āha etāni tāvat tvayā 
suralokanivāsinā satā dṛṣṭāni, yānyapi bahūni aparimeyāṇi 
adṛṣṭapūrvāṇi na kadācidālokitāni āścaryāṇi adbhutāni, tānyapi 
sarvāṇi paśya kiṃ bahunā iha mama saṃbandhini dehe ekasminneva rūpe 
ekatra sthitaṃ kṛtsnaṃ akhilaṃ carācararūpatvāt saṃkṣepeṇa 
dviprakāraṃ, yadanyat kiñcit

p. 244) yathābhilaṣitaṃ draṣṭumabhivāñchasi, tadapi ekasthameva paśya 
ityekasyaiva paramārthasataḥ paramakāraṇasya 
svamāyāśaktyavabhāsitānantānyonyabhinnabhāvātmakatvamidamatyad-

ata eva atyadbhutatvādasya arthasya �

na tu māṃ śakyase draṣṭumanenaiva svacakṣuṣā |
divyaṃ dadāni te cakṣuḥ paśya me yogamaiśvaram || 8 ||

	anenaiva martyabhāvāt parimitadarśinaiva cakṣuṣā 
golakādhiṣṭhānayā dṛkśaktyā punaḥ māṃ 
pradarśayiṣyamāṇāścaryarūpaṃ draṣṭuṃ ālokayituṃ na śakyase na 
kṣamo bhaviṣyasi | tato divyaṃ sarvadarśitvaśaktiyogāt 
paramātmarūpaparamākāśabhavaṃ prakṛṣṭajñānātmakaṃ cakṣuḥ 
netraṃ te dadāni pāramaiśvaryā svaśaktyā niṣprayatnasyaiva 
āvirbhāvayeyam | tena mama aiśvaraṃ īśvarasya saṃbandhi 
pradarśayiṣyamāṇaṃ rūpaṃ paśya sākṣāt nirūpayeti || 8 ||

	atha bhagavatā parameṇa samādhinā niratiśayanijarūpa[darśana] 
yogyatādānāya arjuno yathā anugṛhītaḥ, tathaiva pūrva 
bhagavadvyāsamuniprasādādāsāditadivyacakṣurdhṛtarāṣṭrāya 
pratipādayituṃ

p. 245)	gatārthametat ślokatrayam | kintu ekameva aiśvaraṃ

	ata eva ekasya paramārthādvaitacitprakāśātmano vaiśvarūpyeṇa

evamanuttaraprakāśātmani �

p. 246) jagat arjunaḥ apaśyat sākṣāt kṛtvān, iti saṃjayodivyena cakṣuṣā

kimityāha �

paśyāmi devāṃstava deva dehe 
sarvāṃstathā bhūtaviśeṣasaṃghān |
brahmāṇamīśaṃ kamalāsanastha-
mṛṣīṃśca sarvānuragāṃśca dīptān || 15 ||

anyānapi carācarāṇāṃ bhūtaviśeṣāṇāṃ praṇyantarāṇāṃ

samastaviśvadarśanaṃ bhagavadvapuṣi pūrvaṃ pratipādya, krameṇa 
tadviśeṣadarśanaṃ pratipādayitumāha brahmāṇaṃ prajāpatiṃ īśaṃ 
prabhuṃ kamalāsanasthaṃ puṇḍarīkaviṣṭarasaṃniviṣṭaṃ paśyāmi, 
ityanena brahmalokāntamūrdhvabhuvanadarśanamuktavān | ṛṣīṃśca 
sarvān paśyāmi ityanena prādhānyāpekṣayā

paśyāmi ityaena

dīptānalārkadyutimaprameyam || 17 ||

avataraṇikayaiva vyākhyātatātparyametat || 17 ||

	ata eva parameśvarapratipādakāni viśeṣaṇāni tasya rūpasya 
pratipādayitumāha �

tvamakṣaraṃ paramaṃ veditavyaṃ 
tvamasya viśvasya paraṃ nidhānam |
p. 248) tvamavyayaḥ sāttvatadharmagoptā
sanātanastvaṃ puruṣo mato me || 18 ||

	akṣaraṃ svarūpapracyutilakṣaṇakṣobharahitaṃ yat paramaṃ 
niruttaraṃ vedyaṃ jñeyaṃ tattvaṃ, tat bhavān | tathā asya 
anantaprapañcasya jagataḥ paraṃ prakṛṣṭaṃ nidhānaṃ tvayi etat sarvaṃ 
jagat niviśate | nanu asti jagato nidhānaṃ pradhānaṃ, yatra tat pralaye 
nidhīyate | satyamasti tat pradhānam | tadapi paramakāraṇe bhagavati nilīyate, 
tata eva ca prabhavatīti pradhānasya api nidhānatvāt tvaṃ paramaṃ 
nidhānaṃ jagata iti uktam | tathā kālāvacchedāviṣayatvāt avyayaḥ 
akṣayaḥ sāttvatadharmagoptā tvam | saditi brahmanāma yadvakṣyati 

oṃ tatsaditi nirdeśo brahmaṇastrividhaḥ smṛtaḥ | (17|23)

iti | tat vidyate yasya sa sattvān parameśvaraḥ paramaṃ brahma, sa devatā 
yeṣāṃ te sāttvatā brahmavidaḥ, teṣāṃ dharmo 
yathāpratipāditajñānakriyāsamuccayānuṣṭhānarūpaḥ sadācāraḥ, 
tasya goptā kālāpacayavaśāt tirobhavataḥ punaḥ punaḥ praṇayanāt 
tadvādhakavighnavighaṭanāt ca tvameva rakṣiteti || 18 ||

ata eva �

anādimahyāntamanantavīrya-
manantabāhuṃ śaśisūryanetram |
paśyāmi tvāṃ dīptahutāśavaktraṃ 
svatejasā viśvamidaṃ tapantam || 19 ||

	anantatvādādyantamadhyarahitam, avacchedyasya hi vastunaḥ 
ādyādivibhāgavattvam | itthaṃ jagatprapañcaviracanasāmarthyaṃ 
vīryamanantaṃ

p. 249) na kadācit nivṛttaṃ yasya, tam | tathā sarvakṣetrajñānāṃ 
tattatkriyāsādhanabhūtā ye'nantā bahavaste paramārthikasyaiva kartuḥ 
parameśvarasya saṃbandhina iti anantabāhutvam | tathā kṣetrajñaśarīre 
cakṣurbhyā niyataviṣayaprakāśanaṃ kriyate iti śaśisūryanetratvam | 
tathā kriyāvidbhirvividhairyajñakriyāviśeṣairvaiśvānaro viśvamūrtiḥ 
parameśvara eva ekastarpyate iti tasyaiva dīpto hutāśo'gnirvaktramiti uktam |

dyotayatītyevaṃvidhaṃ viśvātmānaṃ tvāṃ paśyāmīti saṃbandha iti || 
19 ||

	atha sarvaśakteḥ parameśvarasya 
kurusainyasaṃhāraprasṛtakālākhyaśaktiyuktatvāt ghoratvaviśiṣṭaṃ 
rūpaṃ varṇayitumāha �

dyāvāpṛthivyoridamantaraṃ hi

	yasmāt mahatā rūpeṇa gaganabhūmyantarālaṃ sarvāśca 
kakubhaḥ tvayaikena vyāptāḥ pūritāḥ, tadidamīdṛgvidhaṃ tava ghoraṃ 
rūpaṃ dṛṣṭvā lokatrayaṃ trailokyanivāsitaddarśanāyogyadevādirūpaṃ 
pravyathitaṃ prakarṣeṇa bhītam | evaṃ mahātman iti 
rūpamahattāpekṣamāmantraṇamiti || 20 ||

	teṣāṃ devādīnāṃ tathāvidharūpadarśanabhayotpannāśceṣṭāḥ 
pratipādayitumāha �

amī hi tvā surasaṃghā viśanti 
kecidbhītāḥ prāñjalayo gṛṇanti |
p. 250) svastīti coktvaiva maharṣisaṃghāḥ 
stuvanti tvāṃ stutibhiḥ puṣkalābhiḥ || 21 ||

	amī yasmāt tvāṃ duṣṭakṣayāya āviṣkṛtadāruṇākāraṃ 
śakrādidevasamūhāḥ viśanti tvayi eva līyante | kecicca bhītāḥ 
santrastāḥ santaḥ prāñjalayaḥ viracitakarapuṭāḥ gṛṇanti stutīḥ paṭhanti | 
ataśca maharṣisaṃghāḥ munipravarasārthā jagatkṣemāya 
svastiśabdamuccārya puṣkalābhiḥ mukhyārthatvāt saṃbaddhābhiḥ 
stutibhiḥ tattaccaritodīraṇaiḥ tvāṃ stuvanti praśaṃsantīti || 21 ||

kiṃca �

rudrādityā vasavo ye ca sādhyā 
viśve'śvinau marutaścoṣmapāśca |
gandharvayakṣāsurasiddhasaṃghā 
vīkṣante tvāṃ vismitāścaiva sarve || 22 ||

rudrādayo devaviśeṣā vyākhyātāḥ | ūṣmapākhyāḥ pitaraḥ | 
gandharvādayaḥ prasiddhāḥ | ete sarve devayonitvāt 
jñānaviśeṣasaṃbhave sati tvāṃ vīkṣante paśyanti ghoratvādapūrvasya 
rūpasya vismayāviṣṭāḥ iti || 22 ||

	yādṛśaṃ rūpaṃ dṛṣṭvā te evaṃvidhāḥ saṃvṛttāḥ, tat 
varṇayitumāha �

rūpaṃ mahatte bahuvaktranetraṃ 
mahābāho bahubāhūrupādam |

dṛṣṭvā lokāḥ pravyathitāstathāham || 23 ||

spaṣṭārthaḥ ślokaḥ || 23 ||

p. 251) tadrūpadarśanena viśeṣataḥ svāmavasthāṃ darśayitumāha-

nabhaḥspṛśaṃ dīptamanekavarṇaṃ
vyāttānanaṃ dīptaviśālanetram |
dṛṣṭvā hi tvā pravyathitāntarātmā
dhṛtiṃ na vindāmi śamaṃ ca viṣṇo || 24 ||

	ayamapi śloko'vagatārtha eva | kintu 
divyacakṣurdānalakṣaṇabhagavadanugrahakṣaṇe dṛṣṭvā divyena jñānena 
anubhūya, adhunā martyadharmāveśe sati tat tvadrūpaṃ smaran 
bhītacittaḥ san dhairyaṃ nirākulacittattvaṃ na prāpnomi, ityato 
darśanakāle eva na bhayādīnāṃ saṃbhavo veditavyaḥ | anyatra api 
vartamānākhyātaprayoge pūrvadṛṣṭasmaraṇaṃ kāraṇatvena 
boddhavyamiti || 24 ||

kiṃ ca �

daṃṣṭrākarālāni ca te mukhāni
dṛṣṭvaiva kālānalasaṃnibhāni |
diśo na jāne na labhe ca śarma

	sphuṭārthametadvākyam | kintu bhagavato 
daṃṣṭrākarālabahumukhatvaṃ 
saṃhārapravṛttabahuvikṛtakṛtāntapuruṣāveśāditi || 25 ||

	ata eva kṛtāntapuruṣākrāntānāṃ kurvādīnāṃ ceṣṭitaṃ 
varṇayitumāha �

amī sarve dhṛtarāṣṭrasya putrāḥ 
sarvaiḥ sahaivāvanipālasaṃghaiḥ |
bhīṣmo droṇaḥ sūtaputrastathāsau 
sahāsmadīyairapi yodhamukhyaiḥ || 26 ||

p. 252) vaktrāṇi te tvaramāṇā viśanti 
daṃṣṭrākarālāni bhayānakāni |
kecid vilagnā daśanāntareṣu
saṃdṛśyante cūrṇitairuttamāṅgaiḥ || 27 ||

nānārūpaiḥ puruṣairbādhyamānā
viśanti te vaktramacintyarūpam |
yaudhiṣṭhirā dhārtarāṣṭrāśca yodhāḥ 
śastraiḥ kṛttā vividhaiḥ sarva eva || 28 ||

tvattejasā nihatā nūnamete
tathāhīme tvaccharīre praviṣṭāḥ |
yathā nadīnāṃ bahavo'mbuvegāḥ 
samudramevābhimukhā dravanti || 29 ||

tathā tavāmī naralokavīrā
viśanti vaktrāṇyabhito jvalanti |
yathā pradīptaṃ jvalanaṃ pataṅgā
viśanti nāśāya samṛddhavegāḥ || 30 ||

tathaiva nāśāya viśanti lokā-
stavāpi vaktrāṇi samiddhavegāḥ |

	etat ślokapañcakaṃ nigadavyākhyātaprāyam | kintu tava vakrāṇi 
viśantīti bahuvacanena daṃṣṭrākarālāni ityādiviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭāni 
vaktrāṇi upakramya acintyarūpaṃ vaktraṃ tava viśanti iti yaduktaṃ, tat 
kālapuruṣasaṃbandhinyo ghorākṛtiviśeṣarūpā nānāvidhāḥ śaktaya 
ekasyāṃ tvadīyāyāṃ parasyāṃ sāmānyaśaktau pariṇamanti, iti 
bhagavataḥ sarvaśaktyāśrayatvapratipādanaparaṃ boddhavyam, yata 
ekavaktraśabdena pratipāditāyāḥ 
sāmānyaśakteravyapadeśyatvādacintyarūpatvena viśeṣaṇamuktam, 
tathāhīme tvaccharīre iti anena tadeva sphuṭīkṛtam | atra ślokapañcake 
sārdhe nānārūpaiḥ ityādi tvat śarīre praviṣṭāḥ ityantaṃ 
ślokasārdhakaṃ ślokasāmyārthaṃ kecit na paṭhanti | kaścit tu tvattejasā 
nihatāḥ ityādi ślokārdhamanenaiva abhiprāyeṇa na paṭhati | iha tu 
bahupustakadarśanāt bhārate ca tatra sārdhaślokapraṇayanāt vyāsamuneḥ 
ślokapañcakaṃ sārdhameva paṭhitamiti || 30 ||

	atha tathāvidhāṃ ghorarūpāṃ bhagavataḥ śaktiṃ dṛṣṭvā 
tattattvajijñāsārthamidamāha �

lelihyase grasamānaḥ samantā-
llokān samagrān vadanairjvaladbhiḥ || 31 ||

tejobhirāpūrya jagat samagraṃ
bhāsastavogrāḥ pratapanti viṣṇo |
ākhyāhi me ko bhavānugrarūpo 
namo'stu te devavara prasīda |
vijñātumicchāmi bhavantamādyaṃ
na hi prajānāmi tava pravṛttim || 32 ||

	imān samagrān samastān lokān samantāt sarvataḥ 
jvaladbhirvadanaiḥ sānalairmukhaiḥ grasamānaḥ kavalayan lelihyase 
abhīkṣṇaṃ lekṣi āsvādayasi | ata eva tavogrā bhāsaḥ ghoradīptayaḥ 
samastaṃ viśvaṃ tejobhiḥ vyāpya pratapanti dedīpyante |

p. 254) yata evaṃ paramapraśāntacinmātrasvabhāvasya tava ghoraṃ 
rūpaṃ paśyāmi, tato devavara sakalasurottama tubhyaṃ namaḥ prasādaṃ 
ca kuru | ko bhavānugrarūpaḥ saṃpravṛtta iti mahyamācakṣva | yasmāt 
tava evaṃvidhaghorarūpagrahaṇe pravṛttiṃ prasaraṃ na jānāmi tattvato na 
avagacchāmi, ata ādyaṃ paramakāraṇaṃ bhavantaṃ vijñātuṃ viśeṣeṇa 
boddhum icchāmi iti || 32 ||
	kena hetunā kaḥ san tvamevaṃ ghorarūpāṃ śaktimāviṣkaroṣīti 
vijñapto bhagavānuvāca �

kālo'smi lokakṣayakṛt pravṛddhāṃ-
llokān samāhartumiha pravṛttaḥ |
ṛte'pi tvāṃ na bhaviṣyanti sarve 
ye'vasthitāḥ pratyanīkeṣu yodhāḥ || 33 ||

	kālo'smi nirupaplavasvarūpāpracyutasyaiva 
vijṛmbhamāṇaśāntograghorarūpasamagraśakteḥ kālākhyā ghorā mama 
iyaṃ śaktirityarthaḥ | śakteśca śaktimadabhedādahameva kāla iti uktam | 
kīdṛśaḥ kālaḥ | sakalalokasaṃhāraikavyāpāraḥ | adhunā tu iha 
pṛthivyāṃ pravṛddhān madodriktān pṛthivībhārabhūtān lokān imān 
samāhartuṃ kṣayaṃ netuṃ pravṛttaḥ prasṛtaḥ | tataḥ ṛte'pi tvāṃ vināpi 
paripūrṇapāpātmakatvāt sarva ete na bhaviṣyanti tvayi ayotsyamāne'pi 
vinaṅkṣyantyeva | ke te | yodhāḥ saṃprahāriṇaḥ pratyanīkeṣu 
pratipakṣasainyeṣu ye'vasthitāḥ || 33 ||

	yata evamete saṃplavantyeva avaśyaṃbhāvinā vināśena 
āghrātāḥ 

tasmāt tvamuttiṣṭha yaśo labhasva 
jitvā śatrūn bhuṅkṣva rājyaṃ samṛddham |

nimittamātraṃ bhava savyasācin || 34 ||

kālātmikayā macchaktyā prāgeva prastanetān nighnan tvaṃ 
kāraṇamātraṃ saṃpadyasva, iti vyaktārtha eva eṣa ślokaḥ || 34 ||

	droṇādayaśca apratimaparākramatvāt durjayā iti ca mā maṃsthā 
ityāha �

droṇaṃ ca bhīṣmaṃ ca jayadrathaṃ ca

	droṇādayo'pi sakalalokaikavīrāḥ kālasya durjayatvāt mayā 
vyāpāditā iti tān sujayān matvā mā bhaiṣīriti tātparyam || 35 ||

	evaṃ bhagavadukterarjunāvasthāṃ dhṛtarāṣṭrāya saṃjaya uvāca

sagadgadaṃ bhītabhītaḥ praṇamya || 36 ||

spaṣṭārthaḥ ślokaḥ || 36 ||

kṛṣṇaṃ kimarjunaḥ prāhetyāha �

sthāne hṛṣikeśa tava prakīrtyā
jagat prahṛṣyatyanurajyate ca |

p. 256) itthaṃ sarvaśakteḥ sarveśvarasya tava prakīrtyā praśaṃsayā 
sarvaṃ jagat praharṣaṃ bhajate, ata eva ca tvayi anuraktaṃ bhavati | tathā 
tadvyāpakāni rakṣāṃsi yātudhānā bhayāt yatastataḥ palāyante 
siddhasaṃghāḥ devaviśeṣasamūhāḥ samastāḥ praṇamanti ca | tat sarvaṃ 
sthāne yuktam || 37 ||

	tvadanugrahādeva sākṣātkṛtaṃ paraṃ bhagavatsvarūpaṃ 
parāmṛśan punarapi āha �

kasmācca te na nameran mahātman 
garīyase brahmaṇo'pyādikartre |
ananta deveśa jagannivāsa
tvamakṣaraṃ sadasataḥ paraṃ yat || 38 ||

	athavā kasmāt kutaśca hetorete siddhādayastubhyaṃ na nameyuḥ 
mahātman paramapuruṣa | kīdṛśāya te tubhyam | brahmaṇo'pi 
prajāpaterapi garīyase paramagurave yata ādikartre ādyāya sraṣṭre tasyāpi 
janmahetave | ananta aparicchinnamāhātmya deveśa sakalasuranātha 
jagannivāsa samastabhuvanaikanidhāna yatastvaṃ sataḥ 
indriyagocarādidantāvadhāraṇīyāt vastuno yat paraṃ vyatiriktam, tathā 
asataśca abhāvarūpādapi yat paramakṣaram avikāraṃ niruttaraṃ 
paramārthasatattvam, ato vedakaikasvabhāvamananyavedyaṃ 
paramakāraṇaṃ tvāṃ kathaṃ te siddhā viditavedyāḥ santo na 
namasyeyuriti tātparyamiti || 38 ||

atha yathānubhūtabhagavaddharmānudīrayati �

tvamādidevaḥ puruṣaḥ purāṇa-

p. 257) vettāsi vedyaṃ ca paraṃ ca dhāma

bahuśo'pi vyākhyātārthā ete bhagavadviṣayāḥ śabdāḥ || 39 ||

kiṃca �

vāyuryamo'gnirvaruṇaḥ śaśāṅkaḥ 
prajāpatistvaṃ prapitāmahaśca |
anādimānapratimaprabhāvaḥ
sarveśvaraḥ sarvamahāvibhūtiḥ || 40 ||

	sarvebhya aiśvaryaguṇayuktebhyo mahatī prakṛṣṭā vibhūtiḥ 
śaktivijṛmbhā yasya tathāvidho hi etādṛśo bhagavān sarveśvaraḥ ata eva 
apratimaprabhāvaḥ ananyasāmānyamāhātmyaḥ, tathā anādimān 
ādirvidyate yasya sa ādimān, na ādimānanādimān sarvaprathama 
ityarthaḥ | evaṃvidhaḥ tvam eva vāyvādayo daivataviśeṣāḥ | yathā ca 
vāyvādīnāmīśvarasvarūpādabhinnatvaṃ, tathā sarvamādau 
vyākhyātameveti || 40 ||

parameśvarasya sarvākāratvaṃ parāmṛśannāha �

namo namaste'stu sahasrakṛtvaḥ
punaśca bhūyo'pi namo namaste |
namaḥ purastādatha pṛṣṭhataste
namo'stu te sarvata eva sarva || 41 ||

sarvaṃ spaṣṭārthametat || 41 ||

kiṃca �

nahi tvadanyaḥ kaścidapīha deva
lokatraye dṛśyate'cintyakarmā |
p. 258) anantavīryāmitavikramastvaṃ
sarvaṃ samāpnoṣi tato'si sarvaḥ || 42 ||

	atra api na kiṃcidavyākhyātamasti | sarvaṃ svaśaktyā samyak 
vyāpnoṣi yataḥ, tataḥ sarvātmakastvameva | vīryam utsāhaśaktiḥ | 
parākramaḥ kriyāśaktiḥ || 42 ||

	atha vijñātabhagavatparasvabhāvatvāt pūrvaṃ 
puruṣatvapratipattyā bhagavati vihitamatikramaṃ 
manyamānastatkṣāntyarthaṃ ślokacatuṣṭayamāha �

sakheti matvā prasabhaṃ yaduktaṃ
he kṛṣṇa he yādava he sakhe ca |
ajānatā mahimānaṃ tavemaṃ 
mayā pramādāt praṇayena vāpi || 43 ||

yaccāvahāsārthamasatkṛto'si
vihāraśayyāsanabhojaneṣu |
eko'thavāpyacyuta satsamakṣaṃ 
tat kṣāmaye tvāmahamaprameyam || 44 ||

pitāsi lokasya carācarasya
tvamasya pūjyaśca gururgarīyān |
na tvatsamo'styabhyadhikaḥ kuto'nyo 
lokatraye'pyapratimaprabhāvaḥ || 45 ||

tasmātpraṇamya praṇidhāya kāyaṃ 
prasādaye tvāmahamīśamīḍyam |
piteva putrasya sakheva sakhyuḥ
priyaḥ priyasyārhasi deva soḍhum || 46 ||

p. 259) subodhametat | kintu sakhā tvaṃ mama iti, matvā yat 
yādavādiśabdena āmantrito'si, yo'pi anyo vyatikramaḥ kṛtaḥ, 
tatsarvamajānato bhagavanmāhātmyaṃ tadānīmajñātavato me 
kṣantavyamiti saṃbandhaḥ | satsamakṣaṃ iti sabhāyāṃ vā satāṃ 
sannidhau | lokatraye'pi tava samo nāsti, kuto'nyo'dhiko bhaviṣyatīti || 46 ||
	atha vijñātamahimānamāvedayituṃ ślokatrayamāha �

divyāni karmāṇi tavādbhutāni
pūrvāṇi pūrve'pyṝṣayaḥ smaranti |
nānyo'sti kartā jagatastvameko
dhātā vidhātā ca vibhurbhavaśca || 47 ||

tavādbhutaṃ kiṃ nu bhavedasahyaṃ
kiṃ vā śakyaṃ parataḥ kīrtayiṣye |
kartāsya sarvasya yataḥ svayaṃ vai 
vibho tataḥ sarvamidaṃ tvameva || 48 ||

atyadbhutaṃ karma na duṣkaraṃ te
karmopamānaṃ nahi vidyate te |
na te guṇānāṃ parimāṇamasti 
na tejaso nāpi balasya narddheḥ || 49 ||

pūrve purātanā api sarvajñāḥ ṛṣayastava karmāṇi smaranti ata eva 
teṣāmṛṣīṇāmapi tāni pūrvāṇi iti | jagatsargapralayādīnāṃ 
bhagavatkarmaṇāmanādiprabandhapravṛttatvapratipādanaparametat pūrvaṃ 
vyākhyātaprāyam | anyat subodham | kintu dhātā dhārayitā vidhātā 
sraṣṭā, tava sraṣṭavyaḥ saṃsāraḥ | kimadbhutam āścaryarūpaṃ

p. 260) karma svecchāmātropakaraṇasya ekasya kartustava asahyaṃ, ata eva 
sahakāryādinirapekṣatvāt parataḥ svātmavyatiriktāt kutaścit śakyaṃ 
sādhyaṃ kiṃvā te kathayiṣyāmi | itthaṃ ca ananyāpekṣatayā yatastvaṃ 
sarvasya vastujātasya nirmātā, tato hetvantarābhāvāt tvamevedaṃ sarvaṃ 
tvacchaktireva itthaṃ cakāstītyarthaḥ | tataśca pratyāścaryarūpamapi 
karma na te duṣkaraṃ ityādi spaṣṭamiti || 49 ||

	atha 
anugrahakṣaṇānubhūtabhagavatsvarūpāvyatirekasamādherniṣkrāntas-
tathāvidhamatyugraṃ bhagavatsvarūpaṃ 
manuṣyadharmapratyāpatteściraṃ draṣṭumaśaktaḥ prāktanameva 
didṛkṣamāṇa idamāha �

adṛṣṭapūrvaṃ hṛṣito'smi dṛṣṭvā
bhayena ca pravyathitaṃ mano me |
tadeva me darśaya devarūpaṃ
prasīda deveśa jagannivāsa || 50 ||

kirīṭinaṃ gadinaṃ cakrahasta-
micchāmi tvāṃ draṣṭumahaṃ tathaiva |
tenaiva rūpeṇa caturbhujena
sahasrabāho bhava viśvamūrte || 51 ||

spaṣṭārthametat || 51 ||

atha evaṃvidhamukto bhagavānuvāca �

mayā prasannena tavārjunedaṃ
rūpaṃ paraṃ darśitamātmayogāt |
tejomayaṃ viśvamanantamādyaṃ
yanme tvadanyena na dṛṣṭapūrvam || 52 ||

subodhametat | kiṃ tu ātmayogāt darśitam ityetadevaṃ

p. 261) boddhavyam yadā anugrahapareṇa mayā tvamātmani parasmin 
svabhāve yogaṃ samādhiṃ prāpitaḥ, tadā etadrūpaṃ tava 
pratyakṣīkṛtaṃ, vyatiriktatvena asya pārameśvarasya svarūpasya 
draṣṭumaśakyatvāt || 52 ||

ata eva āha �

na vedayajñādhigamairna dānai-
rnaca kriyābhirna tapobhirugraiḥ |
evaṃrūpaṃ śakyamahaṃ nṛloke 
draṣṭuṃ tvadanyena kurupravīra || 53 ||

mā te vyathā mā ca vimūḍhatā bhūd-
dṛṣṭvā rūpaṃ ghoramugraṃ mamedam |
vyapetabhīḥ prītamanāḥ punastvaṃ
tadeva me rūpamidaṃ prapaśya || 54 ||

	vedānāṃ yajñānāṃ bahutvādadhigamabahutvam, 
adhigamaśabdena ca adhyayanaśravaṇādayo guṇāḥ pratipāditāḥ | 
dānakriyātapaḥśabdaistadanuṣṭhānaṃ, kriyāśabdaśca 
anuṣṭhīyamānabrahmacaryādikarmaviśeṣavācakaḥ | yataśca 
evaṃvidhasadācāravyavasthitairapi 
parajñānaśūnyatvādaśakyadarśanametat paramaṃ mama rūpaṃ 
madanugrahādeva unmīlitadivyacakṣuṣā tvayā dṛṣṭam | tato 
bhayamohādīnāṃ ko'vasaraḥ, pratyuta 
matsvarūpanityānusandhānāvahitātmanā tvayā tadeva smartavyamiti 
abhiprāyaḥ | ghoraṃ kālapuruṣādyākṛtiyogāt | ugraṃ 
saṃhārādikarmaṇā | śiṣṭaṃ spaṣṭam || 54 ||

	atha etat dhṛtarāṣṭrāya saṃjaya uvāca �

ityarjunaṃ vāsudevastathoktvā
svakaṃ rūpaṃ darśayāmāsa bhūyaḥ |
p. 262) āśvāsayāmāsa ca bhītamenaṃ
bhūtvā punaḥ saumyavapurmahātmā || 55 ||

	spaṣṭārthametat | kintu mahātmā viśvarūpaḥ san saumyavapuḥ

evaṃ pratyāgataprakṛtirarjuna uvāca �
