spaṣṭametat || 56 ||

arjunāśvāsanāya bhagavānuvāca �

sudurdarśamidaṃ rūpaṃ dṛṣṭvānasi yanmama |
devā apyasya rūpasya nityaṃ darśanakāṅkṣiṇaḥ || 57 ||

nāhaṃ vaidairna tapasā na dānena na cejyayā |
śakya evaṃvidho draṣṭuṃ dṛṣṭvānasi māṃ yathā || 58 ||

nigadavyākhyātaprāyametat | yataḥ 
sargāditattatsvādhikāravyagracittatvādaparāmṛṣṭasvabhāvā brahmādayo 
devā apyasya rūpasya sadā sākṣātkāramabhikāṅkṣanti, natu labhante 
tataḥ sutarāṃ sarveṇa kenacit duṣkaradarśanametat yat tvayā 
matprasādāvirbhūtaparajñānacakṣuṣā dṛṣṭam | ato yathā māṃ tvaṃ 
tattvataḥ sākṣātkṛtavān, tathā dānādikriyāsaktaiḥ draṣṭum 
aśakyo'hamiti || 57-58 ||

kena tarhi upāyena śakyo'smītyāha �

bhaktyā tvananyayā śakyo hyahamevaṃvidho'rjuna |
jñātuṃ draṣṭuṃ ca tattvena praveṣṭuṃ ca parantapa || 59 ||

p. 263) bhaktyā madicchayaiva paradaivasargābhijātajanasulabhayā 
paricaryayā, tatprabhāvādeva āvirbhūtasamyagjñānatvāt 
avidyamānamadvyatiriktabhajanīyaviṣayatve sati ananyayā | jñātuṃ 
avisaṃvādinā avagamena boddhuṃ, tathā samyagjñānalakṣaṇena 
upāyena draṣṭuṃ gocarīkartuṃ, tathā 
tādṛśopāyapariśīlanābhyāsātiśayāt praveṣṭuṃ ātmatayā 
avagāḍhum evaṃvidhaḥ yathā tvayā anubhūtaḥ śakyo'ham na 
anyairupāyaiḥ | tvaṃ ca parasya asya rūpasya sākṣāddarśanāt

sudustaradvaitamohasāgarāduttīrṇaḥ samāśvasihi | kutastava 
idānīmadṛṣṭaparamārthajanocitairbhayādibhirdoṣaiḥ saṃbandhaḥ 
ityarthaḥ || 59 ||

etameva arthaṃ sidhāntīkurvannadhyāyamupasaṃharati �

matkarmakṛnmatparamo madbhaktaḥ saṅgavarjitaḥ |
nirvairaḥ sarvabhūteṣu yaḥ sa māmeti pāṇḍava || 60 ||

	ya evaṃguṇaviśiṣṭo'nyo'pi kaścidabhiyuktaḥ sa māmeti 
madabhedalakṣaṇāṃ siddhimadhigacchati | kīdṛśaḥ | matkarmakṛt 
śāstracoditāni tāni tāni varṇāśramakramāgatāni karmāṇi karoti, kiṃtu 
madarthaṃ yathopapattyā matprāptaye, natu phalāntarasiddhaye | ata eva 
matparamaḥ ahameva upādeyatayā prakṛṣṭo yasya | tataśca 
madvyatiriktasamastaheyopādeyavastuviraktacittatvāt madbhaktaḥ mamaiva 
bhaktaḥ, sarvāsu avasthāsu mayi eva samarpitāntaḥ karaṇaḥ | tathā sa hi 
sarva bhūteṣu carācareṣu bhāveṣu saṅgavarjitaḥ niḥsaṅgaḥ rāgarahito 
nirvairaḥ dveṣavarjitaḥ | yo hi atattvatayā rāgadveṣaviṣayatvena 
sarvasaṃsāribhiḥ parigṛhīteṣu vastuṣu niratiśayānandanirbharaṃ 
matsvarūpameva satatamanubhavati, sa māmeva eti 

p. 264) ------------------------------- siddho mukta ihocyate |

iti tātparyamityom || 60 ||

atha dvādaśo'dhyāyaḥ

	evaṃ paramāptabhagavadvacanalakṣaṇāt

parajñānarūpaṃ divyacakṣurunmeṣakāraṇaṃ

pārameśvaraparānugrahaprabhāvamātrādeva kṣaṇamātraṃ

tattvamupāsāditavyamiti svaparārthaghanaprataropāyaṃ jijñāsurarjuna 
uvāca �

evaṃ satatayuktā ye bhaktāstvāṃ paryupāsate | 
ye cāpyakṣaramavyaktaṃ teṣāṃ ke yogavittamāḥ || 1 ||

	yathā viśvātītasvarūpa eva viśvātmakatayā sākṣātkṛto'si, 
evamanena prakāreṇa ye satatayuktāḥ satataṃ sarvadā jāgarādiṣu 
sarvāsu avasthāsu yuktāḥ samāhitāḥ santo bhaktāḥ praṇayinaḥ tvāṃ 
paryupāsate sevante, ye ca kecana akṣaraṃ 
cinmātraikasvarūpāsaṃcalanaśīlam, ata eva avyaktaṃ vyaktādasmāt 
sarvendriyagocarāt nānābhāvalakṣaṇāt vastuno'nyaṃ 
sarvārthaniṣedhasamādhiniṣṇātadhiṣaṇāḥ tvāṃ paryupāsate teṣāṃ 
ubhayeṣāṃ yogināṃ madhye

p. 266) ke yogavittamāḥ atiśayena tvatsvarūpopalabdhiphalasamādhijñā iti 
|| 1 ||

	evaṃ vijñapta ekatarapakṣaprādhānyapratipādanārthaṃ 
bhagavānuvāca �

mayyāveśya mano ye māṃ nityayuktā upāsate |
śraddhayā parayopetāste me yuktatamā matāḥ || 2 ||

	te evaṃvidhā yogino yuktatamāḥ niratiśayasamādhibhājo mama 
matāḥ abhipretāḥ | tānahaṃ paramayogino manye, na anyānityarthaḥ | ke te 
| ye mayi sakalavastuprakāśakānanyaprakāśyacitprakāśaikasvabhāve 
manaḥ antaḥkaraṇaṃ āveśya tatsamāpannaṃ kṛtvā parayā 
samastasaṃśayādivikalpavicchedādatyutkṛṣṭayā śraddhayā 
yathoktaphalasaṃbhāvanayā upetāḥ samanvitāḥ santo nityayuktāḥ 
nityaṃ sthūlasūkṣmasarvabhāvānubhavamayīṣu tāsu tāsu avasthāsu 
yathoktasamādhyavasthitāḥ samupāsate svarūpapratyavamarśalakṣaṇayā 
niruttarayā bhaktyā sevante || 2 ||

	tadevaṃ karmayogināmeva yogavitsu atiśayo'nena pratipāditaḥ | ata

duṣkaratvādipratipādanena avaratvameva pratipādayitumāha �

ye tvakṣaramanirdeśyamavyaktaṃ paryupāsate |

	ye tu ye punaryoginaḥ indriyagrāmaṃ bāhyānāmābhyantarāṇāṃ 
ca indriyāṇāṃ samūhaṃ saṃniyamya samyak apramattatayā niyamya, 
sarvebhyo viṣayebhyaḥ pratiṣidhya, māmupāsate | kīdṛśam | 
akṣaramavyaktaṃ ca prāgvyākhyātasvarūpam | ata eva anirdeśyam 
idantāvaseyasamastasaṃvedyabhāvavilakṣaṇatvāt nirdeṣṭumaśakyaṃ 
vākpathātītam |

p. 267) tathā sarvatragaṃ sarvaṃ vyāpya avatiṣṭhamānamapi acintyaṃ 
buddhigocarātītam | tathā paramārthanityatvāt kūṭasthaṃ 
nirupaplavāvinaśvaraikasvabhāvaniṣṭham | ata eva acalaṃ dhruvaṃ nahi 
ayaṃ parvatādikūṭasthapadārthavat calanalakṣaṇena upaplavena, 
atyantābhāvalakṣaṇena vā nāśena kadācit saṃyujyate | kīdṛśāḥ 
santaḥ evaṃ vidhaṃ māmupāsate | sarvatra samabuddhayaḥ sarvasmin 
vastuni matsvarūpaikadarśitvāt samā nirviśeṣā buddhiryeṣāṃ te 
tathoktāḥ | samabuddhitvādeva sarvabhūtānāmeva hite ratāḥ sarvasya 
ātmatvena pratipatterna kvacit dveṣadoṣakāluṣyamanubhavantaḥ | anena 
viśeṣaṇadvayena vinā asya samādheraniṣpattiḥ pratipāditā | nahi 
sarvasāmyadaśānadhirūḍho yogī niṣpanna iti ucyate | evaṃvidhā yogino 
ye māmupāsate, te satyaṃ māmeva prāpnuvanti yathoktayā itikartavyatayā 
niṣpannasamādhīnāṃ matsvarūpasamāpattireva samādhiphalaṃ teṣāṃ, 
na anyat || 4 ||

kintu �

kleśo'dhikatarasteṣāmavyaktāsaktacetasām |
avyaktā hi gatirduḥkhaṃ dehabhṛddbhiravāpyate || 5 ||

	teṣāṃ sarvabhāvaniṣedhasamādhiniṣṭhānāṃ yogināṃ 
sarvabhāvaniṣṭhatvena madupāsanaparebhyo yuktatamatvena 
prākpratipāditebhyo yogibhyaḥ adhikataraḥ sātiśayaḥ kleśaḥ | 
kīdṛśānāṃ satām | avyaktāsaktacetasāṃ avyakte 
vāṅmanasagocarātikrāntasvabhāve mayi āsaktamālambanīyatvena 
tatraiva niṣaṇṇaṃ ceto'ntaḥ karaṇaṃ yeṣāṃ, tathāvidhānām | 
kathamavyaktāsaktacittatvamadhikatarakleśakāraṇamiti

p. 268) upapādayitumāha yasmāt dehabhṛdbhiḥ dehaṃ 
niyatajātyabhimānāvacchinnaṃ śarīraṃ ahamitipratipattyā 
dhārayadbhistaistādṛśairyogibhiḥ avyaktā sarvātmanā nirābhāsā gatiḥ 
samādhiprāptiḥ duḥkham atikleśena avāpyate adhigamyate | 
ayamabhiprāyaḥ śarīre'hantayā pratipādyamāne

sumahān kleśa eveti || 5 ||

	atha asti kācit yuktiryayā deha eva madbhāvena pratipattuṃ śakyaḥ 
sarvātmanā sarvakriyoparamasya asaṃbhāvitvena prākpratipāditatvāt | 
tasmāt śāstracoditāsu kriyāsveva vartamānaḥ 
pāramārthikamatsvarūpamasau yathā sukhamavāpnoti, na tathā 
duṣkarasakalakriyāniṣedhābhiniviṣṭaḥ | na ca tādṛso yoginaḥ sarvatra 
samabuddhitvamupapadyate sarvārtheṣu niṣedhyatayā dveṣādivyakteḥ |

saiṣā mukhyā bhagavatsvarūpopāsā caturthī bhaktyavasthā | yā tu

anayaiva dṛṣṭyā upapanneti pratipādayitumāha �

ye tu sarvāṇi karmāṇi mayi saṃnyasya matparāḥ |

kriyāḥ mayi sakalakartṛtvena pratipāditasvarūpe

punarnāśotpādaprabandhapayodheḥ samuddhartā samyaguttārako bhavāmi

p. 270) samarpaya yadyat buddhyā niścinuṣe, tat madātmakatvena jānīhi |

abhyāse'pyasamarthaḥ san matkarmaparamo bhava |
madarthamapi karmāṇi kurvan siddhimavāpsyasi || 10 ||

p. 271) athaitadapyaśakto'si kartuṃ madyogamāsthitaḥ |
sarvakarmaphalatyāgaṃ tataḥ kuru yatātmavān || 11 ||

	atheti prakāropanyāsārtham | yadi yathāpratipāditena prakāreṇa 
yathoktasvarūpe mayicittaṃ manobuddhivṛttyātmakamantaḥkaraṇaṃ 
sthiraṃ sarvāsu avasthāsu avicalāvadhānatayā dṛḍham āveśayituṃ 
samarpayituṃ matsvarūpābhedena bhāvayituṃ na śaknoṣi na prabhavasi 
tato'bhyāsayogena karmayogāṃśatayā 
ṣaṣṭhādhyāyasaṃsūcitasaṃvidhāanena 
tattadyogaśāstrapraṇītetikarttavyatāviśiṣṭena vā 
cittavṛttinirodhaprayatnātmakābhyāsapradhānena samādhinā māṃ 
yathoktasvarūpam āptum abhedena pratipattuṃ iccha abhivāñchasva 
matsvarūpaprāptyabhisandhyaparityāgena abhyāsayogamapi 
karmayogayogyatāhetubhūtamanutiṣṭhetyarthaḥ | evaṃ vidhe'pi abhyāse 
abhyāsayoge asamarthaḥ aśaktaḥ san matkarmaparamaḥ madarthaṃ 
karmāṇi, matkarmāṇi tānyeva paramāṇi abhīṣṭasādhanatayā 
prakṛṣṭāni yasya sa tathāvidhaḥ bhava karmayogāṅgatayā abhyupagatasya 
īśvarapraṇidhānākhyasya kriyāyogasya anuṣṭhānamācara ityarthaḥ | 
tato'pi hi matsvarūpāvāptilakṣaṇasamādhiphalasiddhirbhavati | ata eva āha 
madarthamapi karmāṇi kurvan siddhimavāpsyasi iti | athaitadapi yathoktaṃ 
kartuṃ niṣpādayituṃ aśakto'si asamarthastvaṃ bhavasi, tadā madyogaṃ 
madviṣayaṃ samādhim āsthitaḥ phalatvena samāśritaḥ san 
sarvakarmaphalatyāgaṃ sarveṣāṃ pūrvoktānāṃ 
karmaṇāmanuṣṭhīyamānānāṃ yat phalaṃ 
tattallokāntaropabhogyanānābhogātmakasiddhirūpaṃ tasya 
tyāgamanabhisandhinā saṃnyāsaṃ yatātmavān sarvasmāt viṣayāt 
nigṛhītāntaḥkaraṇena yuktaḥ san kuru niṣpādaya mayi manaḥ samādhāya 
śāstravihitāni sarvāṇi karmāṇi yathoktajñānaprasādhanopāyatayā 
phalāntarābhisandhānaṃ vinā anutiṣṭhetyarthaḥ || 9-11 ||

p. 272) evamupadiṣṭānāṃ bhagavadupāsābhedānāṃ krameṇa pūrvasya 
pūrvasya anuṣṭhānāsāmarthye sati uttarasya uttarasya anuṣṭhānaṃ 
yadupadiṣṭaṃ, tat na avaratvāpekṣayā; kintu adhikāriviṣayasya tyāgasya 
sukarataratvameva boddhavyamiti pratipādayitumāha �

śreyo hi jñānamabhyāsājjñānāddhyānaṃ viśiṣyate |
dhyānāt karmaphalatyāgastyāgācchāntiranantarā || 12 ||

	hiśabdo hetau | yasmādevaṃ vidho vyākhyātakrama iha 
vivakṣitaḥ, tasmāt matprāptaye sarvakarmaphalatyāgaṃ 
yogyatātiśayavaśāt sukarataraṃ tvaṃ kuru, iti 
pūrvāparayorvākyayoḥ saṃbandhaḥ | kīdṛśaḥ kramaḥ | iha 
jñānaṃ yathoktayā upapattyā sarvabhāvānāṃ 
parabrahmarūpatāvagamaḥ, tat abhyāsāt abhyāsayogāt pūrvoktāt 
śreyaḥ sukaratvāt praśasyataram | abhyāsayogo hi 
sarvaviṣayapratiṣedhakrameṇa cittavṛttīnāṃ nirodhe yatnaḥ | sa ca 
yathoktajñānalabdhapratiṣṭhabuddhyadhikāriviṣayaḥ | tataḥ 
pratiṣṭhitabuddhyadhikāriviṣayaṃ jñānaṃ tadapekṣayā 
sukaratvāt śreyaḥ uktam | ata eva 
abhyāsayogināmavyaktāsaktacetasāmadhikataraḥ kleśa iti 
prāguktam | evaṃ vidhācca jñānāt dhyānaṃ 
jñānaniścitopeyavastucintanaṃ viśiṣyate sukaratvādatiricyate | 
tasya hi jñānāpekṣayā 
tattvasākṣātkārapravṛtterviśiṣṭo'dhikārī, tadviṣayatvena eva 
tasya sukaratvam | evaṃvidhācca dhyānāt karmaphalatyāgaḥ 
viśiṣyate, yato ghaṭamānāvastho'sau tādṛgdhyānalakṣaṇe hi 
karmaṇi vyatirekabuddhiḥ sphuratyeva | tadadhikāryapekṣayā tyāgaḥ 
siddhyavasthādhikāriviṣayastādṛśasya eva sukara iti dhyānāt 
karmaphalatyāgo viśiṣyate iti uktam | yasmāt neha evaṃ vidhastyāgo 
vivakṣitaḥ, yasya nāhaṃ kartā na me karmaphalena saṃbandhaḥ

p. 273) iti pralāpamātreṇa niṣpattiḥ, kintu 
vyatikrāntābhyāsādipūrvāvasthāsiddhayogiviṣayaṃ 
sarvabhāvānāṃ parabrahmasvarūpe nityamanubhavāt vyatiriktasya 
phalasya adarśanameva tyāga ucyate | ata eva āha 
tyāgācchāntiranantarā iti | śāntiḥ 
saṃkalpavikalpakṣobhaprakṣayādadvayacinmātrasvarūpe 
paramātmani eva bhagavati ahamityabhedena nirupaplavā saṃvido 
viśrāntiḥ | tasyāstyāgasya ca na asti antaraṃ vyavadhānaṃ; ya eva 
tyāgaḥ, saiva śāntirīśvarasamāpattilakṣaṇā siddhiriti tyāgāt 
śāntiranantarā, iti uktam | anantaramiti pāṭhe kriyāviśeṣaṇatvena 
eṣa eva artho boddhavya iti || 12 ||

	atha īdṛśasya tyāginaḥ parasāmyasamāpannasaṃvidātmabhūtasya 
bhaktasya lakṣaṇamāha �

adveṣṭā sarvabhūtānāṃ maitraḥ karuṇa eva ca |
nirmamo nirahaṅkāraḥ samaduḥkhasukhaḥ kṣamī || 13 ||

santuṣṭaḥ satataṃ yogī yatātmā dṛḍhaniścayaḥ |
mayyarpitamanobuddhiryo madbhaktaḥ sa me priyaḥ || 14 ||

parabrahmātmakamatsvarūpasamāpattisūcakaivaṃvidhaguṇaviśiṣṭo yaḥ 
sa madbhaktaḥ sa eva ca me priyaḥ ātmabhūtatvāt dayitaḥ | kīdṛśaḥ | 
sarvasāmyasamāpannatvāt carācareṣu akhileṣu bhūteṣu adveṣṭā 
dveṣalakṣaṇadoṣarahitaḥ svātmatayā sarvaṃ bhāvajātaṃ paśyatastasya 
heyābhāvāt dveṣābhāva ityarthaḥ | anena bhāvaniṣedhātmake samādhau 
dveṣadoṣānubandhacchedaḥ prāgukto'nūditaḥ | ata eva maitraḥ sarvatra 
sauhārdavān | tathā karuṇaḥ dayāvān yato nirmamaḥ mama 
idametadarthakriyākāritayā svamiti parigrahabuddherniṣkrāntaḥ | tathā 
dehādyanityavastuniṣṭhādahaṅkārapratyayāt niṣkrāntaḥ nirahaṅkāraḥ 

p. 274) tathā same vedyatvamātreṇa 
pratibhāsamānatvādanantarāviṣkṛtadveṣarāgātmakavikāratvāt tulye 
sukhaduḥkhe yasya | tathā kṣamī krodhādivikārahetuṣu titikṣāvān | tathā 
svikṛtajñānaparāmṛtatvāt nityatuṣṭaḥ sadātinirabhikāṅkṣaḥ | tathā 
yatātmā yataḥ svātmani eva avasthāpita ātmā yena, sa tathā | sarve hi 
aprabuddhā anātmani dhanādau niyamitātmapratyayāḥ tathā 
dṛḍhaniścayaḥ kenacit vikalpena prakampayitumaśakyatvāt dṛḍhaḥ 
susthiro'dhyavasāyo yasya, sa tādṛśaḥ | tathā pūrvavyākhyātakrameṇa 
mayyarpitamanobuddhiḥ mayi paramātmani arpite niveśite manobuddhī yena 
saḥ | evaṃ vidhaguṇaviśiṣṭho madbhakta ucyate, evaṃvidhasāmagrī 
madārādhanopakaraṇasaṃpaditi atra tātparyamiti || 14 ||

	idānīmuttareṣu ślokeṣu prakṛṣṭabhaktaviśeṣaṇatvena upāttāni 
padāni bahuśo vyākhyātaprāyārthatvāt paṭhyanta eva �

yasmānnodvijate loke lokānnodvijate ca yaḥ |
harṣāmarṣabhayakrodhairmukto yaḥ sa ca me priyaḥ || 15 ||

anapekṣaḥ śucirdakṣa udāsīno gatavyathaḥ |
sarvārambhaparityāgī yo madbhaktaḥ sa me priyaḥ || 16 ||

yo na hṛṣyati na dveṣṭi na śocati na kāṅkṣati |
śubhāśubhaphalatyāgī bhaktimān yaḥ sa me priyaḥ || 17 ||

samaḥ śatrau ca mitre ca tathā mānāvamānayoḥ |
śītoṣṇasukhaduḥkheṣu samaḥ saṅgavivarjitaḥ || 18 ||

tulyanindāstutirmaunī saṃtuṣṭo yena kenacit |
aniketaḥ sthitamatirbhaktimān me priyo naraḥ || 19 ||

	gatārthāni etāni viśeṣaṇāni | kintu kṛtakṛtyatāvyaktihetubhiḥ

p. 275) paramayogisaṃbaddhairevaṃvidhairguṇairyukto yaḥ, sa madbhakto 
bhavati, ityetāvadatra tātparyaṃ prāgeva uktam || 15-19 ||

atha adhyāyamupasaṃharannāha �

ye tu dharmāmṛtamidaṃ yathoktaṃ paryupāsate |
śraddadhānā matparamā bhaktāste'tīva me priyāḥ || 20 ||

ye bhaktāḥ matsaparyāparā matparāyaṇā idaṃ yathāpratipāditaṃ 
dharmāmṛtaṃ 
matsvarūpopāsanātmakasadācāramayamamṛtamavināśikatvakāraṇaṃ 
vastu upāsate nityānuśīlanena upasevante, te atīva sutarāṃ mama priyāḥ 
vallabhā na te matto vyatiriktā ityarthaḥ | kīdṛśāḥ santa upāsate | 
śraddadhānāḥ samastasiddhibījabhūtāṃ śraddhāṃ bhajantaḥ, ata eva ca 
matparamāḥ ahameva yathoktasvarūpaḥ parameśvaraḥ paramātmā 
sarvopāyairupeyatayā paramaḥ prakṛṣṭo yeṣāṃ te, tathā 
śraddhāmūlamatsamāpattiphalāṃ parāṃ siddhimupāgatā ityom || 20 ||

sarvārthaprārthanehārahitamapi satāṃ mānasaṃ lobhayantī
lakṣmyā yatra svayaiṣā vikasati bhagavadbhaktikalpadrumaśrīḥ |
tāṃ nityaṃ nandanorvīviharaṇavirasāḥ kautukāt kāmapīmā-
mekāṃ viśrāntibhūmiṃ śrayata kṛtadhiyo dvādaśādhyāyavṛttim ||

iti śrīrājānakarāmakaṇṭhaviracite vākyārthānvayamātre 
sarvatobhadranāmni bhagavadgītāvivaraṇe dvādaśo'dhyāyaḥ || 12 ||


atha trayodaśo'dhyāyaḥ

	adhyāyadvādaśakena 
prakrāntaprayojanaviṣayamaśeṣamabhidheyatattvamabhihitaṃ, prāyaśo na 
kaścit vaktavyaśeṣo vidyate | kevalamukta eva artho'vaśiṣṭena 
adhyāyapañcakena nirmalīkaraṇāya idānīṃ prapañcyate | tathāca 
prastāvanāpradarśitakriyākrameṇa 
pāramārthikayathoktaikasvarūpavyavasthito'pi parameśvaro 
nirupamanijakrīḍāmātraprayojanatayā ekameva svasvabhāvaṃ 
vedyavedakavibhāgaparikalpanayā śākhādvayena prasṛtamavabhāsya 
yāmimāṃ māyāmayīṃ mahāvāgurāmaprathayat, sā 
viṣayaviṣayivibhāgena śarīraśarīribhāvena ca avabhāsamānā samastaṃ 
saṃsāricakraṃ paryavarudhya avasthitā | tasyāḥ svecchayā 
anugrāhyatvena parigṛhītaṃ janaṃ prati udgrathanopāyabhūte sakale 
prakaraṇe tatra tatra pratipāditasvarūpaṃ jñānalakṣaṇamarthaṃ 
parameśvara eva upadiṣṭavān | tatra viṣayaviṣayivibhāgena 
avasthitāyāstasyā udveṣṭanāya saptame jñānalakṣaṇo'rthaḥ 
prapañcitaḥ | tathā hi tatra parasyaiva tattvasya jaḍacetanavibhāgena 
sthūlasūkṣmaprakṛtiprapañcavivekapurassaratayā tasya arthasya

ahaṃ kṛtsnasya jagataḥ prabhavaḥ pralayastathā | (7|6)

ityādinā pratipāditayā brahmatattvalakṣaṇayā paratarayā prakṛtyā 
parāparayoḥ prakṛtyorabhedaparamārthatāpratipādanena nirṇayaḥ kṛtaḥ | 
idānīṃ śarīraśarīribhāvena vyavasthitāyāstasyā 
udveṣṭanopāyabhūtatvena jñānasya nirṇayārthaṃ 
samanantaramadhyāyamārabhate | tatra tāvat tayoḥ śarīraśarīriṇorvitatya 
vyākhyāsyamānapratipādakasaṃjñānirdeśena sāmānyalakṣaṇaṃ 
pratipādayan saṃkṣepato vivakṣitasya jñānasya svarūpamapi

p. 277) śrībhagavānuvāca �

idaṃ śarīraṃ kaunteya kṣetramityabhidhīyate |
etadyo veda taṃ prāhuḥ kṣetrajñamiti tadvidaḥ || 1 ||

	idaṃ sarvapramātṛprasiddhatayā vyavasthitaṃ śarīraṃ dehaḥ 
kṣetrasādharmyāt kṣetramiti ucyate kṣetrasaṃjñayā kathyate | yathā 
kusūlādigatānāṃ dhānyādibījānāṃ 
mūlastambaśākhāśilādiviśeṣaprasavābhivyaktyadhikaraṇaḥ 
pṛthivyuddeśaḥ kṣetramityucyate, tathā karmāśayasaṃbhṛtānāṃ 
karmaṇāṃ 
jātyāyurbhogalakṣaṇavipākaviśeṣābhivyaktyadhikaraṇabhūtaṃ 
śarīramapi idaṃ kṣetramiti ucyate | tena tatkarmavipākāspadatvena 
yadanubhūyate, tat śarīramiti veditavyam | etat ca evaṃ vidhaṃ kṣetraṃ yo 
veda jānāti, taṃ tadvidaḥ kṣetrajñasvarūpajñāḥ kṣetrajñamiti 
vidurjānate | kimanena uktaṃ bhavati | asmin śarīre dviprakārā pratipattireva 
sthitā sarvadehināmahantayā idantayā ca | tathāca ahaṃ brāhmaṇo 
yajñadattaḥ ityādinā dehamahantayā vyavasyanti, kadācit mamedaṃ 
śarīraṃ itīdantayā vyavasyanti; ato vyavasāyitā pramātā tāvat 
kaścidatra vidyata eva | yaśca aṃśaḥ pramātṛtayā pratīyate, sa kṣetrajño 
jīvaścetanaḥ iti pratipattavyaḥ | evamasmin śarīre yadyat vedyatayā 
vedakasaṃbandhamātrāt pratīyate, tat kṣetrāṃśatayā veditavyam | yo'sau 
vedakatayā pratīyate, sa kṣetrajña ātmā iti veditavyaḥ | etāvatyeva 
nityāvahitatvaṃ śarīraśarīriṇorvivekajñānakāraṇam | tacca 
vedyavedakasaṃbandhamātranibandhanasakalaparāparavyavahārāṇāmapi 
sarvadehināṃ māyānimīlitatattvadṛśāṃ svapne'pi na unmiṣati, 
yāvadīśvarānugrahaśaktyaiva sā tattvadṛk

p. 278) teṣāṃ na unmīlitā | tena anugṛhīta eva viṣayo'sya jñānasya, iti 
tātparyam | yata uktam tadvidaḥ kecanaiva yoginastaṃ viduriti || 1 ||

	nanu paramārthata eka eva pramātā pratipāditaḥ | pratikṣetraṃ ca 
pṛthakpṛthak vedakapratītirabādhitā vyavasthitā, iti 
svasiddhāntavirodhabhrāntiṃ parihartumāha �

kṣetrajñaṃ cāpi māṃ viddhi sarvakṣetreṣu bhārata |
kṣetrakṣetrajñayorjñānaṃ yattajjñānaṃ mataṃ mama || 2 ||

	iha vedakatvamātramekamavyabhicāri asādhāraṇamātmano 
lakṣaṇamuktam | ataḥ parasparabhinnānāṃ kṣaṇikanānāvasthājuṣāṃ 
vedyānāṃ śarīrāṇāmeva bhinnarūpatvaṃ vyavasthitam | na tu 
vedakatvamātratayaiva tadadhiṣṭhāyakatvena vyavasthitasya 
pāramārthikasya ekasya vedakasya ātmano nānātvamasti 
sarvadehagatatve'pi vedakaikalakṣaṇāvyabhicārāt | yā tu pratiśarīraṃ 
jātyavacchedena pṛthakpratipattiḥ, tadacintyeśvaramāyāśaktivilasitameva | 
atastadvyudāsārthameva ca ayamupadeśaḥ prabuddhānprati arjunamukhena 
ucyate | sarvakṣetreṣu caturdaśavidhabhūtasargabhedena avasthiteṣu 
aparisaṃkhyeyeṣu śarīreṣu ekameva māṃ kṣetrajñaṃ paramātmānaṃ 
viddhi jānīhi | yathā ekakṣetragatanānāvayavavedakatvenaiva 
kṣetrajñapratītirmāyādaśāyāmavasthitā, tathā 
samastaśarīravedakatvena ekaḥ pāramārthiko'hameva kṣetrajñaḥ | tataśca 
evaṃvidhayoḥ kṣetrakṣetrajñayoryajjñānaṃ samyakpratipattistadeva 
mama mataṃ jñānatvena abhipretam | natu sāṃkhyādipratipāditayoḥ 
śarīraśarīriṇorvivekajñānamātraṃ tāttvikaṃ jñānamiti tātparyam || 2 
||

	idānīṃ nānādarśanabhedena vyavasthāpitanānārūpasya api 
kṣetrasya

p. 279) vedyatvamātrasāmānyalakṣaṇābhedāt kāṃcideva 
darśanaprakriyāmāśritya tatsvarūpe pratipādayitavye 
vedādyāgamaprasiddhasāṃkhyaprakriyāsamāśrayaṇena 
pratipādayiṣyamāṇakṣetrasya svarūpamupakṣeptumāha �

tat kṣetraṃ yacca yādṛkka yadvikāri yataśca yat |
sa ca yo yatsvabhāvaśca tatsamāsena me śṛṇu || 3 ||

ṛṣibhirbahudhā gītaṃ chandobhirvividhaiḥ pṛthak |
brahmasūtrapadaiścaiva hetumadbhirviniścitam || 4 ||

	tat kṣetrajñajñeyatayā upakrāntaṃ kṣetraṃ yat 
yadabhidhānamityarthaḥ, tathā yādṛk yādṛśena prakārabhedena sthitaṃ 
tat saṃkṣepeṇa avadhāraya | tasmiṃśca kṣetre yat vastu vikāri 
viśiṣṭakāryakāraṇatvāt vikāravat vikārasaṃyuktaṃ, tadapi śṛṇu | 
tathā yataśca vikāriṇo yat vikārabhūtaṃ vastu upapadyate, tadapi śṛṇu | 
tathā sa ca kṣetrajña evameva yo yatparyāyo yatsvabhāvaḥ yādṛśaśca, 
tatsarvaṃ saṃgraheṇa ucyamānamavadhāraya | asya vastunaḥ 
smṛtiśrutiprasiddhatvapratipādanārthamāha ṛṣibhiḥ smṛtikartṛbhiḥ 
bahudhā bahuprakāraṃ tatra tatra smṛtiśāstreṣu gītam | tathā 
chandobhirvividhaiḥ vedaśākhābhedena nānārūpairata eva pṛthak 
pṛthakprakaraṇabhedena gītam | brahmasūtrapadaiḥ brahmasūtraśabdena 
rahasyabhāgo vedasya vedāntasaṃjña ucyate, tasya yāni padāni 
avāntaravākyāni; taiḥ hetumadbhiḥ upapattisaṃyuktaiḥ viniścitaṃ 
viśeṣeṇa kṛtaniścayamiti || 3-4 ||

	evaṃ vedavedāntasmṛtiprasiddhatvamanena vakṣyamāṇasya 
kṣetrādipratipādakasya arthasya proktam | tatra yat kṣetraṃ yaśca kṣetrajña 
iti yaduktaṃ, tatra kṣetraṃ śarīraṃ; yaśca tadvedakaḥ, sa kṣetrajña iti 
prāgeva uktaṃ svarūpamanayormanyamāno yādṛgityādinā śiṣṭaṃ 
vaktumāha �

p. 280) mahābhūtānyahaṅkāro buddhiravyaktameva ca | 
indriyāṇi daśaikaṃ ca pañca cendriyagocarāḥ || 5 ||

icchā dveṣaḥ sukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ saṃghātaścetanā dhṛtiḥ |
etat kṣetraṃ samāsena savikāramudāhṛtam || 6 ||

etat mahābhūtādivastusamudāyātmakaṃ kṣetraṃ vikārasaṃyuktaṃ 
vikāragrahaṇāt prakṛtibhūtasaṃyuktamapi saṃkṣepeṇa kathitam | tatra 
mahābhūtaśabdena vyāpakatvādimahattve sati tanmātrāṇyeva uktāni 
veditavyāni | yataḥ ślokārdhena kāraṇabhūtaṃ padārthacatuṣṭayaṃ 
saṅgṛhītam | tatra ahaṅkāraḥ asmītipratyayo'smitāśabdena kvaciduktaḥ | 
buddhiḥ niścayātmakaṃ jñānaṃ mahattattvamiti prasiddham | tathā 
avyaktaṃ guṇatrayātmikā prakṛtiḥ | etāni catvāri kāraṇabhūtāni 
sthulādikrameṇa nirdiṣṭāni | tāni ca pṛthivyādīnāṃ bhūtānāṃ 
tanmātrāṇi kāraṇam | ahaṅkārastaijasavaikārikabhūtādibhedena 
trividhaḥ | tatra taijasatvena manasaḥ kāraṇam, vaikārikatvena indriyāṇām, 
bhūtātmatvena tanmātrāṇām, iti ahaṅkārasya trividhaguṇātmakasya 
kāraṇatvam | kecittu ahaṅkāratanmātralakṣaṇasya ṣaṭkasya mattattvaṃ 
kāraṇamāhuḥ, kecidahaṅkārasyaiveti buddheḥ kāraṇatvam | buddheśca 
guṇatrayarūpā prakṛtiravyaktaśabdena uktā kāraṇamiti 
kāraṇabhūtavastucatuṣṭayapratipādanena yadvikāri iti prakrānto'rtho 
nirṇītaḥ | atra hi avyaktaṃ svādhikārāpekṣayā mūlakāraṇam | 
ahaṅkāraśca buddhikāryaḥ san indriyādikāraṇam | tanmātrāṇi 
ahaṅkārakāryāṇi buddhikāryāṇi vā pṛthivyādikāraṇamiti 
caturṇāmapi kāraṇatvāt vikāritvam | atha kāryavargaṃ 
pratipādayitumāha indriyāṇi pañca buddhīndriyāṇi śrotrādīni 
prasiddhāni, pañca vāgādīni

p. 281) karmendriyāṇyapi tattvam, manaśca ekādaśaṃ 
saṃkalpavikalpātmakam | pañca indriyagocarāḥ iti indriyagocaraśabdena 
yadyapi śabdādayo bhūtaguṇā ucyante, tathāpi mahābhūtānāṃ 
svaguṇopalabdhidvāreṇa svarūpopalabdherindriyagocaraśabdena 
sthūlānyeva bhūtāni uktāni yataḥ ṣoḍaśako'sau vikāravargaḥ, eṣa ca 
kārya eva, na kāraṇamiti | evaṃ sāṃkhyadarśanaprasiddhā

ātmadharmatvena abhyupagatāste vedyatvātkṣetrameva | tathā

pramātrekasvabhāvasya ātmanaḥ kathaṃ prameyatvaṃ, kathaṃ vā 
sarvavyavahārānupapattisiddhānusaṃdhātṛlakṣaṇasvarūpasya tasya

yāvajjīvabhāvitvaṃ, ityādinānāpravādapratikṣepāya bahavo vipaścitaḥ 
kṛtapariśramāḥ | sā ca tattvasya sādhyamānāvasthā 
sandigdhabuddhitvadaśānuttīirṇapuruṣaviṣayatayā | asyāṃ 
siddhāntakathāyāmīśvarānugrahāvirbhūtaviśuddhaśraddhādiguṇāpi"
sunitasaṃśayādidoṣābhāvanirmalaprajñopadeśyapuruṣaviśeṣaviṣayā-
yāmupanyastāyāṃ matikāluṣyādṛte na kiñcit prayojanam | nahi

tatsādhanāvasthāpratipādanena prastutāhāravairasyajananamātrātiriktaḥ

p. 283) sadācāramātrasya 
saṃsāraśvabhrapātaphalatāmāvahannāsurasargabhāgjanaviṣayo mahān 
doṣaḥ | tathāca uktaṃ

mānāgnihotramuta mānamaunaṃ 
mānenādhītamuta mānayajñaḥ |
catvāryetānyabhayaṅkarāṇi
bhayaṃ prayacchantyayathākṛtāni || (mahābhā0)

iti | catvāri agnihotrādīni vastūni āśramadharmarūpāṇi 
śāstracoditatvādanuṣṭhīyamānāni abhayaṃ tāvat kurvanti, 
mānādidoṣadūṣitacittatvena kṛtāni pratyuta mahadbhayamanuṣṭhātuḥ 
phalantītyapi hi asya ślokasya arthaḥ | evaṃvidho māno vidyate yasya, sa 
mānī; tasya bhāvo mānitvaṃ, tadabhāvaḥ amānitvaṃ 
śuddhasaṃkalpatvaṃ nāma guṇo jñānābhivyaktihetutvāt 
jñānamityuktam | evameva adambhitvam | dambho hi 
mānādidoṣadūṣitasaṃkalpatve sati dṛṣṭamātraphalatayā 
sadācārānuṣṭhānaṃ, vyājena dharmācaraṇamityarthaḥ | sa vidyate yasya 
asau dambhī mithyācāraḥ, tasya bhāvo dambhitvaṃ; tadabhāvaḥ 
adambhitvaṃ samyak kriyānuṣṭhānamityarthaḥ, tadapi pūrvavat 
jñānameva | tathā ahiṃsā | hiṃsā nāma śāstrīyaniyamanairapekṣyeṇa 
dveṣalaulyādiprasaktasya prāṇināṃ jighāṃsā, tadabhāvo'hiṃsā 
nisargataḥ sarvabhūteṣu karuṇārdracittatvādanabhidrohaḥ, tadapi jñānam 
| tathā kṣāntiḥ parairabhidruhyamāṇasya satyapi pratīkārasāmarthye 
svabhāvataḥ praśāntacittatvāt vāṅmanaḥ karmabhiravikāritvaṃ, sāpi hi 
jñānam | nanu ahiṃsākṣāntyoryadi jñānatvaṃ, tat kathaṃ 
tattvajñānopadeśaviṣayatvena parigṛhītasya arjunasya 
yajñasaṃgrāmādikryānuṣṭhāne niyujyamānasya tadgatā hiṃsā 
akṣāntiśca anuṣṭheyatvena upadiṣṭā | ucyate

p. 284) yathoktajñānaniṣṭhasya avaśyānuṣṭheyasvakarmabhāvena

sarvaṃ jihmaṃ mṛtyupadamārjavaṃ brahmaṇaḥ padam |

iti | tadapi jñānam | evaṃ pañcaite dharmāḥ 
parameśvarānugrahasūcakadaivasargabhājo, na anyasya 
prakṣīyamāṇasadasatkarmasantānasya 
saṃsārasvarūpapratyavekṣāyogyaprajñasya sahasaiva jāyante | te ca 
taddhṛdayakṛtapratiṣṭhasya jñānatarormūlasantatisthānīyāḥ | tadyuktasya 
puruṣasya anugrahaśaktyā kalyāṇamitrasaṃstavādinā preryamāṇasya 
ācāryopāsanaṃ sadgurvārādhanaṃ nāma dharmo ya utpadyate, sa 
jñānam | jñātaroḥ kila asau skandhasthānīyo dharmaḥ, ṣaḍāntarīyakāḥ 
śākhāpratānakalpā amī dharmāḥ prādurbhavanti | tadyathā 
sāstrādhigamapurassaraḥ saṃskāraviśeṣaḥ, tathā heyopādeyavastuviṣayo 
vivekaḥ, vijñātatatsvarūpasya 
heyāpavarjanapūrvamupādeyānuṣṭhānaunmukhyam | 
ācāryopāsanādevaṃvidhadharmodaye sati pallavapuṣpasthānīyāḥ 
śaucādayo janmādidoṣadarśanaparyantā dharmaviśeṣāḥ samunmiṣanti | 
tatra viśiṣṭakriyānuṣṭhāne prāptādhikārasya kalyāṇahetuṣu tāsu tāsu 
kriyāsu āsaktacittasya tadguṇaiḥ sambadhyamānasya prathamaḥ śauco 
nāma guṇaḥ sarvakriyāṇāṃ tatpūrvakatvāt | tacca dviprakāraṃ 
bāhyamābhyantaraṃ ca | tatra bāhyam 
adhikārideśakālādyavasthāpekṣayā niyatetikartavyatayā mṛjjalādinā 
duṣṭadravyagandhalepādyapāsanaparyantā mṛjā | ābhyantaraṃ 
bāhyanivṛttau satyāṃ śuddho'smīti nirviplavā pratipattiḥ, yāṃ 
bhāvaśuddhimityāhuḥ | etacca śaucaṃ kāyavāṅmanonirvartyānāṃ 
tapaḥ

p. 285) svādhyāyadhyānādīnāṃ guṇāntarāṇāmupalakṣaṇaṃ 
veditavyam | atha sthiryaṃ saṃśayādidoṣanivṛttyā anuṣṭheyeṣu artheṣu 
acañcalatā | tasmiṃśca sati ātmavinigrahaḥ ātmano'ntaḥkaraṇasya 
niṣiddhāt viṣayāt vinigraho niyamanam | tataśca indriyārtheṣu viṣayeṣu 
dṛṣṭānuśravikeṣu kṣayātiśayādidoṣadarśanāt vairāgyaṃ 
rāgadveṣarahitatvaṃ, tat prakarṣamāsādayat sattvāntareṣu guṇeṣvapi 
paryavasyati | tasmiṃśca tādṛśe guṇe sati anahaṅkāraḥ 
īdṛśo'smītidurabhimānābhāvaḥ, yadivā 
śarīrādāvahamasmītipratyayanivṛttiḥ | tataśca janmādidoṣaprabhavasya 
duḥkhasyaiva mahādoṣasya anudarśanaṃ pṛṣṭhato nirūpaṇam | tadyathā 
garbhavāsādiniratiśayakleśānubhavādikaṃ janmano duḥkhaṃ,

vārdhakyarogādiduḥkhamiti śarīritvaṃ nāma ādimadhyāntāvasthāsu 
duḥkhamayameva, iti vivekaharmyādhirūḍhaḥ svaparaviṣayatvena vivektuṃ 
kṣamaḥ | yathoktaṃ 

prajñāprāsādamāruhya aśocyaḥ śocato janān |
bhūmiṣṭhāniva śailasthaḥ sarvān prājño'nupaśyati ||

iti | tathāvidhasya ca svargādiphaleṣu kriyāviśeṣeṣu asaktiḥ asaṅga 
audāsīnyam | tathā dṛṣṭasukhahetuṣu sutakalatrabhavanādiṣu 
anabhiṣvaṅgaḥ sarvasvagrahaparityāgaḥ | etāṃ ca daśāṃ prāptasya 
iṣṭāniṣṭopapattiṣu priyāpriyapadārthaprāptiṣu satīṣu samacittatvaṃ 
harṣaśokādivikārarahitāntaḥkaraṇatvam | evameva tasyāṃ 
jñānatarornānāvidhapallavapuṣpasthānīyāyāṃ guṇasāmagryāṃ

------------ jñānī tvātmaiva me mataḥ | (7|18)

iti | tasyāṃ ca satyāṃ viviktadeśasevitvaṃ viviktadeśasya 
tādṛśaniruttarabhaktirasāmṛtāsvādavighnakāricetanācetanavasturahitasya 
giriguhāpuṇyasaritpulinaguptagṛhadeśasya sevitvaṃ bhajanaśīlatā | 
tadviparītāyāṃ janasaṃsadi cittavyutthānahutulokasabhāyāṃ aratiḥ 
udvegastatpariharaṇamityarthaḥ | tathā adhyātmajñānanityatvam 
adhyātmamātmani yat jñānaṃ paramātmasvabhāvasaṃvedanaṃ, tat 
nityamavyabhicāri yasya, sa tathā tasya bhāvastattvam | tasmiṃśca sati 
tattvajñānārthadarśanaṃ tattvasya paramātmasvarūpalakṣaṇasya parasya 
bhāvasya jñānaṃ bodhaḥ svasaṃvedanānubhavaḥ | pūrveṇa padena 
jñānasya siddhiṃ kalpayatā uttareṇa siddhatvameva pratipāditaṃ, sa eva 
arthaḥ prayojanaṃ phalaṃ yasyeti tasya darśanaṃ sākṣātkāro niruttarā 
siddhirityarthaḥ | ete viviktadeśasevitvādayastattvajñānārthadarśanāntā 
dharmāḥ phalabhūtāyā bhakte rasasthānīyāḥ, ityetāvatī guṇasāmagrī 
jñānamiti proktaṃ spaṣṭo'rthaḥ | ata uktāt guṇasamūhāt anyathā 
anyena viparītena prakāreṇa mānitvadambhitvādinā yatsthitaṃ, tatsarvaṃ 
ajñānaṃ jñānaviparītaṃ veditavyam | etacca purastāt 
devāsurasargakathanaprasaṅgena punarapi jñānājñānalakṣaṇaṃ 
vakṣyati || 7-11 ||
evaṃvidhena ca jñānena �

jñeyaṃ yattatpravakṣyāmi yajjñātvāmṛtamaśnute |

p. 287) jñeyaṃ jñātavyaṃ yat yathoktena jñānena phalatayā avasthitaṃ 
paramātmalakṣaṇaṃ vastu tat idānīṃ pravakṣyāmi tatsvarūpaṃ 
prakarṣeṇa kathayiṣyāmi | yajjñātvā yadbuddhvā amṛtam 
avināśisvarūpaṃ aśnute vyāpnoti | nanu ananyasaṃvedyasya 
sarvasaṃvedakasvabhāvasya kathamidantāvaseyavastvantaravat jñeyatvena 
vyapadeśaḥ kṛtaḥ | satyaṃ, na tasya vastunaḥ paramārthato jñeyatā 
saṃbhavati | kintu ārurukṣāvasthāyāṃ 
jñātṛjñānajñeyavibhāgātmakatvādupacāreṇa jñeyaśabdastatra 
pravartate, natu vāstavena rūpeṇa tasya jñeyatā vivakṣitā | yataḥ 
so'yamahamiti svātmatvena pratyavamarśa eva tasya jñānaṃ, 
tadvadahamidaṃ vedmi iti, vyatirekeṇa tasya ekasya 
anubhavitṛtvamātralakṣaṇasya tattvasya avagamaḥ saṃbhavati ||

tathāvidhānyeva tadviśeṣaṇānyāha �

anādimat paraṃ brahma na sannāsat taducyate || 12 ||

sarvataḥ pāṇipādaṃ tat sarvato'kṣiśiromukham |
sarvataḥ śrutimalloke sarvamāvṛtya tiṣṭhati || 13 ||

sarvendriyaguṇābhāsaṃ sarvendriyavivarjitam |
asaktaṃ sarvabhṛccaiva nirguṇaṃ guṇabhoktṛ ca || 14 ||

bahirantaśca bhūtānāmacaraṃ carameva ca |
sūkṣmatvāttadavijñeyaṃ dūrasthaṃ cāntike ca yat || 15 ||

avibhaktaṃ vibhakteṣu vibhaktamiva saṃsthitam |
bhūtabhartṛ ca tajjñeyaṃ grasiṣṇu prabhaviṣṇu ca || 16 ||

jyotiṣāmapi tajjyotistamasaḥ paramucyate |
jñānajñeyaṃ jñānagamyaṃ hṛdi sarvasya viṣṭhitam || 17 ||

	kiṃ tajjñeyamiti sasaṃjñaṃ nirdiśati paraṃ brahmeti | 
paramārthataḥ

p. 288) sarvasya vastuno brahmarūpatve sthite yat jñeyatayā saṃprati 
ābhāsate, tadaparaṃ; yattu jñātṛmātrasvarūpaṃ tatparaṃ brahma 
ityuktam | kīdṛśaṃ tat | anādimat | idaṃ vedyaṃ vastu 
sarvakṣetrajñasādhāraṇaṃ pṛthivyādirūpaṃ viṣayatayā vyavasthitaṃ, 
tathā pratikṣetrajñaṃ śarīratayā vyavasthitam, ubhayarūpatayā 
prākpratipāditaṃ, kāraṇāpekṣatayā sarvamādimat, 
āgamāpāyadharmakatvādādikoṭyavacchinnaṃ, tataśca 
antakoṭyavacchinnamapi | tadviparītaṃ tu kāraṇāntaranirapekṣasadbhāvaṃ 
anādimat paraṃ brahma ityuktam | anyacca kīdṛśam | na sat | iha hi 
yadindriyairbāhyābhyantarairidamiti gṛhyate, tat māyīyaiḥ pramātṛbhiḥ 
saditi pratipadyate | brahma tu pramātrekasvabhāvatvāt 
sarvendriyagocarabhūtaṃ tathā sat na bhavati | naca evam asat taducyate | yata 
evamapi sattvena pratipannasya sarvasya vastumastathāvabhāsakatvāt tadeva 
sadbhāvakāraṇaṃ kathamasaditi vaktuṃ śakyam | ata eva tasya 
paramārthasadekapramātṛrūpasya viśvarūpatvaṃ pratipādayitumāha 
sarvataḥ pāṇipādaṃ tat ityādinā | sarveṣāṃ kṣetrajñānāṃ yāni 
pādādīni karmendriyāṇi, yāni ca cakṣuḥśrotrādīni buddhīndriyāṇi 
tattatkriyājñānasādhanatvena vyavasthitāni; tāni 
asādhāraṇakartṛjñātṛśakterekasyaiva tasya viśvātmanaḥ śaktibheda iti 
tātparyam | yata āha sarvamāvṛtya tiṣṭhati iti | ekameva tattvaṃ sarvamidaṃ 
jagat kartṛtayā jñātṛtayā ca vyāpya vartata iti | ata eva āha 
sarvendriyaguṇābhāsaṃ iti | yāni nānākṣetrajñasaṃbaddhatvena 
avabhāsamānāni sarvāṇi bāhyābhyantarāṇi indriyāṇi teṣāṃ 
śabdādiviṣayāvabhāsakatvādayo ye guṇā dharmāḥ, tānavabhāsayati 
prakāśayati yat; tat tathoktam, svayaṃ tu sarvendriyavivarjitam nahi tasya

p. 289) māyāvabhāsitāvacchinnasvarūpakṣetrajñavat 
niyatendriyādhīnaṃ jñānamityarthaḥ | tathā asaktaṃ 
viśuddhacinmātraikasvabhāvavyavasthitatvāt vedyarūpeṇa prākṛtena 
prapañcena amiśrībhūtam | atha ca sarvabhṛt sarvaṃ bibharti dhārayati yat | 
tatra hi tattve vedyavyatiriktasvabhāve'pi sarvamidamavasthitaṃ tadvinā 
kasyacidapi svarūpalābhābhāvāt | ata eva nirguṇṃ guṇebhyaḥ 
sattvādibhyaḥ prakāśapravṛttiniyamātmakebhyaḥ 
sukhaduḥkhamohavṛttibhyo niṣkrāntaṃ tebhyo'tyantavyatiriktasvarūpam | 
nahi brahma anityasukhādyātmakaṃ bhavati, kevalamākāśamiva 
sūryātaparajastimirādibhistaistairguṇairatyantāsaṃspṛṣṭasvarūpamapi 
tattvavicāraśūnyaiḥ pramātṛbhistadvattayā gṛhyate | vastutastu 
guṇabhoktṛtvāt guṇānanubhavitṛtayā bhuṅkte, natu 
taistatsvarūpamāvriyate | ata eva 
anavacchinnanijamāhātmyaviśvarūpatvādākāśamiva ghaṭādīnāṃ 
sarvabhūtānāṃ carācarāṇāṃ bahiḥ jñeyabhāvena antaḥ 
jñātṛbhāvena ca avabhāsamānamapi paramārthataḥ 
sāmānyasaṃvitsvabhāvamevaikaṃ tattattvaṃ vyavasthitaṃ, 
dehādipratyayavyavadhānamātrakṛto bāhyābhyantaravyapadeśaḥ | ata eva 
āha nityaniścalasvabhāvamātrapratiṣṭhitatvāt acaraṃ 
gatidharmavivarjitaṃ sarvavastuvedakatayā nityaprasṛtaśaktitvāt caraṃ 
gamanaśīlamiva | tathā idantāvaseyasarvavastuvilakṣaṇasvarūpatvāt 
paramasūkṣmaṃ tat tasmācca saukṣmyāt kāraṇāt avijñeyam 
idamityanavadhāryasvarūpam | ata eva sarvapramātṝṇāṃ 
jñeyatvābhāvāt dūrastham ativiprakṛṣṭam | atha ca tadvinā kasyacidapi 
jñānasya karmaṇo vā api anupapatteḥ sarveṣāṃ antike abhyāśe sthitam | 
tathā vibhakteṣu devamanuṣyatiryagāditvāt parasparabhinneṣu vastuṣu 
sāmānyasaṃvidātmakatvena avibhaktam abhinnaikasvarūpam | athaca 
vibhaktavacca

p. 290) saṃsthitam bhedena avabhāsamānatvāt | tathā tat tattvaṃ 
bhūtabhartṛsarvabhūtānāṃ tasya tasya rūpasya avasthityekahetutvāt 
dhārakam | tathā grasiṣṇu saṃhāraśaktiyuktatvāt grasanaśīlam | tathā 
prabhaviṣṇu utpādanahetutvāt sthitipralayasargakāraṇamityarthaḥ | tathā 
jyotiṣāṃ sūryādīnāṃ prakāśakānāṃ prakāśahetutvāt jyotiḥ 
prakāśakam | tāni hi jagadbhāvaprakāśakānyapi 
saṃvitprakāśaprakāśyāni | ata eva tamasaḥ paramucyate | yadyat 
paraprakāśyaṃ, tattat svayamaprakāśātmakatvāt tamorūpam | tasmāt tat 
tattvaṃ paraṃ sarvaprakāśakarūpatvāt prakṛṣṭaṃ vyatiriktaṃ vā | tathā 
jñānajñeyaṃ jñānena yathāpratipāditasvarūpeṇa upāyena 
jñeyamātmatayā pratyabhijñeyamityarthaḥ | yato jñānagamyaṃ 
jñānaṃ vinā tathā viṣayīkartumaśakyam | paramārthatastu sarvasya 
kasyacit pramātuḥ hṛdi hṛdaye saṃvettṛtayā viśeṣeṇa sthitaṃ 
siddhyavasthāyāṃ tathā svasaṃvedanasaṃvedyatvāt || 12-17 ||

madbhakta etadvijñāya madbhāvāyopapadyate || 18 ||

mahābhūtāni ----------------------- | (13|2)

ityādinā prākṛtabhāvamayaṃ śarīraṃ, tathā 
amānitvāditattvajñānārthadarśanāntadharmarūpaṃ jñānaṃ tathā 

kṣetrajñaṃ cāpi māṃ viddhi sarvakṣetreṣu ------- | (13|5)

ityādinā mukhyakṣetrajñarūpaṃ jñeyaṃ tattattvamiti pradhānaṃ 
padārthatrayametaduktam | yato madbhakto madanugrahādeva 
matsaparyāparaḥ etat

p. 291) tritayaṃ jñātvā madbhāvāya matsvarūpasamāpattaye upapadyate 
upapannaḥ samartho bhavati || 18 ||

	atha yadetadatra jñānamupadiṣṭaṃ, tena 
prakṛtikṣetrajñaparamātmalakṣaṇaṃ padārthatrayaṃ nirṇītam, iti 
saṃgraheṇa krameṇa pradarśayitumāha �

prakṛtiṃ puruṣaṃ caiva viddhyanādī ubhāvapi |
vikārāṃśca guṇāṃścaiva viddhi prakṛtisaṃbhavān || 19 ||

	yaiṣā prakṛtiḥ saptame'dhyāye viṣayabhāvena, saṃprati 
śarīrabhāvena vibhajya pratipāditasvarūpā, tathaiva yaḥ puruṣaḥ 
viṣayitvena śarīritvena pratipāditaḥ, etāveva anyonyasaṃbaddhatvena 
anādī viddhi jānīhi | nahi etayorekatāsaṃbandhasya pūrvakoṭirupalabhyate, 
jñānāttu yathoktasvarūpādantakoṭirastyeva, anayorevamanāditvaṃ 
saṃbandhasya, iti atra tātparyaṃ; natu samaśīrṣikayā nityatvapratipādanam 
| yataḥ prakṛtervedyatvādudayavyayadharmayogitve sati pravāhanityatvaṃ, 
puruṣasya tu paramārthataḥ saṃvidrūpatvāt kūṭasthanityatvam | ata eva 
āha vikārān mahadādīn guṇāṃśca sattvādīn vedyaikasvabhāvān 
prakṛtisaṃbhavān pradhānakāraṇān jānīhi || 19 ||
	evaṃ sāmānyena prakṛtipuruṣayoḥ svarūpaṃ pratipādya, idānīṃ 
yathā prakṛtiḥ pratipuruṣaṃ nānāśarīrabhāvena saṃbaddhā, puruṣaśca 
prati śarīraṃ nānākṣetrajñabhāvena yathā saṃbaddhastathā 
pratipādayitumāha �

kāryakāraṇakartṛtve hetuḥ prakṛtirucyate |
puruṣaḥ sukhaduḥkhānāṃ bhoktṛtve heturucyate || 20 ||

	kāryaṃ mahābhūtādyātmakaṃ, kāraṇāni bāhyābhyantarāṇi 
indriyāṇi, iti śarīrādyātmakaḥ samūhaḥ kāryakāraṇaśabdena ucyate | 
tasya kartṛtve tāvanmātraniviṣṭāhaṃpratyayarūpe prakṛtiḥ avyaktaṃ 
hetuḥ kāraṇam | sā hi śarīrātmanā vyavasthitā sati 
tanniviṣṭāhaṃpratyayanimittam |

p. 292) tathā sukhādīnāṃ vedyatvāt bhogyānāmātmano yo 
bhoktṛtvābhimānaḥ, tatra vedakasvabhāvatvāt puruṣa eva kāraṇam | sati 
puruṣapratyaye sukhaduḥkhātmano bhogyasya viṣayatvaṃ, puruṣasya ca 
viṣayitvavyapadeśaḥ || 20 ||

kutaḥ �

puruṣaḥ prakṛtistho hi bhuṅkte prakṛtijān guṇān |
kāraṇaṃ guṇasaṃṅgo'sya sadasadyonijanmasu || 21 ||

	yasmāt prakṛtau nānākāryakāraṇātmakaśarīrabhāvena pariṇate 
pradhāne sthito māyāvaśāt tāvanmātrāhaṅkārāvaṣṭambhena 
vartamānaḥ puruṣaḥ prakṛtijān prakṛteḥ samudbhūtān guṇān sattvādīn 
sukhādyātmanā pariṇatān bhuṅkte vedakatvena viṣayīkaroti | yato 
mahābhūtaguṇāḥ śabdādayaḥ sukhādyātmanā vedyatvamāpannāḥ 
puruṣasya viṣayitvavyapadeśahetavaḥ | evaṃ śarīraśarīribhāvena 
viṣayaviṣayibhāvena ca prakṛtipuruṣayoravivekajaḥ saṃbandhaḥ | tataśca 
asya puruṣasya guṇeṣu śarīrātmanā viṣayātmanā ca 
saṅgo'haṅkāramamakārātmako dṛḍhaḥ saṃbandhaḥ, tat satīṣu 
sattvaguṇapradhānāsu, asatīṣu ca rajastamaḥpradhānāsu yoniṣu 
utpattikāraṇamūrtiṣu yāni janmāni puruṣādirūpatayā saṃbhavāḥ, teṣu 
guṇasaṅgaḥ eva kāraṇaṃ hetuḥ prakṛtipuruṣavivekābhāvamātranimittā 
saṃsāritā puruṣasyetyarthaḥ || 21 ||

	yataḥ parasya tattvasya dehe'smin nānāpuruṣasaṃbandhini dvidhā 
sthitirityāha �

upadraṣṭānumantā ca bhartā bhoktā maheśvaraḥ |
paramātmeti cāpyukto dehe'sminpuruṣaḥ paraḥ || 22 ||

p. 293) maheśvaraḥ sarvapuruṣaiśvaryāpekṣayā mahānatyutkṛṣṭa 
īśvaraḥ prabhuḥ parabrahmaśabdāparaparyāyaḥ 
svamāyodbhāvitapuruṣavyapekṣayā dehe'sminnupadraṣṭā bhartā 
anumantā bhoktā ca iti vyavasthitaḥ | yataḥ 
prakṛtyādimahadahaṅkārātmanā dṛśyatvena upasthāpitamarthaṃ 
puruṣaḥ paśyati, ityasau upadraṣṭā ityucyate | tathā dṛśyameva śarīrādi 
ahantayā parāmṛśan bhartā kāryakāraṇasya ahantayā dhārakaḥ sa eva 
vyapadiśyate | tathā prakṛtyā mataṃ jñātaṃ vedyatvena pratipannaṃ 
sukhādiviṣayabhūtaṃ vastu pṛṣṭhataḥ puruṣo manyata iti anumantā sa 
ucyate | tataśca tasyaiva bhoktṛtvavyapadeśaḥ | itthaṃ sāṃkhyaprakriyayā 
puruṣasya śarīritvaviṣayitvāropo ya ucyate, tatra vivekābhāvaḥ kāraṇaṃ; 
sa ca māyānibandhanaḥ | yadā tu vidyodayāt vivekajajñānāvirbhāvaḥ, 
tadā asāveva puruṣaḥ paramātmā ityabhidhīyate prabandhato 
yathāpratipāditādvayacidekasvabhāvabrahmasvarūpatayā ucyate || 22 ||

evaṃ prakṛtipuruṣeśvaravivekalakṣaṇasya jñānasya phalamāha �

ya evaṃ vetti puruṣaṃ prakṛtiṃ ca guṇaiḥ saha |
sarvathā vartamāno'pi na sa bhūyo'bhijāyate || 23 ||

	yo yogī evaṃ uktena prakāreṇa puruṣaṃ nānākṣetrajñarūpatayā 
avasthitaṃ guṇaiḥ sukhādyātmakaviṣayabhāvena avasthitaiḥ saha vetti, sa 
tattvatrayaparamārthaveditvāt sarvathā sarveṇāpi prakāreṇa 
tattadvarṇadharmātmakena tattadāśramadharmātmakena ca vartamānaḥ 
tadanuṣṭhātṛtayā tiṣṭhannapi bhūyaḥ punaḥ na abhijāyate na saṃsārī 
bhavati yathoktena jñānāgninā saṃsāraprasavahetūnāṃ 
bījasthānīyānāṃ karmaṇāṃ dāhāditi | yathoktaṃ

p. 294)	bījānyagnyupadagdhāni na rohanti ythā punaḥ |
	jñānadagdhaistathā kleśairnātmā saṃpadyate punaḥ ||

iti spaṣṭo'rthaḥ || 23 ||

	atra ca ātmano darśane yathoktaṃ jñānameva yadyapi mukhyatayā 
upāyaḥ, tathāpyupāyāntarairgauṇairapi asau kathañcit gamyata ityāha �

dhyānenātmani paśyanti kecidātmānamātmanā |
anye sāṃkhyena yogena karmayogena cāpare || 24 ||

anye tvevamajānantaḥ śrutvānyebhya upāsate |
te'pi cātitarantyeva mṛtyuṃ śrutiparāyaṇāḥ || 25 ||

	kecit sahajasiddhatattvajñānā niruttaraparameśvarānugrahabhājo 
dhyānena ātmānaṃ paśyanti yathoktabrahmasvarūpatānusmaraṇena 
sākṣātkurvanti | ata eva kena kva paśyantītyabhedapratipādanārthamāha 
ātmanātmānamātmani paśyanti iti | kimuktaṃ bhavati | yatra adhikaraṇe 
hṛdayādau dhyānaṃ kurvanti, yena ca antaḥkaraṇena kurvanti, yacca tena 
dhyānena upalabhante, tat sarvaṃ parabrahmarūpatayaiva pratyavamṛśantīti 
tātparyam | tathāca parā kāṣṭhā ātmajñānasya | saṃpratyetāvatīṃ 
yogyatāmaprāptāḥ anye sāṃkhyena prakṛtipuruṣavivekarūpeṇa 
jñānena ātmānaṃ paśyanti | anye yogena pātañjalādipraṇītavidhinā 
samādhinā, anye tu karmayogena yathoktajñānaniṣṭhatayā 
svakarmānuṣṭhānarūpeṇa samādhinā ātmānaṃ paśyanti, iti 
ātmajñānasya parāparabhedena prakāradvayamupanyastam | tatra 
dhyānenetyādinā śuddhajñānamātrasvarūpatayā ya uktaḥ, sā ca 
karmayogināmārūḍhāvasthā | yathoktaṃ

ārurukṣormuneryogaṃ ------------------ | 6|3)

ityādinā prākpratipāditā, iti dviprakāraḥ para upāyaḥ, sāṃkhyena 
yogena ca, iti aparaḥ sthūlabhedapratipatteranumānāt | anye 
punardviprakāre'pi upāye saṃprati yogyatābhāvāt evaṃ yathoktena

anyebhyo dṛṣṭānumitaṃ tata āptebhyaḥ śrutvā avadhārya 
āgamamātraśaraṇāstam upāsate bhajante | te'pi tathāvidhāḥ 
śraddhālakṣaṇasya siddhibījasya kṣetrībhūtāḥ santo mṛtyumatitarantyeva 
avināśinamātmānaṃ krameṇa pratipadyante satyārthābhiniveśitvāt |

prāpnuvanti | tathāca uktam

iti || 24-25 ||

uktameva arthaṃ sphuṭataraṃ pratipādayitumā adhyāyaparisamāpterāha-

yāvat saṃbhavati kiñcit sattvaṃ sthāvarajaṅgamam |
kṣetrakṣetrajñasaṃyogāt tadviddhi bharatarṣabha || 26 ||

	yāvat yatparimāṇamaparisaṃkhyeyaṃ kiñcit sattvaṃ 
kenacidbhedena yuktaṃ prāṇijātaṃ saṃbhavati vidyate tat sarvaṃ kṣetrasya

saṃyogādanādiprabandhapravṛttādanyonyasaṃbandhāt hetoḥ viddhi 
vijānīhi | kimuktaṃ bhavati |

p. 296) asya aparyavasānasya nānācarācarabhāvarūpasya prapañcasya 
avivecitasvarūpatayā prakṛtipuruṣayoḥ śarīraśarīribhāvena ekatayā

	yogī paramātmanaḥ svābhāvikaikyadarśanāt samyagdarśī 
bhavatītyāha �

samaṃ sarveṣu bhūteṣu tiṣṭhantaṃ parameśvaram |
ninaśyatsvavinaśyantaṃ yaḥ paśyati sa paśyati || 27 ||

	sarveṣu jaṅgameṣu sthāvareṣu ca dviprakāreṣu api 
avāntarabhedādanantaviśeṣeṣu api bhūteṣu samaṃ 
janmādivikārarahitatvāt nirviśeṣaṃ tiṣṭhantaṃ paramārthanityatvāt 
sadā vartamānaṃ parameśvaraṃ svatantraṃ paramātmānaṃ yo yogī 
paśyati ātmatvena pratyavamarśāt sākṣātkurute, sa paśyati tasya eva 
satyārthadarśitvāt pāramārthikaṃ draṣṭṭvaṃ 
tadvyatiriktātmanānātvadarśinaḥ sarve mithyārthadarśina ityarthaḥ | 
kīdṛśeṣu bhūteṣu kīdṛśaṃ paśyati | vinaśyatsu 
vināśāntajanmādivikārabhāgiṣu avinaśyantaṃ 
vedakatvamātrādekasmāt svabhāvādapracyavamānam | sa hi tathāvidho 
yogī samunmiṣitena yathopavarṇitena jñānamayena cakṣuṣā 
prādurbhāvatirobhāvadharmakasakalavedyāṃśaviviktasvarūpaṃ 
vedakaikalakṣaṇaṃ paramātmānaṃ sarvāvasthāsu ekatattvamātmatvena 
sākṣātkurvan samyagdarśana ucyate, anye tu viparītadarśanā ityarthaḥ | sa 
evaca ātmavāniti || 27 ||

tatra hetutvamāha �

samaṃ paśyan hi sarvatra samavasthitamīśvaram |
na hinastyātmanātmānaṃ tato yāti parāṃ gatim || 28 ||

p. 297) yasmāt yathoktena prakāreṇa sarvatra prākṛtabhāvamayavedyavarge 
samaṃ sāmānyasaṃvidrūpatayā tulyaṃ kṛtvā samavasthitaṃ

yāvat saṃbhavati kiñcit ------------ | (13|26)

ityādinā ślokena eṣa eva artho'bhihitaḥ | tadevaṃrūpayā prakṛtyaiva 
kevalayā kriyamāṇāni nirvartyamānāni nānāśarīrasaṃbaddhatvena 
avabhāsamānāni kṛtsnāni karmāṇi vividhāḥ kriyāḥ yaḥ

p. 298) ātmatattvadarśī paśyati samyak nirūpayati, sa tādṛśo yogī tathā 
tena prakāreṇa ātmānaṃ brahmasvarūpaṃ satyasvabhāvaṃ sarvadā 
viśuddhacinmātrasvarūpe svātmani eva supratiṣṭhitaṃ santaṃ akartāraṃ 
paramārthataḥ sarvakriyāsu avyāpṛtatvāt 
svarūpavyatiriktakāryanirvṛttiprayatnarahitaṃ paśyati | kimuktaṃ bhavati | 
yathā kṣetrajñasya prakṛtyabhedābhimānena kartṛtvaṃ, na tathā 
paramātmanaḥ | tasya hi svarūpapratyavamarśamātre mukhyā kartṛtā, 
kṣetrajñasya kartṛtodbhāvanena gauṇī, iti yo'yaṃ kartṛkāryavyapadeśaḥ, 
sa sarvo bhedanibandhanaḥ, bhedaśca māyāmātravilasito na 
vastusattāmavalambate | tata ekasya tattvasya vyatiriktakāryanibandhanaṃ 
kartṛtvaṃ na vāstavamiti tātparyam || 29 ||

pratipādayitumāha �

yadā bhūtaprṭhagbhāvamekasthamanupaśyati |
tata eva ca vistāraṃ brahma saṃpadyate tadā || 30 ||

	tadā tasmin kāle yogī brahma saṃpadyate yathoktaparamātmani 
paramārthasatye ekasmiṃstattve samāpattimāpnoti | kadā | yadā yasmin 
kāle vivekajñānanairmalyāt samyak 
prakāśamānasarvagataikakṣetrajñasvarūpo yogī bhūtapṛthagbhāvaṃ 
bhūtānāṃ carācarāṇāmanantabhedānāṃ 
pṛthagbhāvamanyonyavyatiriktatvam ekasthamanupaśyati ekasmin 
paramātmani abhinnasaṃvitprakāśamātre sthitaṃ tadātmakatvena eva 
vartamānaṃ sākṣātkaroti | kimanena uktaṃ bhavati | 
prakāśapadavījūṣāṃ sarvabhāvānāṃ 
prakāśamānatāmātrādhīnātmalābhānāṃ vāstavīṃ 
paraprakāśaikarūpatāṃ yadā anubhavati yogī, yathāca sarvamekasthaṃ 
paśyati,

p. 299) tathaiva yadā tata eva ekasmāt tattvāt vistāraṃ nānābhāvamayaṃ 
vibhūtivaitatyaṃ paśyati, tadā brahma saṃpadyate | anena api kimuktaṃ 
bhavati | vivekajajñānaniṣṇātanirmalamatiryogī yathā ekasmiṃstattve 
līnaṃ sarvaṃ bhedajātaṃ tadabhinnaṃ paśyati, tathaiva tadeva ekaṃ 
tattvaṃ viśvabhāvabhedātmanā prasṛtaṃ tasya sarvātmanā 
bhāvanānātvapratipattibhramo vigalatītyarthaḥ | tathā ca ayameva arthaḥ 
kenāpi vipaścitā anūditaḥ 

yadyat pṛthaktvasaṃdigdhaṃ tatpṛthaktvānna bhidyate | 

iti || 30 ||

	tadevaṃvidhasya paramātmano bahuśo'pi uktaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ 
prasaṅgāt punarāha �

anāditvānnirguṇatvātparamātmāyamavyayaḥ |
śarīrastho'pi kaunteya na karoti na lipyate || 31 ||

	ayaṃ bhagavān paramātmā paraṃ tattvaṃ svabhāvata eva sarvatra 
sarvadā ca avyāhatasadbhāvaḥ, tataśca sarvasmin śarīre sthitirabādhitā 
eva | tathāpica tatrastho'pi pratipāditaikasvarūpamātre susthitatvāt na karoti 
na bhinnakāryanibandhanaṃ kartṛtvamanubhavati, tataśca kā kathā 
karmaphalānubhavasyeti na lipyate | yasmāt anāditvāt sarvakāraṇatvāt, ata 
eva nirguṇatvāt guṇebhya udayavyayadharmakebhyo niṣkrāntatvāt 
tadviviktasvabhāvatvāt avyayaḥ guṇavat vyayena vināśena rahitaḥ | yadyapi 
anāditvamavyayatvaṃ ca kṣetrajñasya api uktaṃ, tathāpi tasya guṇamayyā 
prakṛtyā saṃbaddhasya tatsvarūpānuvidhāyitvāt 
tattadudayavyayadharmakatvamupacaryate ā vidyodayāt | paramātmanastu 
upacāro'pi nāsti | ataḥ kṣetrajñavat na tasya kartṛtvaṃ, na 
karmaphalasaṃyoga iti || 31 ||

p. 300)	yathāca etasya sarvaśarīrasthatve'pi 
karmaphalasaṃyogalakṣaṇakalaṅkasaṃparkābhāvastathā upamānena 
darśayitumāha �

yathā sarvagataṃ saukṣmyādākāśaṃ nopalipyate |
sarvatrāvasthito dehe tathātmā nopalipyate || 32 ||

	yathā saukṣmyāt aṇīyastvāt sarvagataṃ samastavastuvyāpakaṃ 
deśakālādyanavacchinnamāhātmyam ākāśam 
avacchinnairvyāpyairbhāvaiḥ na upalipyate svadharmeṇa na saṃyojyate, 
tathā eva sarvatra sarvasmin suranaratiryagādyanantabhede'pi dehe śarīre 
vyāpakatayā avasthitaḥ nityasaṃnihito'pi ayaṃ paramātmā taddharmaiḥ 
sukhaduḥkhādibhirutpādavināśādibhirvā 
karmaphalasaṃbandhitvābhimānajanmabhiḥ nopalipyate na saṃspṛśyate | 
tathā ca uktaṃ brahmavidā kenacit 

yathaikasminghaṭākāśe rajodhūmādibhirvṛte |
saṃyujyante na sarvāṇi tathā jīvāḥ sukhādibhiḥ ||

iti || 32 ||

	evaṃ sarvātmanā karmaphalasaṃparkābhāve'pi 
ananyasādhāraṇaprakāśakatvadharmayogāt sa eva ekaḥ 
kṣetrakṣetrajñavyavahāraprakāśaka ityupamānāntareṇa darśayitumāha 
�

yathā prakāśayatyekaḥ kṛtsnaṃ lokamimaṃ raviḥ |
kṣetraṃ kṣetrī tathā kṛtsnaṃ prakāśayati bhārata || 33 ||

	yathā yena prakāreṇa ayaṃ sakalalokapratyakṣaparidṛśyasvabhāvo 
bhagavān bhāskāraḥ kṛtsnam akhilaṃ lokaṃ nānābhāvamayaṃ viśvam 
eka eva asahāyaḥ prakāśayati svasvarūpaṃ lambhayati, tena eva prakāreṇa 
ayaṃ kṣerī sarvakṣetrajñavyāpaka ekaḥ paramātmā

p. 301) kṣetraṃ prakāśayati iti | kimuktaṃ bhavati | 
prakāśyavastunānātvāt prakāśasya paramātmadharmasya yadi paraṃ 
nānātvopacāraḥ paramārthatastu eka eva 
sakalaprakāśyavastuprakāśakaḥ | prakāśakatvamātre ca 
upamānopameyabhāvo ravikṣetriṇoḥ | yathā hi ravirbhāvānāṃ 
prakāśako, na tathā ca kṣetrī | tathāhi kutaścit kāraṇāt labdhātmanāṃ 
ghaṭagavādīnāmarthānāmabhivyañjakatvamātreṇa raviḥ prakāśako na 
janakatvena, kṣetrī tu prakāśyatvāpādanena eva tān janayati, ato 
janakatvaṃ tasya, iti prāk pratipāditameva || 33 ||

taditthaṃrūpamadhyāyena 
kṣetrakṣetrajñaparamātmalakṣaṇatattvatrayanirṇayamupasaṃharannāha �

kṣetrakṣetrajñayorevamantaraṃ jñānacakṣuṣā |
bhūtaprakṛtimokṣaṃ ca ye viduryānti te param || 34 ||

	ye yoginaḥ evaṃ uktena prakāreṇa kṣetrasya śarīrabhāvena 
pariṇatāyā aparāyāḥ prakṛteḥ, tathā kṣetrajñasya 
nānākṣetrāhaṅkārāvaṣṭambhena avasthitāyā jīvarūpāyāḥ 
parāyāḥ prakṛteḥ antaraṃ viśeṣaṃ parasparaviviktaṃ svarūpaṃ viduḥ 
jānate | kena | amānitvādinā tattvadarśanaparyantena jñānena eva 
cakṣuṣā | na kevalaṃ tayorantaraṃ jānate, yāvat bhūtaprakṛtimokṣaṃ ca 
ye jānate | bhūtātmikā yā iyaṃ prakṛtiḥ sthūlā nirdiṣṭā, tato mokṣaṃ 
punastatra praveśābhāvādātyantikaṃ bandhatyāgaṃ ya vidanti | 
kimuktaṃ bhavati | kṣetrakṣetrajñayoḥ 
parasparaviviktasvarūpāvagamamātramāgamopapattipariśīlanadvāreṇa 
kadācit saṃbhavati, natu tāvatā prakṛtermokṣaḥ prabhavati, sa hi 
tadviviktātmasvarūpanityāvahitatvena eva bhavati | tataḥ pṛthaguktaṃ 
bhūtaprakṛtimokṣaṃ ca iti | te evaṃvidhāḥ paraṃ

p. 302) niruttaraṃ tattvaṃ saptame parataraprakṛtitvena pratipāditaṃ 
paramātmānaṃ yānti pratipadyante tadabhedalakṣaṇāṃ parāṃ 
siddhimadhigacchanti, ityom || 34 ||

bhavibhirakhilairnityaṃ tāpatrayīpariśoṣitāt
paramavirasāt kṣetrādbhogo yataḥ kila bhujyate |
cidamṛtaghanāt tadvo'dhyāyāt trayodaśataḥ srutai-
rvacanapṛṣataiḥ siktaṃ sūtāṃ vimuktiphalasriyam ||

sarvatobhadranāmni bhagavadgītāvivaraṇe trayodaśo'dhyāyaḥ || 13 ||

bhagavānuvāca �

paraṃ bhūyaḥ pravakṣyāmi jñānānāṃ jñānamuttamam |
yajjñātvā munayaḥ sarve parāṃ siddhimito gatāḥ || 1 ||

	uktena rūpeṇa paraṃ prakṛṣṭaṃ bhūyaḥ punarbahutaraṃ vā 
jñānānāṃ kṣetrajñādiviṣayāṇāṃ madhye uttamaṃ pradhānaṃ 
jñānaṃ prakarṣeṇa kathayiṣyāmi | vakṣyamāṇena jñānena 
pūrvoktānāṃ jñānānāṃ samyakpratipattirbhaviṣyatīti teṣāṃ madhye 
tasya uttamatvam | ata eva āha yat viditvā pūrve munayaḥ saṃyaminaḥ sarve 
eva parāṃ siddhiṃ parasamāpattilakṣaṇāṃ prakṛṣṭāṃ phalaniṣpattiṃ 
itaḥ saṃsārāt prāptāḥ || 1 ||

yato'sya jñānasya ayaṃ prabhāvaḥ �

idaṃ jñānamupāśritya mama sādharmyamāgatāḥ |
sarge'pi nopajāyante pralaye na vyathanti ca || 2 ||

	idaṃ vakṣyamāṇaṃ jñānam upāśritya sugṛhītaṃ kṛtvā anye'pi 
yogino mama sādharmyaṃ madbhāvābhedaṃ prāptāḥ santaḥ 
etajjñānaśūnyakṣetrajñāntaravat sargapralayayorapi na 
utpādavināśavyapadeśabhājo bhavanti pāramārthikanityatvābhivyakteriti 
|| 2 ||

p.305) atha etajjñānaṃ vaktumāha �

mama yonirmahadbrahma tasmin garbhaṃ dadhāmyaham |
saṃbhavaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ tato bhavati bhārata || 3 ||

	yadetat bhūtaprakṛtikāraṇabhāvena vyavasthitaṃ tattvaṃ 
parabrahmaṇo bhinnatvena āśaṅkitaṃ, tat tasmāt paramārthato na 
bhinnaṃ, brahmaiva tat; kintumahat tadapekṣayā sthūlaṃ yato vedyatayā 
parāmṛśyamānam | mama parabrahmasvarūpasya 
jagadbhāvakrīḍanakotpādanādhikaraṇatvāt yoniḥ tat | tasmin 
paramārthato hi yonibhūte mahati brahmaṇi ahaṃ garbhaṃ dadhāmi 
saṃvillakṣaṇasvavīryasaṃkrāntiṃ karomītyarthaḥ | kimanena uktaṃ 
bhavati | yathoktalakṣaṇaparabrahmasvarūpaṃ parāmṛśan 
prakṛtilakṣaṇaṃ tattvamavabhāsayāmi | tasya idantāyāmapi 
citprakāśānupraveśaṃ vinā prakāśamānatvābhāvāt 
brahmasvarūpatvaṃ, vyavacchinnavedyasvarūpatvāttu sthūlatvamiti 
sakalajagadbhāvabhedaikahetubhūtedantātmakaṃ mahadbrahma mama 
jagatsisṛkṣārasikasya yonisthānīyam | ata eva āha sarvabhūtānāṃ 
carācarāṇāṃ samastānāṃ prāṇabhṛtāṃ tataḥ tasmādidantayā 
parāmṛśyamānāt svabhāvādeva saṃbhavaḥ utpattiḥ | 
tadetadatyadbhutarūpatvaṃ pārameśvaryā māyāśakteḥ 
suprabuddhāvaseyaparamārthamanena pratipāditaṃ yat manāgapi 
svarūpapracyutyabhāve sati itthaṃ nānājagadbhāvaprapañcarūpatayā 
avabhāsamānatvam | etacca evaṃvidhamāścaryarūpatvaṃ garbhīkṛtya 
svarūpaṃ prāk pratipāditaṃ 

daivī hyeṣā guṇamayī mama māyā duratyayā |
māmeva ye prapadyante māyāmatitaranti te || (7|14)

iti || 3 ||

p. 306)	atha laukikārtharūpaṇagarbhīkāreṇa yonyādiśabdaprayogo'tra 
yaḥ kṛtaḥ, tameva sphuṭīkartumāha �

sarvayoniṣu kaunteya mūrtayaḥ saṃbhavanti yāḥ |
tāsāṃ brahma mahadyonirahaṃ bījapradaḥ pitā || 4 ||

	sarvayoniṣu samasteṣu bhāvābhivyaktyadhikaraṇabhūteṣu kṣetreṣu 
stryādyāśriteṣu yā mūrtayo 
jarāyujatvādicaturutpattiprakāraiścaturvidhasaṃsthānabhedena ākṛtayo 
bhūtānāṃ saṃbhavanti utpadyante, tāsāṃ sarvāsāṃ 
yathāpratipāditasvarūpaṃ mahadbrahma idantāsāmānyalakṣaṇaṃ 
prakṛtyātmakaṃ yoniḥ mukhyamabhivyaktyadhikaraṇaṃ, tasyāṃ ca yonau 
saṃvillakṣaṇasya bījasya svavīryasya pradānāt ahaṃ pitā | yathā 
kaścidbrāhmaṇādiḥ puruṣo bhāryātvena parigṛhīte vanitākṣetre 
santānotpādanāya vīryamavasṛjan garbhamādhatte, naca 
rūpāntaramāsādayati, evamahaṃ svarūpādapracyuta eva yathokte mahati 
brahmaṇi jagadbhāvahetuṃ saṃvillakṣaṇaṃ bījamavasṛjāmi, iti | 
svarūpāpracyutimātre pitṛtvena ātmano'dhikaraṇamātratvena yonitvena 
sthūlasya brahmaṇo rūpaṇaṃ nānābhāvahetutve bījatvena cidaṃśasya 
nirūpaṇamiti | yathāhi adhikaraṇabhūtāṃ yonimantareṇa 
santānābhivyaktyabhāvaḥ puruṣavīryasya, 
evamidantāparāmarśamātrasvarūpasthūlabrahmātmakaprakṛtimantareṇa 
sisṛkṣoḥ parameśvarasya 
jagadbhāvakrīḍanakodbhavābhāvaścillakṣaṇasya vīryasyetyarthaḥ || 4 ||

	evamidamiti parāmṛśyamānaṃ svarūpameva prakṛtiḥ, na 
vastvantaraṃ kiñcit; tasyā aparasya brahmaṇaḥ 
prakāśyaprakāśakabhāvalakṣaṇaḥ saṃbandhaḥ, sa ca prakāśādvaitaṃ 
vinā na ghaṭate prakāśyasya prakāśarūpatāmantareṇa tathā 
svarūpalābhābhāvāditi prakṛtiviṣayaṃ tāvat

p. 307) jñānamuktam | idānīmevaṃ ca labdhajanmanastasyāḥ 
śarīrādibhāvena vyavasthitabhūtādivikārarūpāyāḥ kathaṃ nirmokṣa

pratipāditasvarūpā prakṛtiḥ, sā śarīraviṣayabhāvena ābhāsamānaiḥ 
svavikāraiḥ prakṛtyādibhiśca dehinaṃ badhnāti yena tatparihārādeva tato 
nirmokṣaḥ syāt | kintu yaiḥ prakāraiste saṃvedyatāmāpadyante, te

nirmokṣo bhūtaprakṛtinirmokṣo bhavatīti krameṇa pratipādayitumāha �

sattvaṃ rajastama iti guṇāḥ prakṛtisaṃbhavāḥ |

kathamiti teṣāṃ bandhakatvena vibhāgamāha �

	tatra teṣāṃ madhye sattvaṃ nāmaguṇo jñānasaṅgena badhnāti 
niyataviṣayaṃ niyatakaraṇādhīnaṃ yat jñānaṃ, tatra yaḥ saṅgaḥ 
idamahaṃ jānāmi ityabhimānena āsaktistallīnatvaṃ tena paratantrīkaroti | 
tathā sukhasaṅgena badhnāti sukhaṃ tattadabhīṣṭaviṣayaprāptyā 
viṣayāntarākāṅkṣānivṛttiparyavasāyinī nirvṛtiḥ, tatra saṅgaḥ 
sukhito'haṃ sukhaṃ mamāstīti vā ahaṅkāramamakārāvaṣṭambhena 
tallīnatvaṃ; tena paratantrīkaroti | kutaḥ | yataḥ evaṃsvabhāvaṃ sattvaṃ tat 
nirmalatvāt

p. 308) āvarakatamolakṣaṇamalaviviktasvarūpatvāt prakāśakaṃ

badhnāti sūkṣmatvāt | jñānasaṅgaḥ pūrvaṃ vyākhyātaḥ || 6 ||

tathā �

rajo rāgātmakaṃ viddhi tṛṣṇāsaṅgasamudbhavam |

eva tṛṣṇāsaṅgasamudbhavam anādiprabandhapravṛttatvāt tṛṣṇā 
viṣayarasapipāsā, tatra saṅgastadekalīnatvaṃ tataḥ samudbhavo janma 
yasya, tat tādṛśaṃ jānīhi | tat evaṃsvabhāvaṃ rajaḥ karmasaṅgena 
kṣetrajñaṃ nibadhnāti karmarāgaprayuktasya 
iṣṭaviṣayāharaṇāniṣṭaviṣayaparihāreṇa pravṛttistatra saṅgastatparatvaṃ, 
tena niyatakāryakartṛtābhimānarūpeṇa paravaśīkaroti || 7 ||

tathā �

tamastvajñānajaṃ viddhiṃ mohanaṃ sarvadehinām |
pramādālasyanidrābhistannibadhnāti bhārata || 8 ||

	tamolakṣaṇaṃ punarguṇam ajñānajaṃ 
prakāśasvabhāvasattvaguṇapraviviktatvāt jñānābhāvaprabhavaṃ, 
tataśca sarvaśarīriṇāṃ mohanaṃ jñeyaviṣayāpratipattijananaṃ jānīhi | 
tat tathāvidhaṃ tamaḥ pramādena upāyopeyavivekanirapekṣatvena, tathā 
ālasyena pravṛttyātmakarajoviviktarūpatvāt kāryeṣu anudyogena, tathā 
nidrayā mohanarūpeṇa sarvendriyaśaktivirāmeṇa dehinaṃ nibadhnāti 
niyacchati, iti guṇatrayasya svarūpaṃ bandhaprakāraśca pratipāditaḥ || 8 ||

p. 909) idānīṃ yasmin svakārye sati yasya guṇasya utkarṣo bhavati, tathā 
tatsvarūpaṃ pratipādayitumāha �

sattvaṃ sukhe sañjayati rajaḥ karmaṇi bhārata |
jñānamāvṛtya tu tamaḥ pramāde sañjayatyuta || 9 ||

sukhalakṣaṇe sattvaguṇakārye sati sattvaṃ sañjayati samyagatiśayaṃ 
labhate | evaṃ rajoguṇakārye karmaṇi rajaḥ sañjayati | tathā pramāde 
pūrvoktalakṣaṇe sati tamaḥ sañjayati | kiṃ kṛtvā | jñānaṃ 
viṣayāvagamam āvṛtya tiraskṛtya || 9 ||

atha eṣāmanyonyābhibhavavṛttitvaṃ pratipādayitumāha �

rajastamaścābhibhūya sattvaṃ bhārata vardhate |
rajaḥ sattvaṃ tamaścaiva tamaḥ sattvaṃ rajastathā || 10 ||

	rajastamasī abhibhūya nyakkṛtya sattvaguṇo vardhate | kimuktaṃ 
bhavati | yadā sattvaṃ vivṛddhaṃ balavat bhavati, na tadā taditaro guṇaḥ 
svakāryamārabhate | evaṃ tamaḥsattve abhibhūya rajoguṇo vardhate | tathā 
rajaḥsattve abhibhūya tamoguṇo vardhate || 10 ||

atha eṣāṃ guṇānāṃ vivṛddhatvavyañjanāni liṅgānyāha �

sarvadvāreṣu dehe'sminprakāśamupajāyate |
jñānaṃ yadā tadā vidyādvivṛddhaṃ sattvamityuta || 11 ||

lobhaḥ pravṛttirārambhaḥ karmaṇāmaśamaśca tṛṭ |
rajasyetāni jāyante vivṛddhe bharatarṣabha || 12 ||

aprakāśo'pravṛttiśca pramādo moha eva ca |
tamasyetāni jāyante vivṛddhe kurunandana || 13 ||

	asmin śarīre yadā yasmin kṣaṇe sarvadvāreṣu śrotrādiṣu 
prakāśaṃ sphuṭamasandigdhaṃ kṛtvā jñānaṃ 
śabdādiviṣayasaṃvedanamutpadyate; tadā 
sattvaguṇamabhibhūtarajastamaskatvāt vivṛddham upacitam,

p. 310) iti jānīyāt | tathā rajasi pravṛddhe 
abhibhūtasattvatamaskatvādupacite sati etāni liṅgāni utpadyante | tadyathā 
lobhaḥ iṣṭaviṣaye prāpte'pi naiva nivṛttiḥ, tadāharaṇārthaṃ pravṛttiḥ 
unmukhībhāvaḥ | tataśca anantaraṃ ārambhaḥ kriyā tadāharaṇavyāpāra 
ityarthaḥ | tathā prāpte'pi kasmiṃścidviṣaye viṣayāntaraviṣayāṇāṃ 
kriyāṇām aśamaḥ śāntyabhāvaḥ | tathā tṛṭ tṛṣṇābhilāṣo rāga 
ityarthaḥ | tathā tamasi vivṛddhe sattvarajo'bhibhavāt labdhabale sati etāni 
liṅgāni jāyante | tadyathā aprakāśaḥ sattvaguṇābhibhavāt 
prakāśābhāvo viṣayāsaṃvedanam | tathā rajoguṇābhibhavāt apravṛttiḥ 
kriyābhāvaḥ | tataśca svayaṃ vivṛddhatvāt 
pramādamohāvuktalakṣaṇāviti || 11-13 ||
	atha eṣāṃ vṛddhau satyāṃ prāṇaprayāṇe sati 
viśiṣṭalokāntaraprāptinirdeśena bandhahetutvameva pratipādayitumāh �

yadā sattve vivṛddhe tu pralayaṃ yāti dehabhṛt |
tadottamavidāṃ lokānamalān pratipadyate || 14 ||

rajasi pralayaṃ gatvā karmasaṅgiṣu jāyate |
tathā pralīnastamasi mūḍhayoniṣu jāyate || 15 ||

	yadā yasmin kāle sattvaguṇe vivṛddhe balavati sati dehabhṛt śarīrī 
pralayaṃ yāti vināśavyapadeśaṃ labhate, tadā uttamavidāṃ 
prakṛṣṭajñānānāṃ devādīnāṃ saṃbandhino lokān bhuvanāni 
sattvaguṇamayatvāt amalān viśuddhān prāpnoti tatra jātavyapadeśaṃ 
labhate | tathā rajasi pravṛddhe sati pralīnaḥ san karmasaṅgiṣu 
rajoguṇapradhānatvāt tattatkriyāviśeṣapareṣu martyeṣu jāyate | tathaiva 
tamasi pravṛddhe pralīnaḥ san mūḍhayoniṣu tiryaksthāvarādiṣu 
tamaḥpradhāneṣu jāyate iti || 14, 15 ||

p. 311) prayāṇasamaye sattvādiguṇavṛddhau phalanirdeśārthamāha �

karmaṇaḥ sukṛtasyāhuḥ sāttvikaṃ nirmalaṃ phalam |
rajasastu phalaṃ duḥkhamajñānaṃ tamasaḥ phalam || 16 ||

sukṛtasya sattvavivṛddhau satyāṃ yathāśāstraṃ samyaganuṣṭhitasya 
karmaṇaḥ yajñādeḥ kriyāviśeṣasya sāttvikaṃ sattvaguṇādāgataṃ 
nirmalaṃ viśuddhaṃ viśiṣṭajñānasukhasvarūpaṃ phalaṃ bhavatīti 
śeṣaḥ | rajasastu rajovivṛddhau satyāṃ duḥkhaṃ phalaṃ 
saṃśayādiduṣṭacittattvāt, śraddhādiguṇaviśuddhyabhāve yat karma 
kriyate tat pravṛttilakṣaṇaṃ duḥkhameva phalatītyarthaḥ | evameva tamaso 
vivṛddhau satyāṃ kṛtasya karmaṇo mohādiduṣṭacittatvāt 
śāstravidhivaiparītyena prayuktasya karmaṇaḥ ajñānaṃ moha eva phalam 
|| 16 ||

saṃgraheṇa etadeva āha �

sattvāt saṃjāyate jñānaṃ rajaso lobha eva ca |
pramādamohau jāyete tamaso'jñānameva ca || 17 ||

	spaṭārthaḥ ślokaḥ | etasya sattvādīnāṃ guṇānāṃ 
pṛthaksvarūpasamudāyācāraphalādibhedaprapañcapratipādanasya 
sarvāsvavasthāsu teṣāṃ vedyaikasvarūpatāpratipādanatātparyaṃ 
veditavyamiti || 17 ||

asya ca prayojanamāha �

ūrdhvaṃ gacchanti sattvasthā madhye tiṣṭhanti rājasāḥ |
jaghanyaguṇavṛttisthā adho gacchanti tāmasāḥ || 18 ||

nānyaṃ guṇebhyaḥ kartāraṃ yadā draṣṭānupaśyati |
guṇebhyaśca paraṃ vetti madbhāvaṃ so'dhigacchati || 19 ||

spaṭārthau ślokau || 18, 19 ||

p. 312) guṇānetānatītya trīn dehī dehasamudbhavān |
janmamṛtyujarāduḥkhairvimukto'mṛtamaśnute || 20 ||

	śarīraviṣayādibhāvena pariṇatāḥ sarve prakṛtivikārarūpā 
bhāvā dehasamudbhavāḥ, sati hi dehe śarīraviṣayātmakatvena 
samudbhavantīti tadātmanaiva upalabhyatāmabhiprapadyante | tat etān 
pratipāditasvarūpān trīn sattvādīn guṇānvedakaikasvabhāvāt svātmano 
vyatirecanena atītya nistīrya dehī kṣetrajñastadvyatirecanādeva śarīrādito 
nivṛttāhaṅkāramamakārābhimānatvāt śuddhacinmātrasvabhāva eva 
pratiṣṭhitatāttvikāhaṅkāro guṇamayaśarīrādibhāvapratibaddhaiḥ 
janmamṛtyujarāduḥkhaiḥ utpādavināśapariṇāmakleśaiḥ vimukto 
vivarjitaḥ san amṛtaṃ paraṃ brahma paramātmānaṃ aśnute prāpnoti 
tadabhedaṃ gacchatītyarthaḥ | kimanena uktaṃ bhavati | guṇebhyaḥ 
pratipāditasvarūpebhya ātmanaḥ svābhāvikaviviktatvabhāvanā 
bhūtaprakṛtermokṣo, natu śarīraviṣayādirūpāyāstasyāḥ 
parihāramātraṃ mokṣa iti || 20 ||

atha guṇātītasvarūpaṃ viśeṣato jijñāsurarjuna uvāca �

kairliṅgaistrīn guṇānetānatīto bhavati prabho |
kimācāraḥ kathaṃ caitāṃstrīn guṇānatīvartate || 21 ||

	kairliṅgaiḥ abhivyañjakairlakṣaṇaiḥ etān uktarūpān guṇānatīto 
bhavati tebhyo vimuktaḥ saṃpadyate | yaśca guṇātītaḥ, sa kimācāraḥ kiṃ 
tasya ceṣṭhitamityarthaḥ | kena ca prakāreṇa etānativartate vyatikrāmati 
guṇātītatvaprāptihetuḥ ko vidhiriti || 21 ||

praśnatrayanirṇayārthamuttaraṃ bhagavānuvāca �

prakāśaṃ ca prav.vrttiṃ ca mohameva ca pāṇḍava |
na dveṣṭi saṃpravṛttāni na nivṛttāni kāṅkṣati || 22 ||

p. 313) udāsīnavadāsīno guṇairyo na vicālyate |
guṇā vartanta ityevaṃ yo jñastiṣṭhati neṅgate || 23 ||

samaduḥkhasukhasvapnaḥ samaloṣṭāśmakāñcanaḥ |
tulyapriyāpriyo dhīrastulyanindātmasaṃstutiḥ || 24 ||

mānāvamānayostulyastulyo mitrāripakṣayoḥ |
sarvārambhaphalatyāgī guṇātītaḥ sa ucyate || 25 ||

	sa evaṃvidhaḥ ślokacatuṣṭayapratipādyaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭo 
guṇātītaḥ ucyate iti saṃbandhaḥ | ko'sau | ya etāni vastūni saṃpravṛttāni 
samyagabhivyaktāni santi na dveṣṭi samādhiviparyayatvāt teṣu aprītiṃ na 
bhajate ityarthaḥ | tathā nivṛttāni alabdhavṛttaya eva ete bhavatu, iti na 
prārthayate ityarthaḥ | kāni tānītyāha prakāśaṃ sattvaguṇasya 
vivṛddhyabhivyañjakaṃ jñānādirūpaṃ liṅgaṃ, tathā pravṛttiṃ 
rajoguṇasya tathāvidhaṃ kriyātmakaṃ liṅgaṃ, tathā mohaṃ 
tamoguṇasya tādṛśameva ajñānātmakaṃ liṅgamiti | eteṣu 
sattvādiguṇavṛddhicihnabhūteṣu vastuṣu pravṛttihetuṣu nivṛttihetuṣu vā 
nityanirvikārasvarūpapratiṣṭhatvāt yo rāgadveṣābhyāṃ manāgapi na 
āvriyate, sa guṇātīta ucyate iti | etanmukhyaṃ guṇātītasattāliṅgam | 
etacca guṇātītalakṣaṇamuktaṃ vakṣyamāṇaṃ ca dvitīye 
sthiraprajñatvalakṣaṇakathanaprasaṅgena pratipāditamihāpi anuvartanīyam 
| nanu pravṛttimohau pravṛtau santau 
rajastamovivṛddhiliṅgabhūtāvaprakāśatvāt dveṣyau bhavatāṃ nāma 
yoginaḥ, prakāśastu sattvavivṛddhiliṅgabhūtaḥpravṛttaḥ san kasya dveṣyo 
bhavet yena uktaṃ prakāśaṃ ca pravṛttiṃ yo na dveṣṭi sa guṇātītaḥ iti | 
atra ucyate iha abhyāsayogino rajastamoliṅgavat sattvaliṅgamapi 
prakāśātmakaṃ pravṛttaṃ niṣedhyatvāt

p. 314) dveṣyaṃ bhavati | tathā ca pātañjale citiśaktisvarūpamuktvā

	sattvaguṇātmikā ceyamato'viparītā vivekakhyātirityatastasyāṃ 
viraktaṃ cittaṃ tāmapi khyātiṃ niruṇaddhi |

iti uktam | karmayoginastu guṇātītadaśādhirūḍhasya viśeṣo'yamuktaḥ 
yatprakāśamapi pravṛttaṃ na dveṣṭi, naca tasya nivṛttimabhikāṅkṣati | 
tathāhi sati dveṣarāgānuṣaṅgāt guṇātītatā na ghaṭate | tena 
viśuddhasvabhāvāpracyutasaṃvidaḥ prakāśādīni pravṛttāni nivṛttāni 
vā heyatayā upādeyatayā vā yasya dveṣarāgau na udbhāvayataḥ, sa 
guṇātīta ucyate; tadetat guṇātītaliṅgamityarthaḥ | tasya liṅgāntaramāha 
udāsīnavadāsīnaḥ ityādi | yo guṇaiḥ pravṛttairnivṛttairvā na vicālyate 
svarūpāt na pracyāvyate, sa guṇātīta iti svarūpaḥ | kuto na vicālyate iti 
hetugarbhaṃ viśeṣaṇamāha udāsīnavadāsīna iti yaḥ parasya sukhaṃ 
duḥkhaṃ vā pravartamānaṃ tena amiśrībhūtacittatvāt svastho 
nirvikārastaṭastha eva paśyati, sa udāsīna iti ucyate | tena tulyaḥ sa eva 
prakāśādipravṛttinivṛttyoḥ parakīyayoriva nirvikāra eva āsīnaḥ 
svarūpaviśrāntaḥ | ata eva guṇā vartante pravṛttimanubhavantīti | etāvati 
yo jño boddhā san tiṣṭhati svasthitiṃ bhajate, ata eva neṅgate na svarūpāt 
calati, sa guṇātīta ucyate tadasya liṅgamityarthaḥ | etena 
kairliṅgairguṇātīto bhavatītyatra uttaramuktam | kimācāra ityatra 
uttaramāha samaduḥkhasukha ityādi | duḥkhasukhasvapnā iti 
rajaḥsattvatamovṛttaya uktāḥ | samā aviśeṣeṇa nirūpyamāṇatvāt 
tulyāste yasya sa tathoktaḥ | na sukhe prīyate, na duḥkhaṃ dveṣṭi, na svapne 
muhyatītyevaṃvidhastasya samācāraḥ | tulyo mitrāripakṣayoḥ

p. 315) ityetadantamanayaiva diśā kimācāra ityasya uttarametat | kathaṃ ca 
etān guṇānativartate ityatra uttaramāha sarva ityādi 
sarveṣāmārambhāṇāṃ 
yajñatapodānādikriyāviṣayāṇāmudyamānāṃ 
niratiśayanijānandanirbharanirbhayasvātmopāsāpravaṇatvāt phalasya 
svargādibhogaprāptilakṣaṇasya tyāgī nirabhisandhānatvāt varjayitā 
jñānaniṣṭhatvena phalābhisandhiśūnyatayā 
śāstroktasvakarmānuṣṭhānalakṣaṇo 
vidhirguṇātītatvaprāptiheturityarthaḥ || 22-24 ||

guṇātītatvaprāptihetuṃ paraṃ vidhimāha �

māṃ ca yo'vyabhicāreṇa bhaktiyogena sevate |
sa guṇānsamatītyaitān brahmabhūyāya kalpate || 26 ||

	yaḥ ca yathoktasvarūpaṃ māmavyabhicāreṇa 
madvyatiriktaviṣayāntarālambanavyabhicārarahitena bhaktiyogena 
prākpratipāditacatuṣprakāreṇa bhaktirūpeṇa samādhinā sevate 
ārtatvādijñānitvaparyantāvasthāsamucitavidhipūrvamupāste, sa parāṃ 
kāṣṭhāṃ prāptaḥ san etān yathoktān guṇān samatītya atikramya 
brahmabhūyāya brahmabhāvāya kalpate yathoktasvarūpaṃ brahmaiva 
saṃpadyate iti idānīmeva upāyāntaradustarāt 
dvaitamohamahāsāgarāduttirṇo bhavatītyarthaḥ | yato yathoktena krameṇa 
pratyabhijñātādvayacinmātrasvarūpaparabrahmarūpe mayi paramātmani 
suviśrāntasaṃvido yogino natu guṇarūpaṃ kiñcit vyatiriktaṃ 
padārthāntaramavaśiṣyate | yasmāt yathāpratipāditaṃ tāttvikaṃ 
matsvarūpameva acintyamanmāyāmahimnaiva idantayā 
parāmṛśyamānaṃ yathā prakṛtyākhyabhāvasvarūpatāmāpatsyate, 
tathaiva tāttvikā maddharmā amī aparāmṛśyamānāḥ 
sattvādiguṇavyapadeśabhājo bhavanti | tathāca prakāśo nāma maddharmo 
vyatiriktaprakāśyānapekṣanityoditacidātmakajyotirūpaḥ,

p. 316) sa vyatiriktaparimitaprakāśyaniṣṭhatayā tatsvarūpānuvidhāyitvāt 
parimitatvamiva āpannaḥ sattvaguṇavyapadeśaṃ labhate | evaṃ 
svarūpānubhavamātralakṣaṇakriyātmāpi maddharmaḥ, sa 
vyatiriktaparimitaprakāśyaniṣṭhatayā rajoguṇavyapadeśaṃ labhate | tathā 
deśakālādyanavacchinnamahimni svābhāvikīnityā yā sthitiḥ, saiva 
bhinnāśrayaniṣṭhatayā niyamātmakatamoguṇavyapadeśaṃ labhate | evaṃ 
parāmarśavaicitryāt bhāvanāmātravyāpāro māyāśaktiḥ 
prakṛtiguṇādibhāvavikalpātmakamatyadbhutamidaṃ 
trailokyālekhyamullikhantī sarvadehino mohayati | saiva yadā bhaktirūpeṇa 
vijṛmbhamāṇayā vidyāśaktyā tiraskṛtā bhavati, tadā pāramārthikaṃ 
brahmaiva ekaṃ tattvamavaśiṣyate | tata uktaṃ yo bhaktiyogena māṃ sevate, 
sa brahmabhūyāya kalpate iti || 26 ||

atra hetumāha �

brahmaṇo hi pratiṣṭhāhamamṛtasyāvyayasya ca |
śāśvatasya ca dharmasya sukhasyaikāntikasya ca || 27 ||

	yasmāt brahmaṇo yathoktasya ca parasya tattvasya ahaṃ 
pratiṣṭhāmayi tadavyabhicāritayā tattvasvarūpānuvidhāyi pratiṣṭhitam | 
yataḥ kṣetrajñakṣetrādibhāvena avasthitaṃ brahmaiva yadyapi uktaṃ, 
tathāpi tadavacchinnavastuniṣṭhatayā tatsvarūpānuvidhāyi sarvatra 
pratiṣṭhitam | kīdṛśasya brahmaṇo'haṃ pratiṣṭhā | amṛtasyāvyayasya ca 
na vyayo'pakṣayalakṣaṇao vikāraḥ, tena rahitasya; ata eva amṛtasya 
nityasadbhāvasya | na kevalaṃ brahmaṇo'haṃ pratiṣṭhā, yāvat 
taddharmasya śāśvatikasya citprakāśātmano'pi ahaṃ pratiṣṭhā, 
anapekṣitavyatiriktakāraṇaḥ sahaja ānanda aikāntikaṃ sukhaṃ, tasyāpi 
ahaṃ pratiṣṭhā | kimuktaṃ

p. 317) bhavati | prakāśānandākṣaratvarūpaṃ yat paraṃ brahma, so'haṃ 
bhaktilakṣaṇenaiva ekena upāyena prāpya ityom || 27 ||

yaistucchaistrapusīsatāmramalinaiḥ sattvādibhī rūpakai
rdaurgatyaṃ bhajate jano vyavaharansaṃsāradīrghāpaṇe |
siddhi cāsya rase caturdaśamahādhyāyābhidheyātmake 
nītāste paramārthatāṃ dadatu vo lakṣmīṃ kṣaṇādakṣayām ||

iti śrīmadrājānakarāmakaṇṭhaviracite vākyārthānvayamātre 
sarvatobhadranāmni bhagavadgītāvivaraṇe caturdaśo'dhyāyaḥ || 14 ||
